Exact solutions for eigenstates and spectra are derived and compared with the continuum case, showing good agreement in tabulated results. This approach offers a systematic framework for analyzing relativistic quantum transport on branched structures relevant to nanostructures and quantum device design.

</details>


### [104] [Quantum Approximate Counting with Bernoulli Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07604)
*Chengshen Gao,Yongzhen Xu,Lvzhou Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了带伯努利预言机的量子计数问题，其中每个元素以未知概率被标记为正类。作者通过量子奇异值变换（QSVT）实现偏差间隙的相干放大，并结合两阶段自适应振幅估计，给出了$\tilde{O}(\frac{\sqrtρ}{Δε}+\frac{1}{Δ\sqrtε})$的查询上界，实现了对经典采样复杂度的二次加速。同时，通过量子对抗方法的布尔-平均情形组合定理，证明了$\Omega(\frac{\sqrtρ}{Δε})$的近乎匹配下界。对于常数间隙$\Delta=\Theta(1)$（即有界误差预言机）的特殊情形，上界和下界分别特殊化为$\tilde{O}(\frac{\sqrtρ}ε+\frac{1}{\sqrtε})$和$\Omega(\frac{\sqrtρ}ε)$，从而完整刻画了有界误差预言机下量子计数的查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量子计数算法假设成员资格预言机能够精确标注每个元素，然而在实际场景中标签本质上是概率性的（即伯努利预言机），这一假设往往不成立。作者旨在将量子计数推广到更现实的概率标签设定下，探索在该设定下是否能保持相对于经典方法的二次加速，并给出严格的理论保证。

Method: 算法方面：（1）首先利用量子奇异值变换（QSVT）对偏差间隙进行相干放大，在不坍缩分布间叠加态的前提下放大信号；（2）随后应用两阶段自适应振幅估计来估算正分布的比例。下界证明方面：通过量子对抗方法提出一个新的组合定理，作用于布尔-平均情况下（Boolean-over-average-case）方向，由此推导出匹配的查询复杂度下界。

Result: 上界为$\tilde{O}(\frac{\sqrtρ}{Δε}+\frac{1}{Δ\sqrtε})$，较经典的$\Theta(ρ/Δ^2ε^2)$复杂度实现了二次加速。下界为$\Omega(\frac{\sqrtρ}{Δε})$，与上界近乎匹配。对于常数间隙$\Delta=\Theta(1)$（即有界误差预言机），结果特殊化为$\tilde{O}(\frac{\sqrtρ}ε+\frac{1}{\sqrtε})$上界和$\Omega(\frac{\sqrtρ}ε)$下界。

Conclusion: 论文完整刻画了伯努利预言机下量子计数的查询复杂度，证明了即便标签是概率性的，量子方法仍能实现相对于经典采样的二次加速，为量子算法在噪声/概率标签场景下的应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Quantum counting is a fundamental quantum algorithm that estimates the fraction of marked elements using a membership oracle, achieving a quadratic speedup over classical sampling. The membership oracle, however, assumes exact labeling of each element, but this assumption fails when the labels are inherently probabilistic. We study quantum counting with \emph{Bernoulli oracles}, where given $m$ Bernoulli distributions with unknown biases $p_1,\dots,p_m$ and a gap parameter $Δ$, the goal is to estimate the fraction $ρ$ of \emph{positive} distributions ($p_i\ge1/2+Δ$) to within additive error $ε$. We prove an upper bound of $\tilde{O}\!\big(\frac{\sqrtρ}{Δε}+\frac{1}{Δ\sqrtε}\big)$ queries, achieving a quadratic speedup over the classical sample complexity. % of $Θ(ρ/Δ^2ε^2)$.
  Our algorithm first uses the Quantum Singular Value Transformation (QSVT) to coherently amplify the bias gap without collapsing the superposition over distributions, and then applies two-stage adaptive amplitude estimation. We complement this upper bound with a near-matching lower bound of $Ω(\sqrtρ/(Δε))$ via a new composition theorem for the quantum adversary method in the Boolean-over-average-case direction. For the special case of a constant gap $Δ=Θ(1)$, which corresponds to the bounded-error oracle where each query returns the correct label with constant probability, our bounds specialize to $\tilde{O}\big(\frac{\sqrtρ}ε+\frac{1}{\sqrtε}\big)$ and $Ω(\frac{\sqrtρ}ε)$, thereby characterizing the query complexity of quantum counting with bounded-error oracles.

</details>


### [105] [Experimental realization of quantum-computing-enhanced sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07613)
*Yifang Xu,Hongwei Huang,Yilong Zhou,Yunlai Zhu,Chuanlong Ma,Lida Sun,Lintao Xiao,Ziyue Hua,Weiting Wang,Zijie Chen,Weizhou Cai,Hekang Li,Haohua Wang,Xiaoting Wang,Ming Li,Chang-Ling Zou,Luyan Sun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文在自旋-振荡器架构中实现了量子计算增强传感，其中微波腔提供高维量子寄存器，超导量子比特作为传感器。未知信号直接生成相位Oracle操作，首次在玻色子模式中实验演示了Grover搜索，在超过128个光子的希尔伯特空间中观察到量子振幅放大，相比经典顺序搜索能提取更多信息并分辨更多频率候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法具有计算优势，但如何将这些优势融入量子传感并将其转化为增强的信息获取能力仍然具有挑战性。需要建立量子计算与传感之间的自然物理接口。

Method: 采用自旋-振荡器架构：微波腔作为高维量子寄存器，耦合的超导量子比特作为传感器。未知信号直接产生相位Oracle操作，以此建立量子计算与传感的物理接口，并在玻色子模式中实现Grover搜索和量子振幅放大。

Result: 首次在玻色子模式中实验实现Grover搜索，在超过128个光子的希尔伯特空间中观察到量子振幅放大。在相同传感迭代次数下，该协议比经典顺序搜索能提取更多信息并分辨更多频率候选。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明信号驱动的Oracle能够利用基于Oracle的量子算法，为实现量子计算增强传感提供了一条有效途径。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms offer computational advantages, yet incorporating them into quantum sensing and converting these advantages into enhanced information acquisition remains challenging. Here, we realize quantum-computing-enhanced sensing in a spin-oscillator architecture, where a microwave cavity provides a high-dimensional quantum register and a coupled superconducting qubit serves as the sensor. The unknown signal directly generates the phase oracle operation, establishing a natural physical interface between quantum computation and sensing. We experimentally demonstrate the first Grover search in a bosonic mode and observe quantum amplitude amplification in a Hilbert space spanning more than 128 photons. For the same number of sensing iterations, our protocol extracts more information and resolves more frequency candidates than classical sequential search. Our results establish signal-driven oracles as a route for harnessing oracle-based quantum algorithms and realizing quantum-computing-enhanced sensing.

</details>


### [106] [Benchmarking dynamical-structure-factor protocols in programmable neutral-atom geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07634)
*Matteo Grotti,Sergi Julià-Farré,Elisa Ercolessi,Alexandre Dauphin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟中性原子量子处理单元，评估了测量动力学结构因子的协议在更广泛伊辛类自旋系统中的可行性，包括一维二聚化链和二维横场伊辛模型，并分析了其在实际实验条件下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学结构因子是凝聚态物理中的核心可观测量，传统上通过非弹性中子散射在真实材料中获取。近期研究表明可在量子模拟器中通过动力学协议提取类似信息，但这些方法此前主要在一维可积模型中展示。作者希望将其扩展到更广的、更难用经典数值方法处理的伊辛类自旋系统。

Method: 作者利用中性原子量子处理单元的数值仿真（emulation），对测量动力学结构因子的协议进行基准测试。测试对象包括：标准一维横场伊辛链、具有二聚化相互作用的一维链，以及二维横场伊辛模型。同时分析了在真实实验条件下协议的鲁棒性，包括有限模拟时间、脉冲调制、位置无序和激光噪声等因素。

Result: 结果表明，中性原子量子模拟器能够为在经典模拟日益困难的参数区域探测动力学响应函数提供实用途径，该协议在多个非可积/高维体系中均能有效工作，并能在考虑现实实验误差下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了中性原子量子模拟器在超越经典可计算性边界区域测量动力学结构因子的可行性，为实验实施铺平了道路。

Abstract: The dynamical structure factor is a central observable in condensed-matter physics, providing direct insight into the excitation spectrum and dynamical response of quantum materials. While it is traditionally accessed in real materials through inelastic neutron scattering, recent works have shown that analogous information can be extracted in quantum simulators using suitable dynamical protocols. So far, these approaches have been demonstrated mainly in paradigmatic integrable one-dimensional models. In this work, we use numerical emulations of a neutral-atom quantum processing unit to assess the feasibility of measuring the dynamical structure factor in a broader class of Ising-like spin systems. Beyond the standard one-dimensional transverse-field Ising chain, we benchmark the protocol in chains with dimerized interactions and in the two-dimensional transverse-field Ising model, where spectral properties are difficult to access with classical numerical methods at large scales. We further analyze the robustness of the protocol under realistic experimental conditions, including finite simulation times, pulse modulation, positional disorder, and laser noise. Our results show that neutral-atom quantum simulators can provide a practical route to probing dynamical response functions in regimes where classical simulations become increasingly demanding, paving the way toward experimental implementation.

</details>


### [107] [Analog-to-digital conversion in the quantum regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07643)
*Thomas Pousset,Matteo Schiavon,Guillaume Ricard,Elie Awwad,Romain Alléaume,Nicolas Fabre*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对双零差检测的数字化链路（光学、电学、采样阶段）进行了严格的连续模式描述，提出了一个推广的奈奎斯特-香农准则，将测量信号的量子涨落（而非仅带宽）纳入考量，并给出了加性白电子噪声下最优信噪比对应的电子带宽设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管线性信号处理在双零差检测中已被研究，但现有工作通常假设所有后测量处理可被反向传播到光学域，缺乏对涵盖光学、电子和采样阶段的完整数字化链路的严格处理。需要建立更完备的理论框架来实现优化设计。

Method: 采用连续模式描述双零差检测，完整刻画最大模式匹配系数与电子噪声、信号带宽、接收机电子带宽及采样率之间的函数关系；提出推广的奈奎斯特-香农准则以纳入量子涨落的影响；在加性白电子噪声情形下进行数值分析以确定最优信噪比条件。

Result: 数值结果表明：最优信噪比在电子带宽足够大以通过信号、同时又窄于采样率以抑制噪声放大时达到，从而为相干检测提供了具体的设计准则。

Conclusion: 本文填补了双零差检测数字化链路严格分析的空缺，确立了考虑量子涨落后推广的维度确定规则，为连续变量量子信息编码中相干检测的工程设计提供了可操作的指导。

Abstract: Digital signal processing has become essential in quantum optics, particularly for continuous-variable (CV) information encoding, enabling coherent detection schemes and mitigation of linear impairments. While linear signal processing in double homodyne detection has been studied under the assumption that all post-measurement processing can be backpropagated in the optical domain, a rigorous treatment of the digitization chain, spanning optical, electronic, and sampling stages, is still lacking. In this work, we consider the continuous mode description of double homodyne detection to fully capture the maximum mode-matching coefficient achievable as a function of electronic noise, signal bandwidth, receiver electronics bandwidth, and sampling rate, that is the entry point of digital signal processing. We establish a dimensioning rule that generalizes the Nyquist-Shannon criterion to account for quantum fluctuations of the measured signal, not merely its bandwidth. Analyzing the case of additive white electronic noise, we find numerically that optimal signal-to-noise ratio is achieved when the electronic bandwidth is large enough to pass the signal yet narrower than the sampling rate in order to mitigate noise amplification, yielding a concrete design criterion for coherent detection.

</details>


### [108] [Geometric inflation of deviations challenges neural quantum states in dynamics of quantum Ising models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07645)
*Wladislaw Krinitsin,Jonas B. Rigo,Mohammad Abedi,Markus Schmitt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现，神经量子态（NQS）在一维量子Ising模型淬火动力学中精度受限的原因并非表达能力不足，而是极小时间依赖变分原理（TDVP）求解时量子几何张量（QGT）核空间的旋转会将微小的参数偏差放大为物理上显著的误差，且该敏感性与神经网络ansatz的非线性有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NQS是模拟非平衡动力学的有力工具，但在某些复杂度有限的物理区域中，准确的NQS模拟出人意料地困难。作者希望厘清这种困难的根本原因——究竟是表达能力不足还是数值求解方法（TDVP）本身存在缺陷。

Method: 以一维量子Ising模型的淬火动力学作为可控基准，通过有监督的状态重构建立参数数量需求的经验上界以排除表达能力瓶颈；分析TDVP演化中QGT核空间的动力学旋转对参数偏差的影响，并与矩阵乘积态（MPS）的同类TDVP解进行对比以定位敏感性的来源。

Result: 发现NQS所需的参数数量远比先前估计的少，表明表达能力并非关键障碍；真正的问题在于TDVP求解过程中QGT的核空间旋转会将原本无关紧要的参数微小偏差突然放大为显著的物理误差，即存在"几何膨胀"效应。MPS在同种TDVP下保持稳定，说明敏感性源于神经网络的非线性结构。

Conclusion: 揭示了QGT零空间旋转是NQS动力学模拟敏感性的几何诊断指标，并指明了改进TDVP算法的具体方向。

Abstract: Neural quantum states (NQS) have emerged as a powerful framework for simulating non-equilibrium dynamics in strongly correlated quantum systems, offering scalable variational representations of highly entangled states. Yet, accurate NQS simulations have been found to be surprisingly challenging in some physical regimes of limited complexity. Here, we address paradigmatic quench dynamics of a one-dimensional quantum Ising model as a controlled benchmark. Through supervised state reconstruction we establish substantially tighter empirical upper bounds on the required parameter count than previous estimates, ruling out representational limitations as the key obstruction. Instead, we uncover a geometric inflation of small deviations as a hitherto overlooked challenge for accurate solutions of the infinitesimal time-dependent variational principle (TDVP): the dynamical rotation of the kernel of the quantum geometric tensor (QGT) can suddenly lend physical significance to previously irrelevant parameter deviations. The stability of matrix product state solutions of the same TDVP suggests that the non-linearity of the neural network ansatz is the origin of the sensitivity. These results identify QGT-null-space rotation as a geometric diagnostic of sensitive NQS dynamics and as a concrete target for improving TDVP algorithms

</details>


### [109] [Microwave-Free $^{13}$C Hyperpolarization of Diamond Particles Enabled by Magic Angle Spinning and NV Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07653)
*Rémi Blinder,Guzel Musabirova,Daehee Kim,Anshuman Nayak,Jason Twamley,Viateschlav N. Agafonov,Raiker Witter,Jörg Matysik,Fedor Jelezko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了在随机取向的金刚石微粒中利用光学照明结合魔角旋转（MAS）实现¹³C核超极化的方法，无需微波辐照。在7.1 T和9.4 T磁场下，对0.2-2 μm尺寸的金刚石颗粒（含天然丰度和20%同位素富集样品）实现了最大411倍的光诱导极化增强，稳态绝对¹³C极化水平超过0.1%，并通过NV-P1-¹³C三自旋团簇在能级反交叉（LAC）附近的旋动力学模型进行了解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于微波驱动的动态核极化（DNP）在固体核磁超极化中应用广泛，但存在技术复杂性。金刚石中的NV色心可通过光学方法在室温下极化其基态自旋，为核超极化提供了微波之外的替代方案。然而NV自旋哈密顿量的各向异性限制了极化转移效率，需要开发无需微波且能克服各向异性限制的极化方案。

Method: 采用光学照明（绿色激光泵浦NV色心）结合魔角旋转（MAS）的方案，在7.1 T和9.4 T磁场下对随机取向的纳米/微米金刚石颗粒进行¹³C核极化。系统研究了不同MAS频率（0-6 kHz）、不同¹³C同位素丰度（天然1.1%和20%富集）下的极化行为，并提出基于NV、P1和¹³C三自旋团簇在能级反交叉（LAC）附近自旋动力学的理论模型来解释实验结果。

Result: 在7.1 T磁场下，天然丰度样品获得411倍最大光诱导极化增强，20%富集样品获得280倍增强。连续照明下稳态绝对¹³C极化水平超过0.1%。该方案使超过99.9%的NV取向能够参与极化转移过程，显著缓解了NV自旋哈密顿量各向异性的不利影响。

Conclusion: 该工作在随机取向的金刚石微粒中实现了无需微波的光学核超极化，为将核极化从金刚石颗粒转移到外部核素奠定了第一步基础，有望应用于室温下高灵敏、高分辨率的核磁共振（NMR）研究。

Abstract: Nuclear hyperpolarization from optically pumped color centers in solids offers an alternative to conventional microwave-driven dynamic nuclear polarization (DNP). Diamond can host the nitrogen vacancy (NV) center, whose ground spin state can be readily polarized by light at room temperature, making diamond a candidate platform for nuclear hyperpolarization. We report $^{13}{\rm C}$ nuclear hyperpolarization in randomly oriented diamond particles with sizes ranging from 0.2 to 2 $μ$m, both at natural $^{13}{\rm C}$ abundance (1.1 %) and at 20 % isotopic enrichment, at magnetic fields of 7.1 T and 9.4 T. The protocol combines optical illumination with magic angle spinning (MAS) and does not require microwave irradiation. By investigating the nuclear polarization as a function of the MAS frequency between 0 and 6 kHz at the magnetic field of 7.1 T, we find maximum light-induced polarization enhancements of $280$-fold for the isotopically enriched sample and $411$-fold for the natural abundance sample. Under continuous illumination, steady-state absolute $^{13}{\rm C}$ polarization levels above 0.1 % are reached. A model involving optical pumping of NV centers and spin dynamics near level anticrossings (LACs) in three-spin clusters formed by NV, a substitutional nitrogen (P1) and $^{13}{\rm C}$ is used to describe these findings. The protocol strongly mitigates the effect of the anisotropy of the NV spin Hamiltonian, allowing more than $99.9\%$ of NV orientations to participate in the polarization transfer process. These results represent a first step toward transferring nuclear polarization from diamond particles to external nuclei, with potential applications in sensitive and high-resolution NMR at room temperature.

</details>


### [110] [Dynamical Crossover of the Quantum Fisher Information in the Spin-Boson Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07674)
*D. Parlato,G. Di Bello,F. Pavan,G. De Filippis,C. A. Perroni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了与玻色子环境耦合的二能级系统中动力学量子Fisher信息（QFI）对能隙参数估计的行为。研究发现零温纯退相干欧姆环境下，长时间QFI呈现耦合依赖的代数行为，并出现动力学交叉：弱耦合时无界增长，交叉耦合处趋于有限值，强耦合时趋于零。有限温度会抑制长时增长并产生有限时间最大值。零温非欧姆环境下交叉消失（亚欧姆为0，超欧姆发散），但在有限温超欧姆二次浴中交叉重新出现且交叉耦合温度依赖。加入振幅阻尼弛豫通道后，无界增长被有限渐近QFI取代，但纯退相干交叉的印记仍保留在早期动力学中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在于揭示玻色子环境低频谱结构（欧姆/非欧姆特性）与动力学量子传感渐进度量行为之间的直接联系，理清热噪声和能量弛豫如何调控理想纯退相干框架下量子Fisher信息的动力学交叉现象，从而为开放量子系统中的量子计量学提供理论指导。

Method: 采用解析计算与数值控制的矩阵乘积态（MPS）模拟相结合的方法。具体研究了零温纯退相干欧姆浴的精确可解情形，并在非欧姆浴及有限温情形下推广分析；同时引入额外的振幅阻尼通道来考察能量弛豫的影响，利用QFI描述能隙参数的估计精度极限。

Result: (1)零温纯退相干欧姆浴：长时间QFI呈耦合依赖的代数行为，存在动力学交叉——弱耦合无界增长、交叉耦合处趋于有限渐近值、强耦合趋于零；(2)有限温度抑制长时增长并产生有限时间最大值，但保留零温交叉的印记；(3)零温非欧姆浴中交叉消失：亚欧姆QFI归零、超欧姆发散；(4)有限温超欧姆二次浴中交叉出现且交叉耦合温度依赖；(5)零温欧姆浴中加入振幅阻尼后，无界长时增长被有限渐近QFI（与约化相互作用基态相关）取代，纯退相干的交叉印记仍保留在早期动力学中。

Conclusion: 浴的低频谱结构直接决定动力学间隙感测的渐近度量行为：热涨落与能量弛豫共同起到"正则化"作用，将理想纯退相干动力学交叉从理想极限平滑化为物理上可实现的结果，为理解开放系统量子计量中的环境诱导相变提供了系统性框架。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical quantum Fisher information of a two-level system coupled to a bosonic environment, focusing on the estimation of the qubit gap. We combine analytical calculations with numerically controlled matrix-product-state simulations. In the exactly solvable pure-dephasing Ohmic regime at zero temperature, the long-time quantum Fisher information displays a coupling-dependent algebraic behavior, leading to a dynamical crossover: it grows without bound at weak coupling, approaches a finite asymptotic value at the crossover coupling, and vanishes at strong coupling. At finite temperature, thermal fluctuations suppress the long-time growth and generate a finite-time maximum, whose dependence on the dephasing coupling retains a clear signa- ture of the zero-temperature crossover. We show that this crossover is absent at zero temperature in non-Ohmic baths: the long-time quantum Fisher information vanishes in the sub-Ohmic and diverges in the super-Ohmic regimes. At non-zero temperature instead, the crossover appears for a super-Ohmic quadratic bath and the crossover coupling becomes temperature-dependent. Moreover, in the zero-temperature Ohmic regime, we introduce an additional amplitude-damping system-bath coupling that induces energy relaxation. This relaxation channel replaces the unbounded long-time growth with a finite asymptotic quantum Fisher information associated with the reduced interacting ground state, while a signature of the pure-dephasing crossover persists in the early-time dynamics. These results establish a direct connection between the low-frequency structure of the bath and the asymptotic metrological behavior of dynamical gap sensing, and show how thermal fluctuations and energy relaxation regularize the ideal pure-dephasing dynamical crossover of the quantum Fisher information.

</details>


### [111] [Subsystem self-correction of the GKP qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07702)
*Brian Chung Hang Cheung,Lasse Bjørn Kristensen,Frederik Nathan,Michael Kastoryano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从量子信息角度出发，基于子系统码分解，对理想GKP哈密顿量的自校正行为进行了自包含的推导，展示了逻辑寿命的Arrhenius型标度。与基于自旋的量子存储（如二维表面码）不同，GKP哈密顿量在模相空间中实现了有效的弦张力，使得错误接近逻辑扇区边界时能量成本增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动量子纠错（自校正）是量子信息科学中的圣杯。近期理论进展表明GKP码可能具备自校正特性，是自校正量子存储器的候选方案。本文旨在从量子信息视角出发，提供对理想GKP哈密顿量自校正行为的自包含推导。

Method: 基于子系统码分解的量子信息方法。通过物理正交变量q和p耦合时，满足细致平衡的跳变算符被分解为主导部分（仅作用于规范子系统）和边界项（非平凡地作用于逻辑量子比特）。边界项通过模单元边界附近的吉布斯权重被指数抑制。

Result: 理想GKP哈密顿量展现出自校正行为，逻辑寿命呈现Arrhenius型标度。与自旋基量子存储器（如二维表面码）形成对比，GKP哈密顿量在模相空间中实现了有效弦张力，能量成本随错误接近逻辑扇区边界而增加。

Conclusion: GKP码的哈密顿量具有自校正特性，其物理机制源于模相空间中的有效弦张力，这为在连续变量系统中实现自校正量子存储器提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Passive quantum error correction, also known as self-correction, is a holy grail in quantum information science. Recent theoretical advances suggest that the Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) code can exhibit self-correction properties, positioning it as a candidate for the realization of self-correcting quantum memories. In this article, we provide a self-contained derivation of the self-correcting behavior of the ideal GKP Hamiltonian, manifested in the Arrhenius type scaling of the logical lifetime, from a quantum-information perspective based on the subsystem code decomposition. When coupling through the physical quadrature $q$ and $p$, the detailed-balance jump operators decompose into a dominant part acting only on the gauge subsystem and a boundary term that acts non-trivially on the logical qubit. The boundary term is then exponentially suppressed by the Gibbs weights near the edge of the modular cells. In contrast to spin-based quantum memories such as the two-dimensional surface code, the GKP Hamiltonian realizes an effective string tension in modular phase space, whereby the energy cost increases as an error approaches the boundary of a logical sector.

</details>


### [112] [Single- and Two-Qubit Gates Driven by an Integrated Photonic Laser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07708)
*Chris Caron,Zhenyu Wei,Andrei Isichenko,David Heim,Meiting Song,Nick Montifiore,Kaikai Liu,Josiah Dill,Daniel J. Blumenthal,Robert J. Niffenegger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了使用集成可见光波长布里渊激光器（稳定在集成线圈谐振器上）驱动88Sr+光学量子比特的相干单比特和双比特门操作，首次实现了集成激光源驱动的相干量子逻辑门，单比特门保真度达99.61%，双比特Bell态保真度达92.35%，证明集成窄线宽光子激光器能够满足俘获离子量子逻辑的相位噪声要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 俘获离子是量子计算领先技术之一，但其依赖定制化的桌面激光和光学系统，这成为扩展性和鲁棒性的主要障碍。尽管集成氮化硅激光器已实现量子态制备、测量和光钟跃迁探测，但尚未实现相干量子门操作，因为相干量子逻辑对激光器的相位噪声等性能提出了更为严苛的要求。因此，验证集成激光源能否满足相干量子逻辑门的性能需求是当前的关键挑战。

Method: 使用可见光波长集成布里渊激光器，将其稳定在集成线圈谐振器上，用于驱动88Sr+光学量子比特。通过随机基准测试（randomized benchmarking）测量单比特Clifford门平均保真度，并利用双比特Mølmer-Sørensen相互作用产生纠缠Bell态。同时测量了量子比特的裸Ramsey相干时间和自旋回波（spin echo）扩展后的相干时间。

Result: 单比特门平均保真度为99.61% ± 0.03%（每个Clifford门），双比特Mølmer-Sørensen相互作用产生的Entangled Bell态保真度为92.35% ± 1.50%。裸Ramsey相干时间为660 ± 9 μs，通过自旋回波扩展至1.750 ± 0.033 ms。

Conclusion: 集成可见光波长窄线宽光子激光器能够满足俘获离子相干量子逻辑的相位噪声要求，为将光学系统与俘获离子量子处理器实现单片集成、构建可扩展量子处理系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Trapped ions are a leading technology for quantum computing, but their reliance on bespoke tabletop laser and optical systems remains a major obstacle to scaling and robustness. Integrated silicon nitride lasers, compatible with future monolithic integration with surface electrode ion traps, have recently demonstrated frequency-selective qubit state preparation and measurement as well as interrogation of an optical clock transition. However, coherent qubit gates driven by integrated laser sources have not yet been demonstrated because coherent quantum logic imposes substantially more stringent performance requirements on the laser. Here, we use a visible-wavelength integrated Brillouin laser stabilized to an integrated coil resonator to drive coherent single- and two-qubit gates with $^{88}$Sr$^+$ optical qubits. We measure an average single-qubit fidelity of 99.61% $\pm$ 0.03% per Clifford gate with randomized benchmarking and use a two-qubit Mølmer-Sørensen interaction to generate an entangled Bell state with a fidelity of 92.35% $\pm$ 1.50%. The qubit exhibits a bare Ramsey coherence time of 660 $\pm$ 9 $μ$s, extended to 1.750 $\pm$ 0.033 ms by spin echo. These results demonstrate that integrated visible-wavelength narrow-linewidth photonic lasers can meet the phase-noise requirements for coherent trapped ion quantum logic, providing a path for scalable optical systems integrated within trapped ion quantum processors.

</details>


### [113] [Propaq: A Python package for Heisenberg Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07730)
*Hrishikesh Belagali,Ryan LaRose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Propaq，一个用于经典模拟量子电路的高性能、灵活的Python软件包，实现了海森堡传播技术，兼容Qiskit和Cirq等主流量子软件包，并提供多基底海森堡传播、混合薛定谔-海森堡模拟、自定义截断和噪声模拟等独特功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟量子电路是量子计算研究中的重要工具，但现有的海森堡传播相关软件包在性能、灵活性和功能性上存在不足，需要更高效且功能更全面的解决方案。

Method: Propaq实现了海森堡传播技术，支持任何基下的海森堡传播、混合薛定谔-海森堡模拟、自定义截断与噪声模拟方法，并提供详细的日志记录以分析模拟精度、运行时和内存使用情况；后端实现经过优化以匹配或超越同类最新软件包的性能。

Result: Propaq在性能上匹配或优于同类最先进的海森堡传播软件包，并提供多项区分性功能，包括多基底支持、混合模拟、自定义截断、噪声模拟及详细的精度/资源日志分析能力。

Conclusion: Propaq是一个兼具高性能与高灵活性的海森堡传播模拟工具，可为量子电路经典模拟及相关研究提供全面支持，适配主流量子计算生态系统。

Abstract: We introduce Propaq, a performant and flexible Python package implementing Heisenberg propagation techniques for classically simulating quantum circuits. Propaq is compatible with major quantum software packages like Qiskit and Cirq, and matches or outperforms similar state-of-the-art packages for Heisenberg propagation. Beyond this, Propaq has multiple distinguishing features for simulation and research. These include Heisenberg propagation in any basis, hybrid Schrödinger-Heisenberg simulation, custom truncation and noisy simulation methods, and detailed logging information for analyzing simulation accuracy, runtime, and memory usage. We describe these features, how to use Propaq for research, and its backend implementation.

</details>


### [114] [QROB: Quantifying Realization Overhead in Quantum Compilation via Reverse Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07776)
*Jintao Li,Kaiqi Li,Rui Wang,Yilun Zhao,Kaixuan Huang,Ying Wang,Jialin Zhang,Zheng-An Wang,Xiaoming Sun,Heng Fan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了QROB，一种反向构建的量子编译基准生成方法论，从可直接实现的配置反向生成编译实例，作为编译器无关的可行参考。在9至156量子比特系统上验证，发现NISQ编译器最多达24.1x参考SWAP开销、容错编译器最多达7.0x参考makespan，且基于QROB参考训练的router在84.8%真实电路上优于Qiskit SABRE，QROB参考实现的中位镜像电路存活率是完整Qiskit O3的1.65倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子编译需要将程序理想化交互拓扑与硬件局部性约束相协调，但大规模评估缺乏经校准的实现开销参考基准。现有评估方法无法提供编译器无关的可行参考路径，导致难以量化不同编译器的实现差距。

Method: QROB采用可扩展的反向构建（reverse-construction）方法，从可直接实现的配置出发向后生成编译实例，并保留逆路径作为可行且与编译器无关的参考。该方法提供统一的评估基底，适用于NISQ SWAP路由、容错晶格手术调度，并扩展至容量受限的量子存储访问调度场景。

Result: 在9至156量子比特系统上：1）NISQ编译器产生最高达参考SWAP成本24.1倍的额外开销，容错编译器makespan最高达参考的7.0倍；2）基于QROB参考训练的路由器在84.8%真实应用电路上优于Qiskit SABRE；3）在三个156量子比特IBM Heron-r2处理器上，QROB参考实现的镜像电路中位存活率是完整Qiskit O3编译的1.65倍。

Conclusion: QROB不仅可作为诊断基准工具和数据来源，还证明缩小算法级编译差距可直接转化为物理层面的保真度提升，为量子编译领域提供了可扩展、可校准的评估范式。

Abstract: Quantum compilation reconciles a program's idealized interaction topology with hardware locality constraints, yet evaluations at scale lack calibrated references for realization overhead. We present QROB, a scalable reverse-construction methodology that generates compilation instances backward from directly realizable configurations, retaining the inverse paths as feasible, compiler-independent references. QROB provides a common evaluation substrate for NISQ SWAP routing and fault-tolerant lattice-surgery scheduling, while extending its reference-preserving principle to capacity-constrained quantum memory-access scheduling.
  Across systems ranging from 9 to 156 qubits, evaluations highlight QROB's utility as both a diagnostic benchmark and a data source. First, for compiler characterization, QROB reveals substantial realization gaps in existing tools, with NISQ compilers incurring up to 24.1x the reference SWAP cost and fault-tolerant compilers requiring up to 7.0x the reference makespan. Second, as a supervision source for data-driven compilation, a router trained on QROB references outperforms Qiskit SABRE on 84.8% of real-world application circuits. Finally, on real hardware, QROB reference realizations achieve a median mirror-circuit survival rate 1.65x that of full Qiskit O3 compilations across three 156-qubit IBM Heron-r2 processors, demonstrating that closing algorithmic compilation gaps translates directly into physical fidelity gains.

</details>


### [115] [Realization of decoherence-induced averaged symmetry-protected topological phases on quantum processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07831)
*Ruizhe Shen,Xue-Jia Yu,Ching Hua Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在可编程量子处理器上实现了去相干诱导的平均对称性保护拓扑（ASPT）序。从一维团簇SPT态出发，通过辅助量子电路实现子晶格选择性泡利-Z去相位，观测到对称性电荷的衰变所揭示的对称性转换，并通过破坏性SWAP读取的Renyi-2弦关联函数验证了密度矩阵层面编码的ASPT结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SPT相是为纯态在精确对称性保护下构建的，但开放量子系统中退相干产生混合态系综，其平均对称性只能在对微观轨迹取平均后涌现。因此需要探索如何在实际量子处理器上实现并表征退相干诱导的平均对称性保护拓扑序。

Method: 从一个一维团簇SPT态出发，利用辅助辅助量子电路实现子晶格选择性的泡利-Z去相位（sublattice-selective Pauli-Z dephasing）；通过对称性电荷的衰变观测对称性转换；利用破坏性SWAP读出测量两副本Renyi-2弦关联函数；分析约化稳定子扇区间的谱权重重分布以及半链纠缠谱的双重配对特征。

Result: 观测到对称性电荷随去相干的衰变过程，表明对称性发生转换；Renyi-2弦关联函数在密度矩阵层面保持非平凡值，证实了ASPT结构的编码；工程化去相位在约化稳定子扇区之间重新分配谱权重，同时保持了半链纠缠谱特有的双重配对结构。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了在门级量子电路上工程化与探测ASPT序的路线，证明了结构化退相干可作为可编程资源用于实现混合态拓扑量子物质，为开放量子系统中拓扑相的研究提供了新的实验平台和表征手段。

Abstract: Symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases are conventionally formulated for pure states protected by exact symmetries. In open quantum systems, however, decoherence generates mixed-state ensembles in which average symmetry can instead emerge only after averaging over microscopic trajectories. In this work, we realize decoherence-induced averaged SPT (ASPT) order on programmable quantum processors. Starting from a one-dimensional cluster SPT, we engineer sublattice-selective Pauli-$Z$ dephasing through ancilla-assisted quantum circuits. We observe the resulting symmetry conversion through the decay of the symmetry charge. Moreover, a two-replica Renyi-2 string correlator measured through destructive SWAP readout remains nontrivial, revealing the ASPT structure encoded at the density-matrix level. We further show that the engineered dephasing redistributes spectral weight among reduced-stabilizer sectors while preserving the characteristic twofold pairing of the half-chain entanglement spectrum. These results establish a gate-based route to engineering and probing ASPT order and demonstrate structured decoherence as a programmable resource for realizing mixed-state topological quantum matter.

</details>


### [116] [Snakes on a Hyper-Plane: Dynamical and Ontological Locality in Everettian Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07860)
*Travis Norsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文质疑"时空态实在论版本的埃弗雷特量子力学与相对论性因果关系相容"的主流共识，指出该理论引入非局域可变量（非局域区域的约化密度矩阵）会给现有的动力学局域性表述带来困难，暗示该理论可能实际上与相对论因果性不相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学界似乎已形成共识，认为Wallace和Timpson的时空态实在论版本的埃弗雷特量子力学虽然在本体论上是非局域的（非可分离性），但其局域可变量不受过去光锥外干预影响，因此与相对论要求的动力学局域性完全相容。作者认为这一共识需要被审查和质疑。

Method: 通过对时空态实在论中局域与非局域约化密度矩阵的本体论地位进行逻辑分析，论证非局域可变量的存在对现行动力学局域性 formulations 造成的困难。

Result: 该理论除了局域可变量外还设定了非局域区域的约化密度矩阵作为可变量，这对现行动力学局域性的表述引入严重困难，使理论是否与相对论因果性相容变得存疑。

Conclusion: 与主流共识相反，时空态实在论版本的埃弗雷特量子力学也许不应被视为与相对论因果性相容。

Abstract: An apparently emerging consensus holds that Everettian Quantum Mechanics (EQM), in the Spacetime State Realism (SSR) version of Wallace and Timpson, involves ontological non-locality (i.e., non-separability) but is fully compatible with the dynamical notion of locality usually taken to be required by relativity. The claim of dynamical locality is based on the fact that the local beables postulated by the theory (namely, Reduced Density Matrices, RDMs, associated with localized regions) are not affected by interventions outside their past light cones. However, the theory's non-separability means that it posits non-local beables (namely RDMs for non-localized regions) in addition to the local ones. We argue here that the involvement of non-local beables introduces complications for extant formulations of dynamical locality and at least suggests, contrary to the emerging consensus, that SSR EQM should perhaps not be considered compatible with relativistic causality after all.

</details>


### [117] [Quantifying Margenau--Hill Nonclassicality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07869)
*Sudip Chakrabarty*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究基于Margenau-Hill准概率(MHQ)分布的量子非经典性量化问题，引入对数MHQ负性作为非经典性度量，推导出基于低阶矩的严格下界层级（其中四阶矩界在偶矩中最强），并为量子比特导出精确MHQ负性的紧上界及相应极值态。作者还给出任意量子比特观测对基于四阶矩的最优正性阈值，并证明相关矩具有精确多副本表示，可通过干涉测量或经典阴影技术估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准概率分布是描述量子系统非经典特征的重要工具，但完整重构分布往往代价高昂。作者旨在无需重构完整MHQ分布的情况下，仅通过低阶矩来量化量子非经典性，建立更高效实用的判定与量化框架。

Method: 提出对数MHQ负性作为非经典性量化指标；推导基于低阶矩的严格下界不等式层级；对量子比特系统推导精确MHQ负性的紧上界并识别极值态；建立基于四阶矩的最优正性阈值；证明相关矩可通过多副本量子测量（干涉测量或经典阴影）进行精确估计。

Result: (1) 建立了矩下界层级，四阶矩界在偶矩中为最强；(2) 对量子比特获得紧上界和极值态；(3) 得到任意量子比特可观测量对的基于四阶矩的最优正性阈值；(4) 证明矩具有精确多副本表示，支持实验估计。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了基于矩的MHQ负性量化框架，使得仅从有限组低阶可观测量即可提取关于量子非经典性的定量信息，为实验实现提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Quasiprobability distributions offer a useful way of describing nonclassical features of quantum systems through their departure from classical probability theory. In this work, we investigate the quantification of nonclassicality associated with the Margenau--Hill quasiprobability (MHQ) distribution using its moments, without requiring reconstruction of the full distribution. First we introduce the logarithmic MHQ negativity as a quantifier of nonclassicality, and then derive a hierarchy of rigorous lower bounds in terms of low-order moments. Within the resulting hierarchy, the fourth-moment bound is the strongest among the bounds based on even moments. For qubits, we further derive a tight upper bound on the exact MHQ negativity and identify the corresponding extremal state. We also obtain an optimal positivity threshold based on the fourth moment for arbitrary pairs of qubit observables. Finally, we show that the relevant moments admit exact multicopy representations, providing a route to their estimation using interferometry or classical shadow techniques. Our results establish a moment-based framework for extracting quantitative information about MHQ negativity from a finite set of low-order observables.

</details>


### [118] [Measurement-induced remote activation of nonclassicality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07875)
*Sudip Chakrabarty*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明非经典性（通过Wigner和Kirkwood-Dirac等准概率分布的非正性定义）可以被"远程激活"：仅由关联合作者进行局域测量，即可将约化态为经典的一方驱动为真正非经典的受控条件态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非经典性被视为量子优势的关键资源，但传统观点认为非经典条件态的生成需要局域操作。本文旨在探索是否可以通过远程测量（steering）的方式，在不直接操作目标方的情况下激活其非经典性，从而将测量诱导的steering识别为非经典性激活的操作性资源。

Method: 针对两比特系统，推导了最大单分支效应及其概率加权平均值在最优二元投影测量下的闭式解，并证明当共享态无局域偏差时该测量为最优。分析了局域噪声（非酉信道与酉信道对比）对激活效果的影响；通过一个可精确求解的混合qubit-oscillator模型验证了相同的机制能产生Wigner负性。

Result: 找到了两比特系统下最大单分支效应及概率加权平均效应的闭式解；证明非酉且与目标方轴向错位的局域噪声能增强激活效果，而任何酉信道均无帮助；被激活的非经典性使得相同参考可观测量在被测量方未被steer时不可能的异常弱值变为可实现；混合qubit-oscillator模型确认该机制可生成Wigner负性。

Conclusion: 测量诱导的steering是一种可远程激活非经典性的操作化资源，其效果可通过最优投影测量和适当的非酉局部噪声进一步增强，并可在任意后选择下解锁异常弱值，同时在连续变量系统中实现Wigner负性激活。

Abstract: Nonclassicality, defined through nonpositivity of quasiprobability distributions such as the Wigner and Kirkwood-Dirac distributions, underlies key quantum advantages. We show that it can be remotely activated: local measurements by a correlated partner alone can drive a party whose reduced state is classical into a genuinely nonclassical conditional state. For two-qubit systems we obtain closed forms for the maximal single-branch effect and for its probability-weighted average under the best two-outcome projective measurement, the latter proven optimal over all measurements when the shared state carries no local bias. Local noise on the steered party, evaluated relative to its own noise-updated reference basis, can enhance the effect when non-unital and misaligned with that party's axis, whereas no unital channel ever helps. This nonclassicality is shown to unlock, for the same reference observable, anomalous weak values unavailable to the unsteered state under arbitrary postselection. An exactly solvable hybrid qubit-oscillator model shows the same mechanism generates Wigner negativity. These results identify measurement-induced steering as an operational resource for remotely activating nonclassicality.

</details>


### [119] [Riemannian optimization for linear optical problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07890)
*Pablo V. Parellada*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了定义在多端口干涉仪酉矩阵上的任意可微函数的黎曼梯度的闭式解析公式，并将其用于黎曼优化算法（如梯度下降或BFGS）中寻找干涉仪的最优配置。作者以线性光学态制备为应用案例，改进了文献中NOON态和光子催化态制备的成功概率，并展示了其优化器比其他方法快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性光学量子科学中的许多应用需要搜索多端口干涉仪的最优配置，例如通过优化干涉仪来最大化态制备的保真度和成功概率。然而，此前缺乏高效的方法来求解这一优化问题，需要一种基于梯度的黎曼优化框架来高效找到最优干涉仪设置。

Method: 作者推导了定义在干涉仪酉矩阵流形上的任意可微函数的黎曼梯度的闭式解析公式。利用该梯度，将其嵌入到黎曼优化算法中，如黎曼梯度下降或黎曼BFGS，以搜索干涉仪的最优配置。

Result: 在NOON态和光子催化态制备等线性光学态制备任务中，改进了一些文献报告中报道的成功概率；同时，所提出的优化器比其他文献方法快数个数量级，能够处理涉及更多模式和光子的线性光学优化问题。

Conclusion: 该黎曼梯度公式及优化框架为线性光学干涉仪的优化提供了一种高效、通用的方法，显著提升了优化速度和可扩展性，为涉及更多模式和光子的线性光学问题打开了解决之门。

Abstract: Many applications of linear optics in quantum science involve searching for some optimal configuration of a multiport interferometer. For example, to find heralded state preparations, one can optimize the interferometer to maximize the fidelity and success probability of the state preparation. In this paper, we derive a closed analytical formula for the Riemannian gradient of any differentiable function defined over the interferometer unitaries. We use this gradient in Riemannian optimization algorithms, such as gradient descent or BFGS, to find the optimal setup of the interferometer. As an interesting use case, we search for linear optical state preparations, improving some of the success probabilities reported in the literature for NOON or photon catalysis preparations. Finally, we show that our optimizer is orders of magnitude faster than other methods in the literature, opening the door for solving linear optical problems involving more modes and photons.

</details>


### [120] [Few-Body Decay Dynamics in Colloidal CsPbI3 Quantum-Dot Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07892)
*Emma Daggett,Christian M. Lange,Nicholas Favate,Ankit Kundu,Ishita Agarwal,Arya D. Keni,Adhyyan S. Mansukhani,Christina W. Li,Libai Huang,Jonathan D. Hood*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统表征了CsPbI3钙钛矿量子点从孤立发射体到含有多达十个点的自组装簇的光学行为。通过光子自相关、闪烁统计和发光亮度估计每个簇中发射体数量，发现两个或更多发射体的簇表现出双指数衰减，并观察到发射体数量依赖的衰变动力学，即使远离寿命极限的相干机制下依然如此。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶体钙钛矿量子点兼具明亮发射和自组装成紧密结构的能力，使其成为研究从孤立发射体到少量子点簇辐射动力学的理想平台。然而，目前对于发射体数量如何影响集合光学行为（如衰变动力学）的理解仍不充分，亟需建立从单发射体到系综极限之间的桥梁。

Method: 作者制备CsPbI3钙钛矿量子点自组装簇，利用光子自相关、闪烁统计和发光亮度三种手段组合估算每个簇中的发射体数目；通过时间分辨荧光衰变测测定不同数目发射体的衰变动力学；借助光子相关傅里叶光谱评估单点发射的相干性。

Result: 与孤立量子点相比，含两个或更多发射体的簇呈现双指数衰减行为：短寿命分量随发射体数目增加而缩短，同时出现额外长寿命分量。尽管光子相关傅里叶光谱表明单点发射远未达到寿命极限（低相干性），但发射体数目依赖的衰变动力学依然被观测到。

Conclusion: 这些测量共同确立了自组装钙钛矿量子点簇作为研究集合光学行为从单发射体到系综极限之间如何涌现的研究平台，为理解集体光学现象奠定了基础。

Abstract: Colloidal perovskite quantum dots combine bright emission with the ability to self- assemble into closely spaced structures, enabling radiative dynamics to be studied from isolated emitters to few-dot clusters. Here, we characterize CsPbI3 perovskite quan- tum dots from isolated emitters to self-assembled clusters containing up to ten dots. We estimate the number of emitters in each cluster using a combination of photon autocorrelation, blinking statistics, and emission brightness. Compared with isolated dots, clusters containing two or more emitters exhibit biexponential decay, with a short lifetime that decreases with emitter number and an additional long-lived component. Photon-correlation Fourier spectroscopy shows that the single-dot emission remains far from the lifetime-limited regime. Nevertheless, we observe emitter-number-dependent decay dynamics even in this low-coherence regime. Together, these measurements es- tablish self-assembled perovskite quantum-dot clusters as a platform for studying how collective optical behavior emerges between the single-emitter and ensemble limits

</details>


### [121] [A Novel Steganography Scheme Using Quantum Hilbert Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07894)
*Nitin Jha,Abhishek Parakh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于有限维量子希尔伯特变换（QHT）的量子隐写方案，将经典比特以弱有符号相位扰动的形式嵌入量子载体的相位域，接收方通过二元态判别、块聚合和经典纠错解码来恢复隐藏消息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐写术的核心目标是在看似合法的通信消息中传输隐藏信息，但相位域信息隐藏方法在量子系统中尚未被充分探索。现有的量子隐写研究大多集中在其它自由度，相位域作为信息承载维度还未得到系统性的利用。

Method: 首先引入基于量子傅里叶变换（QFT）构造的有限维量子希尔伯特变换（QHT），将其作为一种幺正相位算子。基于该QHT算子，设计了一种量子隐写方案：将经典比特以弱有符号相位扰动的方式嵌入量子覆盖态中。接收端（Bob）通过二元态判别、块聚合以及经典纠错译码的组合策略来恢复隐藏消息。

Result: 提出了一种新颖的QHT-based量子隐写框架，实现了在量子系统相位域中嵌入和恢复经典隐藏信息的完整流程，包括嵌入端的弱相位扰动编码以及接收端的多阶段解码机制。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了量子隐写在相位域信息隐藏方面的空白，通过QHT构造为量子隐写提供了新的技术路径，有望拓展量子安全通信中隐蔽信道设计的可能性。

Abstract: The main goal of steganography is to transmit hidden messages in legitimate-looking communication messages. Phase-domain information hiding, however, has not been fully explored for quantum systems. This work introduces a finite-dimensional Quantum Hilbert Transform (QHT) as a unitary phase operator based on the Quantum Fourier Transform. Using this construction, we develop a QHT-based quantum steganography scheme that embeds classical bits as weak signed phase perturbations of quantum cover states. Bob recovers the hidden message through binary state discrimination, block aggregation, and classical error-correcting decoding.

</details>


### [122] [Quantum Wavefunction Augmentation via Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07972)
*Sonaldeep Halder,Chayan Patra,Rahul Maitra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-WAVE方法，结合SqDRIFT Krylov电路采样和CISD行列式，利用β-退火变分自编码器（VAE）生成新的主导行列式来扩展量子波函数的支持结构。该方法在H₂O和N₂解离中达到相对于全组态相互作用的亚毫哈特精度，并在52量子比特的乙烯系统和60量子比特的Cr₂高度关联体系中展示了可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 样本量子对角化（SQD）在强关联体系中精度下降，因为相关行列式空间超出了有限次采样所能覆盖的范围，需要一种能超越原始硬件采样限制的方法来扩充行列式基。

Method: Q-WAVE采用混合策略：将从SqDRIFT Krylov电路采样的行列式与CISD行列式合并作为种子集，通过定制的β-退火变分自编码器（VAE）在连续潜空间中学习波函数的主要支持结构，迭代生成超越任何固定激发层级的新主导行列式，以扩展基组并使其逼近变分基态。

Result: 在H₂O和N₂解离曲线中实现相对FCI的亚毫哈特精度；在52量子比特乙烯体系中达到相对CCSD(T)的亚毫哈特精度；在60量子比特的高度关联Cr₂压力测试体系中，经最终微扰修正后达到化学精度。

Conclusion: Q-WAVE生成的紧凑波函数超越了直接从原始硬件样本中可提取的信息，为强关联体系中的量子中心超级计算提供了一条可扩展的增强路径。

Abstract: Sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD) has emerged as a promising route for quantum-centric supercomputing, relying on classical diagonalization of the molecular Hamiltonian within a hardware-sampled determinant subspace. However, its accuracy degrades in strongly correlated regimes where the relevant determinant space exceeds what finite-shot sampling can capture. In this work, we introduce Quantum Wavefunction Augmentation via Variational Autoencoders (Q-WAVE), a hybrid method that combines determinants sampled via SqDRIFT Krylov circuits and configuration interaction singles and doubles (CISD) determinants with generative machine learning. Using a custom $β$-annealed variational autoencoder (VAE) model, Q-WAVE iteratively expands this basis toward the variational ground state. The VAE learns the wavefunction's primary support structure from the combined hardware and CISD seed in a continuous latent space, generating new dominant determinants beyond any fixed excitation hierarchy. The resulting compact wavefunction exceeds what can be extracted from raw hardware samples alone. We demonstrate sub-millihartree accuracy compared to full configuration interaction for $\text{H}_2\text{O}$ and $\text{N}_2$ dissociation. Finally, we establish Q-WAVE's scalability on a 52-qubit ethylene system (achieving sub-millihartree accuracy versus CCSD(T)) and a highly correlated 60-qubit $\text{Cr}_2$ stress test that attains chemical accuracy upon a final perturbative correction.

</details>


### [123] [Learned Diffractive Optics for Quantum-Optimal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08014)
*Matthew J. Filipovich,Alexander Duplinskii,A. I. Lvovsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用可学习的衍射光学神经网络（物理可实现的神经网络），在没有先验知道最优测量的情况下，直接针对具体任务指标进行优化，实现相干光和衍射极限非相干光场的态区分与参数估计，显著超越标准测量方法并逼近量子极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学设定了光子受限传感的终极界限，但实际测量中能达到这些界限的方案仅在特殊情况下已知。尤其在视觉传感问题中，目标是从遥远物体发射或反射光场的空间结构中推断其特征，由于物体和光场具有潜在复杂结构，构建最优测量方案是一项具有挑战性的任务。

Method: 将学习型衍射光学元件应用于相干光与衍射极限非相干光场的态区分和参数估计任务，在受限光子预算下进行优化。衍射光学神经网络针对每个任务的品质因数（figure of merit）直接优化，整个过程不需要预知最优测量的先验信息。

Result: 物理可实现的衍射光学神经网络在优化后显著优于标准测量方案，并且在给定光子数条件下以及渐近极限中均趋近于量子极限。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过学习型衍射光学可以在不依赖先验最优测量知识的前提下，于实际物理可实现系统中逼近量子极限，为光子受限的视觉传感问题提供了新的解决路径。

Abstract: Quantum mechanics sets the ultimate bounds on photon-limited sensing, yet practical measurements attaining these bounds are known only in special cases. This is particularly the case for visual sensing problems, where the goal is to infer features of a distant object based on the spatial structure of the light field it emits or reflects. Because of the potentially complex structure of such objects and fields, constructing optimal measurements on them is a challenging task. Here, we apply learned diffractive optics to state discrimination and parameter estimation of coherent and diffraction-limited incoherent light fields under a restricted photon budget. Optimized directly on each task's figure of merit, without prior knowledge of the optimal measurement, the physically realizable diffractive optical neural networks substantially outperform standard measurements and approach the quantum limits for a given number of photons as well as in the asymptotic limit.

</details>


### [124] [Sklar's Theorem and Quantum State Reconstruction from All-Context Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08035)
*Yi-Yu Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从Sklar定理的视角出发，将联合分布的边缘分布与依赖结构分离，应用于局部量子测量产生的Born统计量。研究发现，对于非乘积两比特态，所有局部二元投影测量语境下的"依赖核"可以确定该量子态，最多存在一个离散的双自旋翻转不确定性。结合标量优化与量子总关联，可以复现全局量子失谐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sklar定理将联合分布的边缘与其依赖结构分离，作者希望将该统计视角应用于量子测量产生的Born概率分布，从而提供一种新的量子态刻画和量子关联量化途径，尤其是对全局量子失谐的重新理解。

Method: 利用Sklar定理将局部量子测量得到的Born统计量分解为边缘分布与依赖结构（依赖核）。对两比特非乘积态，考虑所有局部二元投影测量语境，分析依赖核是否足以反推量子态。通过标量优化的方法将依赖核信息与量子总关联结合，用以构造全局量子失谐。

Result: 非乘积两比特态的所有局部二元投影测量语境下的依赖核可以唯一确定量子态，至多存在一个双自旋翻转（double spin-flip）的离散歧义。由依赖核导出的标量优化函数与量子总关联相结合，能够精确复现全局量子失谐。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了copula理论（Sklar定理）与量子关联度量之间的联系，表明依赖核作为一种测量语境下的概率结构特征，足以承载量子态的关键信息，并为全局量子失谐提供了一种全新的构造性诠释。

Abstract: Sklar's theorem separates the marginals of a joint distribution from its dependence structure. We apply this viewpoint to Born statistics generated by local quantum measurements. For non-product two-qubit states, the dependence nuclei over all local binary projective measurement contexts determine the state up to at most a discrete double-spin-flip ambiguity. The corresponding scalar optimization, combined with the quantum total correlation, reproduces global quantum discord.

</details>


### [125] [Quantum magnonics with engineered dissipative and coherent interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08050)
*Debsuvra Mukhopadhyay,Jayakrishnan M. P. Nair,Girish S. Agarwal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是腔磁子学领域的一篇前瞻性综述（Perspective），探讨了该领域从传统相干自旋-光子杂化范式向耗散与相干相互作用的受控工程、Floquet动力学及量子涨落调控方向的扩展，并论述了这些新要素如何重塑集体动力学、推动量子态工程、输运与传感等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔磁子学正超越传统相干自旋-光子杂化范式，进入耗散耦合、非厄米物理、Floquet动力学与量子涨落等新领域。作者旨在系统梳理这些新要素如何改变集体动力学，并展示其在量子态工程、输运与传感等方向开辟的新机遇。

Method: 本文采用前瞻性综述的视角，围绕三个核心主题展开：(1) 耗散耦合与反奇偶时间（anti-PT）对称性如何实现长寿命集体模与增强的谱响应；(2) Floquet工程如何实现非互易输运与相位控制相互作用；(3) 量子磁子学进展，包括压缩与纠缠磁子态（含热磁子压缩）、磁子薛定谔猫态制备方案、参数驱动与有源增益对混合磁化率及磁子-光子响应的放大作用。

Result: 作者总结认为，上述耗散、Floquet、参数驱动与量子调控等手段显著扩展了腔磁子学的应用范围，使其超越传统极化激元物理，成为传感、信号转导、自旋输运及混合量子技术中一个多功能的平台。

Conclusion: 腔磁子学正从传统相干极化激元物理演进为一种可精密调控的混合量子平台，耗散工程、Floquet调控和量子涨落利用将在未来量子技术中扮演关键角色。

Abstract: Cavity magnonics is moving beyond the conventional paradigm of coherent spin--photon hybridization toward the controlled engineering of dissipative and coherent interactions, Floquet dynamics, and quantum fluctuations. This Perspective examines how these ingredients reshape collective dynamics and open new avenues for quantum state engineering, transport, and sensing. We discuss the role of dissipative coupling and anti-$\mathcal{PT}$ symmetry in enabling long-lived collective modes and enhanced spectral response, and highlight Floquet engineering as a means of engineering nonreciprocal transport and phase-controlled interactions. We then review recent progress in quantum magnonics, ranging from squeezed and entangled magnon states, including the squeezing of thermal magnons, to prospective schemes for preparing magnonic Schrödinger-cat states. We also discuss how parametric driving and active gain strongly modify hybrid susceptibilities and amplify magnon--photon response. These advances expand the scope of cavity magnonics beyond conventional polariton physics, establishing it as a versatile platform for sensing, transduction, spin transport, and hybrid quantum technologies.

</details>


### [126] [From Bits to Qubits: The Theory and Practice of Quantum Data Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08058)
*Xiao-Ming Zhang,Arthur G. Rattew,Bujiao Wu,Georgios Styliaris,Xiaoming Sun,Bálint Koczor,Xiao Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于量子数据编码的全面综述，系统梳理了将经典数据编码为量子系统的各类方法，包括态制备、幺正合成、QRAM和block encoding等访问模型，并分析了容错实现所需的电路资源、结构化数据的编码策略以及未来实现量子优势的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典数据编码到量子系统是几乎所有量子算法执行的基石步骤，也是实现实用量子优势的关键瓶颈。由于该领域发展迅速、涉及概念众多且分散于不同文献，亟需一篇系统性的综述来整合概念、算法与实际考量，既作为初学者的教学指南，也作为研究者的参考手册。

Method: 本文采用综述性方法，从历史发展脉络出发，系统分类并比较了主要的量子数据编码访问模型（量子态制备、幺正合成、QRAM和block encoding），从电路尺寸、深度、时空权衡以及容错实现所需的非Clifford资源等多维度进行分析，并专门讨论了结构化数据（稀疏数据、布尔函数和张量网络表示的数据）的编码策略。

Result: 该综述全面呈现了量子数据编码的研究全景，阐明了不同访问模型在量子算法中的角色差异，给出了各类编码方法在容错实现下的资源开销对比，并归纳了结构化数据的专门处理方法，指出量子数据编码在连接理论与应用中的重要桥梁作用。

Conclusion: 量子数据编码是量子计算实现实际优势的关键环节。未来需要在编码资源优化、新型访问模型探索以及与具体量子算法的协同设计等方面继续深入研究，以最终实现实用化的量子优势。

Abstract: Encoding classical data into quantum systems is a foundational step in the execution of nearly all quantum algorithms, and a critical bottleneck in realizing practical quantum advantage. This review provides a comprehensive account of the concepts, algorithms, and practical considerations associated with quantum data encoding. We trace the development from its early conceptual foundations to recent advances, considering commonly used access models, such as quantum state preparation, unitary synthesis, QRAM and block encoding. We survey the circuit size, depth, space-time tradeoffs, as well as non-Clifford resources required for fault-tolerant implementation. We also discuss the roles of different access models in quantum algorithms. Special attention is given to structured data, such as sparse data, Boolean functions and data represented by tensor networks. This review bridges theory and applications, serving both as a pedagogical guide for newcomers and as a reference for active researchers. We also highlight the pivotal role of quantum data encoding in quantum computing and provide insights into future directions that will enable quantum advantage.

</details>


### [127] [Bottom-Up Design of Quantum Optical Experiments Using Discrete Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08073)
*Isaac L. Huidobro-Meezs,Simón Paiva-Ortega,Rodrigo A. Vargas-Hernández*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Grinch，一种基于奖励驱动的自底向上生成框架，直接从保真度奖励中学习采样光学图结构，无需预训练数据集，可用于多体纠缠态、图态/簇态以及非局部光子门目标的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计量子光学实验需要搜索离散电路拓扑结构和连续参数，且同一目标态常有多种实现方式。传统基于图的方法通常优化稠密图再裁剪为单一电路，缺乏直接的奖励驱动生成机制，也难以灵活融入硬件约束。

Method: Grinch采用自底向上的奖励驱动生成框架，直接从保真度奖励中学习采样光学图结构，不依赖预训练数据集。该框架能对无法由图精确生成的目标态识别渐近解，并能融入硬件连通性约束（如限制辅助节点间连接）进行搜索。

Result: 在多体纠缠态、图态和簇态以及非局部光子门目标上取得多个高保真度光学图；识别出无法由图精确生成的目标态的渐近解；发现仅需两个辅助比特的非局部Toffoli门构造；在硬件约束条件下得到CNOT和Toffoli门的替代码构造。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了奖励驱动的量子光学逆向设计方法，能够在搜索过程中直接融入连通性约束，探索替代电路拓扑，为量子光学实验自动化设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Designing quantum optical experiments requires searching over discrete circuit topologies and continuous parameters, often with multiple realizations of the same target state. Graph-based methods commonly address this problem by optimizing a dense graph and pruning it toward a single circuit. We introduce \texttt{Grinch}, a bottom-up reward-driven generative framework that learns to sample optical graphs directly from fidelity-based rewards without relying on a pre-existing training dataset. We demonstrate the framework on multipartite entangled states, graph and cluster states, and nonlocal photonic-gate targets, obtaining multiple high-fidelity optical graphs. We identify asymptotic solutions for states that cannot be generated exactly by graphs, as well as nonlocal Toffoli gate constructions requiring two ancillas. Our work presents hardware-constraint objectives, which lead to alternative constructions of CNOT and Toffoli gates when connections between ancilla nodes are restricted. These findings illustrate a reward-driven approach to quantum optical inverse design, exploring alternative circuit topologies while directly incorporating connectivity constraints into the search process.

</details>


### [128] [Spectral Sign Implication for Quantum Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08099)
*Kenji Tokuo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子逻辑中的谱符号蕴涵（spectral sign implication），该算子通过后件投影减去前件投影所得算子的非负谱投影来定义。它兼容经典实质蕴涵，满足Hardegree的四条最小蕴涵条件、逆否律（contraposition）及假条件，但不同于标准多项式蕴涵——其值不必属于由参数生成的ortholattice。在满足特定条件的Borel构造类中，满足推导、逆否与假条件的算子恰好对应谱分支的可测选择；在连续子类中这三条公理唯一确定该蕴涵。有限维下，该运算还是Helstrom判别最优投影测试的接受投影中最大的一个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子逻辑中的蕴涵算子长期缺乏一个同时满足基本蕴涵条件又与经典逻辑相容的构造。已有多项式蕴涵虽满足某些条件，但其值受限于参数生成的ortholattice，且缺乏与算子理论和量子信息理论的自然联系。作者旨在引入一种基于两投影之差谱结构的构造，统一满足多条公理，并揭示其与量子判别理论的深层关联。

Method: 本文提出谱符号蕴涵：对量子逻辑命题（投影）p与q，构造q−p这一自伴算子的非负谱投影E((0,∞))(q−p)作为蕴涵p→q的结果。作者验证其满足Hardegree四条最小蕴涵条件、逆否律及假条件；在一般的Borel两投影构造类中刻画满足entailment、contraposition与falsity条件的运算与可测谱分支选择之间的对应关系；证明连续子类中这些条件唯一确定该蕴涵；在有限维情形将其与Helstrom判别中基于rank的先验最优投影测试相联系，证明其为最大接受投影。

Result: 谱符号蕴涵在交换投影上与经典材料蕴涵一致；对任意投影对通过其差值的谱结构进行比较。它满足一系列关键公理。不同于多项式蕴涵，其输出不必属于参数生成的ortholattice。在Borel构造的统一类中，三条条件（entailment、contraposition、falsity）精确刻画可测谱分支选择；连续情形下唯一性成立。有限维中，该蕴涵等于Helstrom最优测试中的最大接受投影，建立了量子蕴涵与二元量子判别之间的联系。

Conclusion: 谱符号蕴涵为量子逻辑提供了一种自然、公理化上完备的蕴涵算子，它通过与量子信息论中判别理论的连接，展现了投影算子代数结构的丰富性。该工作为量子蕴涵的谱构造方向开辟了新路径，并可作为后续在无穷维量子逻辑中研究熵、判别与推理关系的基础。

Abstract: We study the spectral sign implication for quantum logic, defined by the nonnegative spectral projection of the operator obtained by subtracting the antecedent projection from the consequent projection. The construction agrees with classical material implication on commuting projections and compares arbitrary pairs of projections through the spectral structure of their difference. It satisfies Hardegree's four minimal implicative conditions, his law of contraposition, and a falsity condition. It differs from the standard polynomial implications in that its value need not belong to the ortholattice generated by its arguments. Within a uniform class of Borel constructions for pairs of projections, the operations satisfying entailment, contraposition, and the falsity condition correspond exactly to measurable choices of spectral branch. In the continuous subclass, these three conditions determine the spectral sign implication uniquely. In finite dimensions, the operation is also the largest among the acceptance projections of optimal projective tests for Helstrom discrimination between subspace states with priors proportional to rank. These results connect quantum implication with the operator theory of two projections and binary quantum discrimination.

</details>


### [129] [Quantum algorithm for PageRank computation through multistep quantum resonant transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08140)
*Chuqing Wang,Hefeng Wang,Hua Xiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多步量子共振跃迁（mQRT）的量子算法，用于获得编码Google矩阵PageRank向量的量子态。通过将网页图划分为嵌套子图并构造一系列中间哈密顿量，形成从简单初始哈密顿量到问题哈密顿量的演化路径，逐步通过QRT获得问题哈密顿量的基态。该算法仅需一个辅助量子比特，运行时间与步数成正比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PageRank向量在大规模网络分析中具有重要应用，但经典计算在大规模网络中面临效率瓶颈。现有量子PageRank算法通常需要多个辅助量子比特或多个查询步骤，存在资源开销大、实现复杂等问题。因此需要一种更高效、更简洁的量子算法来获得PageRank向量的量子态。

Method: 该算法将PageRank向量编码为与Google矩阵关联的问题哈密顿量的基态。具体步骤为：1) 将对应Google矩阵的网页图划分为具有嵌套结构的一系列子图；2) 基于这些子图构造一组中间哈密顿量序列；3) 形成从简单初始哈密顿量到问题哈密顿量的演化路径；4) 通过多步量子共振跃迁（mQRT），依次经过各中间哈密顿量的基态，最终到达问题哈密顿量的基态。

Result: 该算法仅需要一个辅助量子比特，比现有方法所需资源更少。算法运行时间与步数（即构造的哈密顿量序列长度）成正比，具有线性时间复杂度。该方法为高效获取大规模网络PageRank向量的量子态提供了新途径。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种新颖且资源高效的量子PageRank算法，通过多步量子共振跃迁实现了PageRank向量量子态的获取，在辅助量子比特开销和运行时间方面均具有优势，为大规模网络分析中的量子计算应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present a quantum algorithm for obtaining a quantum state that encodes the PageRank vector of the Google matrix through multistep quantum resonant transition (mQRT). In the algorithm, the PageRank vector is encoded in the ground state of a problem Hamiltonian associated with the Google matrix. By dividing the web graph corresponding to the Google matrix into a series of subgraphs with nested structure, we construct a sequence of Hamiltonians based on the subgraphs to form a Hamiltonian evolution path from a simple initial Hamiltonian to the problem Hamiltonian. The ground state of the problem Hamiltonian is obtained by going through ground states of the intermediate Hamiltonians via QRT step by step. This algorithm requires only one ancillary qubit, and the runtime of the algorithm is proportional to the number of steps. It provides a new way for efficiently obtaining the quantum state of the PageRank vector of large-scale networks.

</details>


### [130] [Quantum-state texture measure and texture transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08163)
*Yufan Lin,Yu Guo,Fei He,Shuanping Du*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在多个方向上扩展了量子态纹理的理论：研究了纯态在纹理自由操作下的可转换性，并完整解决了量子比特情况下任意两态间的确定性转换问题；提出了基于凸函数的纹理度量和基于纯态最小纹理代价的纹理成本两类新的度量；并证明了每个纹理单调性都能导出一个满足凸包扩展所需性质的函数，为该构造提供了反方向的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态纹理量化了某一选定基下态的结构不规则性，已成为通用电路中门表征的重要资源。此前已提出基于收缩距离的纹理度量类以及凸包扩展纹理度量的构造框架，本文旨在从转换性、新度量类以及构造框架的完备性等方向进一步扩展该理论。

Method: 本文采用资源理论的方法：(1) 研究纯态在纹理自由操作下到任意态的转换性，并在量子比特情形下完整求解任意两态间的确定性转换问题；(2) 提出两类新的纹理度量：基于凸函数的度量，以及通过纯态所含纹理的最小代价定义的纹理成本；(3) 证明每个纹理单调性都可导出具有凸包扩展中所用函数相同性质的非负函数，从而为该构造提供逆命题。

Result: 纯态到任意态在纹理自由操作下的可转换性得到了检验；量子比特情形下任意两态的确定性转换问题被完整求解；提出了凸函数类纹理度量和纹理成本两类新度量；建立了纹理单调性与凸包扩展所需函数之间的等价关系（逆命题成立）。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子态纹理提供了较为全面的资源理论表述，涵盖态转换、新型度量类构造以及凸包扩展框架的完备性证明，从而为纹理在量子信息处理中的应用（如门表征）奠定了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Quantum-state texture quantifies structural irregularities of a state in a selected basis and has emerged as a valuable resource for gate characterization in universal circuits. Consequently, a class of contractive distance based texture measures and a framework for constructing convex-roof extended texture measure have been proposed. Here, we extend this theory in several directions. We examine the convertibility of a pure state to any state under texture-free operations, and fully solve the deterministic transformation problem between any two states in the qubit case. We then propose two classes of texture measures: convex-function-based measures and texture cost defined via minimal cost of texture contained in pure states. Moreover, we show that every texture monotone gives rise to a non-negative function which admits the same properties as the function used in the convex-roof extension---thus providing a converse to that construction. Our work thereby offers a relatively comprehensive resource-theoretic formulation of quantum-state texture.

</details>


### [131] [Saving resources through repeat-until-success positive-operator-valued-measure measurements in quantum computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08168)
*Hefeng Wang,Sixia Yu,Hua Xiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于POVM测量的量子计算方法，通过辅助量子比特与工作量子比特的耦合及重复直至成功的测量过程，引导计算沿给定路径进行。与将中间测量延迟到电路末端的方案相比，该方法可在多项式级别减少量子比特数和酉操作数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子电路通常将所有测量延迟到电路末端（延迟测量原理），但中间测量在某些情况下可能带来计算效率或资源上的优势。作者希望探索引入中间POVM测量是否能为量子计算提供资源优化，特别是在减少量子比特数和酉操作数量方面。

Method: 该方法的核心步骤为：(1) 将一个辅助量子比特与工作量子比特寄存器耦合；(2) 通过整体酉操作配合对辅助比特的投影测量，在工作量子比特上有效实现POVM测量；(3) 每一步计算均为重复直至成功（repeat-until-success）过程，通过POVM测量在工作量子比特上确定性地获得期望状态；(4) 将辅助比特上的中间测量引入计算过程而非延迟到电路末端；(5) 提供了该方法的实验实现方案。

Result: 作者证明了在该方法中引入辅助比特上的中间测量相比于将中间测量延迟到计算末端，可在多项式级别上减少所需的量子比特数量和酉操作数量。同时给出了该方法的物理实现方案。

Conclusion: 通过引入POVM中间测量，量子计算可以在资源消耗（量子比特数与酉操作数）方面获得多项式级别的优化，说明延迟测量原理虽在功能上等价，但在资源效率上可能不是最优的，为量子计算资源优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present a quantum computation approach in which computation is guided by positive-operator-valuedmeasure (POVM) measurements following a given computation path in multisteps. In this approach, one ancillary qubit is coupled to a register of working qubits, and a POVM measurement is implemented effectively on the working qubits by applying a unitary operation on the whole system followed by a projective measurement performed on the ancillary qubit. Each step of the computation is a repeat-until-success procedure such that the desired state of the step is obtained deterministically on the working qubits via POVM measurements. The principle of deferred measurement states that measurements can always be moved from an intermediate stage of a quantum circuit to the end of the circuit without affecting the efficiency of the computation.We demonstrate that in our approach, by introducing intermediate measurements on the ancillary qubit in the computation process, both the number of qubits and unitary operations can be reduced polynomially, compared to the case where the intermediate measurements are deferred to the end of the computation. We also provide a method for implementation of the approach.

</details>


### [132] [Classical Active-Space Hybrid Quantum Subspace Expansion (CASH-QSE): Quantum Corrections without Remeasuring the Classically Calculable Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08170)
*Artur F. Izmaylov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出CASH-QSE方法，将波函数表示为经典CASSCF参考态与精确正交的量子态的组合，避免重叠测量并简化算符，在H₂O和N₂键拉伸等弱到强关联区间达到化学精度，且相比ADAPT-VQE大幅降低采样成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQE制备的波函数往往含有大量经典可处理成分，却仍需在量子设备上采样其能量贡献；现有方法（将波函数拆分为经典参考与量子态组合并只对量子部分采样）存在近线性依赖问题，会放大测量误差并使能量估计不稳定。

Method: CASH-QSE将CASSCF参考态经典保留，利用占据结构构造与参考态彼此精确正交的量子态，从而将问题转化为一个无需重叠测量的普通厄米特征值问题；同时占据约束简化了被测算符，并使用全组态相互作用（FCI）指导组件的选取。

Result: 在H₂O和N₂沿键拉伸坐标（覆盖弱到强关联区间）的基准测试中（STO-3G以及H₂O的受限cc-pVDZ轨道空间），CASH-QSE达到化学精度，最大的完整测量电路限制在几百个all-to-all逻辑CNOT门以内；在有利情形下，理想化最终能量采样成本相比ADAPT-VQE降低若干数量级。

Conclusion: CASH-QSE通过经典-量子混合的子空间展开策略，在保持化学精度的同时显著降低量子电路复杂度和采样开销，为VQE类量子化学计算提供了一种更高效且数值上更稳健的替代方案。

Abstract: The wave function prepared by a variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) can contain a substantial classically tractable component, yet its energy contribution is sampled on the quantum device. Representing the wave function as a combination of a classical reference and separately prepared quantum states allows the reference energy to be evaluated without quantum sampling. However, near-linear dependence among these components can amplify measurement errors and destabilize the resulting energy estimate. We introduce Classical Active-Space Hybrid Quantum Subspace Expansion (CASH-QSE), which retains a complete-active-space self-consistent field (CASSCF) reference classically and uses occupation structure to construct quantum states exactly orthogonal to the reference and to one another. The energy follows from an ordinary Hermitian eigenvalue problem without overlap measurements, while the same occupation constraints simplify the measured operators. Using full configuration interaction to guide component selection, we test CASH-QSE along H$_2$O and N$_2$ bond-stretching coordinates spanning weakly to strongly correlated regimes. The benchmarks use STO--3G for both molecules and a restricted cc-pVDZ orbital space for H$_2$O. CASH-QSE reaches chemical accuracy while limiting the largest complete measurement circuits to a few hundred all-to-all logical controlled-NOT gates. In favorable cases, it also reduces the idealized final-energy sampling cost by several orders of magnitude relative to VQE with an adaptive derivative-assembled pseudo-Trotter ansatz (ADAPT-VQE).

</details>


### [133] [Transversal Fanout for Fault Tolerant Distributed Quantum Computing: Analysis and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08233)
*Seng W. Loke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在容错分布式量子计算中，利用量子纠错码块上的横向操作来实现逻辑扇出操作（fanon-out）的资源高效方法。通过利用编码块的结构和横向逻辑操作，构造了能够减少所需非本地通信的分布式扇出电路，同时保持逻辑作用不变。研究以Bivariate Bicycle (BB)码块为例进行说明，分析了物理门、纠缠、电路深度和辅助量子比特等需求，并进一步研究了利用分布式横向扇出的并发性实现全局门GCZ的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子计算中，逻辑扇出操作（从公共控制量子比特到远程节点的多个目标量子比特的CNOT操作）是重要原语，但直接在编码块之间实现时需要大量非本地通信。需要一种资源高效的方法来减少通信开销，同时保持容错和逻辑正确性。

Method: 论文利用量子纠错码编码块的结构以及横向逻辑操作的可用性，构造分布式扇出电路。该方法针对编码后的量子信息开发，以BB码块为例进行示例说明。通过减少所需的非本地操作来降低通信成本，同时保持扇出操作的逻辑作用。此外，利用分布式横向扇出的并发性，实现涉及BB码逻辑量子比特的全局门GCZ的分布式实现。

Result: 对所得方法的物理门数量、纠缠资源、电路深度和辅助量子比特需求进行了系统分析。该方案为在分布式纠错量子处理器上实现大规模逻辑扇出操作提供了系统性方法，并展示了利用横向扇出并发性实现全局门GCZ的可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种系统性的途径，可在分布式容错量子处理器上高效实现大型逻辑扇出操作和全局GCZ门，通过横向操作减少非本地通信开销，为分布式量子计算中的关键通信原语提供了资源优化方案。

Abstract: We study a resource-efficient approach for implementing logical fanout operations in fault-tolerant distributed quantum computing using transversal operations on quantum error-correcting code blocks. Logical fanout, comprising multiple controlled-NOT operations from a common control qubit to target qubits located at remote nodes, is an important primitive for distributed quantum computation but can require substantial non-local communication when implemented directly between encoded blocks. We exploit the structure of encoded blocks and the availability of transversal logical operations to construct distributed fanout circuits that reduce the required non-local operations while preserving the logical action of the fanout operation. The construction is developed for encoded quantum information and illustrated using Bivariate Bicycle (BB)-code blocks. We analyze the resulting physical gate, entanglement, circuit-depth, and ancilla requirements. The approach provides a systematic method for implementing large logical fanout operations across distributed error-corrected quantum processors. Also, we study a distributed implementation of the global gate GCZ involving logical qubits (encoded using BB-code blocks), exploiting the concurrency in transversal distributed fanouts.

</details>


### [134] [Instantaneous-Frame Theory of Strongly Driven Parametric Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08348)
*Kentaro Kubo,Shinichi Inoue,Jushin Tei,Yinghao Ho,Yasunobu Nakamura,Hayato Goto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于瞬时本征态的参量双量子比特门理论框架。与传统的空闲本征基失理论不同，本文将参量门描述为瞬时本征态之间的相干旋转，利用非阿贝尔Berry联络来刻画跃迁及有效交换耦合和ZZ耦合。该理论在多个可调耦合器架构（双transmon、电容分流双transmon、单transmon耦合器）中与精确数值模拟定量吻合，且在传统空闲框架失效的大振幅区域仍保持准确，显著降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可调耦合器的参量双量子比特门是实现可扩展超导量子处理器的有前景方案，但传统在空闲本征基下建立的理论在大振幅（高速门操作所需）区域会失效，亟需一种能准确描述该区域的新理论框架。

Method: 本文将参量门重新描述为瞬时本征态之间的相干旋转（而非空闲本征态），利用非阿贝尔Berry联络作为驱动动力学中的自然移动框架来描述跃迁，并由此给出交换耦合和有效ZZ耦合的紧凑表达式。作者在多种可调耦合器架构中对该理论进行了数值验证。

Result: 该瞬时框架理论在双transmon、电容分流双transmon及单transmon等多种代表性可调耦合器架构中与精确数值模拟取得定量一致。即使在空闲本征框架描述失效的大振幅区域，该理论仍保持准确，同时大幅减少了对计算密集型精确模拟的依赖。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了超越传统空闲框架的参量门瞬时框架理论，为理解参量门及驱动量子动力学提供了新视角，有助于降低超导量子处理器设计中精确模拟的计算负担。

Abstract: Parametric two-qubit gates using tunable couplers are a promising approach to scalable superconducting quantum processors. However, conventional theories formulated in the idle eigenbasis break down in the large-amplitude regime required for fast gate operations. Here, we show that parametric gates are more naturally and accurately described as coherent rotations between instantaneous eigenstates rather than idle eigenstates. This approach may appear counterintuitive, since instantaneous eigenbases are usually associated with adiabatic dynamics. However, they also form a natural moving frame for driven dynamics, where the non-Abelian Berry connection governs transitions and provides a compact description of the exchange and effective $ZZ$ couplings. We show that this theory achieves quantitative agreement with exact numerical simulations across representative tunable-coupler architectures, including double-transmon, capacitively shunted double-transmon, and single-transmon couplers. The description remains accurate even in regimes where idle-frame descriptions fail, while substantially reducing the reliance on computationally demanding exact simulations. These results establish an instantaneous-frame theory of parametric gates beyond conventional idle-frame models and provide a new perspective on driven quantum dynamics.

</details>


### [135] [Scaling-Enhanced Rapid Readout of a Qubit Ensemble Assisted by High-Frequency Detector Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08374)
*Hiroki Nakabayashi,Yuichiro Matsuzaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型量子比特集合集体读出的时间标度问题，建立了两种探测器类别的读时标度规律：无紫外高频模式的探测器遵循N^(-1/2)基准，而具有无界高频模式的探测器可超越此基准，实现N^(-1/(2-ν))的特征时间标度（0<ν<2），表明高频探测器模式是实现集体量子读出标度优势的关键资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子比特集合的集体读出的时间如何随量子比特数N标度尚不清楚，理解这一标度关系对于快速集体量子读出的实现至关重要，但目前缺乏系统的理论和基准。

Method: 作者通过理论分析建立了基准标度N^(-1/2)，适用于不包含紫外高频模式的一大类探测器；随后分析了具有无界高频模式的探测器，发现其能超越该基准，并推导出由探测器谱结构参数ν控制标度指数的新标度律N^(-1/(2-ν))。

Result: 论文表明，探测器的高频模式可作为资源以实现在集体量子读出中的标度优势，探测器的频谱结构直接控制读出时间的标度指数。无紫外高频模式探测器的读出时间基准为N^(-1/2)，有界高频模式探测器可实现更优的N^(-1/(2-ν))标度。

Conclusion: 研究建立了集体量子读出的标度理论基准，揭示高频探测器模式的作用，指出探测器频谱结构是调控读出时间标度性能的核心因素，为实现快速集体量子测量提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Quantum measurements of large qubit ensembles are often performed indirectly by coupling the ensemble to a detector and subsequently measuring the detector. Despite the importance of rapid collective readout, it remains unclear what determines how the readout time scales with the number of qubits $N$. Here, we first establish a benchmark $N^{-1/2}$ for a broad class of detectors without ultraviolet high-frequency modes. We then show that the detector with unbounded high-frequency modes can surpass this benchmark and yield a characteristic time scaling $N^{-1/(2-ν)}$ for $0 < ν< 2$, where $ν$ characterizes the spectral structure of the detector. Our results establish high-frequency detector modes as a resource for achieving a scaling advantage in collective quantum readout, with the detector spectrum directly controlling the scaling exponent of the readout time.

</details>


### [136] [Dynamics of entanglement in non-Hermitian system of nonreciprocal coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08388)
*Jiguo Wu,Shaojie Yang,Xiong Liu,Zhibo Jiang,Zhiyou Zhang,Rongchun Ge*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非厄米量子系统中高阶奇异点（EPs）之间的相互作用，通过实验可实现的非互易耦合腔系统展示二阶和三阶EPs，并观察到一种全新的纯量子连续纠缠相变，揭示多EP相互作用如何根据参数区域的不同改变纠缠动力学，同时保持加速纠缠生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米量子系统近年因实验进展而重获关注，工程调控高阶奇异点（EPs）的阶数对量子控制应用（如加速纠缠生成）至关重要，但目前对高阶EPs之间相互作用的研究仍然缺失，本文旨在弥补这一空白。

Method: 采用实验上可通过二维可重构非厄米规整激光阵列实现的非互易耦合腔系统，通过精心设计使系统同时呈现二阶和三阶奇异点，研究它们之间的相互作用对纠缠动力学的影响。

Result: 系统观察到一种全新的、纯粹量子的连续纠缠相变，揭示了多EP相互作用如何在保持加速生成的同时改变不同区域的纠缠动力学。研究展示了高阶EPs所体现的真正量子非厄米物理。

Conclusion: 该发现为高阶简并（degeneracy）应用及量子系统动态相变的未来研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The exploration of the effect of non-Hermitian (NH) in quantum systems is gaining renewed momentum as a result of the recent progress in experiments. It has been indicated that the ability to engineer the orders of exceptional points (EPs) can be fundamental to its applications in quantum control, such as accelerating entanglement generation; however, an initial survey of the interactions between different orders of EPs is still missing. In this work, we try to partially address this issue by employing a non-reciprocally coupled cavity system which can be experimentally realizable with a two-dimensional reconfigurable NH gauged laser array. With deliberate design, our systems display both second and third orders of EPs. The sign of a novel, purely quantum continuous entanglement phase transition is observed, which reveals how multi-EP interactions alter entanglement dynamics across different regimes while maintaining accelerated generation. Our findings reveil the genuinely quantum NH physics of higher-order EPs, which has opened up a new avenue for future investigations of the higher-order degeneracy application and the dynamic phase transitions of quantum systems.

</details>


### [137] [A theoretical study of subcarrier-wave quantum key distribution system integration with an optical transport network utilizing dense wavelength division multiplexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08389)
*Fedor Kiselev,Natalia Veselkova,Roman Goncharov,Vladimir Egorov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了副载波量子密钥分发（SCW QKD）在采用密集波分复用（DWDM）的光传输网络经典信道共存下的性能表现，分析了自发拉曼散射噪声、四波混频及信道隔离效率的影响，并计算了不同DWDM参数和量子信道分配方案下的量子比特错误率与安全密钥生成率。结果表明，将量子信道分配在1310 nm波长优于C波段，尽管该波长处光纤衰减更大，但其非线性噪声更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子密钥分发在实际部署中需要与现有光传输网络的经典信道共存，而DWDM系统中的非线性效应（如四波混频）和散射噪声（如自发拉曼散射）会严重干扰量子信道，因此需要系统评估SCW QKD在不同波长分配方案下的实际性能，以指导量子信道的合理规划。

Method: 作者建立了SCW QKD在DWDM环境中与经典信道共存的噪声模型，综合考虑自发拉曼散射噪声、四波混频效应以及信道隔离效率三个主要干扰源；通过计算量子比特错误率（QBER）和安全密钥生成率，对不同DWDM系统参数及量子信道波长分配方案进行了量化比较。

Result: 计算结果显示，将量子信道分配在1310 nm波长处比分配在C波段更为有利。尽管1310 nm处光纤衰减较大，但由于该波段远离经典信道的C波段，非线性噪声显著降低，综合性能更优。

Conclusion: 在DWDM光网络中部署SCW QKD时，量子信道应优先考虑1310 nm而非C波段波长分配，因为非线性噪声的降低对系统性能的改善超过了光纤衰减增大带来的不利影响。

Abstract: In this paper we study the performance of subcarrier-wave quantum key distribution (SCW QKD) in the presence of classical channels of optical transport network utilizing dense wavelength division multiplexing (DWDM). We consider the impact of spontaneous Raman scattering noise as well as the four-wave mixing and channel isolation efficiency. We calculate quantum bit error rate as well as the secure key generation rate of SCW-QKD protocol for different parameters of DWDM system and quantum channel allocations. Our calculations show, that quantum channel allocation at the wavelength of 1310 nm is preferable to allocation at C-band due to lower nonlinear noise, despite fiber's larger attenuation at this wavelength.

</details>


### [138] [Performance of the subcarrier-wave quantum key distribution in the presence of spontaneous Raman scattering noise generated by classical DWDM channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08406)
*Fedor Kiselev,Roman Goncharov,Natalia Veselkova,Eduard Samsonov,Alexei D. Kiselev,Vladimir Egorov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了DWDM网络中经典信道产生的自发拉曼散射（SpRS）噪声对副载波量子密钥分发（SCW QKD）系统性能的影响，建立了包含SpRS噪声的QBER和安全密钥生成率数学模型，并比较了连续波与脉冲两种工作模式。结果表明脉冲模式在安全密钥生成率和最大传输距离上均优于连续波模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子密钥分发系统与经典DWDM网络共存于同一根单模光纤中，经典信道产生的自发拉曼散射噪声会严重干扰量子密钥分发系统的性能。为了评估和优化共纤传输场景下SCW QKD系统的实际可用性，需要建立考虑SpRS噪声影响的系统性能分析模型。

Method: 建立了考虑SpRS噪声的数学分析模型，用于评估量子比特错误率（QBER）和安全密钥生成率。分别针对连续波（CW）激光驱动的连续波模式和脉冲模式两种SCW QKD工作体制进行分析，并考察了不同经典DWDM接收机灵敏度条件下系统的性能变化。

Result: 研究发现，在SpRS噪声影响下，脉冲模式在安全密钥生成率方面优于连续波模式，并且能够支持更长的最大可实现传输距离。

Conclusion: 在经典DWDM信道共存并通过SpRS噪声干扰的情况下，SCW QKD系统采用脉冲工作模式比连续波模式更具优势，能够获得更高的安全密钥率和更远的传输距离。

Abstract: In this paper we study performance of the subcarrier-wave quantum key distribution system (SCW QKD) in the presence of spontaneous Raman scattering (SpRS) noise generated by classical channels of dense wavelength division multiplexing (DWDM) network within a single-mode optical fiber. We present the mathematical model for evaluation of the quantum bit error rate (QBER) and the secure key generation rate with the SpRS noise taken into account. We consider two regimes of the SCW QKD system: the continuous wave regime that uses continuous wave laser and the pulsed regime. For these regimes, performance of the system is analyzed depending on receiver sensitivity of classical DWDM. It is found that the pulsed regime outperforms the continuous wave regime in both the secure key generation rate and the maximum achievable distance.

</details>


### [139] [Efficient quantum state preparation on Quantinuum hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08414)
*Archie Butterworth,Josh Green,Yusen Wu,Jie Pan,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个端到端框架，将资源高效的量子态制备与快速、鲁棒的保真度验证相结合，在Quantinuum H2-1陷阱离子平台上实验制备并验证了编码数字声学信号的结构化量子态，实现了F_hw=0.929的高硬件保真度。通过引入预测量的基变换技术，将验证参数τ降低了超过10个数量级，仅需1000次测量即可完成鲁棒验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态制备与验证对于量子设备发挥超越经典计算的优势至关重要。现有的量子态认证方法（如shadow overlap）对随机态有效，但对于实际算法中使用的结构化量子态，其样本复杂度过高，无法在实际噪声和测量预算下高效验证。

Method: 1) 设计资源最小化的电路以优化NISQ时代和早期容错设备；2) 引入预测量基变换技术，降低结构化目标的验证参数τ；3) 将张量网络态制备与shadow验证整合为统一工作流；4) 在Quantinuum H2-1陷阱离子上实现端到端实验验证。

Result: 在Quantinuum H2-1平台上对编码数字声学信号的结构化量子态实现硬件保真度F_hw=0.929；通过基变换技术将验证参数τ降低超过10个数量级；整个验证过程仅需1000次测量即可完成，实现了可扩展的基准测试标准。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在不依赖理想化假设和深度容错开销的前提下，可以通过资源最小化的电路和优化的验证技术在近-term硬件上高保真地制备和验证结构化量子态，为结构化经典数据映射到量子硬件提供了新的范式，并为NISQ和早期容错设备提供了可扩展的基准测试标准。

Abstract: Preparation and verification of specific quantum states is an important capability for quantum devices to realise advantages over classical computations and algorithms. In this work, we have demonstrated an end-to-end framework that combines resource-efficient quantum state preparation with rapid, robust fidelity verification on near-term quantum hardware. By experimentally preparing and validating a structured complex quantum state encoding a digitized acoustic signal on the Quantinuum H2-1 trapped-ion platform, we achieved a high hardware fidelity of $F_{\mathrm{hw}} = 0.929$. Crucially, this milestone was realized without relying on idealized assumptions or deep fault-tolerant overhead, but rather through resource-minimal circuits optimized for NISQ-era and early fault-tolerant devices.
  Furthermore, we addressed a key limitation in current quantum state certification. While validation methods like shadow overlap work well for random states, their sample complexity can become prohibitively high for the structured states used in practical algorithms. We mitigate this by introducing a pre-measurement basis-change technique that reduces the verification parameter, $τ$, by over 10 orders of magnitude for structured targets. This approach tightens the theoretical certification guarantees of the shadow overlap method and integrates tensor-network preparation and shadow validation into a unified workflow. These results shift the paradigm of how structured classical data can be mapped to and verified on quantum hardware under realistic noise and measurement budgets. By compressing a robust verification procedure to just 1,000 measurement shots, this framework offers an immediate, scalable benchmarking standard.

</details>


### [140] [Time-Dependent Quantum Monte Carlo for fermions: from Bayesian conditioning to spinor dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08415)
*Ivan P. Christov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从贝叶斯定理和经验替换出发，推导了时间相关量子蒙特卡洛方法中使用的条件相互作用（Nadaraya-Watson核形式），赋予非局域长度以条件宽度而非拟合参数的物理意义，并将该形式推广至自旋子及交换作用活跃的费米子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间依赖量子蒙特卡洛方法此前大多应用于反平行自旋电子（交换作用处于休眠状态），而对交换作用活跃的体系需要严格推导而非假设条件相互作用的形式；同时需要对非局域长度进行物理解释，并解决费米子体系中的符号问题和正交归一约束。

Method: 将walker视为其电子在有限分辨率下的局域化表示，利用贝叶斯定理和单一经验替换推导出Nadaraya-Watson形式的条件相互作用；对费米子，交换保留在波函数扇区，使正walker采样免受符号问题；通过约束项强制正交归一性以取代被条件化移除的规范自由度；并将该公式推广至自旋子情形，Pauli抑制在重合点处由局域自旋排列分级调节。

Result: 推导得到与启发式Nadaraya-Watson形式一致的条件相互作用，非局域长度获得条件宽度而非拟合耦合的明确物理含义；对偶极限和平均场极限可从同一构造导出；费米子体系的正walker采样免于符号问题；推广至自旋子后通过共线极限和平均场极限回归已知的两粒子自旋方程。

Conclusion: 该工作为TDOQMC方法的条件相互作用提供了第一性推导基础，在交换作用活跃的费米子系统与自旋子场景中建立了统一的理论框架，同时保持了多项式计算成本并规避了符号问题。

Abstract: The time-dependent quantum Monte Carlo method represents a many-electron state by an ensemble of replicas, in which each electron is described by a cloud of walkers which samples its density in physical space, one walker and one guide wave per replica, and it replaces the Hartree potential by a conditional interaction built from the walker positions. The sampling is in physical space rather than in configuration space, which is what keeps the cost polynomial. Here that conditional interaction is derived rather than postulated. The method has so far been applied mostly to opposite-spin electrons, where the exchange is dormant rather than absent, and the present formulation addresses the regime in which it is active. Treating the walker as a localization of its electron to a finite resolution, Bayes theorem and a single empirical substitution yield the Nadaraya-Watson form which the method has used heuristically. The nonlocality length thereby acquires a meaning as the width of the conditioning rather than as a fitted coupling, and the pair and mean-field limits follow from one construction. For fermions, exchange cannot be carried by the walkers and stays in the wave sector, which is what leaves the positive walker sampling free of the sign problem. Because the conditioning removes the gauge freedom which eliminates the orthonormality multipliers in Hartree-Fock, orthonormality is enforced here by a term derived from the constraint. The formulation is generalized to spinors, where the Pauli suppression at coincidence becomes graded by the local spin alignment, and it reduces through the collinear and mean-field limits to the known two-particle spin equations.

</details>


### [141] [Trapdoor Functions with Secure Key Leasing and Copy Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08423)
*Fuyuki Kitagawa,Ryo Nishimaki,Yuki Shirakawa,Takashi Yamakawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在安全密钥租赁和复制保护框架下研究了陷门函数（TDFs），提出了TDF-SKL和TDF-CP两种新原语，分别基于LWE假设和iO+LWE进行构造，并利用它们构建了具有鲁棒量子解密密钥的PKE-SKL和单解密器加密（SDE）方案，解决了现有方案中量子解密密钥被恶意密文破坏的漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学中的不可克隆定理启发了多种具有不可克隆功能的量子密码原语（如安全密钥租赁和复制保护），但作为公钥密码学基础原语的陷门函数尚未在这些领域得到充分研究。此外，现有的PKE-SKL和SDE方案存在关键漏洞：量子解密密钥在解密恶意选择的密文后可能被破坏，因此需要新的工具来解决这一问题。

Method: (1) 提出TDF-SKL的形式化定义（含与不含域采样器两种变体），基于LWE假设构造无域采样器的TDF-SKL，基于任意标准PKE结合提示性伪随机生成器（Koppula-Waters, CRYPTO 2019）构造含域采样器的TDF-SKL；(2) 定义TDF-CP，在不可区分混淆（iO）和LWE假设下，沿用Ananth-Behera（CRYPTO 2024）的模块化复制保护框架构造TDF-CP；(3) 从TDF-SKL和TDF-CP分别构建具有鲁棒量子解密密钥的PKE-SKL和SDE方案。

Result: 给出了TDF-SKL的两种变体形式化定义及具体构造方案，给出了TDF-CP的定义与基于iO和LWE的构造，并证明了从TDF-SKL/TDF-CP可以构造出对任意恶意密文解密后仍可重复使用的鲁棒PKE-SKL和SDE方案，克服了现有方案中量子解密密钥易被破坏的缺陷。

Conclusion: 该工作将TDFs成功引入安全密钥租赁和复制保护研究领域，建立了从不可克隆TDF到鲁棒PKE-SKL和SDE的转换路径，为量子公钥密码学中抗量子密钥破坏的加密方案提供了新的理论基矗。
