### [166] [Kibble-Zurek Dynamics in Two-dimensional Frustrated Systems with a Neural Foundation-state Subspace Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05556)
*Linda Mauron,Luciano Loris Viteritti,Zakari Denis,Riccardo Rende,Giuseppe Carleo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出神经基态子空间（NFS）方法，用于二维强关联量子多体系统的近绝热动力学模拟。该方法利用基础神经网络量子态表示沿驱动路径的基态流形，再通过保真度选取的小子集构造固定变分子空间，将多体薛定谔方程简化为少量线性系数的演化。在二维横场Ising模型上验证了Kibble-Zurek标度，并在受挫J1-J2海森堡模型中提供了Néel到自旋液体相变存在Kibble-Zurek机制的强数值证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维强关联量子多体系统中数值获取跨连续相变的普适标度（Kibble-Zurek机制）非常具有挑战性，因为这需要对关联量子态进行精确的实时演化并扫描多个系统尺寸和驱动速率。

Method: 提出的NFS方法包含：（1）用基础神经网络量子态表示沿驱动路径的基态流形；（2）通过保真度选择标准在流形上选取少量代表态构成固定变分子空间；（3）将多体薛定谔方程投影到该子空间，使动力学简化为少数线性系数的演化，且投影算子可在不同扫描时长间复用。

Result: 在二维横场Ising模型上恢复了预期的Kibble-Zurek标度和临界指数，与基态量子蒙特卡洛估计定量一致。应用于受挫方格子J1-J2海森堡模型（最大16×16格点），在J2/J1=0.49处的Néel到自旋液体相变中获得ν=1.23(15)和η=0.409(19)（固定z=1），与静态估计一致。

Conclusion: NFS方法在二维量子系统中成功提供了跨连续相变的Kibble-Zurek机制的强数值证据，支持Néel到自旋液体相变具有连续临界行为的理论假设，为研究二维量子临界动力学提供了高效的数值工具。

Abstract: Universal scaling generated when a strongly interacting quantum many-body system is driven across a continuous phase transition provides a dynamical probe of equilibrium criticality. Accessing this regime numerically in two dimensions is challenging because it requires accurate real-time evolution of correlated many-body states over many system sizes and driving rates. We introduce a Neural Foundation-state Subspace (NFS) method for near-adiabatic dynamics. A foundation neural-network quantum state represents the ground-state manifold along the driving path, and a small fidelity-selected subset defines a fixed variational subspace. The many-body Schrödinger equation then reduces to the evolution of a few linear coefficients, with projected operators reusable across ramp times. We validate the method on the two-dimensional transverse-field Ising model, recovering the expected Kibble-Zurek scaling and critical exponents in quantitative agreement with ground-state quantum Monte Carlo estimates. Applied to the frustrated square-lattice $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg model up to $16 \times 16$ clusters, our approach provides strong numerical evidence of Kibble-Zurek mechanism across the Néel-to-spin-liquid transition at $J_2/J_1=0.49$, yielding ${ν=1.23(15)}$ and ${η=0.409(19)}$ at fixed $z=1$, consistent with static estimates and supporting the proposed continuous critical behavior.

</details>


### [167] [Deviation from Fermi-liquid $T^2$ resistivity caused by collective transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05724)
*Kamran Behnia*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文指出费米液体行为（电阻率随温度呈二次方依赖）在有限温度下会偏离，且这种偏离在弱关联与强关联金属中方向相反：弱关联金属中向上偏离源于声子附加散射，强关联金属中向下偏离则可能源于散射减少或额外导电通道（如正常液态³He中声模式对热传输的贡献），且该通道在T_F附近平滑过渡到Bridgman公式描述的经典液体热导行为。在UPt₃、Sr₂RuO₄和重度过掺杂LSCO三种金属中，T/T_F≈0.02处过量电导的归一化幅度与³He相当，暗示可能具有共同起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米液体行为在有限温度下的偏离机理尚不清楚，且不同关联强度金属中偏离方向相反的现象缺乏统一解释。作者希望通过对比弱关联与强关联系统，特别是借鉴液态³He中已被识别的声模式热输运机制，为强关联金属中向下的电阻率偏离提供可能的物理解释。

Method: 本文采用现象学比较分析方法：首先分类讨论弱关联和强关联费米液体中电阻率对T²行为的偏离方向及可能起因；然后以正常液态³He为参照，其中声模式对热传导有独立贡献且平滑过渡到Bridgman公式描述的经典液体行为；最后将UPt₃、Sr₂RuO₄和重度过掺杂LSCO三种金属的实验数据在T/T_F≈0.02处的过量电导归一化幅度与³He进行定量对比。

Result: 发现弱关联金属中费米液体电阻率向上偏离T²行为可归因于声子的附加散射（对应更高指数）；强关联金属中向下偏离则暗示散射减少或附加导电通道。在三种金属（UPt₃、Sr₂RuO₄、重度过掺杂LSCO）中，T/T_F≈0.02处过量电导的归一化幅度与液态³He中观察到的数值相当，提示可能存在类似声模式的额外输运通道。该通道在T_F附近平滑过渡至Bridgman公式描述的经典液体行为。

Conclusion: 强关联金属中电阻率对于T²行为的向下偏离可能与额外的输运通道（类比³He中的声模式热输运）有关，三种被考察金属与该机制的相容性提示其可能具有共同起源，但作者强调这仅是"提示"而非"证实"，需要进一步研究验证。

Abstract: `Fermi-liquid behavior' is nowadays used as a shorthand for quadratic temperature dependence of the electrical resistivity. In a metal, for this to occur, electrons must be cooled well below the Fermi degeneracy temperature, $T_{\mathrm{F}}$. A departure from this behavior is observed at finite temperature. Here, we note that this departure has opposite signs in two distinct types of Fermi liquids. In weakly correlated ones, the upward departure implies a higher exponent of the inelastic resistivity, attributable to \textit{additional scattering} by phonons. In contrast, there is a downward deviation in strongly correlated metals, which implies either \textit{reduced scattering} or \textit{additional conduction}, as in the case of normal liquid $^3$He, in which a distinct contribution to heat transport by a sound mode has been recently identified. Such a channel of conduction smoothly transforms across $T_{\mathrm{F}}$ to the one captured by the Bridgman formula for thermal conductivity of classical liquids. In three metals (UPt$_3$, Sr$_2$RuO$_4$, and heavily overdoped LSCO), according to the available experimental data, the normalized amplitude of excess conductivity at $T/T_{\mathrm{F}}\simeq0.02$ is comparable to what has been seen in $^3$He, suggesting, without establishing, a common origin.

</details>


### [168] [Ultrafast electron crystallography reveals the atomic pathway of a light-driven correlated insulator-to-metal transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05747)
*Arthur Niedermayr,Hongyi Xu,Chin Shen Ong,Patrik Thunström,Michael Yannai,Jianyu Wu,Gaolong Cao,Lior Kornblum,Ido Kaminer,Oscar Grånäs,Xiaodong Zou,Jonas Weissenrieder*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过引入超快三维电子衍射技术，在VO2的绝缘体-金属相变过程中精修了瞬态晶胞及内部V和O原子坐标，揭示出V-V二聚化和锯齿运动的真实原子轨迹为线性路径，并结合量子多体计算阐明二聚化导致能级劈裂是电子带隙的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关材料中的超快相变通常仅通过选定的衍射峰强度或位移推断，难以获得潜在的三维原子轨迹。解析这些轨迹对于识别哪些原子运动驱动电子性质变化以及它们如何与电子自由度耦合至关重要，因此有必要发展能够直接追踪瞬态原子坐标的方法。

Method: 引入超快三维电子衍射技术，对VO2在单斜M1绝缘相与金红石R金属相之间的相变过程进行时间分辨的瞬态晶胞精修，获取V和O原子坐标随时间的演化；结合量子多体计算，将每个V-V对视为关联单元，分析二聚化和非局域电子相互作用对能级劈裂与电子带隙的影响。

Result: 精修得到的原子坐标在相变过程中沿实空间线性轨迹演化，而非此前提到的非线性或逐级转变路径；量子多体计算表明二聚化产生responsible能级劈裂是电子带隙的根源，配对V原子间的非局域电子相互作用进一步增强该劈裂，带隙在二聚化消失时坍缩。

Conclusion: 这项工作将时间分辨衍射从序参量追踪转变为瞬态晶体学，将原子轨迹直接关联到相关材料中的电子机制，为理解超快相变的微观驱动力提供了新范式。

Abstract: Ultrafast phase transitions in correlated materials are often inferred from selected diffraction peak intensities or diffraction peak displacements, leaving the underlying three-dimensional atomic trajectories elusive. Resolving these trajectories is essential for identifying which atomic motions drive changes in electronic properties and how they couple to electronic degrees of freedom. We address this challenge in vanadium dioxide (VO2), a correlated oxide with a near-room-temperature transition between the insulating monoclinic (M1) phase and metallic rutile (R) phase. For this purpose, we introduce ultrafast three-dimensional electron diffraction, which enables refinement of the transient unit cell and internal V and O coordinates, revealing the V-V dimerization and zigzag motion during the phase transition. The refined atomic coordinates follow a linear trajectory in real space during the transition, in contrast to nonlinear or sequential transformation pathways inferred from more indirect observables in earlier work. Quantum many-body calculations treating each V-V pair as a correlated unit show that dimerization creates the level splitting responsible for the electronic gap, which is further enhanced by nonlocal electronic interactions between the paired V atoms. The gap collapses when dimerization is lost. This work turns time-resolved diffraction from order-parameter tracking into transient crystallography, directly connecting atomic trajectories to electronic mechanisms in correlated materials.

</details>


### [169] [Magnetization processes and spin dynamics across field-induced phase transitions in the quasi-two-dimensional quantum magnet Cu$_2$(OH)$_3$Br](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06246)
*Anneke Reinold,Dirk Wulferding,Laur Peedu,Kirill Amelin,Urmas Nagel,Toomas Rõõm,Zhiying Zhao,Patrick Pilch,Changqing Zhu,Hans Engelkamp,Lucas Berger,Denis I. Gorbunov,Yurii Skourski,Kwang-Yong Choi,Thomas Lorenz,Zhe Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过太赫兹光谱、Raman光谱和高场磁化测量，系统研究了准二维自旋1/2磁体Cu₂(OH)₃Br中磁场依赖的磁化与自旋动力学演化，揭示了不同磁场方向下铁磁Cu1链与反铁磁Cu2链子系统的重构及场致相变行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解准二维低维量子磁体中铁磁与反铁磁链子系统在磁场作用下的耦合与重构机制，探索磁场方向依赖的磁激发谱演变规律，为揭示耦合自旋链体系的量子磁性提供关键实验证据。

Method: 采用太赫兹时域光谱探测低能磁激发谱，结合Raman光谱分辨磁振子、自旋子及声子模式，并利用高场磁化测量独立确定自旋翻转转变（spin-flop）位置，综合分析磁场方向依赖的激发谱重构与子系统极化过程。

Result: 对于垂直于自旋链的磁场（B∥a和B∥c*），光谱在自旋翻转转变处发生突变重构；对于B∥b方向，无自旋翻转发生，激发谱随磁场连续演化并表现出强烈太赫兹辐射偏振依赖，与铁磁Cu1子系统逐步极化相关。高场下低场复杂谱被更少的宽激发取代，表明铁磁与反铁磁链子系统间耦合减弱。

Conclusion: 磁场能够以方向依赖的方式重构Cu₂(OH)₃Br中铁磁与反铁磁自旋链子系统的耦合，并驱动其跨越多种场致相变；综合光谱与磁化测量结果完整描绘了磁场诱导的系统重组图像。

Abstract: We present magnetic-field-dependent evolution of magnetization and spin dynamics in the quasi-two-dimensional spin-$1/2$ magnet $\mathrm{Cu_2(OH)_3Br}$, consisting of alternately coupled ferromagnetic Cu1 and antiferromagnetic Cu2 spin chains. Terahertz spectroscopy reveals a pronounced field-direction dependence of the low-energy magnetic excitation spectrum. For magnetic fields applied perpendicular to the spin chains, $B\parallel a$ and $B\parallel c^*$, the spectra undergo abrupt reconstructions at the spin-flop transitions identified independently by high-field magnetization measurements. For $B\parallel b$, by contrast, no spin-flop occurs; instead, the excitation spectrum evolves continuously with field and exhibits a strong terahertz radiation polarization dependence as the ferromagnetic Cu1 subsystem becomes progressively polarized. At higher fields, the complex low-field spectrum is replaced by a reduced set of broad excitations, consistent with a weakening of the coupling between the ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic chain subsystems. Complementary Raman spectroscopy resolves magnon and spinon excitations alongside several phonon modes and traces the characteristic temperature- and magnetic-field-dependent evolution of the magnetic excitations. The combined spectroscopic and magnetization results map out how a magnetic field reorganizes the coupled ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic subsystems in $\mathrm{Cu_2(OH)_3Br}$ across field-induced phase transitions.

</details>


### [170] [Signatures of spin-wave dynamics in quasiparticle interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06398)
*Alireza Akbari,Peter Thalmeier*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究了局域-巡游反铁磁体中动量分辨的准粒子干涉（QPI），揭示了静态反铁磁序与动力学单磁子重整化对QPI谱的不同影响，并发现QPI谱中存在特征性交叉行为，表明傅里叶变换隧道谱学能够区分静态磁重构与动态自旋波重整化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解局域磁矩与巡游电子耦合系统中，静态磁有序和动力学自旋-波激发如何分别影响准粒子性质及其可观测谱学特征，是区分不同物理机制的关键问题。

Method: 将静态反铁磁序引起的能带重构与单磁子过程产生的动量-频率依赖自能重整化直接纳入Born杂质散射框架，计算动量分辨的准粒子干涉谱并分析其行为。

Result: 发现QPI谱存在特征性交叉：在较低磁子发射边以下，修正主要由自能实部主导呈色散性质；在此能量之上，自能虚部导致显著的展宽和散射强度再分布。动力学响应持续到高于上磁子能，且在粒子-空穴不对称能带中对隧穿偏压强烈不对称。

Conclusion: 傅里叶变换隧道谱学能够作为区分静态磁重构与动力学自旋波重整化的有效实验手段。

Abstract: We investigate momentum-resolved quasiparticle interference (QPI) in a localized-itinerant antiferromagnet, where ordered local moments are exchange coupled to conduction electrons. Static antiferromagnetic order reconstructs the electronic bands, while one-magnon processes further dress the quasiparticles through a momentum- and frequency-dependent self-energy. Incorporating this dynamical renormalization directly into the Born impurity scattering, we find a characteristic crossover in the QPI spectrum. Below the lower magnon-emission edge, the modification is predominantly dispersive and governed by the real part of the self-energy, whereas above this scale its imaginary part produces pronounced broadening and redistribution of the scattering intensity. The dynamical response persists beyond the upper magnon energy and is strongly asymmetric in tunneling bias for a particle--hole-asymmetric band. These results show that Fourier-transform tunneling spectroscopy can distinguish static magnetic reconstruction from dynamical spin-wave renormalization.

</details>


### [171] [NMR evidence of pressure-induced structural transition and enhanced spin fluctuations up to 14~GPa in SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06472)
*Zhanlong Wu,Kefan Du,Shuo Li,Tong Shi,Ying Chen,Qingxin Dong,Rui Zhou,Rong Yu,Juanjuan Liu,Bosen Wang,Jinguang Cheng,Weiqiang Yu,Yi Cui*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过高压11B核磁共振（NMR）测量，研究了SrCu₂(BO₃)₂在压力高达14 GPa下的结构与磁性相变，揭示了两种压力诱导的单斜相，并鉴定了无隙3D反铁磁相、二维短程有序反铁磁相以及扩展的关联顺磁态等多个量子相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shastry-Sutherland化合物SrCu₂(BO₃)₂是研究磁阻挫驱动的量子相和量子相变的重要平台，但其压力诱导的结构与磁性相变仍存在争议，因此需要高精度的实验手段来澄清这些问题。

Method: 对SrCu₂(BO₃)₂进行了高达14 GPa的高压11B核磁共振（NMR）测量，通过分析NMR谱线、自旋-晶格弛豫率1/T₁的温度与压力依赖性来表征各相的性质。

Result: （1）NMR谱揭示两种压力诱导的单斜相；（2）压力>4 GPa、温度<10 K时，NMR谱快速展宽及1/T₁呈现幂律行为，证实单斜相中存在无隙3D反铁磁相；（3）在约20 K的中间温度范围内，场依赖的NMR谱线分裂表明存在二维短程有序反铁磁相；（4）温度>30 K时，1/T₁的亚线性幂律行为表明存在扩展的关联顺磁区。

Conclusion: 高压NMR测量澄清了SrCu₂(BO₃)₂中压力诱导相变的争议，全面刻画了从关联顺磁态到二维短程有序反铁磁相再到无隙3D反铁磁相的完整相图演化，为理解阻挫量子磁体中的量子相变提供了关键实验证据。

Abstract: The Shastry-Sutherland compound SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$ has attracted considerable interest as a platform for exploring quantum phases and quantum phase transitions driven by magnetic frustration. The pressure-induced structural and magnetic phase transitions in SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$, however, remain controversial. To address this issue, we performed high-pressure $^{11}$B nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) measurements on SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$ up to 14~GPa. The NMR spectra reveal two pressure-induced monoclinic phases. With pressure above 4~GPa and with temperature below 10~K, the rapid broadening of the NMR spectrum and the power-law behavior in the spin-lattice relaxation rate $1/T_1$ provide clear evidence for a gapless 3D antiferromagnetic (AFM) phase in the monoclinic phase. At an intermediate temperature range around 20~K, the emergence of the field-dependent NMR line splits resolves a two-dimensional, short-range ordered AFM phase; at temperature above 30~K, the sublinear power-law behavior of $1/T_1$ identifies an extended correlated paramagnetic regime.

</details>


### [172] [Electronic correlations and fluctuating lattice distortions in vanadium dioxide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06707)
*Antonio Picano,Martin Eckstein,Francesco Grandi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种随机半经典动力学平均场理论的扩展形式，将关联电子与涨落的晶格畸变统一演化，应用于VO2单斜相的双轨道模型，发现两种畸变在不同温度熔化，产生绝缘、中间金属和高对称三个相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联材料中的金属-绝缘体转变常伴随晶体结构变化，但传统相干晶格近似忽略了晶格自由度携带的熵，而转变熵在电子与晶格之间的分配往往决定转变温度。因此需要一种能同时处理关联电子与涨落晶格的理论框架。

Method: 提出了动力学平均场理论的随机半经典扩展：将关联电子与涨落的晶格畸变在共同的时间演化中处理，从线性电子-声子相互作用推广到非线性情形，从单一晶格模式扩展到多个耦合模式。得到的非守恒Langevin方程中，确定性力、阻尼和关联噪声均由相互作用电子子系统自洽产生。

Result: 将该方法应用于VO2单斜相中两个对称性不同的畸变的双轨道最小模型，发现两种畸变在明显分离的温度下熔化，产生三个相：绝缘相、中间金属相和高对称相。两个转变的起因不同：二聚化的熔化由关联电子耦合驱动，在相干晶格近似中即可捕获；而无畸变结构的恢复需要涨落晶格的熵。

Conclusion: 该框架基于非平衡动力学平均场理论处理电子子系统，未来可扩展到具有非热电子分布的光激发系统，为研究光致相变等超快现象提供理论工具。

Abstract: Metal-insulator transitions in correlated materials are often accompanied by a change of crystal structure. They are commonly described within a coherent lattice approximation, which combines a correlated treatment of the electrons with a lattice represented by one or a few classical distortion coordinates fixed by minimizing an energy. The structural degrees of freedom then carry no entropy of their own, even though the partition of the transition entropy between electrons and lattice is often what decides the transition temperature. Here we develop a stochastic semiclassical extension of dynamical mean-field theory in which correlated electrons and fluctuating lattice distortions are evolved together, extending previous formulations from linear to nonlinear electron-phonon interactions and from a single to several coupled lattice modes. The resulting non-conservative Langevin equations have deterministic forces, damping, and correlated noise generated self-consistently by the interacting electronic subsystem. Applying the approach to a minimal two-orbital model containing the two symmetry-distinct distortions of the monoclinic phase of vanadium dioxide, we find that the two distortions melt at well separated temperatures, giving an insulating, an intermediate metallic, and a high-symmetry phase. The two transitions have different origins: the melting of the dimerization is driven by the coupling to the correlated electrons and is captured already within the coherent lattice approximation, whereas the restoration of the undistorted structure requires the entropy of the fluctuating lattice. Since the electronic subsystem is treated within nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory, the framework can be extended in future work to photoexcited systems with nonthermal electronic distributions.

</details>


### [173] [Ginzburg-Landau Theory for Non-Invertible Symmetry-Breaking Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06751)
*Vibhu Ravindran,Luisa Eck,Xie Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文遵循Ginzburg-Landau哲学，利用Symmetry Topological Field Theory (SymTFT)形式体系，为广义Landau范式中由非可逆对称性破缺驱动的"非常规"相变（如拓扑/退禁闭转变）发展了一套场论描述。作者以色散性非可逆对称性Rep($S_3$)为例，完整展示了从(2+1)D拓扑序的Lagrangian代数识别局部序参量代数，进而构建临界点场论描述的步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Landau范式提出后，虽然已理解许多超越传统对称性破缺框架的非常规相变（如(1+1)D中的拓扑/退禁闭转变）可被映射为非可逆对称性的对称性破缺转变，但如何从中提取这些转变的普适动力学性质仍不清楚。需要建立类似传统Ginzburg-Landau理论中场论级描述的方法。

Method: 本文将Ginzburg-Landau理念推广到非可逆对称性的情形：通过有效场论描述临界点处非可逆对称性的局部序参量的涨落。核心工具是SymTFT形式体系，利用(2+1)D拓扑序的Lagrangian代数来识别非可逆对称性局部序参量所满足的代数结构。

Result: 以色散性非可逆对称性Rep($S_3$)为例，完整演示了广义Ginzburg-Landau程序的所有关键步骤，阐明了如何从SymTFT出发识别局部序参量代数并构建描述带隙相之间转变的场论。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解(1+1)D中由非可逆对称性驱动的广义Landau范式中相变的普适动力学性质提供了一套系统的场论框架，将非可逆对称性破缺转变纳入了可计算、可描述的Ginzburg-Landau式体系。

Abstract: Recently, a generalized Landau paradigm has been proposed, in which a broad class of "unconventional" phase transitions lying beyond the original symmetry-breaking framework of Landau are understood as the symmetry-breaking transitions of generalized symmetries. In particular, in (1+1)D, such "topological" or "deconfined" transitions can be mapped to the symmetry-breaking transitions of non-invertible symmetries. How to extract the universal dynamical properties of the transitions from this understanding? In this paper, we follow the Ginzburg-Landau philosophy and develop a field-theoretic description of such critical points in terms of the fluctuations of local order parameters, now of non-invertible symmetries. The Symmetry Topological Field Theory formalism plays a central role in our analysis, allowing us to identify the algebra of local order parameters from the Lagrangian algebra of a (2+1)D topological order. We illustrate the key ideas and the full steps of this generalized Ginzburg-Landau procedure using the transitions between gapped phases with a simple non-invertible symmetry --- the Rep($S_3$) symmetry.

</details>


### [174] [Spin--orbit-enhanced correlation sensitivity and anomalous magnetic response in non-dimerized $4d^4$ ilmenite CdRuO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06902)
*Yuya Haraguchi,Hayato Yatsuzuka,Hiroko Aruga Katori*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了名义上为Ru^{4+} 4d^4 钛铁矿结构的CdRuO_3的合成与物理性质研究。实验与理论计算表明，该材料呈现非二聚化的R̄3结构，电阻表现为非金属但非Arrhenius行为，比热中存在残余线性项。理论计算显示，考虑自旋轨道耦合后能显著增强U的敏感性并打开约55 meV的直接带隙，因此CdRuO_3实现了钌钛铁矿中预测的非二聚化结构分支，但并非简单的稳健多轨道金属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索4d过渡金属氧化物中自旋轨道耦合、电子关联与晶体结构畸变之间的相互作用，特别是钌钛铁矿体系中非二聚化结构分支是否能够被实验实现，以及该材料是否表现为简单金属或多轨道金属行为。

Method: 采用固相合成法制备CdRuO_3多晶样品；通过粉末X射线衍射确定晶体结构；通过压片电阻率测量与比热测量表征输运和热力学性质；并结合不考虑/考虑自旋轨道耦合的PBE+U第一性原理计算进行对比分析。

Result: CdRuO_3具有非二聚化的R̄3结构，Ru-Ru键长等价但RuO_6八面体强畸变。电阻呈非金属但非Arrhenius型，比热含有限残余线性项。计算结果显示无SOC时直到U_eff=3 eV仍为金属或半金属，而含SOC时随U_eff强烈变化并在U_eff=2.5 eV处打开约55 meV的直接带隙。扣除稀薄居里-外斯缺陷贡献后，磁化率弱非单调，既不符合普通Pauli响应也不符合独立的S=1自旋矩。

Conclusion: CdRuO_3实现了理论上预言的非二聚化结构分支，但自旋轨道耦合显著增强了非立方Ru t_2g轨道多重态的关联敏感性，最终材料并非简单的稳健多轨道金属，而是呈现出由SOC与关联效应共同决定的复杂电子态。

Abstract: We report the synthesis and physical properties of CdRuO$_3$, a nominal Ru$^{4+}$ $4d^4$ ilmenite with edge-sharing RuO$_6$ honeycomb layers. Powder X-ray diffraction establishes a crystallographically non-dimerized $R\bar{3}$ structure with equivalent Ru-Ru bonds and a strongly distorted RuO$_6$ environment. The compacted-pellet resistivity is nonmetallic but non-Arrhenius, while the heat capacity contains a finite residual linear term. Matched nonmagnetic calculations show that PBE+$U$ without spin-orbit coupling remains metallic or semimetallic up to $U_{\mathrm{eff}} = 3$ eV, whereas PBE+SOC+$U$ exhibits a strong $U_{\mathrm{eff}}$ dependence and opens a direct gap of approximately 55 meV at $Γ$ for $U_{\mathrm{eff}} = 2.5$ eV. Spin-orbit coupling therefore markedly enhances the correlation sensitivity of the non-cubic Ru $t_{2g}$ manifold. After subtraction of a dilute Curie-Weiss defect contribution, the susceptibility remains weakly nonmonotonic and is inconsistent with both an ordinary Pauli response and independent spin-only $S = 1$ moments. CdRuO$_3$ thus realizes the non-dimerized structural branch predicted for ruthenium ilmenites, but not a simple robust multiorbital metal.

</details>


### [175] [Raman signatures of a non-reciprocal magnetic phase transition in Ca$_2$RuO$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07565)
*Giacomo Jarc,Giovanni Tartaglia,Francesco Gabriele,Filomena Forte,Anita Guarino,Angela Montanaro,Enrico Maria Rigoni,Nitesh Khatiwada,Costanza Lincetto,Gabriele Bartolini,Antonio Mastropasqua,Shahla Yasmin Mathengattil,Marco Malvestuto,Muhammad Waqee Ur Rehman,Rosalba Fittipaldi,Joachim Deisenhofer,Alexander A. Tsirlin,Antonio Vecchione,Mario Cuoco,Daniele Fausti*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过拉曼光谱测量揭示了Ca₂RuO₄在外加c轴磁场驱动下发生磁性一级相变，表现为磁起源模式的出现及声子与Higgs振幅模式耦合的改变，且两种拉曼特征均呈现非互易磁滞。相变不影响面内磁振子，表明偶极反铁磁序保持不变，暗示了新的四极子场控态的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ca₂RuO₄因其强电子关联、磁弹相互作用和大自旋轨道耦合的相互交织而展现出独特的磁性行为，低温和的Mott绝缘态伴随复杂反铁磁序和集体振幅激发（类比Higgs模式），但其磁场响应机制尚不清楚，需要用拉曼光谱揭示其磁场驱动的相变性质。

Method: 采用拉曼散射光谱测量，在沿晶体c轴方向施加面外磁场条件下，系统地观测Ca₂RuO₄的声子模式、磁振子及Higgs振幅模式随磁场的变化，分析各光谱特征在磁场循环中的滞后行为。

Result: 实验发现外加c轴磁场驱动了一个磁性一级相变，表现为：(1) 出现新的磁起源拉曼模式；(2) 声子与Higgs振幅模式之间的耦合发生改变；(3) 两种拉曼特征均呈非互易磁滞。然而面内磁振子的拉曼散射不受相变影响，说明偶极反铁磁序得以保留，与四极子场控态的产生相一致，该态可通过涨落结构驱动晶格与磁性Higgs模式间的杂化。

Conclusion: Ca₂RuO₄在c轴磁场下经历一级磁性相变进入一种新的四极子场控态，该态保持偶极反铁磁序不变，但通过涨落介导的晶格-磁性Higgs模式杂化产生了可观测的拉曼特征，为理解该类材料中磁序、轨道自由度与晶格耦合的复杂相互作用提供了新见解。

Abstract: The magnetic behavior of Ca$_2$RuO$_4$ represents a unique puzzle due to the interplay of strong electronic correlations, magneto-elastic interactions, and large spin-orbit coupling. At low temperatures, an anomalous Mott insulating state emerges, characterized by a complex antiferromagnetic order with a collective amplitude excitation of the magnetic moment which has been discussed in analogy with the Higgs mode. We report here evidence of a magnetic first-order phase transition driven by an out-of-plane magnetic field along the crystallographic $c$-axis of Ca$_2$RuO$_4$. Raman measurements in magnetic field reveal the emergence of mode of magnetic origin and a concomitant modification of the coupling between a phonon and the Higgs amplitude mode. Both the Raman features are characterized by a non-reciprocal hysteresis in magnetic field. Surprisingly, the observed phase transition does not affect the Raman scattering from the in-plane magnons, indicating that the dipolar antiferromagnetic order is preserved. This is consistent with the onset of a new quadrupolar field-controlled state, whose fluctuations structure can trigger an hybridization between the lattice and the magnetic Higgs mode resulting in the observed Raman features.

</details>


### [176] [Exact fermionic dual of the Bose-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07830)
*Lei Su,Ivar Martin,Aashish A. Clerk*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过费米子规范（gauging）实现了广义Jordan-Wigner变换，将具有U(1)全局对称性的Bose-Hubbard模型精确对偶到费米子复合算符（玻色子和费米子构成）的描述。在1D中，该对偶将硬核玻色子与自旋费米子之间的已知映射推广到软核相互作用；低能下退化为sine-Gordon模型与Thirring模型间的等价性。无隙相中费米子复合关联函数的振荡波矢由密度固定，体现Luttinger定理的新表现形式。作者用DMRG验证了该精确对偶，并证明无隙相与相变受紧致玻色子CFT控制。该构造自然推广到一般玻色系统和高维，为光晶格Bose-Fermi混合物等强关联量子系统研究开辟新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近的发展已确立具有Z₂对称性的精确玻色化和费米子化可作为通过规范化的对偶。本文旨在将此框架推广到具有更高对称性（U(1)全局对称）的Bose-Hubbard模型，通过费米子规范化实现广义Jordan-Wigner变换，从而获得精确的对偶描述，拓展已有仅覆盖硬核玻色子情形的对偶关系到更一般的软核相互作用体系。

Method: 作者运用费米子规范化（fermionic gauging）技术，将Bose-Hubbard模型与费米子场耦合后进行规范的积分处理，构造出由玻色子与费米子构成的费米子复合算符描述。在1D低能极限下，通过对偶映射将模型简化为sine-Gordon与Thirring模型之间的等价关系；并采用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）进行数值验证精确对偶关系的正确性。

Result: 研究发现：在无隙相中，费米子复合关联函数的振荡波矢由粒子密度唯一确定，这为Luttinger定理提供了一种新的体现形式；DMRG数值计算确认了对偶的精确性，并显示无隙相和相变点由紧致玻色子共形场论（CFT）描述；该构造不仅适用于1D的软核BH模型，还可推广至一般玻色系统及更高维度。

Conclusion: 本文建立的精确费米子-玻色子对偶为研究软核相互作用下的Bose-Hubbard模型提供了全新视角，弥合了此前仅适用于硬核情形映射的局限。该框架的普适性（可推广至任意维度和一般玻色系统）使其成为研究光晶格Bose-Fermi混合物及其他强关联量子系统的有力工具，同时Luttinger定理的新型表现也加深了对无隙相物理的理解。

Abstract: Recent developments have established exact bosonization and fermionization with a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry as dualities through gauging. In this work, we apply fermionic gauging, which realizes generalized Jordan-Wigner transformations, to the Bose-Hubbard (BH) model with a global $U(1)$ symmetry and derive an exact dual description in terms of fermionic composites, built from bosons and fermions. In 1D, this duality generalizes the exact mapping between the extended hard-core BH model and the spinless Fermi-Hubbard model to now cover soft-core interactions. At low energies, the mapping reduces to the well-known equivalence between the sine-Gordon model and the Thirring model. The oscillation wave vector of the fermionic composite correlation function in the gapless phase is fixed by their density, providing a novel manifestation of Luttinger's theorem. We verify the exact duality using density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations and demonstrate that the gapless phase and the phase transition are governed by the compact boson conformal field theory. Our construction naturally extends to generic bosonic systems and higher dimensions, opening new avenues for studying Bose-Fermi mixtures in optical lattices and other strongly correlated quantum systems.

</details>


### [177] [Hysteretic Coherence Collapse Across the First Order CDW Transition in 1T-TaS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07923)
*Turgut Yilmaz,Anil Rajapitamahuni,Asish K. Kundu,Menka Jain,Elio Vescovo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）对1T-TaS2中近公度（NC-CDW）与公度（C-CDW）电荷密度波相之间的一级相变进行了系统研究，发现C-CDW相中的带隙内态在加热进入NC-CDW相时突然消失，冷却回到C-CDW相时又急剧重现，展现出明显的热滞回现象，并与其电阻率变化紧密关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1T-TaS2中NC-CDW与C-CDW相之间的一级相变支撑其奇异的电子行为，但跨越该相变时低能电子结构的能谱演化仍然至关重要且尚未阐明。

Method: 使用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）研究低温C-CDW相，结合光子能量依赖测量区分低能特征峰（平带与带隙内态）；并在加热和冷却循环中进行变温ARPES测量，追踪各特征的谱权重和位置演化，同时对比电阻率变化。

Result: 发现C-CDW相中存在与下Hubbard带相关的平带及更靠近费米能级的带隙内态；光子能量依赖测量表明二者谱权重演化不同。带隙内态在加热进入NC-CDW相时急剧坍缩，冷却回C-CDW时急剧重现，表现出明显的热滞回，且其消失与恢复与电阻率的相应变化紧密跟踪。

Conclusion: 热滞回现象提供了相变为一级的直接能谱证据，带隙内态与跨C-CDW/NC-CDW相变电子重建密切相关，深化了对1T-TaS2中相变机制与低能电子结构演化的理解。

Abstract: The first order phase transition between the nearly commensurate (NC-CDW) and commensurate (C-CDW) charge density wave phases in 1T-TaS2 underpins its exotic electronic behavior, yet the spectroscopic evolution of the low energy electronic structure across this transition remains crucial to understand. Using angle resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), we investigate the low temperature C-CDW phase, characterized by a flat band commonly associated with the lower Hubbard band and a distinct in-gap state located closer to the Fermi level. Photon energy dependent measurements distinguish these two low energy features through their different spectral weight evolution. Temperature dependent ARPES across heating and cooling cycles reveals that the in-gap state undergoes an abrupt collapse upon heating into the NC-CDW phase and re-emerges sharply upon cooling back into the C-CDW phase. This pronounced thermal hysteresis provides direct spectroscopic evidence of the first order nature of the transition. Furthermore, the disappearance and recovery of the in-gap state closely track the corresponding changes in resistivity, highlighting its intimate connection to the electronic reconstruction across the C-CDW/NC-CDW phase transition.

</details>


### [178] [Vertical quantum confinement in bulk MoS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07950)
*Jairo Obando-Guevara,Álvaro González-García,Marcin Rosmus,Natalia Olszowska,César González,Miguel Ángel González-Barrio,Antonio Tejeda,Arantzazu Mascaraque*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）在块体MoS₂中实验观测到量子局限态。在Γ点处发现与垂直量子局限相关的量子阱态（QWSs），通过k_z方向无色散和光子能量依赖的强度调制得以确认。值得注意的是，QWSs的束缚能与n的关系呈线性而非二维电子气的二次方依赖，与抛物线型量子阱一致。该局限源于机械剥离制备方法导致的多层堆叠从块体分离，DFT计算证实了这一点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究类块体MoS₂中的量子局限现象，既有基础物理意义（研究多层堆叠中的量子现象），又具有应用价值（激发子带跃迁以利用光学性质）。

Method: 使用ARPES实验观测MoS₂的电子能带结构，通过k_z色散分析和光子能量扫描确认量子局限；结合DFT计算验证量子阱态来源于机械剥离引起的多层堆叠从块体分离。

Result: 在块体MoS₂的Γ点观测到量子阱态，验证了垂直方向电子局限的存在。QWSs束缚能随量子数n线性变化，偏离典型二维电子气的二次方关系，呈现抛物线型量子阱特征。

Conclusion: 机械剥离法可在类块体MoS₂中引入量子局限，产生具有非线性色散特征的量子阱态，为进一步探索子带间跃迁和不同厚度多层堆叠的量子现象提供平台。

Abstract: We experimentally observe quantum confinement states in bulk MoS$_2$ using Angle-Resolved Photoemission Spectroscopy (ARPES). The band structure at the $\overlineΓ$ point reveals quantum well states (QWSs) linked to vertical quantum confinement of the electrons, confirmed by the absence of dispersion in k$_z$ and a strong intensity modulation with photon energy. Notably, the binding energy dependence of the QWSs vs $n$ does not follow the quadratic dependence of a two-dimensional electron gas. Instead, a linear behaviour is observed that is consistent with a parabolic-like quantum well. This confinement arises from the mechanical exfoliation preparation method, which leads to the detachment of a multilayer stack from the underlying bulk. This is confirmed by Density Functional Theory (DFT) calculations. The quantum confinement in bulk-like MoS$_2$ not only offers the opportunity to explore intersubband transitions to exploit optical properties but also provides a means to study fundamental quantum phenomena in multilayer stacks of different thicknesses.

</details>


### [179] [Selective coupling of high-order phonons in La2-xSrxCuO4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08076)
*Ke-Jun Xu,Paulina Majchrzak,Nathan Giles-Donovan,Sangheon Kim,Jaewon Choi,Jun Okamoto,Ganesha Channagowdra,Hsiao-Yu Huang,Di-Jing Huang,Seiki Komiya,Takao Sasagawa,Shuichi Wakimoto,Wei-Sheng Lee,Zhi-Xun Shen,Thomas P. Devereaux,Dung-Hai Lee,Robert J. Birgeneau*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）研究了La2-xSrxCuO4中的电子-声子耦合，发现未掺杂样品中在~85、180和330 meV处出现无弥散的尖锐峰，其中180和330 meV激发能与面内Cu-O键伸缩模的双声子和四声子能量一致，表明共振激发的价电子与该模式强耦合。这些激发在10%空穴掺杂时消失，且仅选择性耦合到偶数阶声子，与Franck-Condon图像及Raman谱形成鲜明对比，暗示存在超越传统图像的异常电子-声子耦合，可能源于绝缘铜氧化物中的局域配对准粒子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为强电子-声子耦合下的晶格振动高阶谐波遵循Franck-Condon包络。本文旨在探索铜氧化物超导体La2-xSrxCuO4中是否存在超越Franck-Condon框架的异常电子-声子耦合行为，并通过RIXS手段揭示其背后的物理机制。

Method: 采用共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）技术对La2-xSrxCuO4进行测量，比较未掺杂与不同空穴掺杂水平下的激发谱，并将实验观测到的激发能与Raman光谱中的声子模式及理论上二声子、四声子能量进行对比分析。

Result: 发现未掺杂样品在85±5 meV、180±7 meV和330±5 meV处存在无弥散尖锐峰，180 meV和330 meV激发分别与双声子和四声子Cu-O键伸缩模能量一致；这些激发出现在10%空穴掺杂时无法分辨，而低能电荷激发由弥散等离激元主导。与Raman谱对比发现声子耦合具有显著选择性（仅偶数阶），偏离Franck-Condon行为。

Conclusion: 实验揭示了铜氧化物中一种超越Franck-Condon图像的异常电子-声子耦合机制——选择性耦合到偶数阶声子，暗示可能存在局域配对的准粒子（如双空穴束缚对），这为理解铜氧化物超导体中的电子配对机制提供了新的线索。

Abstract: In crystals and molecules with strong coupling between the charge carriers and atomic vibrations, high order harmonics of the vibrational excitations can be observed with intensity following the Franck-Condon envelope. Here, we uncover a new regime of electron-phonon coupling in La2-xSrxCuO4 using resonant inelastic X-ray scattering. In the undoped compound, we find sharp peaks at approximately 85$\pm$5 meV, 180$\pm$7 meV, and 330$\pm$5 meV with no observable dispersion, and an absence of appreciable intensity from other modes observed in Raman spectroscopy in this energy range. The 180 meV and 330 meV excitations have energies approximately consistent with two-phonon and four-phonon in-plane Cu-O bond stretching mode, suggesting that the resonantly excited valence electrons strongly couple to this mode. These excitations exhibit significant doping dependence, eventually becoming unresolvable at 10% hole doping, where the low energy charge excitations are dominated by dispersive plasmons. The observation of selective coupling to even-ordered phonons and stark contrast with the Raman spectra indicate anomalous electron-phonon coupling beyond the Franck-Condon picture. One intriguing possibility is the existence of locally paired quasiparticles in insulating cuprates.

</details>


### [180] [Textures as a phase-transition probe for quantum spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08120)
*Heitor P. Casagrande,Isaac M. Carvalho,William J. Munro,Krissia Zawadzki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究量子纹理（quantum texture）的粗糙度（rugosity）度量在量子相变探测中的应用，以海森堡XXZ模型为例，展示了全局与对称分辨粗糙度在相图上的不同特征，特别在Δ=1处出现尖锐特征，可能与BKT转变及SU(2)各向同性点的关联层结构敏感性有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纹理此前已被用于量化相干性和量子门识别，但尚未被系统探索用于量子相变探测。作者旨在将粗糙度度量发展为一类简单有效的相变探针，并建立其与自旋关联器层次结构的联系。

Method: 定义计算基下量子纹理的粗糙度度量，建立其与自旋关联器层级结构的解析联系；分别定义全局基态粗糙度和各磁化扇区内基态（对称分辨）粗糙度，并用于分析海森堡XXZ模型的相图；通过数值计算考察不同尺寸系统的粗糙度行为。

Result: 在两个转变点处均观察到不同的粗糙度特征。特别地，在Δ=1处即使对于小系统也出现了尖锐特征，表明纹理编码的关联层级结构对此点具有显著敏感性。由于BKT转变恰与XXZ模型的SU(2)各向同性点重合，该行为可能反映了粗糙度对各向同性点处自旋关联层级结构的特殊敏感性。

Conclusion: 粗糙度度量是一种简单而有效的量子相变探测工具，尤其对BKT转变点（各向同性SU(2)点）表现出突出的灵敏度，值得进一步在其它模型和体系中探索。

Abstract: The idea of quantum texture has been recently proposed and used as a tool for quantifying coherences and for quantum gate identification. In this work we offer a study on its usage to quantum phase transitions, demonstrating the rugosity metric as a simple tool for effective phase-transition probing. We establish the link between rugosity in the computational basis and the hierarchy of spin correlators, and analyze rugosities defined in the global ground-state and in ground-states belonging to different magnetization sectors (to which we refer to as global vs symmetry-resolved rugosities) to study the phase diagram of the Heisenberg XXZ model. We find distinct rugosity signatures at both transition points. In particular, a sharp feature appears at $Δ=1$ already for small systems, revealing a pronounced sensitivity of the correlation hierarchy encoded by the texture to this point. Since the BKT transition coincides with the isotropic $SU(2)$ point of the XXZ model, this behavior may reflect a particular sensitivity of rugosity to the structure of the spin-correlation hierarchy at isotropy.

</details>


### [181] [Observation of multipartite spin entanglement in a cuprate chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08134)
*S. F. R. TenHuisen,Z. Shen,V. Bhartiya,V. Menon,P. Sharma,H. Padma,Z. Guan,W. He,M. K. Lajer,J. Li,D. Banerjee,J. Pelliciari,I. A. Zaliznyak,G. D. Gu,M. D. Lukin,S. Johnston,M. P. M. Dean,V. Bisogni,Y. Wang,M. Mitrano*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用共振非弹性X射线散射(RIXS)作为研究多体自旋纠缠的探针，通过将RIXS截面投影到适当的自旋激发通道，可直接从测量的自旋涨落谱中提取量子Fisher信息，并在准一维铜氧化物Sr₂CuO₃中检测到至少7体自旋纠缠，这是迄今固体中报道的最大纠缠深度之一，与Hubbard模型预期一致且持续到高温。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料被认为承载高度纠缠的量子态，但长期以来在实验上探测和量化这种纠缠一直是巨大挑战，需要一种能够直接提取多体自旋纠缠信息的实验方法。

Method: 使用RIXS作为多体自旋纠缠的探针；将RIXS截面投影到适当的自旋激发通道；从测量的自旋涨落谱中直接提取量子Fisher信息(QFI)；通过计算纠缠深度来量化多体纠缠程度。

Result: 在模型一维铜氧化物Sr₂CuO₃中检测到至少7体自旋纠缠，这是迄今在固体中报道的最大纠缠深度之一。观测到的纠缠深度与Hubbard模型的理论预期一致，并且在升高温度时仍能保持。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了RIXS作为多体自旋纠缠的实验探针，为扩展到电荷和轨道自由度以及非平衡态的研究提供了平台。

Abstract: Quantum materials are believed to host highly entangled states of matter, but probing and quantifying such entanglement has long remained experimentally elusive. Here, we use resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS) as a probe of multipartite spin entanglement. By projecting the RIXS cross section onto the appropriate spin excitation channel, we show that the Quantum Fisher Information can be extracted directly from the measured spin fluctuation spectrum. We detect at least 7-partite spin entanglement in the model one-dimensional cuprate Sr$_2$CuO$_3$, revealing one of the largest entanglement depths yet reported in a solid. The observed entanglement depth agrees with Hubbard-model expectations and persists to elevated temperatures. Our results establish RIXS as an experimental probe of many-body spin entanglement and provide a platform for extensions to charge and orbital sectors and nonequilibrium settings.

</details>


### [182] [Successive Phase Transitions from a Composite Fermion Liquid to a Fractional Quantum Hall State at ν=3/2 Driven by In-Plane Magnetic Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08141)
*Xinghao Wang,L. N. Pfeiffer,A. Gupta,K. W. Baldwin,K. W. West,Rui-Rui Du*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种在超高迁移率GaAs量子阱中，完全由面内磁场B_||诱导的ν=3/2偶数分母分数量子霍尔态。随着B_||增大，系统经历两次连续转变：从复合费米子液体到软能隙分数量子霍尔态（B_||~12.2 T），再通过拓扑相变到硬能隙的稳健分数量子霍尔态（B_||~14.7 T），并伴随ν=19/13的子态。研究表明可通过面内磁场下的k空间费米面劈裂来工程化拓扑序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索偶数分母分数量子霍尔态的调控机制，特别是在仅依赖面内磁场而非传统垂直磁场的条件下实现这种拓扑态的可行性，以扩展对拓扑有序态的工程化理解和控制能力。

Method: 在超高迁移率GaAs量子阱中施加面内磁场B_||，通过精确调节磁场强度，系统性地测量电输运性质，观察ν=3/2处从复合费米子液体到软能隙态再到硬能隙态的两次相变全过程。

Result: 成功实现了完全由面内磁场诱导的ν=3/2偶数分母分数量子霍尔态，观察到两个临界转折点（B_||~12.2 T和B_||~14.7 T），确立了从复合费米子液体到软能隙FQH态再到硬能隙稳健FQH态的演化路径，并发现了ν=19/13的女儿态。

Conclusion: 该工作证明拓扑序可以通过面内磁场导致的k空间费米轮廓劈裂来人工工程化，为偶数分母FQH态的操控和拓扑量子计算应用提供了新的物理机制和实验平台。

Abstract: We report an even-denominator fractional quantum Hall state at ν = 3/2 induced entirely by in-plane magnetic field B_|| in an ultra-high-mobility GaAs quantum well. As B_|| increases, the system undergoes two successive transitions: from a composite fermion liquid to a soft-gap FQH state (B_||~12.2 T), then via a topological phase transition to a hard-gap robust FQH state (B_||~14.7 T), accompanied by a daughter state at ν = 19/13. We present systematic data, and discuss a possible scenario in interpreting these findings. Our work demonstrates that topological order may be engineered through k-space Fermi contour splitting under an in-plane magnetic field.

</details>


### [183] [Kane-Lubensky phonons in Maxwell lattice frustrated Mott insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08262)
*Hong-Hao Song,Gang v. Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在三维Maxwell晶格阻挫Mott绝缘体中，通过磁拓扑力学机制可以产生Kane-Lubensky型零能Weyl线声子。在具有自旋-晶格耦合的烧绿石反铁磁体中，磁场选择的自旋态导致晶格畸变形成P4_3 32拓扑晶格，进而转化为拓扑Maxwell晶格，承载受拓扑保护的体零频Weyl线声子。这些无隙声子不同于Goldstone模式，导致特征的低温度依赖关系（C_ph ~ T^2而非T^3），并可能在Cr基尖晶石体系中找到应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索三维体系中零能无隙Weyl线声子的产生机制，特别是通过磁拓扑力学在阻挫Mott绝缘体中实现，为拓扑声子物理和声子介导的奇异现象建立新平台。

Method: 在具有自旋-晶格耦合的烧绿石反铁磁体中施加磁场以选择特定自旋态，该自旋态诱导晶格畸变生成P4_3 32拓扑晶格。自旋-晶格耦合与自旋构型导致相邻键弯曲，将体系转化为拓扑Maxwell晶格，从而在零频处产生受拓扑保护的Weyl线声子。

Result: 体系展现出受拓扑保护的体零频Weyl线声子，这些无隙声子并非Goldstone模式。一维零模态流形导致低温声子比热呈现C_ph ~ T^2行为，与常规Goldstone声子的C_ph ~ T^3形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了低能平台，其中大量拓扑零频声子可与其他自由度强耦合，开辟了奇异声子介导现象的研究途径，并有望在Cr基尖晶石体系中实现应用。

Abstract: We show that zero-energy gapless Weyl-line phonons of the Kane-Lubensky's type can arise in the three-dimensional Maxwell lattice frustrated Mott insulators through the magnetopological mechanics. In a pyrochlore antiferromagnet with the spin-lattice coupling, a magnetic field selects the spin state whose lattice distortion generates a $P4_3 32$ topological lattice. More crucially, the spin-lattice coupling and the spin configuration cause the bending of the neighbouring bonds, and converts the system into the topological Maxwell lattice. Remarkably, the resulting system is found to host the bulk zero-frequency Weyl-line phonons protected topologically, and these gapless phonons are not Goldstone modes. Unlike the conventional ${C_{\rm ph}\sim T^3}$ for the Goldstone phonons, these one-dimensional zero-mode manifolds yield a characteristic low-temperature phonon specific heat ${C_{\rm ph}\sim T^2}$. Our results could find applications in the Cr-based spinel systems, and moreover, we establish a low-energy platform where an extensive number of topological zero-frequency phonons can strongly couple to other degrees of freedom, opening a route to exotic phonon-mediated phenomena.

</details>


### [184] [Theory of Kondo hybridization wave in Kondo lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08467)
*Yin Zhong*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于大-N费米子平均场近似，在正方晶格Kondo晶格模型中系统研究了Kondo杂化波(KHW)序的微观理论，建立了基态相图，并解释了UTe2和1T/1H-TaS2中观测到的KHW调制现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近的扫描隧道显微镜实验在重费米子超导体UTe2和人工Kondo晶格系统1T/1H-TaS2中发现了空间调制的Kondo杂化波(KHW)序，这挑战了重费米子物理中Kondo杂化空间均匀的传统范式，需要一个微观理论来解释这一新现象。

Method: 基于正方晶格Kondo晶格模型，采用大-N费米子平均场近似，系统地识别稳定的调制KHW相并建立基态相图。

Result: 发现Q=(0,π)的KHW相产生Kondo杂化隙的单轴条纹调制，能忠实再现UTe2中的空间调制模式；该相固有的晶胞加倍调制精确解释了1T/1H-TaS2中的光谱特征。还预测了可用于区分不同调制波矢KHW态的面内电导各向异性特征。

Conclusion: 该工作为观测到的KHW序提供了微观基础，建立了理解重费米子材料中涌现调制杂化现象的统一理论框架。

Abstract: Recent scanning tunneling microscopy experiments have discovered emergent spatially modulated Kondo hybridization wave (KHW) order in the heavy fermion superconductor UTe$_2$ and the artificial Kondo lattice system 1T/1H-TaS$_2$, challenging the conventional paradigm of spatially uniform Kondo hybridization in heavy fermion physics. Here, we develop a microscopic theory for KHW order based on the canonical square-lattice Kondo lattice model within the large-$N$ fermionic mean-field approximation. We systematically identify stable modulated KHW phases and establish their ground-state phase diagram. The prominent $\boldsymbol{Q}=(0,π)$ KHW phase yields uniaxial stripe modulation of the Kondo hybridization gap, which faithfully reproduces the spatial modulation pattern observed in UTe$_2$. Moreover, its inherent unit-cell-doubling modulation precisely accounts for the spectroscopic features measured in 1T/1H-TaS$_2$. We further predict a characteristic in-plane conductivity anisotropy that serves as a definitive transport fingerprint to discriminate KHW states with distinct ordering wavevectors. Our work provides a microscopic foundation for the newly observed KHW order and establishes a unified theoretical framework for understanding emergent modulated hybridization phenomena in heavy fermion materials.

</details>


### [185] [High-resolution angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy with tunable magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08498)
*Jairo Obando-Guevara,Huu-Thong Le,Emiliano Corcino Aracena,Sayan Singha,Kyungchan Lee,Yingdong Guan,Zhiqiang Mao,Dezhe Jin,Chaoxing Liu,Heike Pfau*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文展示了即使在强轨迹像差条件下，也能通过后处理电子轨迹模拟从磁-角分辨光电子能谱(magneto-ARPES)中高精度恢复电子结构，并保持高动量分辨率。研究以激光ARPES系统在偶极场中测量Bi₂Se₃为例，确定了二维动量映射、微米束斑和精确数值场模拟三个关键技术要求，并通过圆二色性提供磁场与自旋耦合的额外信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场是材料研究中调控和扰动量子现象不可或缺的工具，但将磁场实时调控引入ARPES后，电子轨迹像差会造成显著的光谱畸变和展宽，即使在弱场下也如此，这限制了magneto-ARPES的应用。

Method: 使用激光基ARPES系统在偶极场中研究Bi₂Se₃，通过详细的电子轨迹模拟进行后处理以重建电子结构，并分析二维动量映射、束斑尺寸和数值场模拟等关键技术要素。

Result: 在存在强轨迹像差的情况下，成功地从magneto-ARPES光谱中高精度恢复了电子结构并保持了高动量分辨率；确定了实现高分辨率magneto-ARPES的三个关键技术要求：二维动量映射、微米级束斑尺寸和精确的数值磁场模拟；展示了圆二色性可提供磁场-自旋耦合信息。

Conclusion: 实验结果和模拟的标度律为在更大磁场下开展magneto-ARPES提供了路线图，推动该技术向更高场强应用方向发展。

Abstract: The control and perturbation of quantum phenomena with magnetic fields is an indispensable tool in materials research. Recently, in-situ field tuning was implemented into angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (magneto-ARPES), which provides direct momentum and energy-resolved information of the field-dependent electronic structure. However, aberrations of the electron trajectories were shown to be substantial, leading to significant spectral distortions and broadening even in small fields. Here we show that the electronic structure can be recovered from magneto-ARPES spectra with high accuracy while maintaining high momentum resolution even when strong trajectory aberrations are present. We studied Bi$_2$Se$_3$ in a dipole field of a coil using a laser-based ARPES system. The electronic structure was reconstructed in post-processing using detailed electron trajectory simulations. We identify two-dimensional (2D) momentum mapping, a micron beam spot size, and precise numerical field simulations as critical technical requirements for high-resolution magneto-ARPES. We show how circular dichroism can provide additional information about the coupling of the magnetic field to the spin. The experimental achievements and the scaling laws from our simulations provide a road map towards magneto-ARPES in larger fields.

</details>


### [186] [Schwinger boson perturbation theory for spin-$S$ Kitaev-Heisenberg magnets: phase diagram and dynamical response near Kitaev spin liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08548)
*Daiki Sasamoto,Joji Nasu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文针对自旋-S Kitaev-Heisenberg模型发展了一套Schwinger玻色子微扰框架，以纯Kitaev模型的自旋液体鞍点作为无微扰态，利用Klein对偶性将哈密顿量精确分解，并采用随机相位近似计算磁性不稳定性及动力学自旋关联。对S=1/2, 1, 3/2, 2确定了量子自旋液体与磁性有序相的相边界，发现自旋液体区域随S增大迅速收缩。对S=1的动力学计算显示重整化后的动力学自旋结构因子在有限能量处保留宽的二自旋子连续谱，而低能谱权重在相邻磁性相的序波矢处软化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev-Heisenberg模型是研究量子自旋液体与磁性序竞争的重要模型，但此前对有限自旋S的系统性理论研究不足，尤其是如何在保持自旋液体特征的同时计算其磁性不稳定性和动力学性质。作者旨在建立一种能够可靠描述自旋液体附近自旋动力学的理论框架。

Method: 采用Schwinger玻色子方法，以纯Kitaev模型的自旋液体鞍点作为无微扰基态。利用Kitaev-Heisenberg模型内禀的Klein对偶性将哈密顿量精确分解为两部分：对偶不变项作为无微扰项，对偶符号反转项作为微扰项，进行随机相位近似(RPA)计算。

Result: 确定了S=1/2、1、3/2和2时量子自旋液体与磁性有序相之间的相边界；自旋液体区域随S增大快速收缩，在S=2时变得非常狭窄。对S=1的动力学计算表明，重整化后的动力学自旋结构因子在有限能量处保持宽的二自旋子连续谱，同时低能谱权重在相邻磁性相的序波矢处发生软化。

Conclusion: 该框架使得在热力学极限下计算磁性失稳附近的自旋动力学成为可能，为理解自旋液体与磁性序之间的竞争关系以及相关动力学现象提供了有效的理论工具。

Abstract: We develop a Schwinger boson perturbative framework for the spin-$S$ Kitaev-Heisenberg model. The spin-liquid saddle point of the pure Kitaev model is used as the unperturbed state, and magnetic instabilities and dynamical spin correlations are evaluated around this saddle point. We decompose the Hamiltonian exactly into two parts by exploiting the Klein duality intrinsic to the Kitaev-Heisenberg model. We perform the random-phase approximation by taking the part invariant under the duality transformation as the unperturbed term and treating the remaining term, which changes sign under it, as the perturbation. We determine the phase boundaries separating the quantum spin-liquid regimes from the adjacent magnetically ordered phases for $S=1/2$, $1$, $3/2$, and $2$. The spin-liquid regions shrink rapidly with increasing $S$ and become very narrow at $S=2$. We also compute the spin dynamics of the spin-$S$ Kitaev-Heisenberg model, focusing on $S=1$, and find that the dressed dynamical spin structure factor retains a broad two-spinon continuum at finite energies, while the low-energy spectral weight softens at the ordering wave vectors of the adjacent magnetic phases. Our framework thus enables thermodynamic-limit calculations of spin dynamics near magnetic instabilities.

</details>


### [187] [Magnetic phases of Kondo lattice materials Ce$_5$RhGe$_2$ and Ce$_5$IrGe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08580)
*Jiawen Zhang,Yanan Zhang,Mingyi Wang,Ye Chen,Yu Liu,Yongjun Zhang,Michael Smidman,Huiqiu Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Ce5RhGe2和Ce5IrGe2单晶的电阻率、比热和磁化强度，并与Ce5CoGe2进行对比。三者均结晶为正交Pnma结构，晶格参数从Co到Rh到Ir单调增大。Ce5RhGe2在约11.5 K处呈铁磁序，行为与Ce5CoGe2相似；而Ce5IrGe2在12.7 K和11.8 K发生两次连续的磁转变，并在磁场下呈现多个变磁转变，产生Ms/5和Ms/3的磁化平台。Ce5IrGe2在最低测量温度下展现出亚铁磁基态，表现为零场附近的显著磁滞回线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过系统研究同系列稀土化合物Ce5TGe2（T=Co, Rh, Ir）随化学压力变化的磁性与结构性质，揭示负化学压力对磁有序类型、磁转变行为及基态磁结构的影响，特别是理解Ce5IrGe2中独特的亚铁磁基态和磁化平台的形成机制。

Method: 制备Ce5RhGe2和Ce5IrGe2单晶，沿三个主晶轴方向（a、b、c轴）进行电输运（电阻率）、热力学（比热）和磁学（磁化强度）系统的测量，并结合晶体结构分析（Pnma正交结构）及与同系列Ce5CoGe2的对比研究。

Result: 所有三种化合物均结晶为正交Pnma结构，晶格参数随T元素从Co到Rh到Ir单调增大，符合负化学压力效应。a轴为易磁化方向。Ce5RhGe2在约11.5 K呈铁磁有序，行为接近Ce5CoGe2。Ce5IrGe2在TM1=12.7 K和TM2=11.8 K经历两次磁转变，高磁场下存在多个变磁转变，磁化平台约在Ms/5和Ms/3处。沿易磁化轴的低场变磁转变随温度降低向低场移动，最终在最低温度下于零场附近观察到显著磁滞回线，表明其具有亚铁磁基态。

Conclusion: Ce5TGe2系列中，负化学压力的增加（从Co到Ir）显著改变了磁基态性质：Ce5RhGe2保持铁磁序，而Ce5IrGe2则进入亚铁磁基态，并伴随分数磁化平台（Ms/5和Ms/3）及多重变磁转变，这些现象表明Ir化合物的磁结构更加复杂，可能与RKKY相互作用调制、晶体场效应或几何阻挫等因素有关。

Abstract: Single crystals of Ce$_5$RhGe$_2$ and Ce$_5$IrGe$_2$ have been systematically investigated by electrical resistivity, specific heat, and magnetization measurements. Together with Ce$_5$CoGe$_2$, all three compounds crystallize in the orthorhombic \emph{Pnma} structure, with the lattice parameters increasing monotonically from Co to Rh to Ir, consistent with the effect of negative chemical pressure. Magnetization measurements along the three principal crystallographic axes identify the \emph{a} axis as the easy magnetization direction throughout the series. Ce$_5$RhGe$_2$ exhibits ferromagnetic ordering with a Curie temperature of approximately 11.5 K and shows magnetic behavior closely resembling that of Ce$_5$CoGe$_2$. In contrast, Ce$_5$IrGe$_2$ undergoes two successive magnetic transitions at $T_{\rm M1}=12.7$ K and $T_{\rm M2}=11.8$ K, and there are multiple metamagnetic transitions under magnetic fields, giving rise to magnetization plateaus at fractions of the saturation magnetization $M_{\rm s}$ of approximately $M_{\rm s}/5$ and $M_{\rm s}/3$. The low-field metamagnetic transition along the easy axis shifts to lower field with decreasing temperature, and eventually a pronounced hysteresis loop is observed about zero-field, establishing that Ce$_5$IrGe$_2$ exhibits a ferrimagnetic ground state at the lowest measured temperatures.

</details>


### [188] [$g$-wave altermagnetic order parameter in hematite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08693)
*Tianren Wang,Yuehong Li,Yu Feng,Andong Liu,Yuetong Wu,Qian Zhao,Yujie Yan,Wei Luo,Xin Tong,Yi Lu,Yao Shen,Stefano Agrestini,Jaewon Choi,Qisi Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过非弹性中子散射实验，定量绘制了赤铁矿（α-Fe₂O₃）中交替磁（altermagnetic）磁振子劈裂的动量空间对称性，揭示了g波对称性，并证实了交替磁手性磁振子的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁体结合了反铁磁体的净磁化消失特性和依赖于动量的自旋劈裂特性。磁振子带劈裂可直接探测交替磁序，并可能实现手性选择性磁振子输运，但该劈裂的动量空间对称性此前尚未被定量确定。

Method: 利用非弹性中子散射技术，系统绘制赤铁矿中交替磁磁振子劈裂随动量变化的分布图；结合线性自旋波理论计算进行对比验证，基于交替磁模型识别非简并能带为相反手性的磁振子，并将劈裂归因于对称性不等价的长程交换相互作用。

Result: 实验发现磁振子劈裂沿节线（nodal directions）方向消失，并在节点外达到最大值，揭示了交替磁序参量的g波对称性。理论计算与实验结果吻合，确认了非简并分支为相反手性的磁振子，这是对交替磁手性磁振子动量空间对称性的首次定量确定。

Conclusion: 结合赤铁矿的高磁有序温度和低磁振子阻尼特性，该材料可作为一种有前景的平台，用于低耗散、对称性选择性的磁振子应用。

Abstract: Altermagnets combine the vanishing net magnetization of antiferromagnets with momentum-dependent spin splitting. Magnon band splitting provides a direct probe of altermagnetic order and may enable chirality-selective magnon transport, yet the momentum-space symmetry of this splitting has not been determined quantitatively. Here we use inelastic neutron scattering to map the momentum dependence of altermagnetic magnon splitting in hematite ($α$-Fe$_2$O$_3$). The splitting vanishes along nodal directions and reaches maxima off the nodes, revealing the $g$-wave symmetry of the altermagnetic order parameter. These results agree with linear spin-wave theory calculations based on the altermagnetic model, which further identify the nondegenerate branches as magnons of opposite chirality and trace the splitting to symmetry-inequivalent long-range exchange interactions. Our results provide the first quantitative determination of the momentum-space symmetry of altermagnetic chiral magnons. These findings, together with hematite's high magnetic ordering temperature and low magnon damping, establish it as a promising platform for low-dissipation, symmetry-selective magnonic applications.

</details>


### [189] [Examining Convergence of Cluster Perturbation Theory and Cluster Coupled Cluster for J1-J2 Heisenberg Spin Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08768)
*Jeffrey Keyes,Carlos Jimenez-Hoyos*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在之前工作的基础上，将基于簇平均场(cMF)波函数的微扰理论(PT)和耦合簇(CC)方法从cPT4和cCCSD扩展到cPT7和cCCSDTQ，证明了J1-J2海森堡模型在热力学极限下的能量收敛性，并讨论了在热力学极限下高效进行簇基计算的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: cMF的核心主张是将强关联系统转化为弱关联系统，从而可以使用基于弱关联的微扰理论和耦合簇方法。为验证这一主张，需要证明PT和CC级数确实收敛，而先前的工作仅局限在cPT4和cCCSD，无法充分确认收敛行为。

Method: 将簇微扰理论扩展到七阶(cPT7)，并将簇耦合簇扩展至包含三激发、四激发和五激发的CCSDTQ5级别(cCCSDTQ5)，同时开发了在热力学极限下高效执行簇基计算的方法以支撑这些高阶计算。

Result: 在热力学极限下，cPT7和cCCSDTQ5的能量结果显示出收敛趋势，证实了cMF作为零阶波函数能使强关联系统转变为弱关联系统从而支持高阶微扰和耦合簇方法收敛的核心主张。此外，提出的高效计算方法使这些昂贵的高阶计算在热力学极限下变得可行。

Conclusion: 高阶cPT和cCCSDTQ的计算结果表明能量实际收敛，验证了cMF方法的核心假设。这为将簇基方法用于强关联体系的热力学极限研究提供了坚实基础，并使更精确的能量计算成为可能。

Abstract: In previous work, it was shown that using the cluster mean field(cMF) wavefunction as the zeroth order wavefunction for perturbation theory and coupled cluster produces a reasonable approximation for the J 1-J 2 Heisenberg model in the thermodynamic limit(TDL). However, this work was limited to cPT4 and cCCSD. The central claim of cMF is that it takes strongly correlated systems and makes them weakly correlated so that PT weakly correlated methods can be used. To confirm that this claim is true, it is important to show that the PT and CC series converge. In this paper, we extend the previous calculations to cPT7 and cCCSDTQ5 where we show that the energy is converging. We also discuss how to perform cluster based calculations efficiently in the thermodynamic limit, which is crucial for these more expensive calculations.

</details>


### [190] [Positive Temperature Coefficient of Anisotropy due to $d^6L$ Groundstate in $ε$-Fe$_2$O$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08927)
*Rachel Nickel,Kurt Kummer,Johan van Lierop*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了ε-Fe₂O₃及其Cr掺杂体系中正各向异性温度系数(dK/dT>0)的起源，发现金属-配体杂化在磁性、电子和轨道电子学性质调控中的关键作用。纯ε-Fe₂O₃中d⁶L基态导致dK/dT>0，而Cr掺杂削弱Fe-O杂化后系统呈现d⁶+d⁶L²组合态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正各向异性温度系数通常源于磁性3d态与强自旋轨道耦合(SOC)子系统的杂化，但ε-Fe₂O₃在缺乏明显SOC伙伴的情况下仍表现出dK/dT>0（125-200 K），这一反常识现象需要理论解释。

Method: 对比研究纯ε-Fe₂O₃与Cr掺杂（削弱Fe-O杂化）的ε-Fe₂O₃，分析从低温非公度相到高各向异性磁性相的转变过程，识别电子基态构型并关联金属-配体杂化强度。

Result: 纯ε-Fe₂O₃中识别出d⁶L基态（L为配体空穴），其金属-配体杂化贡献了正的温度各向异性系数；Cr掺杂削弱杂化后，体系基态变为d⁶+d⁶L²组合，验证了杂化调控机制。

Conclusion: 金属-配体杂化可作为一种不依赖SOC伙伴的调控手段，用于调节磁性、电子和轨道电子学（orbitronic）性质，为设计具有正各向异性温度系数的磁性材料提供新路径。

Abstract: Positive anisotropy temperature coefficients ($dK/dT>0$) usually arise from hybridization between magnetic 3d states and strongly spin-orbit-coupled (SOC) subsystems. Yet $ε$-Fe$_2$O$_3$ shows $dK/dT>0$ (125-200~K) without an obvious SOC partner. We study pure and Cr-doped (with weakened Fe-O hybridization) $ε$-Fe$_2$O$_3$ across the transition from low temperature incommensurate to high-anisotropy magnetic phases. We identify a $d^6L$ groundstate in $ε$-Fe$_2$O$_3$ while reduced hybridization yields $d^6 + d^6L^2$ in the Cr-doped system, highlighting metal-ligand hybridization as a route to tune magnetic, electronic and orbitronic properties.

</details>


### [191] [Real-time Dyson expansion for the nonequilibrium $GW$ approximation: Correlated spectra and dependence on the reference propagator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08932)
*Erik Schroedter,Jan-Philip Joost,Michael Bonitz,Vojtech Vlcek*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了基于实时Dyson展开（RT-DE）的非平衡GW自能方案，以时间线性成本恢复频谱中的动力学关联信息，并通过多种参考传播子（Hartree-Fock、静态屏蔽交换、基于HF的GKBA）进行基准测试，验证了其在光谱精确性和散射诱导占据动力学方面的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡态下GW近似的全双时间模拟随传播时间立方增长，计算代价高昂；而时间局域近似或广义Kadanoff-Baym近似（GKBA）往往仅保留平均场特征，无法在频谱中恢复动力学关联。已有的RT-DE方案虽能以时间线性成本恢复关联，但仅限于二阶Born近似和平均场参考传播子，尚未推广到非平衡GW自能。

Method: 将RT-DE推广到非平衡GW自能，采用具有时间局域非对角演化的通用形式参考传播子，具体评估了三种参考：Hartree-Fock传播子、含非平衡屏蔽的静态屏蔽交换传播子、以及基于Hartree-Fock GKBA的关联传播子。对具有长程相互作用的驱动双带格点模型进行精确对角化基准测试，并在超出精确方法可达的大系统中探测激子复刻和卫星结构。

Result: 基准测试表明，平均场和静态屏蔽参考传播子提供了最准确的频谱，能恢复平均场水平缺失的卫星结构；而基于GKBA的方法最能捕捉散射诱导的占据数动力学，但会人为展宽和分裂谱峰。应用于大系统时，方案成功分辨出价带的激子复刻和激子shake-up卫星结构，并揭示了这些结构随激发密度增加的重塑行为。

Conclusion: RT-DE-GW方案为大规模非平衡电子光谱模拟提供了高效且准确的新工具：不同参考传播子的选择在光谱精度和动力学描述之间提供了灵活的权衡，使该方案能够同时捕捉关联光谱特征和超越精确对角化能力的大系统行为。

Abstract: Electronic spectra provide direct insight into the excitations and correlations of condensed matter systems. Their description requires electron correlations beyond mean field. In equilibrium, the $GW$ approximation has become the method of choice for many materials. Extending this approximation to nonequilibrium, however, is challenging. Full two-time $GW$ simulations within the nonequilibrium Green's functions approach scale at least cubically with propagation time, whereas improved scaling schemes such as time-local (adiabatic) approximations or the generalized Kadanoff--Baym ansatz usually retain only mean-field character in the spectra. The recently introduced real-time Dyson expansion recovers dynamical correlations in the spectrum at time-linear cost, but has so far been restricted to the second-order Born approximation with mean-field reference propagators. Here we formulate and implement the RT-DE for the nonequilibrium $GW$ self-energy with reference propagators of general form whose off-diagonal evolution is time local. In particular, we assess Hartree--Fock propagators, propagators with statically screened exchange with nonequilibrium screening, and a correlated propagator based on the Hartree--Fock GKBA. Benchmarks against exact diagonalization for a driven two-band lattice model with long-range interactions show that the mean-field and statically screened references yield the most accurate spectra, including satellite structures absent at mean-field level, whereas the GKBA-based approach best captures scattering-induced occupation dynamics, but artificially broadens and splits spectral peaks. When applied to large systems that are beyond the reach of exact methods, the scheme resolves the excitonic replica of the valence band and satellites identified as exciton shake-up, as well as their reshaping with increasing excitation density.

</details>


### [192] [On spectral properties of Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09067)
*Miroslav Hopjan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩展广义Kadanoff-Baym散射（extended GKBA）在Hubbard团簇中恢复标准GKBA碰撞积分内隐藏光谱性质的能力。通过与完整Kadanoff-Baym方程比较基态谱函数，发现中等相互作用强度下两者吻合良好，但在强相互作用区域出现显著偏差，且扩展GKBA得到的谱函数出现负值等非物理性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Kadanoff-Baym散射（GKBA）与平均场传播子结合能显著降低非平衡量子系统模拟的计算成本（计算量随传播时间线性增长），但其时间对角结构导致输运方程碰撞积分中的谱性质被掩盖。作者希望利用扩展GKBA来恢复并研究这些被隐藏的谱性质，以评估GKBA方法的适用边界。

Method: 采用扩展广义Kadanoff-Baym散射（extended GKBA）方法，在Hubbard团簇模型上计算基态谱函数，并与标准Kadanoff-Baym方程的完整结果进行系统对比。研究涵盖从中等相互作用强度到强相互作用强度的参数范围。

Result: 在中等相互作用强度下，扩展GKBA与标准Kadanoff-Baym方程得到的谱函数吻合良好；但在强相互作用区域，两者出现显著偏差，且扩展GKBA得到的谱函数变为负值，表明其在该区域产生非物理的谱性质。

Conclusion: 尽管GKBA具有计算效率优势，但该方法在强相互作用体系中存在重要局限。当相互作用足够强时，GKBA可能产生非物理的谱性质（如负的谱函数），这限制了其在强关联非平衡量子系统模拟中的适用性。

Abstract: The Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz with mean-field propagators is increasingly used to simulate non-equilibrium quantum fermionic and bosonic systems. Compared with the full Kadanoff--Baym equations, its mean-field propagators substantially reduce computational cost, enabling simulations with computational effort that scales linearly with propagation time. However, the time-diagonal structure of the Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz obscures spectral properties within the collision integral of the transport equation. Here, we recover and investigate these hidden spectral properties using the extended Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz. For a Hubbard cluster, we compare ground-state spectral functions obtained with the extended Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz against those from standard Kadanoff--Baym equations. At moderate interaction strengths, the spectral functions show good agreement. At large interaction strengths, however, significant deviations emerge, and the spectral function obtained with the extended Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz becomes negative. These results demonstrate that, despite its computational advantages, the Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz has important limitations in strongly interacting regimes and may produce unphysical spectral properties when interactions become sufficiently large.

</details>


### [193] [Green's Functions from Sample-based Krylov Quantum Diagonalization: An Impurity Solver for Dynamical Mean-Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09147)
*Jay Patel,Chakradhar Rangi,Ka-Ming Tam*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文将基于样本的Krylov量子对角化（SKQD）方法从基态计算推广到单粒子格林函数计算。通过在N±1粒子数扇区构造并采样幺正Krylov子空间，并以经典方式计算所有连接扇区的重叠量，该方法利用Lanczos连分式重构格林函数，同时保持SKQD的浅电路、无辅助比特特性。在粒子-空穴对称的单杂质Anderson模型及DMFT离散浴表示中，该方法以较小的希尔伯特空间占比恢复了谱函数，在包含金属-绝缘体转变的相互作用强度范围内再现了主要谱特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期的量子硬件更适合运行浅电路，但DMFT杂质求解器需要处理多个浴位点，而现有的量子算法在计算格林函数时通常需要较深的电路或辅助量子比特，限制了可处理的浴位点数量。将SKQD方法推广到格林函数计算，有望在近期量子硬件上实现更大规模的DMFT杂质求解。

Method: 在N+1和N-1粒子数扇区中构造并采样幺正Krylov子空间，经典计算所有连接各扇区的重叠矩阵元，然后通过Lanczos连分式重构单粒子格林函数。量子设备仅需制备和采样短时演化态，不需辅助比特或深电路。

Result: 在链几何的粒子-空穴对称单杂质Anderson模型上验证，该方法仅用完整希尔伯特空间的一小部分即恢复了谱函数。在跨越金属-绝缘体转变的相互作用强度范围内，主要谱特征均被正确再现。

Conclusion: SKQD-based格林函数计算可能使DMFT杂质求解器在近期量子硬件上处理更多浴位点，超越当前实际可行的规模，为量子计算辅助强关联材料模拟提供了新路径。

Abstract: We generalize the sample-based Krylov quantum diagonalization (SKQD) method from ground-state calculations to the evaluation of single-particle Green's functions. By constructing and sampling unitary Krylov subspaces in the N +/- 1 particle-number sectors and evaluating all sector-connecting overlaps classically, the approach reconstructs the Green's function via a Lanczos continued fraction while retaining the shallow-circuit, ancilla-free character of SKQD. The quantum device is required only to prepare and sample short-time evolutions. Applied to the particle-hole-symmetric single-impurity Anderson model in chain geometry, with the discrete bath representation used in dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT), the method recovers the spectral function using a relatively small fraction of the full Hilbert space. Across a range of interaction strengths that spans the metal-insulator transition, the main spectral features are reproduced. These results suggest that SKQD-based Green's-function calculations may allow DMFT impurity solvers with a larger number of bath sites on near-term quantum hardware than is currently practical.

</details>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Distribution of currents in a system of active particles with biased hops and Ising interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05649)
*Evgeniy Khain,Shriram Ramesh,Vladimir Chernyak*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究二维格点上受偏置随机游走的粒子系综，粒子间具有最近邻伊辛相互作用导致跳跃概率依赖邻居数量和粘附强度，从而产生宏观电流。作者通过类比Becker-Döring团簇方程推导各种粒子构型（单体、二聚体、三聚体）的浓度速率方程，理论计算了电流分布并通过随机粒子模拟验证。有趣发现：在非零跳跃不对称和粒子相互作用下，左手三聚体与右手三聚体的浓度不相等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解粒子间粘附相互作用与跳跃偏置（非对称性）共同作用下系统的宏观输运性质（如电流）及微观构型统计，为驱动态平衡系统中的团簇动力学提供理论框架。

Method: (1) 构建二维格点上的偏置随机游走模型，粒子间具最近邻伊辛粘附作用；(2) 跳跃概率依赖邻居数和相互作用强度；(3) 建立类似Becker-Döring团簇方程的单体、二聚体及不同三聚体构型浓度的速率方程；(4) 理论上计算宏观电流分布；(5) 通过随机粒子模拟验证理论结果。

Result: 成功推导了各种粒子构型的浓度表达式和电流分布的理论结果，并在随机粒子模拟中得到验证。揭示了非对称跳跃和非零相互作用条件下，手性对映的三聚体构型（左/右手性三聚体）浓度不等这一非平衡对称性破缺现象。

Conclusion: 粘合粒子体系中的偏置随机游走可借助含团簇动力学的速率方程精确描述，而左右手三聚体浓度不等表明驱动态系统可自发打破空间反演（手性）对称性，这对理解非平衡态下的自组装和定向输运具有重要意义。

Abstract: We consider an ensemble of particles performing a biased random walk on a two-dimensional lattice. Due to the adhesion between particles (nearest-neighbor Ising interactions), the probability of hopping depends on the number of neighbors and the strength of adhesive interaction. The asymmetry in the hopping directions leads to a macroscopic current of particles through the system. We theoretically compute the distribution of currents and verify the results in stochastic particle simulations. To make theoretical progress, one has to determine the probabilities of various particle configurations such as single particles, pairs, and different configurations of three-particle clusters. These probabilities are theoretically derived by formulating rate equations for the concentrations of various structures resembling Becker-Döring cluster equations. One intriguing finding is that the concentration of left-handed triplets is not equal to the concentration of right-handed triplets for non-zero asymmetry of hops and non-zero interaction between particles.

</details>


### [195] [Foundations of Many-Body Theory of Quantum Unified Statistics: Green functions and Linear Response Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05878)
*M. Beyrami,M. N. Najafi,H. Esmaili,M. Dournama,H. Mohammadzadeh*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发展了服从量子统一统计（quon）的多粒子系统的完整多体理论，包括Fock空间性质、S矩阵展开、广义Wick定理以及零温和有限温度下的Green函数形式体系，并在此框架下利用随机相位近似推导出介电函数，揭示了传统玻色子或费米子系统中所不具备的异常等离激元模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然玻色子和费米子统计的量子多体理论已十分成熟，但服从更一般量子统计（即quon或无限统计）的粒子系统的多体理论尚不完善，尤其是其集体行为与屏蔽性质的研究缺乏系统性的理论框架。

Method: 首先探索quon系统的Fock空间性质，随后建立系统的S矩阵展开和广义Wick定理；构造零温与有限温度下自洽的Green函数形式体系，并给出适用于无限统计算符代数的广义Wick定理；在此基础上确立相互作用的quon系统的费曼图规则，并采用随机相位近似推导介电函数等物理量。

Result: 推导出了quon系统的介电函数，发现了在传统玻色子或费米子体系中没有直接对应的异常等离激元模式；进一步分析了基态能量、能量损失函数、广义Thomas-Fermi屏蔽波矢及Friedel振荡，阐明了无限统计对集体行为和屏蔽性质的定性改变。

Conclusion: 无限统计（quon统计）从根本上改变了多体系统的集体行为与屏蔽特性，产生了超越传统量子统计框架的新物理现象，为广义统计量子多体理论奠定了系统性的方法基础。

Abstract: We develop a comprehensive many-body theory for systems of particles obeying quantum unified statistics, or quons. After exploring the properties of the Fock space of this system, we formulate a systematic S-matrix expansion and a generalized Wick's theorem. A consistent Green-function formalism is constructed at both zero and finite temperatures, accompanied by a generalized Wick's theorem appropriate for infinite-statistics operator algebras. Within this framework, we establish diagrammatic rules for interacting quon systems. Employing the random phase approximation, we derive the dielectric function and reveal the emergence of anomalous plasmon modes that have no direct counterpart in conventional Bose or Fermi systems. We further analyze the ground-state energy, energy-loss function, generalized Thomas-Fermi screening wave vectors, and Friedel oscillations, elucidating how infinite statistics qualitatively modifies collective behavior and screening properties.

</details>


### [196] [Nanothermodynamics: stable thermal equilibrium and nanoscale fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06121)
*Ralph V. Chamberlin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文综述并重新评估了纳米热力学（nanothermodynamics），强调Hill的细分势(E)在大系统细分为小系统平衡分布中的关键作用。研究展示了纳米热力学对测量、理论和模拟的深刻洞察：测量显示多数材料内部存在多重有效温度(T_i)导致的热力学异质性；理论方面，E=0是Ising有限自旋链模型稳定解的必要条件，并提供了一种使半经典理想气体熵精确广延化的Gibbs佯谬新解法；模拟方面，揭示了标准涨落关系在局部自由度波动快于其与热浴耦合时的修正，并通过Creutz类模型证明最大化总熵需要内在不可逆步骤，为"统计力学源于可逆动力学"提供了反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热力学假设系统足够大以至于边界效应可忽略，但对于纳米尺度的小系统，这种假设不再成立。作者旨在重新审视Hill的纳米热力学理论，强调细分势E在保证任意尺寸系统遵循热力学第一、第二定律中的核心作用，并展示该框架如何为测量、理论和模拟中的诸多问题提供统一的新见解。

Method: 本文采用综述与重新评估的方法，结合三类证据：（1）测量证据，展示材料内部的多重有效温度(T_i)所反映的热力学异质性；（2）理论分析，包括Ising模型有限链的稳定解（要求E=0）以及半经典理想气体熵广延化的Gibbs佯谬新解；（3）计算模拟，包括分子动力学模拟（考察局部自由度涨落快于热浴耦合时标准涨落关系的修正）和Creutz类模型模拟（Ising自旋耦合Einstein振子显式热浴，用于研究第二定律与涨落特性）。

Result: 研究发现：（1）多数材料中存在多重有效温度，说明热力学异质性是普遍现象，需要纳米热力学解释；（2）Ising原始模型（有限自旋链）的稳定解需要E=0，而该解在Ising发表40年后Hill才提出理论，暗示此前未能发现；（3）Gibbs佯谬获得新解，使半经典理想气体熵严格广延；（4）分子动力学模拟修正了标准涨落关系，与测量到的热力学异质性一致；（5）Creutz类模型的最大总熵要求内在不可逆步骤，反驳统计力学由可逆动力学涌现的常见主张；该模型的涨落由Einstein对Boltzmann关系及第二定律的倒置描述最佳，而非近期涨落定理。

Conclusion: 纳米热力学为理解从纳米到宏观尺度的热力学行为提供了统一而必要的框架。细分势E并非仅对小系统重要，而是系统各组成部分间热力学耦合的普适体现。该框架不仅解释了测量中观察到的热力学异质性，还解决了Ising模型和Gibbs佯谬中的理论难题，并通过模拟挑战了统计力学源于可逆动力学的传统观念，提示第二定律的本质联系着不可逆性。

Abstract: Nanothermodynamics describes the process where large systems subdivide into equilibrium distributions of small subsystems. A key ingredient is Hill's subdivision potential (E) that ensures adherence to the 1st and 2nd laws of thermodynamics in systems of any size. In this review and reassessment, it is emphasized that nanothermodynamics gives new insight into many measurements, theories, and simulations. Measurements establishing the need for nanothermodynamics show thermodynamic heterogeneity from multiple effective temperatures (T_i) inside most types of materials. One theoretical result that requires E=0 is the stable solution of Ising's original model for finite chains of interacting spins, a solution Ising could not have found 40 years before Hill's work. Another result is a novel solution to Gibbs' paradox that makes the entropy of the semiclassical ideal gas exactly extensive. Molecular dynamics simulations reveal how a standard fluctuation relation is modified when local degrees of freedom fluctuate faster than their coupling to the heat bath, consistent with the measured thermodynamic heterogeneity. Simulations of a Creutz-like model, comprised of Ising spins coupled to an explicit heat bath of Einstein oscillators, are used to study the 2nd law. It is found that maximizing the total entropy (S_t) requires an intrinsically irreversible step, providing a counterexample to the usual claim that statistical mechanics emerges from reversible dynamics. Furthermore, fluctuations of this model are best described by Einstein's reversal of Boltzmann's relation and the 2nd-law, not by recent fluctuation theorems.

</details>


### [197] [Statistical mechanical evaluation of a spread-spectrum watermarking model with image restoration II AT stability of a hybrid system with message decoding and image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06405)
*Tatsuya Uezu,Kao Hayashi,Masaki Kawamura*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对基于贝叶斯估计和无限范围Ising模型的高斯信道扩频水印模型，在盲检测情形下研究了复制对称(RS)解的de Almeida-Thouless(AT)稳定性，发现理论结果与模拟结果不一致的参数范围内AT稳定性被破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作（arxiv 1209.4772）中，作者对基于无限范围Ising模型的扩频水印系统进行了统计力学分析，使用复制方法推导了RS解，并发现RS解的理论结果与马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛模拟在部分参数范围内存在不一致，本文旨在解释这一偏差的根源。

Method: 采用统计力学中的复制方法，在盲检测情形下对RS解的de Almeida-Thouless(AT)稳定性进行分析，通过AT稳定性条件判定RS解的可靠性，并将AT不稳定区域与先前理论-模拟结果不一致的参数范围进行对照。

Result: 揭示了RS解的AT稳定性在理论结果与模拟结果不一致的参数范围内被破坏，从而解释了先前工作中理论预测与模拟之间偏差产生的原因。

Conclusion: RS解在部分参数范围内的失效源于AT稳定性破缺，这意味着在该区域内需要采用复制对称破缺(RSB)方案或其他方法以获得更精确的理论描述。

Abstract: In the previous paper (arxiv.org/abs/1209.4772), we proposed a spread-spectrum watermarking model with image restoration based on Bayes estimation assuming several prior probabilities and adopting the Gaussian channel model to represent attacks from unauthorized users. When an image is generated from the infinite range Ising model, we analyzed the model using the statistical mechanical method, the replica method, and derived the replica symmetric (RS) solution and performed Markov chain Monte Carlo simulations. The theoretical results of the RS solution and the simulation results were in good agreement except for some range of parameters. We treated the informed case where only the original image is known and the blind case where both the original message and the original image are unknown and found that the difference between these cases was small as long as the embedding and attack rates were small. In this paper, we treat the blind case and investigate the de Almeida-Thouless (AT) stability of the RS solution and reveal that the AT stability is broken in the range of parameters where theoretical and simulation results do not agree.

</details>


### [198] [Improved mean squared displacement analysis for anomalous single particle trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06828)
*Jakub Ślęzak,Joanna Janczura,Diego Krapf,Ralf Metzler*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义最小二乘法（GLS）的框架，用于从短/超短单粒子轨迹中更准确地估计异常扩散参数。该方法通过减少时间平均引入的相关性和偏差，显著降低了方差和偏差，并且能预测估计误差的概率密度（渐近高斯），进而引入去卷积算法重建实验数据中粒子的系综结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异质复杂介质中分析单粒子扩散轨迹时，轨迹通常很短（约10-100个点），传统回归方法在log-log空间中拟合时间平均MSD时表现不佳，因为时间平均引入相关性且异常扩散本身具有相关性，导致大方差和偏差。亟需一种能从短轨迹中提取最大信息的方法。

Method: 采用广义最小二乘（GLS）框架对时间平均MSD进行回归分析，以显式处理时间平均引入的相关性和异常扩散固有的相关结构。该方法完全自动化、无需人为监督。此外，利用GLS框架实现误差概率密度的理论预测（渐近高斯），并基于此开发一种专门的去卷积算法，用于从实验数据中重建潜在的粒子系综分布。

Result: GLS方法在短轨迹（约100点）和超短轨迹（约10点）情况下显著降低了扩散参数估计的方差和偏差。该方法还能预测估计误差的概率密度分布，其渐近形式为高斯分布，并基于这一预测可实现去卷积，以重建实验中的粒子系综结构。

Conclusion: 该研究为异质介质中的短单粒子轨迹分析提供了一种自动化、无监督的鲁棒估计工具，同时通过误差密度预测和去卷积算法开辟了从噪声测量中恢复潜在扩散参数分布的新途径。

Abstract: The mean squared displacement (MSD) is a cornerstone in the analysis of diffusion processes in complex media. When the system is heterogeneous and, in particular, when single-particle trajectories are short, it is essential to extract maximal information from each measured trajectory. This is typically done by time-averaging squared increments and examining the scaling of the time-averaged MSD in log-log space. However, classical regression methods perform poorly in this setting because time-averaging introduces correlations aggravated by those inherent to anomalous diffusion. We tackle these limitations by applying a generalized least squares framework, which substantially reduces variance and bias in diffusion parameter estimates, especially for short (around 100 points) and ultra-short (around 10 points) trajectories. The method is fully automated and requires no supervision. Furthermore, it enables prediction of estimation error probability density, which is asymptotically Gaussian, for both classical and enhanced approaches. Leveraging this prediction, we introduce a specialized deconvolution algorithm that reconstructs the underlying particle ensemble structure from experimental data.

</details>


### [199] [Relaxation of Sliding Friction from a Statistical Model of Aging Contacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06998)
*Ryudo Suzuki*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个最小模型，将接触力的对数老化与反映分形界面粗糙度的幂律接触尺寸分布相结合，解析推导出摩擦弛豫函数，揭示了中间时间尺度上的平台、对数衰减或幂律衰减行为（取决于幂律指数），并与现象学定律中假设的指数衰减形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突发速度变化后的摩擦松弛是描述稳态滑动稳定性和地震断层现象学定律的基础，但现有的现象学定律无法将宏观摩擦弛豫与微观接触的可测量统计量联系起来，需要在微观机制与宏观行为之间建立理论桥梁。

Method: 构建一个最小模型，包含两个关键成分：(1) 接触力的对数老化行为；(2) 由分形界面粗糙度决定的幂律接触尺寸分布。在该框架下解析推导摩擦弛豫函数，分析幂律指数α对中间时间尺度响应行为的影响。

Result: 理论表明，弛豫响应取决于幂律指数α：不同α值下，中间时间响应呈现平台、对数衰减或幂律衰减三种形态，与现象学定律中假设的指数衰减明显不同。理论提供了微观接触统计量与宏观摩擦弛豫之间可供实验验证的连接。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从微观接触统计（大小分布与老化特性）到宏观摩擦弛豫行为的解析理论框架，为通过微观接触测量预测和验证摩擦规律提供了新的理论工具，对理解地震断层的稳定性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Frictional relaxation after a sudden velocity change underlies the phenomenological laws that describe the stability of steady sliding and of seismic faults. To connect this relaxation with measurable statistics of microscopic contacts, we introduce a minimal model incorporating logarithmic aging of contact forces and a power-law contact-size distribution reflecting fractal interface roughness, and derive the relaxation function analytically. Depending on the power-law exponent $α$, the intermediate-time response exhibits a plateau, logarithmic decay, or power-law decay, in contrast to the exponential decay assumed in the phenomenological laws. The theory provides experimentally testable links between microscopic contact statistics and macroscopic frictional relaxation.

</details>


### [200] [Irreversibility of many-body harmonic oscillators characterized by generalized Husimi's adiabaticity parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07039)
*Kyosuke Watanabe,Yuki Izumida*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将Husimi关于单个谐振子在循环操作下平均能量不可减少的经典框架推广到任意连通网络上的多体一维谐振子系统。通过简正模分解，定义了广义Husimi绝热参数（各模式参数的算术平均），推导了平均能量的精确时间演化，并证明其在循环操作下不会减少，数值模拟验证了均匀链和异质网络中的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Husimi在1953年的经典论文中证明了单个谐振子（随时间变化的角频率、初始条件采自平衡分布）在循环操作后平均能量不可减少。本文的动机是将这一不可逆性框架从单粒子系统推广到多体耦合系统，探索广义网络结构中热力学不可逆性的普适规律。

Method: 1) 将多体谐振子系统通过变换到质权坐标并对正定矩阵对角化，解耦为独立简正模；2) 每个简正模具有各自的Husimi绝热参数；3) 定义广义Husimi绝热参数为所有模式的算术平均值；4) 推导平均能量的精确时间演化；5) 通过数值模拟验证均匀最近邻链和异质网络的情形。

Result: 推导了广义Husimi绝热参数框架下平均能量的精确时间演化，证明了在循环操作下平均能量不可减少（非减性）。数值模拟在均匀最近邻链和异质网络上均验证了理论结果的正确性。

Conclusion: Husimi的不可逆性框架可以成功推广到任意连通网络上的多体耦合谐振子系统，网络拓扑通过简正模结构影响绝热参数与能量演化的细节，但循环操作下平均能量的非减性这一核心结论得以保持。

Abstract: In a classical paper [K. Husimi, Prog. Theor. Phys. 9, 381 (1953)], Husimi showed that, for a single harmonic oscillator with a time-dependent angular frequency and initial conditions sampled from an equilibrium distribution, the averaged energy cannot decrease after a cyclic operation. This irreversibility is quantified by Husimi's adiabaticity parameter constituted with adiabatic invariants. In this work, we generalize Husimi's framework to a many-body system of one-dimensional harmonic oscillators coupled on an arbitrary connected network, with all spring constants sharing a common time dependence. For a system attached to a fixed wall, the dynamics can be decomposed into independent normal modes by transforming to mass-weighted coordinates and diagonalizing the resulting positive definite matrix, with each mode characterized by its own Husimi's adiabaticity parameter. By defining the generalized Husimi's adiabaticity parameter as their arithmetic mean, we derive the exact time evolution of the averaged energy and establish its non-decrease under cyclic operations. Numerical simulations confirm these results for both a uniform nearest-neighbor chain and a heterogeneous network.

</details>


### [201] [Probing magnetic correlations in space and time within predefined topological sectors of a macroscopic spin liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07140)
*Rémy Dangoisse,Jeanne Colbois,Laurent Del Rey,Nicolas Rougemaille,Johann Coraux*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过毫米级NdFeB圆柱组成的宏观人工晶格（由机械振动器驱动），实验实现了三角Ising反铁磁模型，首次在实验中触及该模型的极低能物理和真实基态，并能在空间和时间上探测不同拓扑扇区内预选基态口袋的磁性行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角Ising反铁磁体作为二维自旋液体的原型，其残余熵密度和临界关联等性质长期停留在理论研究层面，作者质疑这些性质能否在真实材料中实现，因此希望通过宏观人工体系进行实验验证。

Method: 构造由毫米级NdFeB圆柱组成的人工宏观晶格，通过机械振动器驱动圆柱运动，模拟三角Ising反铁磁体的自旋动力学；实验中手动预选基态流形中的特定区域，近似模拟不同拓扑扇区内的行为，并对其磁性进行空间和时间尺度上的原位探测。

Result: 实验成功触及了该模型的极低能物理和真实基态，能够在人工晶格中达到模型的热力学极限行为；同时实现了对预选基态口袋（近似代表不同拓扑扇区）内磁性行为的时空探测。

Conclusion: 该人工宏观平台为受阻磁性和统计物理模型中多体现象的裸眼可视化与手动操控开辟了新途径，弥合了理论研究与可观测实验之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: The triangular Ising antiferromagnet, with its residual entropy density and critical correlations at absolute zero, is the archetype of a two-dimensional spin liquid. Its ground state is partitioned into topological sectors connected by global spin flip events that wrap the lattice boundaries, and become statistically irrelevant in the thermodynamic limit where magnetic fluctuations are restricted to the dominant sector. Thus far, these properties have been mainly investigated from a theoretical perspective, and one might wonder to what extent they can be transposed to real materials. Here, we present experimental observations obtained in an artificial, macroscopic realisation of the seminal triangular Ising antiferromagnet that consists of a lattice of millimeter-sized NdFeB cylinders put into motion by a mechanical shaker. Specifically, we demonstrate that the very-low-energy physics and the true ground state of this model can be reached experimentally. Besides, we are able to probe, in space and time, the magnetic properties within manually preselected pockets of the ground-state manifold that emulate, to a good approximation, the behaviour in distinct topological sectors. Our approach opens new avenues for naked-eye visualisation and hand manipulation of many-body phenomena associated to frustrated magnetism and models of statistical physics.

</details>


### [202] [Reverse engineering of mechano-kinetic parameters from stochastic force profiles in heterogeneous ensembles of molecular motors and tracks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07170)
*Valentina Buonfiglio,Irene Pertici,Pasquale Bianco,Duccio Fanelli,Stefano Gherardini*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个随机框架来描述由不同机械动力学特性的肌球蛋白马达组成的异质集合，能够推导等长力的概率分布、表征有限尺寸力波动，并通过估计程序同时推断机械动力学参数和马达集合大小。该框架在合成数据和实验数据上均验证有效，可用于定量评估集合异质性和推断未知马达物种的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子马达与其轨道间相互作用中存在的系统异质性在众多细胞生理和病理过程中发挥着关键作用，但假设所有马达具有相同机械动力学特性的理论模型无法捕捉这些异质性效应。

Method: 开发了一个随机框架来描述包含两个不同机械动力学特性群体的肌球蛋白马达异质集合。假设马达作为独立力产生器与肌动蛋白丝相互作用，推导了等长力的概率分布，并表征了有限尺寸力波动的统计特性。框架还包括一个估计程序，能够同时推断机械动力学参数和马达集合大小，无需预先指定。通过合成数据和实验数据进行验证。

Result: 针对合成数据和实验数据的验证表明，该模型能准确捕捉力波动，并提供对集合大小的现实估计。此外，该框架能对集合异质性进行定量评估，并能推断未知马达物种的特性。

Conclusion: 该方法为表征异质肌动蛋白-肌球蛋白系统提供了定量工具，适用于心脏和骨骼肌中不同蛋白质亚型共存，或天然蛋白质被突变衍生或工程化变体部分替代的情况。

Abstract: Heterogeneity in contractile systems of molecular motors interacting with their tracks plays a key role in numerous cellular physiological and pathological processes. However, its effects cannot be captured by theoretical models assuming identical mechano-kinetic properties for all motors. We developed a stochastic framework to describe heterogeneous ensembles of myosin motors comprising two populations with distinct mechano-kinetic properties.
