Assuming that motors interact with the actin filament (their track) as independent force generators, we derived the probability distribution of the isometric force and characterised the statistics of finite-size force fluctuations. The proposed framework includes an estimation procedure that simultaneously infers the mechano-kinetic parameters and the size of the motor ensemble, eliminating the need to prescribe it a priori. Validation against synthetic and experimental data shows the model accurately captures force fluctuations and provides realistic estimates of the ensemble size. Furthermore, the framework enables a quantitative assessment of ensemble heterogeneity and the inference of an unknown motor species properties. This approach provides a quantitative tool for characterising heterogeneous actin-myosin systems, such as those arising from the co-presence of different protein isoforms in cardiac and skeletal muscle, or from the partial replacement of native proteins with mutation-derived or engineered variants.

</details>


### [203] [Emergent Equilibrium Structure Along a Critical Cluster Recursion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07332)
*Shuo Wei,Abbas Ali Saberi,Youjin Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种在每一代都保持临界的双色团簇递归（bicolored cluster recursion），发现即使在无平衡自旋测度或固定耦合的条件下，其历史依赖序列在二维和三维中都会自发发展出Ising/Fortuin-Kasteleyn(FK)兼容的概率结构，表明平衡兼容的条件结构可以在全程临界的轨迹中涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界普适性（critical universality）并不决定概率测度的微观条件结构。作者旨在探究：当系统被约束为在每一代都保持临界时，条件概率的微观结构是否会自发走向某种平衡兼容的形式，而不需要预先施加平衡测度或固定耦合。

Method: 作者引入一个受约束在每代保持临界的双色团簇递归框架。在二维和三维中进行研究，追踪该历史依赖序列的条件法则演化，分析其单点条件律的第二壳层依赖性、近邻有效耦合的收敛行为，以及团簇与界面可观测量的几何组织。同时通过精确的团簇着色分解因子化来检验残余连通权重在两色自旋边际中的行为。

Result: 在二维和三维中，该系统自发发展出共同的Ising/FK兼容结构：单点条件律的第二壳层依赖被强烈抑制，近邻有效耦合逐渐趋近彼此并靠近临界Ising值，团簇与界面可观测物理量围绕相应的FK几何组织。精确的团簇着色因子化表明q=2是残余连通权重从两色自旋边际消失的特定点。

Conclusion: 即使在缺乏平衡测度或固定耦合先验的情况下，仅通过保持每代临界性的约束，系统也能涌现出与平衡Ising/FK兼容的条件结构。这表明平衡兼容的微观结构可以作为临界轨迹的自然产物出现，而q=2在这一过程中具有特殊地位。

Abstract: Critical universality does not determine the microscopic conditional structure of a probability measure. We study a bicolored cluster recursion constrained to remain critical at every generation, with no equilibrium spin measure or fixed coupling imposed. In both two and three dimensions, the resulting history-dependent sequence develops a common Ising/Fortuin--Kasteleyn (FK) compatibility structure: the second-shell dependence of a one-site conditional law is strongly suppressed, nearest-neighbor effective couplings move progressively toward one another near the critical Ising value, and cluster and interface observables organize around the corresponding FK geometry. An exact cluster-coloring factorization singles out $q=2$ as the point where the residual connectivity weight disappears from the two-color spin marginal. Thus equilibrium-compatible conditional structure can emerge along a trajectory that remains critical throughout.

</details>


### [204] [Fragility from Enthalpy Fluctuations and Fictive-Temperature Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07396)
*Biman Bagchi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过构型焓涨落将玻璃形成的两个核心概念——脆度（fragility）和假想温度（fictive temperature）直接联系起来，建立了统一的理论框架。假想温度在冷却或老化过程中的时间演化受焓-焓关联函数控制，该函数也决定了频率依赖的构型热容；脆度通过结构弛豫时间对温度的强烈依赖性决定焓弛豫谱在玻璃转变附近向长时域移动的速率，从而实现对假想温度滞后的时间域直接描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃形成的两个核心度量——脆度和假想温度——之间的关系通常只能通过结构弛豫时间或冷却速率依赖间接表达，缺乏直接、统一的理论框架来描述两者的内在联系。

Method: 作者以构型焓涨落为桥梁，建立了假想温度时间演化与焓-焓关联函数之间的联系。利用脆度控制结构弛豫时间的温度依赖性，进而决定焓弛豫谱在玻璃转变附近向长时间尺度移动的速率，由此推导出假想温度迟滞（lag）的起始与增长的直接时域描述，涵盖德拜极限情形。

Result: 该框架成功将量热弛豫、脆度与非平衡结构阻抑（structural arrest）统一起来，能够在时间域上直接描述冷却或老化过程中假想温度滞后的产生与演变规律。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个以构型焓涨落为核心变量的统一理论框架，使得脆度、假想温度、量热弛豫与结构阻抑等玻璃形成的关键概念可以在共同基础上获得一致的解析描述。

Abstract: Fragility and fictive temperature are two central measures of glass formation, but their connection is usually expressed indirectly through structural relaxation times or cooling-rate dependence. Here we formulate this connection in terms of configurational enthalpy fluctuations. The time evolution of the fictive temperature during cooling or aging is controlled by the enthalpy--enthalpy correlation function that also determines the frequency-dependent configurational heat capacity. Fragility enters through the strong temperature dependence of the structural relaxation time and therefore controls how rapidly the corresponding enthalpy-relaxation spectrum shifts to long times near the glass transition. This leads to a direct time-domain description of the onset and growth of the fictive-temperature lag, including the Debye limit, and connects calorimetric relaxation, fragility, and nonequilibrium structural arrest within a common framework.

</details>


### [205] [Exact Hamiltonian Dynamics of Rare Events in Active Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07496)
*Andrea Crisanti,Matteo Paoluzzi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了活性Ornstein-Uhlenbeck粒子（由指数相关噪声驱动的随机动力学）在复杂环境中的非平衡跃迁路径问题。作者建立了从非马尔可夫最优路径到高维哈密顿动力系统的精确映射，使得任意力场下的最优路径可通过求解哈密顿方程系统计算。通过调节守恒能量，可探索从严格限制在零能量面上的经典瞬子轨迹到仅出现在非零能量下的闭合周期轨道等多种动力学机制，后者代表了非马尔可夫活性动力学的独特特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准平衡态跃迁速率理论不适用于活性系统，因为活性系统通过非平衡涨落导航复杂环境。此外，跃迁速率仅提供非平衡动力学探索亚稳态的部分信息，而最优路径的知识能提供更深入的物理洞察。因此需要一种能够系统计算活性系统中非马尔可夫最优路径的方法。

Method: 以活性Ornstein-Uhlenbeck粒子为范式模型，利用指数相关噪声驱动的随机动力学。作者建立了非马尔可夫最优路径与高维哈密顿动力系统之间的精确映射，从而将任意力场下的最优路径计算问题转化为求解对应的哈密顿方程。

Result: 通过调节哈密顿系统中的守恒能量，揭示了多种动力学机制：零能量面上经典瞬子轨迹（类似于平衡态情况），以及仅在非零能量下出现的闭合周期轨道，后者是活性非马尔可夫动力学的独特标志。

Conclusion: 该哈密顿映射方法为系统计算活性系统中任意力场下的最优路径提供了普适框架，并揭示了非马尔可夫活性动力学中超越经典瞬子图像的丰富动力学现象。

Abstract: Active systems navigate complex environments through non-equilibrium fluctuations, rendering standard equilibrium transition-rate theories inadequate. Moreover, transition rates provide only partial information on how non-equilibrium dynamics explore metastable states, whereas knowledge of the optimal paths offers deeper physical insight. Using an active Ornstein-Uhlenbeck particle as a paradigmatic model, i.e., a stochastic dynamics driven by exponentially correlated noise, we establish an exact mapping of the non-Markovian optimal path onto a higher-dimensional Hamiltonian dynamical system so that, for arbitrary force fields, optimal paths can be computed systematically by solving the corresponding Hamilton equations. Tuning the conserved energy allows us to explore diverse dynamical regimes: ranging from classical instanton trajectories strictly confined to the zero-energy surface, to closed periodic orbits that emerge only at non-zero energies, representing a distinct signature of the non-Markovian active dynamics.

</details>


### [206] [Microcanonical Hamiltonian Monte Carlo and the Helmholtz Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07620)
*Heinrich von Campe,Bjoern Malte Schaefer*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从热力学角度分析微正则哈密顿蒙特卡洛（MCHMC）算法，推导其热力学状态变量和势能，证明该算法形式上代表微正则热力学系综，满足亥姆霍兹定理（热力学第一定律的另一种表述），并扩展出一个适用于低维推断问题的新采样算法。最后论证从热力学和信息论角度看，正则MCMC算法比MCHMC更自然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微正则哈密顿蒙特卡洛算法虽然已被提出，但尚未有人从热力学角度对其进行详细研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，深化对该算法理论基础的理解。

Method: 1) 从热力学角度推导MCHMC算法的热力学状态变量和势能；2) 通过解析分析和数值实验证明算法满足亥姆霍兹定理；3) 构造一个新的采样算法，将原算法扩展到低维推断问题；4) 从热力学和信息论视角对MCHMC与正则MCMC算法进行比较论证。

Result: 证明了MCHMC算法在形式上确实构成一个微正则热力学系综，且满足亥姆霍兹定理（通过解析和数值两种方式验证）。同时提出了一种扩展算法以处理更低维度的推断问题，并得出了正则MCMC算法在热力学和信息论意义上比MCHMC更自然的结论。

Conclusion: MCHMC算法的热力学基础已得到系统阐述；尽管该算法在形式上构成微正则系综，但从热力学与信息论角度而言，正则MCMC算法在推断任务中更具自然性和合理性。

Abstract: The recently proposed Microcanonical Hamiltonian Monte Carlo algorithm has not yet been studied in detail from a thermodynamic point of view; this work aims to fill that gap. We demonstrate how thermodynamical state variables and potentials can be derived and thereby demonstrate that the construction of the algorithm formally represents a microcanonical thermodynamic ensemble. In particular, we demonstrate (analytically and numerically) that the algorithm fulfils the Helmholtz theorem, an alternative formulation of the first law of thermodynamics. Furthermore, we construct a new sampling algorithm that extends the original to lower-dimensional inference problems. Finally, we argue that canonical Markov Chain Monte Carlo algorithms are more natural than Microcanonical Hamiltonian Monte Carlo from the thermodynamic and information-theoretic point of view.

</details>


### [207] [Topographic Disorder, Wind Coupling, and Directional Fire Spread: Critical Behavior in a Terrain-Weighted Forest Fire Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07763)
*Juan M. Florez,Eric Suárez Morell,Cristian Millan,J. Restrepo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出地形加权森林火灾模型（TFFM），在空间相关高斯地形场上研究火势传播。研究发现火势存在尖锐的活跃-非活跃相变，临界阈值随地形坡度不对称性和相关性变化；粗粒地形和低树密度下火甚至无法渗流。前沿速度指数δ≈0.34既不符合有向渗流也不符合各向同性渗流；临界存活概率独立于系统尺寸且衰减极慢，暗示临界点存活概率有限。风偏置可使临界阈值提高2-4倍，并产生地形-风竞争效应。模型预测与卫星火烧疤数据吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有森林火灾模型多假设各向同性的键无序，忽略真实地形（空间相关的高斯高度场）对火势传播的定向影响。作者旨在建立包含地形坡度不对称性和风偏置的火灾模型，研究地形对渗流相变临界行为以及火烧疤形态的影响，并与卫星观测数据对比。

Method: 在空间相关高斯高度场上定义晶格模型TFFM，火势传播采用非对称键概率p=clip[e^{-β+γ(h_j-h_i)},0,1]并叠加风偏置。通过大规模模拟（L最大至8192），结合有限尺寸标度分析，研究临界抑制阈值β_c的标度行为、前沿速度指数δ、单种子存活概率及不同地形条件下的渗流特性，并与理论渗流普适类比较。

Result: 发现β_c是γ的偶函数且随|γ|和地形相关长度σ_h的减小而降低；陡坡不对称性抑制传播（下坡键被惩罚、火在高程局部极大处停滞）。前沿速度指数δ=0.34±0.02，粗糙与平滑地形成相同，排除有向渗流和各向同性渗流。临界存活概率独立于L且极缓慢衰减（运行指数从0.09降至0.04），P^*≈0.5时存活概率降为零。风使β_c提升2-4倍，产生风-地形竞争效应。模型预测与卫星火烧疤数据一致。

Conclusion: 该模型揭示地形坡度不对称性本质上是火势传播的抑制因素，其相变临界行为与标准渗流普适类不同，可能受淬火相关无序导致的慢交叉修正影响。模型能复现真实野火的火烧疤特征和风险阈值，对野火风险评估和预测建模有实际价值。

Abstract: We introduce the Terrain-Weighted Forest Fire Model (TFFM), a lattice model in which fire spreads on a spatially correlated Gaussian height field with the asymmetric bond probability $p_{i\to j}=\mathrm{clip}[e^{-β+γ(h_j-h_i)},0,1]$, plus an additive wind bias. Simulations up to $L=8192$ reveal a sharp active-to-inactive transition whose critical suppression threshold $β_c$ is even in $γ$, decreases with $|γ|$, and decreases as the terrain correlation length $σ_h$ is reduced: slope asymmetry acts as a suppressant because downhill bonds are penalized and fire stalls at local elevation maxima. For rough terrain and low tree density, the fire fails to percolate even at zero suppression. Finite-size scaling gives a front-velocity exponent $δ=0.34\pm0.02$, identical for smooth and rough terrain and matching neither directed percolation ($ν_\parallel-ν_\perp\approx0.56$) nor isotropic percolation ($\approx0.18$). The single-seed survival probability at $β_c$ is independent of $L$ and decays extremely slowly, with a running exponent falling from $\approx0.09$ to $\approx0.04$, excluding directed percolation and suggesting a survival probability that remains finite at criticality, consistent with the $L$-independent value $P^*\approx0.5$ at which $P_{\rm surv}$ drops to zero. The correlation-length exponent cannot be resolved at these sizes, consistent with slow crossover corrections from quenched correlated disorder. Wind raises $β_c$ by a factor of 2--4, produces a sharp onset of downwind fire-scar drift at weak coupling, and, at high terrain coupling, decreases the burned fraction at boundary crossing---a terrain-wind competition effect absent from isotropic bond-disorder models. The model yields fire-risk thresholds and fire-scar signatures comparable to satellite burn-scar data.

</details>


### [208] [Directional memory of early spectral selection during spinodal decomposition in finite systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07764)
*Boliang Yu,Ruixin Zhou,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究Cahn-Hilliard相分离过程中，傅里叶空间内的早期各向异性轴是否与该演化后期粗化域取向轴相关。通过二维模拟（各向同性初始涨落、无外加定向），引入归一化角向均值Q₂度量谱各向异性强度与方向。研究发现早期轴与晚期轴相关，且该方向信息在后期预测中具有预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cahn-Hilliard相分离在傅里叶空间中形成的环形图案具有角向强度偏好（即存在一个优势轴）。核心科学问题是：同一演化过程中早期被选择的方向轴是否与后期粗化域的方向轴存在关联？现有研究对早期线性增长阶段与后期非线性粗化阶段之间的方向记忆性（directional memory）缺乏系统理解。

Method: 1) 对二维各向同性初始涨落、无外加方向场的Cahn-Hilliard系统进行数值模拟；2) 定义归一化角向频谱均值Q₂作为各向异性强度与方向的度量；3) 通过线性增长阶段不同波数的不等放大率，考察早期轴如何从初始频谱的角向不平衡中被选出；4) 比较不同淬火深度（浅淬火 vs 深淬火）下的早期-晚期轴对齐程度；5) 构建基于淬火深度、平均成分、盒尺寸的晚期Q₂预测模型，并加入早期Q₂作为额外特征评估预测精度的提升。

Result: 1) 早期线性增长阶段选出的轴与后期粗化阶段的轴保持相关；2) 在固定时间点比较时，浅淬火展现更强的早期-晚期方向对齐，但当按域生长阶段（而非绝对时间）进行比较时，该优势消失；3) 在基于淬火深度、平均成分和盒尺寸的预测模型中加入早期Q₂，使晚期Q₂的平均绝对误差降低3.53%；4) 仅保留早期Q₂的幅值（丢弃方向信息）或用其他演化的方向替代时，该误差降低效应消失，证明方向信息本身具有预测价值。

Conclusion: 早期傅里叶频谱各向异性（包括方向信息）携带关于同一演化场后期状态的有效信息。早期被线性增长放大的方向轴在粗化阶段仍被记住，形成了跨阶段的方向记忆，这对理解相分离动力学中的谱信息传播及预测后期结构演化具有重要意义。

Abstract: Cahn-Hilliard phase separation turns small composition fluctuations into coarsening domains. In Fourier space, the pattern forms a ring whose angular intensity favors an axis. We ask whether the axis favored early in an evolution remains related to the axis favored later in the same evolution. We analyze two-dimensional simulations with isotropic initial fluctuations and no imposed direction. We introduce $Q_2$, a normalized angular average over the Fourier spectrum, as a measure of the strength and direction of spectral anisotropy. Linear growth amplifies different wave numbers at different rates, selecting an early axis from the angular imbalances in the initial spectrum. This axis remains correlated with the late axis during coarsening. At a fixed time, shallow quenches show stronger early-late alignment. This advantage disappears when the quenches are compared at similar stages of domain growth. Adding the early $Q_2$ to a prediction based on quench depth, mean composition, and box size reduces the mean absolute error for late $Q_2$ by $3.53\%$. This reduction disappears when only the magnitude of the early $Q_2$ is retained, or when its direction is replaced by the direction from another evolution. The early Fourier intensity therefore carries information about the later state of the same evolving field.

</details>


### [209] [Tensor network investigation of the monomer-dimer model on the honeycomb lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07899)
*De-Zhang Li,Jie Liu,Xin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用张量网络方法（VUMPS）研究了蜂窝晶格上等活性（1:1）的单体-二体模型，通过将其精确映射为Kagomé晶格上临界场中的反铁磁Ising模型基态，实现了高精度求解，并讨论了与边着色问题及十六顶点模型的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单体-二体模型是格子统计力学中最著名的未解模型之一，长期以来缺乏精确或高精度的求解方法，因此需要发展新的数值甚至可能的解析工具来研究这一经典难题。

Method: 将单体-二体配置精确映射为Kagomé晶格上临界场H_ex=4J下反铁磁Ising模型的基态；基于每个Ising三角形局部基态构建张量网络，采用VUMPS（变分均匀矩阵乘积态）方法进行张量网络收缩以获得高精度结果。

Result: VUMPS方法对张量网络收缩提供了单体-二体问题的高精度数值结果；重新审视了蜂窝晶格上的边着色问题并阐明了其与单体-二体模型的联系；将单体-二体问题改写为十六顶点模型语言，论证了一般单体-二体模型的不可积性与纯二体模型的可积性。

Conclusion: 张量网络方法（特别是VUMPS）为单体-二体模型提供了有效的数值求解框架；通过等价的Ising映射和十六顶点模型表述，深化了对该模型可积性结构的理解。

Abstract: The monomer-dimer model is one of the most well-known unsolved lattice models. In this paper we study the monomer-dimer model on the honeycomb lattice using the tensor network method, in the case that the dimer and monomer activities are 1. The monomer-dimer configurations are exactly mapped into the ground states of the antiferromagnetic Ising model on the Kagomé lattice in the critical field $H_{\rm{ex}}=4J$, and the tensor network is constructed based on the local ground states of each Ising triangle. The VUMPS approach is employed to contract the tensor network, providing a high-precision result of the monomer-dimer problem. We also revisit the edge coloring problem on the honeycomb lattice and discuss its relationship to the monomer-dimer model. Finally we formulate the monomer-dimer problem in the language of the sixteen-vertex model, and discuss the non-integrability of the general monomer-dimer model and the integrability of the pure dimer model.

</details>


### [210] [Leveraged Learning: entropy destroyed per bit received](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08054)
*Daniel Chernowitz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能体在布尔映射上接收问答时的"杠杆率"（每次收到的比特信息所破坏的熵），证明了在有限个问题、智能先验下杠杆率可以超过1。通过平均后杠杆率完全由答案的平均熵序列G_ℓ决定，并在热力学极限下收敛。本文构造了一种基于F₂多项式度数的简单性先验，利用Reed-Muller容量证明任何非增的熵剖面γ(t)都是可实现的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究中智能体（如大语言模型）从接收答案中获得信息并消除不确定性的基本问题。均匀先验下杠杆率为1，但重要的是理解智能先验如何提升学习效率（杠杆率大于1），以及先验结构如何决定不确定性消除的时间-信息曲线。

Method: (1) 定义杠杆率=被破坏的表熵/收到的惊奇比特数；(2) 通过真值先验和问题顺序的平均化，导出杠杆率精确由答案平均熵序列G_ℓ的增量生成；(3) 取输入比特数趋于无穷、固定已问比例t=ℓ/Q的热力学极限，得到杠杆率的闭合表达L(t)；(4) 利用de Finetti定理分析可交换先验（剖面平坦）与复杂度先验的区别；(5) 构造以F₂上布尔多项式度数分层的简单性先验，结合Reed-Muller容量证明γ(t)=1-F(t)精确成立。

Result: 均匀先验下杠杆率为单位值；智能先验下杠杆率可以严格大于1。可交换先验（de Finetti）给出平坦的γ(t)和双曲型L(t)，认知压缩被限制在t=0的边界层中。构造的简单性先验（按多项式度数的CDF F分级）突破了这一限制，Reed-Muller容量给出γ(t)=1-F(t)，从而任何非增剖面和相应的杠杆曲线在大尺度时间上均可实现。

Conclusion: 为智能体先验如何决定信息获取效率提供了精确的理论框架。核心结果是：均匀先验给出单位杠杆，可交换先验效率局限在初始阶段，而利用结构（多项式度数）的简单性先验能够在宏观时间尺度上维持超单位杠杆率，即持续高效地"每个比特摧毁多个比特的不确定性"。

Abstract: A learner holds a prior belief over boolean maps that answer a finite set of $Q$ questions, and receives answers one by one. Each answer costs surprisal and destroys uncertainty, not only about the question asked but every question still unasked. We call the ratio the leverage: table entropy destroyed per bit of surprisal received. It is unit for a uniform prior, but with an intelligent prior can be higher (not lower).
  Averaged over the truth prior and over the question order, the leverage is found exactly, and is generated by one sequence: the mean entropy $G_\ell$ of the answers to $\ell$ questions. Sending the number of input bits to infinity at fixed asked fraction $t = \ell/Q$, the increments of that sequence become a profile $γ(t)$, and initial question entropy $η_0$. The leverage closes to a thermodynamic limit. $L(t) = [η_0 - (1-t)γ(t)]/\int_0^t γ$.
  Exchangeable priors, by de Finetti, all give a flat $γ(t)$ and hence a hyperbolic $L(t)$, their deduction confined to a boundary layer at $t = 0$. We construct a simplicity prior that escapes this, grading Boolean maps by the degree of their polynomial over $\mathbb{F}_2$ and budgeting weight across degree shells by a CDF $F$. Reed-Muller capacity then gives $γ(t) = 1 - F(t)$ exactly, so any nonincreasing profile, and any leverage curve it generates, is realizable at macroscopic times.

</details>


### [211] [Speed Limit for Information Acquisition in Stochastic Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08219)
*Shuta Kobayashi,Andreas Dechant*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将随机梯度下降(SGD)建模为马尔可夫随机过程，推导出Fisher信息流速度极限，该极限约束了可训练参数从数据生成过程的潜在变量中获取信息的速度。该不等式将信息流分解为漂移项和噪声项，从信息论角度量化了确定性学习力与SGD诱导涨落的各自作用，并在可解析处理的基函数线性回归中验证了该界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络通过学习获得内部表征，但关于训练过程中参数以何种速率、按何种顺序从数据生成机制中获取信息（即潜在变量编码的时间尺度和顺序），目前缺乏一个可量化的框架来进行诊断和解释。

Method: 将SGD形式化为马尔可夫随机过程，利用Fisher信息推导信息流速度极限（不等式），将信息获取速率分解为漂移（确定性学习力）和噪声（SGD随机涨落）两部分贡献；并在解析上可处理的基函数线性回归模型中验证该界的正确性。

Result: 在可解析处理的基函数线性回归中，该信息预算界成功地复现了不同潜在变量被编码进学习参数中的顺序及特征时间尺度，验证了理论预测的有效性。漂移与噪声的分解揭示了二者在信息获取过程中各自的信息论角色。

Conclusion: Fisher信息速度极限可作为定量框架，用于诊断随机学习过程中数据生成机制的不同方面何时以及如何被获取，为理解神经网络表征形成的动力学过程提供了信息论层面的新工具。

Abstract: Neural networks acquire internal representations through learning. In this work, we formulate stochastic gradient descent (SGD) as a Markovian stochastic process and derive a Fisher-information flow speed limit that bounds the rate at which trainable parameters can acquire information about latent variables in the data-generating process. The resulting inequality decomposes the information flow into drift and noise contributions, thereby quantifying the roles of deterministic learning forces and SGD-induced fluctuations from an information-theoretic perspective. We verify the bound in analytically tractable basis-function linear regression, where the information budget predicted by the bound reproduces the ordering and characteristic time scales with which different latent variables are encoded in the learned parameters. These results establish Fisher-information speed limits as a quantitative framework for diagnosing when and how different aspects of the data-generating mechanism are acquired during stochastic learning.

</details>


### [212] [Nonequilibrium stochastic thermodynamics of boundary functionals: From Zubarev's ensemble to stochastic particle separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08477)
*V. V. Ryazanov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将Zubarev非平衡统计算子方法推广到同时包含可加性与非局域边界轨迹泛函的情形，构建了三参数非平衡系统模型（首达时间、水平以上停留时间、过程绝对极值），并建立了最大信息熵方法与Donsker-Varadhan大偏差形式论之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Zubarev非平衡统计算子方法主要处理可加性轨迹泛函，难以同时处理包含非局域边界条件（如首达时间、极值等）的复杂历史依赖过程，需要建立更一般性的统一热力学框架来刻画这类非平衡系统。

Method: 通过统一的统计热力学途径构建包含首达时间、给定水平以上停留时间和绝对极值三个参数的非平衡系统模型；利用Doob h-变换分析固定极值泛函所诱导的有效非马尔可夫输运行为；并与Donsker-Varadhan大偏差形式论建立对应关系。

Result: 展示了轨迹最大信息熵方法与Donsker-Varadhan大偏差形式论之间的等价性；证明固定历史极值泛函会通过Doob h-变换诱导出非马尔可夫输运，系统对历史记录呈现动态适应性；讨论了该框架在"长时间"域的适用性判据及优化周期势中随机粒子分离的潜力。

Conclusion: 该工作为同时包含可加性和非局域边界泛函的非平衡系统提供了统一的统计算子框架，不仅建立了与经典大偏差理论的桥接，还为随机粒子分离等实际应用提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: This paper develops a generalization of Zubarev's nonequilibrium statistical operator method for the case of simultaneous inclusion of additive and nonlocal boundary functionals of trajectories. Using a unified thermodynamic approach, a three-parameter model of nonequilibrium systems is constructed, including the first-passage time, the dwell time above a given level, and the absolute extremum of the process. A correspondence is demonstrated between the maximum information entropy method for trajectories and the Donsker-Varadan large deviation formalism. Using the Doob`s h-transform, it is demonstrated that fixing extremal functionals of history induces efficient non-Markovian transport in the system with dynamic adaptation to historical records. Criteria for the applicability of the developed apparatus in the "large time" domain and the possibilities of its use for optimizing stochastic particle separation in periodic potentials are discussed.

</details>


### [213] [When does nonreciprocity matter? Scale-dependence and nonequilibrium signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08820)
*Sarah A. M. Loos,Robert L. Jack*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文是一篇Perspective综述，探讨非互易性（nonreciprocity）作为连接物理、化学和生物学中多种非平衡现象的统一定性概念，重点讨论其在不同尺度下的物理起源、表现方式与命运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易性虽已日益被视为联系多种非平衡现象的统一概念，但其本质上是尺度依赖的——可能在粗粒化过程中出现或消失。因此，理解非互易性在不同尺度上何时以及如何显现，是当前的核心挑战。

Method: 本文以Perspective的形式，从概念层面系统讨论有效非互易相互作用的物理起源，以及它们在不同尺度上产生的特征不可逆动力学（如不可逆涨落或宏观电流），分析非互易性如何随尺度变化而涌现或湮灭。

Result: 提出了一个关于非互易性跨尺度表现的分析框架，阐释了非互易性如何通过不可逆涨落或宏观电流等特征在不同尺度上显现，以及粗粒化如何影响其可见性。

Conclusion: 非互易性及其非平衡特征是尺度依赖的，理解其在多尺度上的物理起源与命运，是连接微观相互作用与宏观非平衡现象的关鍵。

Abstract: Nonreciprocity is increasingly recognized as a unifying concept linking diverse nonequilibrium phenomena found across physics, chemistry, and biology. It gives rise to distinctive behavior including run-and-chase dynamics and spatio-temporal patterns, often associated with a breaking of time-reversal symmetry. However, nonreciprocity and its nonequilibrium signatures are fundamentally scale-dependent, and may emerge or disappear under coarse-graining. A central challenge is therefore to understand when and how nonreciprocity manifests itself on different scales, for example via irreversible fluctuations or macroscopic currents. In this Perspective, we discuss the physical origin and fate of effective nonreciprocal interactions and the characteristic irreversible dynamics they give rise to across scales.

</details>


### [214] [Stochastic Processes as Non-Metric Geodesics in Information Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08870)
*T. Koide,A. van de Venn*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文建立了高斯统计流形上一参数族α-联络的测地线与一类恒定噪声强度的连续随机过程之间的——对应关系，并证明期望参数下的测地线可分为三类几何类别，其中边界连接类测地线可构造具常数扩散系数的广义桥过程。该过程在极限下退化为Ornstein-Uhlenbeck弛豫或自由布朗运动，且恢复力直接决定联络参数α，并与"引力几何三位一体"建立类比，最终应用于驱动随机热力学中最优功协议与期望测地线的对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究信息几何中高斯统计流形的α-联络几何结构与连续随机过程动力学之间的深层联系，并揭示非度量性这一几何量在物理过程中的主动作用，为统计流形几何提供可观测的物理约束。

Method: 通过建立高斯统计流形上α-联络测地线与特定类连续随机过程的一一对应关系，将测地线分为三类几何类别；利用边界连接类测地线构造具常数扩散的广义桥过程的显式线性随机实现；通过适当操作极限恢复传统随机动力学；并将框架推广至驱动随机热力学的慢驱动最优协议分析。

Result: 建立了几何测地线与随机过程的一一对应关系；揭示恢复力与α参数的直接联系，使几何成为可观测物理量；指出恢复力可归因于标量曲率或纯粹非度量性，类比"引力几何三位一体"；证明慢驱动极限下的功最小化最优协议恰对应具非度量结构的统计流形上的期望测地线。

Conclusion: 非度量性在信息几何中扮演主动物理角色，高斯类连续随机过程可沿几何曲线延拓，这一框架为区分曲率与非度量性两种几何表示提供了明确的物理可观测途径。

Abstract: We establish a one-to-one correspondence between geodesics associated with the one-parameter family of $α$-connections on the Gaussian statistical manifold and a class of continuous stochastic processes characterized by a time-independent noise intensity. We demonstrate that geodesics in expectation parameters naturally classify into three distinct geometric categories, among which the Boundary-connecting class allows us to construct an explicit linear stochastic realization with constant diffusion, representing a generalized bridge process. This result demonstrates how continuous stochastic processes within this Gaussian class can be extended along geometric curves. Under appropriate operational limits, this generalized bridge process reduces to fundamental stochastic dynamics, either Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (OU) relaxation or free Brownian diffusion. Crucially, the physical restoring force governing the resulting OU relaxation directly determines the underlying connection parameter $α$, providing a concrete physical observable to constrain the manifold geometry. Depending on the chosen affine connection representation, this restoring force can be attributed either to scalar curvature or purely to non-metricity, establishing a direct conceptual analogy with the Geometrical Trinity of Gravity. Furthermore, applying this framework to driven stochastic thermodynamics, we show that the work-minimizing optimal protocol in the slow-driving limit coincides precisely with an expectation geodesic of the statistical manifold with non-metricity equipped with $(g, {}^{(1/2)}Γ, {}^{(-1/2)}Γ)$, highlighting the active physical role of non-metricity in information geometry.

</details>


### [215] [Effects of Interaction Range on Fluid Multicriticality: A Computational Study of an Interconverting Lattice Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09074)
*Thomas J. Longo,Sergey V. Buldyrev,Frédéric Caupin,Mikhail A. Anisimov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过可互转换的格点模型研究了相互作用范围对流体多重临界行为的影响，发现最近邻相互作用仅出现两种多临界行为类型，而长程相互作用则涌现四种不同原型，且模拟结果随相互作用邻居数增大趋近平均场理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用范围决定相行为和临界现象的本质，已有伊辛模型研究表明随着相互作用范围增加，蒙特卡罗模拟趋近平均场预测。然而相互作用范围如何影响流体的多临界性（如液体-气体临界性与三临界性共存）尚未被系统探讨，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用一个同时具有伊辛类液-气临界性和对称流体三临界性（类似超流⁴He-³He混合物）的可互转换格点模型，通过平均场理论和三维蒙特卡罗模拟，系统改变相互作用邻居数Z_n从6到388进行研究。

Result: 最近邻相互作用（Z_n=6）仅呈现两种多临界行为类型；随相互作用范围扩大，出现四种截然不同的原型。模拟结果在邻居数趋于无穷时收敛到平均场理论预测。

Conclusion: 研究在交叉临界现象框架内解释了相互作用范围对多重临界行为类型演化的影响，证实长程相互作用抑制临界涨落从而使系统趋近平均场行为。

Abstract: The range of intermolecular interactions plays a central role in determining the nature of phase behavior and critical phenomena. It is well established through studies of the Ising model that as interaction range increases, Monte Carlo simulations progressively approach meanfield predictions as the effects of critical fluctuations are suppressed. In this work, we investigate how varying interaction range influences fluid multicriticality using an interconverting lattice model that exhibits both Ising-like liquid-gas criticality and symmetric fluid tricriticality (similar to that in the superfluid $^4$He-$^3$He mixture). This minimal model serves as a representative system for exploring the evolution of competing critical points within a generic framework. We analyze the model using both meanfield theory and three-dimensional Monte Carlo simulations while systematically varying the number of interacting neighbors, $Z_n$, from 6 to 388. We find that the system with nearest-neighbor interactions ($Z_n=6$) reveals only two types of multicritical behavior, while for larger interaction ranges, four distinct archetypes emerge. We demonstrate the convergence of the simulation results to those of the meanfield theory as the number of interacting neighbors tends to infinity, and we discuss the results within the framework of crossover critical phenomena.

</details>


### [216] [Large-scale dynamics of integrable quenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09139)
*David X. Horvath,Bruno Bertini*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将弹道宏观涨落理论（BMFT）从准平衡背景扩展到可积系统的量子淬火问题，通过鞍点求解路径积分得到描述全计数统计（FCS）演化的偏微分方程组，并在自由费米系统和可积元胞自动机Rule 54中成功复现已知精确结果，同时补全了Rule 54在整个弹道时间窗内的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BMFT此前仅适用于准平衡设定，无法处理可积系统中由淬火引发的非平衡动力学问题。本文旨在将BMFT推广到可积淬火场景，从而能够计算守恒荷的全计数统计随时间演化的行为。

Method: 采用路径积分框架，识别出描述淬火后FCS的相关路径积分，通过鞍点法求解，推导出描述FCS动力学的偏微分方程组；随后在自由费米系统和Rule 54元胞自动机两种模型中显式求解这些方程。

Result: 在自由费米系统和Rule 54中均恢复了已知的精确结果；对于Rule 54，该构造同时补全了之前未完全描述的整个弹道窗口内的解。

Conclusion: 本文成功将BMFT推广至可积淬火问题，验证了其在非平衡可积系统中的应用有效性，并为弹道窗口内的FCS动力学提供了完整而统一的描述框架。

Abstract: The Ballistic Macroscopic Fluctuation Theory (BMFT) is a path-integral based approach to treat large-scale correlations in interacting integrable systems which, so far, has only been applied to quasi-equilibrium settings. In this paper we extend this approach to integrable quench problems and, to illustrate it, we compute the time evolution of the full-counting-statistics (FCS) of a conserved charge. We identify the relevant path integral, solve it via saddle-point, and obtain a set of partial differential equations describing the dynamics of the FCS. We solve these equations explicitly for free fermionic systems and for the interacting cellular automaton Rule 54. In both cases, we recover the known exact results. For Rule 54, our construction moreover completes the solution across the entire ballistic window.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [First principles calculations of electric-field-driven topological phase transitions in silicene, germanene and stanene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05633)
*Julián Antonio Villarreal Murúa,Pablo Roura-Bas,Javier Daniel Fuhr,Ricardo Faccio*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合DFT、最大局域化Wannier函数和Wannier电荷中心演化的第一性原理框架，通过在每个电场强度下进行完全自洽的从头算模拟，精确刻画硅烯、锗烯和锡烯中的拓扑相变，并给出比以往紧束缚模型更准确的临界电场预测值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维拓扑材料（硅烯、锗烯、锡烯）的临界电场E_z^cr精确确定仍然具有挑战性，紧束缚模型往往低估这些值，需要更精确的第一性原理方法来表征拓扑相变。

Method: 将密度泛函理论(DFT)、最大局域化Wannier函数(MWF)和Wannier电荷中心(WCC)演化相结合；在每个电场强度下运行完全自洽的从头算模拟以获取屏蔽电子结构，同时考虑电子和离子的介电响应；利用收敛结果构建每个电场强度下的Wannier紧束缚哈密顿量，进而进行规范不变的Z2拓扑不变量计算。

Result: 该方法给出了显著更准确的硅烯和锗烯的临界电场数值预测，分别为0.020和0.250 V/Å；与以往方法相比，在预测拓扑相边界方面实现了定量改进。

Conclusion: 该框架为基于二维材料的拓扑场效应晶体管和静电控制量子器件的设计提供了关键指导，在预测拓扑相边界方面具有显著的定量精度优势。

Abstract: The emergence of two-dimensional topological materials, particularly the group-14 monolayers known as silicene, germanene, and stanene has opened promising pathways for next-generation nanoelectronics and spintronics. Their buckled honeycomb structure and strong spin-orbit coupling allow for bandgap engineering via a perpendicular electric field, leading to topological phase transitions (TPTs) from non-trivial to trivial insulating states. However, precise determination of the critical electric field $E_z^{\text{cr}}$ at which these transitions occur remains challenging, with tight-binding models often underestimating these values. Here, we present a first-principles framework that combines density-functional theory (DFT), maximally localized Wannier functions, and evolution of the Wannier charge centers (WCC) to accurately characterize TPTs in silicene, germanene, and stanene through the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ topological invariant. In contrast to earlier work, at each electric-field strength we run fully self-consistent ab initio simulations to obtain the screened electronic structure, accounting for the material's dielectric response from both electrons and ions. From these converged results we construct a Wannier tight-binding Hamiltonian at each electric field strength, which then enables a gauge-invariant calculation of the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ topological invariant. This methodology yields significantly more accurate numerical predictions of $E_z^{\text{cr}}$, $0.020$ and $0.250$ V/Å for silicene and germanene, respectively. Compared to previous approaches, our framework delivers a marked quantitative improvement for predicting topological phase boundaries, essential for guiding the design of topological field-effect transistors and electrostatically controlled quantum devices based on two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [218] [Fast universal parametric spin control in an acoustically modulated quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05634)
*Mateusz Kuniej,Michał Gawełczyk*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种参数化声学自旋控制方案，通过远失谐光学耦合到三子态来调制（dress）和混合自旋态，结合声学调制光学跃迁能量，利用与调制自旋分裂的参量共振实现自旋旋转，克服了传统声学量子点自旋控制的旋转轴角度限制和长门时间问题。所提方案实现~155 ps的Pauli-X门（比此前声光方案快~290倍，比法拉第几何光学自旋旋转快~14倍），在~44 GHz声学驱动下对GaAs和InAs量子点核自旋环境达到≥99.9%的平均门保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体量子点自旋量子比特与机械波的相互作用较弱，限制了单量子点自旋量子比特集成到片上声耦合混合系统。现有声学量子点自旋控制理论存在旋转轴角度范围受限的问题，迫使采用复杂的门基元实现和长门时间，几乎没有留给三子衰变和准静态核自旋噪声引起的退相干余量。

Method: 提出参数化控制方案：利用远失谐光学耦合到三子态来调制（dress）并混合自旋态，同时采用声学调制光学跃迁能量。不依赖自旋劈裂的直接声共振（此途径存在显著瓶颈），而是通过与调制自旋劈裂的参量共振来诱导自旋旋转，发展出自旋版的"swing-up"电荷态激发。该方案支持高阶谐波过程，实现快速、具有几乎任意旋转轴的通用量子比特控制。

Result: 所提方案实现~155 ps的Pauli-X门，比此前声光公式化方案快约290倍，比光学法拉第几何自旋旋转快约14倍。在~44 GHz声学驱动下，即使对未冷却核自旋环境的GaAs和InAs量子点，当三子寿命≳1.25 ns时，平均门保真度≥99.9%。

Conclusion: 该方案提供的性能指标表明其具有实际可用的控制能力，并可能引入具有高交互速率、多功能性和多声子过程的自旋-声子界面，这对未来声耦合混合架构至关重要。

Abstract: Quantum communication, distributed computing, and hybrid architectures rely on nodes enabling coherent control of qubits and coupling to propagating quantum modes. While semiconductor quantum-dot (QD) spins couple to microwave and optical photons, weak interaction with mechanical waves has limited the integration of single-QD spin qubits into on-chip, acoustically coupled hybrid systems. The existing theory of acoustic QD spin control suffers from a limited range of rotation-axis angles, enforcing complex realizations of gate primitives and long gate times, leaving little margin against decoherence from trion decay and quasi-static nuclear-spin noise. We propose parametric control that overcomes these problems. We use far-detuned optical coupling to a trion state to dress and thus mix spin states, combined with acoustic modulation of the optical transition energy. Instead of relying on direct acoustic resonance with the spin splitting that leads to significant bottlenecks, we induce spin rotations parametrically via resonance with the dressed-spin splitting. We thus develop a spin analog of the ``swing-up'' charge-state excitation. Our scheme provides fast universal qubit control with nearly arbitrary rotation axes. Our ${\sim}$155 ps Pauli-$X$ gate duration is ${\sim}290\times$ faster than in the previous acousto-optical formulation and ${\sim}14\times$ faster than optical Faraday-geometry spin rotation. The parametric scheme naturally enables higher-harmonic processes. Numerical simulations for ${\sim}44$ GHz acoustic driving show average gate fidelity $\ge99.9\%$ even for uncooled nuclear-spin environments of GaAs and InAs QDs for trion lifetime $\gtrsim1.25$ ns. These metrics suggest practically usable control and may introduce a spin-phonon interface with high interaction rates, versatility, and multi-phonon processes, essential for future acoustically coupled hybrid architectures.

</details>


### [219] [Gauge-covariant magnetic Bloch sums for general multiorbital Hofstadter models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05646)
*Hao Shi,Tianyu Qiao,Wangqian Miao,Jin-Tao Jin,Quansheng Wu,Xi Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文为任意二维多轨道Peierls紧束缚哈密顿量的Hofstadter问题提供了一种统一的规范协变磁Bloch求和基构造方法，可同时处理晶格几何、轨道嵌入、跳跃范围和轨道内容，数值示例涵盖基本晶格、拓扑/平带模型及单层MoS₂的第一性原理Wannier哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Hofstadter问题处理方法往往针对特定晶格或特定类型哈密顿量，缺乏一个能统一处理任意布拉维几何、任意胞内轨道位置和任意跳跃表的通用框架，尤其无法自然地衔接第一性原理计算得到的Wannier哈密顿量。

Method: 采用规范协变磁Bloch求和基构造磁平移算符，将Peierls哈密顿量在有理磁通p/q下约化为qN_orb×qN_orb的有限最小块；推导了斜Landau规范下的显式稀疏矩阵，处理了能带计数、谱冗余、Chern数、磁空间约束和磁通周期性；同时从相同跳跃数据导出精确的广义Harper方程作为补充表示。

Result: 数值演示覆盖基本晶格、拓扑与平带模型，以及具有22条能带的单层MoS₂自旋分辨Wannier哈密顿量，表明该方法可直接与第一性原理电子结构计算对接。

Conclusion: 该框架为一般二维多轨道紧束缚模型中的Hofstadter问题提供了统一的数值与解析工具，块维度仅取决于原胞磁通而与分数轨道坐标是否无理无关，且规范变换仅引起酉共轭而不改变所需磁超胞结构。

Abstract: We formulate a unified treatment of the Hofstadter problem for general two-dimensional multiorbital Peierls tight-binding Hamiltonians using a gauge-covariant magnetic Bloch-sum basis. The construction retains the full Bravais geometry, arbitrary intracell orbital positions, and the original hopping table, so lattice geometry, orbital embedding, hopping range, and orbital content can all be handled within the same framework. At rational flux $Φ/Φ_0=p/q$, commuting magnetic translations reduce the Peierls Hamiltonian to minimal $qN_{\rm orb}\times qN_{\rm orb}$ blocks. Their dimension depends only on the flux through the primitive cell, even when the fractional orbital coordinates are irrational. Electromagnetic gauge transformations act by unitary conjugation within the construction and do not alter the required magnetic supercell. We derive an explicit sparse matrix in an oblique Landau gauge and establish the associated band counting, spectral redundancy, Chern-number formulation, magnetic spatial constraints, and flux periodicity. Numerical examples include elementary lattices, topological and flat-band models, and a spinful 22-band Wannier Hamiltonian of monolayer $\mathrm{MoS}_2$, demonstrating a direct interface with first-principles electronic-structure calculations. As a complementary representation, we also derive exact generalized Harper equations from the same hopping data and relate them to the finite magnetic-Bloch blocks.

</details>


### [220] [A magneto-mechanical gyroscope with spintronic readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05668)
*Andrea Meo,Francesca Garesci,Pedro Bossi Nunez,Maddalena Fiorentino,Teresa Natale,Ludovico Dindelli,Victor Lopez-Dominguez,Banibrato Sinha,Riccardo Tomasello,Francesco Dell'Olio,Pedram Khalili Amiri,Mario Carpentieri,Giovanni Finocchio*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用磁隧道结（MTJ）作为读出机制和机械动力学换能器的类MEMS两自由度（2-DOF）陀螺仪，替代传统电容式传感方案，克服了微米级尺寸缩放限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MEMS陀螺仪依赖电容式传感机制，其间距要求限制了器件尺寸缩放至微米尺度。作者希望引入一种新型读出机制替代电容方案，从而突破尺寸限制。

Method: 利用MTJ的整流功能作为读出机制和机械动力学换能器，构建结合微磁理论与2-DOF机械方程的实验知情磁-机械模型，通过实验表征的MTJ数据进行校准与设计。

Result: 输出与角速率呈线性关系；采用零差解调（homodyne demodulation）方法能够在动态场景下有效提取角速率。

Conclusion: 该工作为紧凑型CMOS兼容读出通路开辟了新途径，降低了对紧密电容间隙的依赖，并促进器件多物理场协同设计。

Abstract: Gyroscopes are essential elements in navigation, consumer electronics, robotics, and aerospace applications. Most micro electro-mechanical systems (MEMS) implementations rely on capacitive sensing mechanisms, which limit the dimensional scaling to the micrometer scale. In this work, we introduce a MEMS-like two-degree-of-freedom (2-DOF) gyroscope that exploits the rectification functionality of magnetic tunnel junctions (MTJs) as its readout mechanism and as the transducer of the mechanical dynamics. Experimentally characterized MTJs have been used to calibrate and perform an experiment-informed design of the magneto-mechanical model combining micromagnetic theory with 2-DOF mechanical equations. We demonstrated that the output is linear with angular rate, and that the proposed device is able to extract the angular rate in dynamic cases exploiting a homodyne demodulation approach. The results open a path towards a compact, complementary metal-oxide semiconductor (CMOS)-compatible readout pathway that relaxes reliance on tight capacitive gaps and motivates multi-physics designs of the device.

</details>


### [221] [Spectroscopy With Intelligent Feature Tracking for automated characterization of excited-state structure in semiconductor quantum dot devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05686)
*A. R. Huffman,Daniel Schug,Merritt Losert,Sanghyeok Park,Daniel King,Yuna Chun,Giordano Scappucci,M. A. Eriksson,Justyna P. Zwolak*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出SWIFT框架，结合机器学习辅助特征识别与物理信息几何处理，从脉冲门控光谱数据中提取量子点器件的激发态能级分裂。该框架将二维光谱分析简化为1D峰值检测，并结合集成置信度指标与低信噪比扫描的顺序累积，支持离线和实时自动化追踪。在Si/SiGe量子点器件上，SWIFT将中位分裂误差从0.11mV降至0.05mV，为大规模量子点器件的自主表征与调优铺平道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表征半导体量子点器件中的激发态结构，是将其调优以进行自旋量子比特操作的关键环节，但传统光谱分析方法在处理低信噪比数据和大规模器件时效率与精度不足，亟需自动化的特征追踪与分裂提取框架。

Method: SWIFT框架包含三大部分：(1)机器学习辅助的特征识别以隔离相关光谱特征；(2)利用特征特有的几何结构将二维光谱分析降维为一维峰值检测问题；(3)基于集成的置信度指标与低信噪比扫描的顺序累积，实现光谱随实验证据累积的迭代评估。系统支持离线和实时两种运行模式，可用于追踪QD激发态和lead共振。

Result: 在Si/SiGe量子点器件上，SWIFT在255个手动标注扫描上基准测试，将中位分裂误差从经典基线的0.11mV降至0.05mV，且在低质量测量数据上改善最为显著。该系统同时展示了离线和实时自动化追踪能力。

Conclusion: SWIFT为量子点激发态光谱提供了可靠的自动化分析路径，使其可集成到大规模量子点器件的自主表征、调优和优化流程中，这对未来可扩展量子比特架构至关重要。

Abstract: The characterization of excited-state structure in semiconductor quantum dot (QD) devices is an important component of tuning them for spin-qubit operation. We present Spectroscopy With Intelligent Feature Tracking (SWIFT), a framework that combines machine-learning (ML)-assisted feature identification with physics-informed geometric processing to extract energy-level splittings from pulsed-gate spectroscopy data. SWIFT isolates the relevant spectral features and exploits their characteristic geometry to reduce the two-dimensional spectroscopy analysis to a one-dimensional peak-detection problem. It further combines an ensemble-based confidence metric with sequential accumulation of rapid, low-SNR scans, allowing the inferred spectrum to be reevaluated as experimental evidence accumulates. Using Si/SiGe QD devices, we demonstrate SWIFT both offline and in real time, including automated tracking of QD excited states and lead resonances. Benchmarking on 255 manually labeled scans shows that SWIFT reduces the median splitting error to 0.05 mV from 0.11 mV for a classical baseline, with the largest improvement on lower-quality measurements. These results provide a path toward incorporating excited-state spectroscopy into autonomous QD characterization, tuning, and optimization, which will be essential in large-scale quantum dot devices.

</details>


### [222] [Large Nernst effect in chemically derived multilayer graphene at millitesla magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05898)
*Valentin Semkin,Denis Borisenko,Yana Litun,Oleg Kononenko,Dmitry Mylnikov,Alexey Bocharov,Dmitry Svintsov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在化学衍生多层石墨烯薄膜中观察到低温磁场下的巨大横向（能斯特）热电效应。在B*≈4 mT的极低磁场下，横向热电电压已达到可与纵向热电电压相比的程度，横向热电系数Sxy可达~10 μV/K，在315 mT下更升至250 μV/K，展示了多层石墨烯在大规模温差发电中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨基化合物合成简单、导电性高且易于与其他材料集成，这促使人们探索石墨基温差发电器件。然而，石墨烯和石墨的半金属特性导致电子-空穴补偿，使得塞贝克系数接近零，纵向热电性能极差。因此需要寻找替代策略来从石墨烯中获取有效热电转换。

Method: 使用扫描激光诱导加热宏观尺度的化学衍生多层石墨烯薄膜（具有强厚度和载流子密度波动），在低磁场下测量横向（能斯特）热电电压。通过激光诱导温度估算横向热电系数Sxy，并在不同磁场强度（最高315 mT，由厘米级永磁体提供）下进行测量，同时探索霍尔探针测量构型对热电信号增强的影响。

Result: 在B*≈4 mT的极低磁场和环境条件下，横向（能斯特）热电电压已达到与金属掺杂石墨烯接触处纵向热电电压相当的水平。Sxy在4 mT时约为10 μV/K，并以亚线性方式随磁场升高，在315 mT时达到250 μV/K。当直流磁场线与局部能斯特电流方向一致时，通过在霍尔探针上测量电压可进一步提高热电信号。

Conclusion: 结果表明，具有厚度和载流子密度波动的多层石墨烯薄膜在低磁场下可产生显著的横向热电效应，为大规模、低成本的石墨烯基温差发电提供了前景和新的实现路径。

Abstract: Simple synthesis of graphitic compounds, their high conductivity, and integrability with other materials motivate the effort toward graphite-based thermoelectric generators. At the same time, semimetallic nature of graphite and graphene results in nearly-zero thermopower due to electron-hole compensation. Here, we observe large transverse thermopower in chemically derived multilayer graphene films with strong fluctuations of thickness and carrier density at low magnetic fields $B$. Using the scanning laser-induced heating of macroscopic film, we find that transverse (Nernst) thermoelectric voltage becomes comparable to the longitudinal thermoelectric voltage at the metal-doped graphene contact at $B^*\approx4$ mT and ambient conditions. Estimates of transverse thermopower $S_{xy}$ based on the known laser-induced temperature show that it is as large as $\sim 10$ $μ$V/K at $B^*$, and raises in a sub-linear fashion to 250 $μ$V/K at $B\approx315$ mT, the maximum field we reach with centimeter-sized permanent magnet. Extra increase in thermoelectric signal is achieved upon voltage measurement at Hall probes when the dc field lines are co-directional with local Nernst current. Our results show the promise of large-scale multilayer graphene for thermoelectricity generation.

</details>


### [223] [Static and Ensemble-Dependent Thermodynamics of the Strain-Induced Parity Anomaly in Gapped Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05999)
*Ara Sedrakyan,Klaus Ziegler*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了赝磁场（valley-odd magnetic field）在亚晶格带隙石墨烯（sublattice-gapped graphene）中的热力学与电响应。推导了该效应的平衡热力学，并将其与有限频率输运响应分离。在固定电化学势下，反转赝磁场可移除所有非零赝朗道能级，剩余巨势差由光谱不对称的零级能级决定。静态电荷响应为热展宽的、限制在带隙内的平台，无金属性m/|μ|尾巴。文章还给出了定电压电路分析、定载流子数条件下的低温和热容结果，并提出了一种无迹三轴对称应变几何及实验可调的插值方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯中赝磁场（由静态形变产生）作用于Dirac电子时表现为谷奇（valley-odd）磁场。在亚晶格带隙石墨烯中，这一场使两个谷在宇称奇响应中相加而非抵消。作者旨在推导该效应的平衡热力学，将其与有限频率输运响应区分开来，并探索通过场反转协议隔离平衡态电机械反常的可行性——无需真实磁场。

Method: 作者基于连续场论分析：1) 推导固定电化学势下的巨势差，由光谱不对称的零级赝朗道能级决定；2) 分析静态电荷响应与热力学量（熵、热容）；3) 构建固定栅压电路的边界条件对测量热容影响的完整公式；4) 用massive-Dirac态密度与精确有限场赝朗道能级计算交叉验证；5) 考虑有限几何电容给出连续可调的插值；6) 提出无迹三轴对称应变几何作为实验可行方案。

Result: 赝场反转移除所有非零赝朗道能级，剩余巨势差由光谱不对称的零级能级固定。静态电荷响应为限制在带隙内的热展宽平台，无金属性尾部。近带边处，赝场反转在低温窗口内转移Bln2的熵（每个未分裂零模态）。固定μ时热容每条边有两个旁瓣，并有普适峰值0.439229 D_B k_B。测量到的片热容依赖电边界条件。固定载流子数时，低温边值为-2(ln2)^2 D_B k_B而非节点。有限几何电容给出连续且可实验调节的插值。

Conclusion: 该项场反转协议可在无真实磁场的条件下隔离出一个平衡态电机械反常。文章提供了无迹三轴对称应变几何、无序-相互作用窗口，以及现实的量热和量子电容尺度，为实验验证提供了完整方案。

Abstract: A static deformation of graphene can act on its Dirac electrons as a valley-odd magnetic field. In sublattice-gapped graphene this field makes the two valleys add in the parity-odd response rather than cancel. We derive the equilibrium thermodynamics of this effect and separate it from the finite-frequency transport response. At fixed electrochemical potential, reversing the pseudomagnetic field removes every nonzero pseudo-Landau level in the continuum theory. The remaining grand-potential difference is fixed by the spectrally asymmetric zeroth level. The static charge response is a thermally broadened plateau confined to the gap and has no metallic $m/|μ|$ tail. Near a band edge, pseudofield reversal transfers $\mathrm{B}\ln2$ of entropy per unsplit zero-mode state in the low-temperature window. The fixed-$μ$ heat capacity has two side lobes per edge and a universal peak $0.439229\,D_B k_{\mathrm B}$. We then formulate a definite constant-gate-voltage circuit and show that the measured sheet heat capacity depends on the electrical boundary condition. The full massive-Dirac density of states and an exact finite-field pseudo-Landau-level calculation give the same gate crossover in their common limit. At fixed carrier number, the low-temperature edge value is $-2(\ln2)^2 D_B k_{\mathrm B}$, rather than a node. A finite geometric capacitance gives a continuous and experimentally tunable interpolation. Finally, we give a trace-free triaxial strain geometry, a disorder--interaction window, and realistic calorimetric and quantum-capacitance scales. The field-reversal protocol isolates an equilibrium electromechanical anomaly without a real magnetic field.

</details>


### [224] [One-dimensional quasicrystals with tensor-network finite-state automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06040)
*Milla Kolehmainen,Jose L. Lado,Anouar Moustaj*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将一维准晶的替位规则编码为确定性有限自动机的方法，并通过与矩阵乘积态的等价性构建其紧束缚哈密顿量的精确矩阵乘积算子，从而利用张量网络技术高效计算超过10^9位点的谱密度，清晰分辨谱的分层结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准晶介于晶体（平移有序）和非晶（无序）之间，其模拟一直具有挑战性。由于准周期结构缺乏晶体所利用的平移对称性，通常需要对大尺寸有限近似体进行昂贵的矩阵对角化，这使得难以处理大型系统的谱计算。

Method: 作者利用准晶的替位规则描述，将其写成适应于替位规则的计数系统后，定义了一个确定性的有限自动机（DFAO）：输入位点索引的位数，自动机返回该位点的字母。进一步利用与矩阵乘积态（MPS）的等价性，转移矩阵恰为MPS的张量，键维等于自动机状态数且与系统尺寸无关，从而在张量列车语言中精确表示任意系统尺寸的矩阵乘积算子（MPO）。作者对金属均值和k-波那契族进行了显式构造（斐波那契、银均值和Tribonacci），并结合张量网络压缩与核多项式方法计算谱密度。

Result: 该框架成功处理了两族一维准晶（金属均值和k-波那契族），并显式构建了斐波那契、银均值和Tribonacci准晶。通过张量网络压缩和核多项式方法，计算了超过10^9位点链的谱密度，并直接分辨出谱的分层结构。

Conclusion: 这项工作建立了替位准晶、有限自动机与矩阵乘积态/张量网络之间深刻的数学联系，提供了一种无需近似便可在任意系统尺寸下精确表示准晶哈密顿量的方法，使超大规模准晶谱计算成为可能。

Abstract: Quasicrystals occupy a distinctive position between the translational order of crystals and the disordered amorphous matter. Simulating this physics has remained challenging, since quasiperiodic structures lack the translational symmetry exploited for crystals and generally require costly diagonalization of large finite approximants. Quasicrystalline order admits two equivalent descriptions, a cut-and-project scheme from a higher-dimensional periodic crystal, and a discrete set of substitution rules acting on a finite alphabet. We show that the latter, written in a numeration system adapted to the substitution, defines a deterministic finite automaton with output, the digits of a site index are fed, and the automaton returns the letter occupying that site. We further exploit another equivalence to a different construction, the transition matrices are exactly the tensors of a matrix product state, whose bond dimension is the number of automaton states and is independent of system size. This allows efficient representation of extremely large tight-binding Hamiltonians in the tensor-train language, thereby yielding an exact matrix product operator for the quasicrystal Hamiltonian at any system size. We show how this framework works for two families of one-dimensional quasicrystals, the metallic-mean and $k$-bonacci families and we explicitly construct the Fibonacci, silver-mean, and Tribonacci quasicrystals. By leveraging efficient tensor-network compression and the kernel polynomial method, we compute spectral densities for chains with more than $10^9$ sites and directly resolve the hierarchical structure of the spectrum.

</details>


### [225] [Purely Electric-Field Control of Topological Magnetism in Two-Dimensional Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06215)
*Zhonglin He,Naafis Ahnaf Shahed,Kai Huang,Himanshu Mavani,Evgeny Y. Tsymbal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种纯电场调控拓扑磁性的通用策略：通过邻近非磁性vdW金属，对二维vdW磁性半导体的轨道角动量极化带边进行静电掺杂，从而主要调控磁各向异性，实现铁磁、斯格明子、螺旋和双半子等磁性相的可逆切换，适用于CrBr3/graphene和Cr2Ge2Te6/TaS2等异质结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现节能拓扑自旋电子学需要电场调控拓扑磁性，但现有电场方法往往通过对低能电子态的非选择性重排来粗粒度地调控磁性竞争相互作用，常需外加磁场来稳定拓扑准粒子；少数纯电场方案又仅局限于特定导电材料。因此亟需一种通用、且无需外加磁场的纯电场调控策略。

Method: 作者建立了一种基于邻近效应的通用方法：将外加电场通过相邻的非磁性vdW金属，对二维vdW磁性半导体的特定轨道角动量极化带边进行静电掺杂。该方法选择性地调控磁性半导体的磁各向异性（MA），同时几乎不改写交换相互作用和Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI），从而在铁磁、斯格明子、螺旋和双半子相之间实现可逆切换。作者在CrBr3/graphene和Cr2Ge2Te6/TaS2两种vdW异质结中验证了该机制，其分别承载不同轨道特征的电子和空穴口袋。

Result: 研究表明，静电掺杂选择性调控二维磁性半导体的某个轨道角动量极化带边，主要调制磁各向异性而对交换作用和DMI影响很小，从而可在无需外加磁场的条件下可逆驱动铁磁、斯格明子、螺旋和双半子等磁性相的转变。该机制在CrBr3/graphene和Cr2Ge2Te6/TaS2异质结中得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为拓扑自旋电子学提供了一种广泛适用的纯电场调控策略，突破了此前方法对材料导电性和外加磁场的限制，为低能耗拓扑磁器件开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Electrical control of topological magnetism is central to realizing energy-efficient topological spintronics. Yet most electric-field approaches modify the competing magnetic interactions through a nonselective rearrangement of low-energy electronic states, yielding coarse magnetic phase control that often requires external magnetic fields to stabilize topological quasiparticles, while few schemes solely based on electric fields are restricted to specific conducting materials. Here, we establish a general approach for purely electric-field control of topological magnetism, in which an applied electric field electrostatically dopes a selected orbital-angular-momentum-polarized band edge of a two-dimensional (2D) van der Waals (vdW) magnetic semiconductor via proximity to an adjacent nonmagnetic vdW metal. We show that the resulting electrostatic doping predominantly tunes magnetic anisotropy of the 2D magnet, while leaving exchange interaction and Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction nearly unchanged, thereby reversibly driving the system through ferromagnetic, skyrmion, spiral, and bimeron phases. We demonstrate this mechanism for CrBr3/graphene and Cr2Ge2Te6/TaS2 vdW heterostructures hosting electron and hole pockets of different orbital characters. These results establish a broadly applicable strategy for purely electric-field control in topological spintronics.

</details>


### [226] [Continuum Limit of Nonlocal Electrostatics in Random Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06354)
*Prashant K. Jha,Kaushik Dayal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文推导了晶格平移下平稳遍历随机电荷密度场的双尺度连续极限，确立了局部与非局部静电能几乎必然收敛到确定性极限的条件，并展示了元胞去极化项在总能量中抵消，总能量由系综平均微观元胞能与宏观静电场能组成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机电荷密度场（如具有偶极涨落的离散介质）的静电能分析需要严格的连续极限框架，以连接微观随机结构与宏观静电行为。现有周期性双尺度极限无法涵盖随机平移不变系统，且微观偶极涨落对局部能量的影响尚未被系统刻画。

Method: 采用两尺度分析方法：微观尺度上每个元胞在每个实现中电荷中性但偶极矩可能涨落且有非零均值；在平稳微观势与有限体积场的假设下，证明局部能量与非局部能量几乎必然收敛到确定性极限。局部极限含系综平均微观Coulomb能与元胞去极化项，非局部极限由有效极化决定。此外对独立元胞随机位移模型验证了定理假设。

Result: （1）局部能量极限由系综平均微观Coulomb能与元胞去极化项组成；（2）非局部能量极限由有效极化决定；（3）元胞去极化项在总能量中相互抵消，总能量即系综平均微观元胞能加宏观静电场能；（4）确定性特例恢复周期性双尺度极限；（5）独立元胞随机位移模型中，居中的微观涨落可改变局部能量而不改变有效极化或非局部能量。

Conclusion: 本文为随机电荷密度场建立了严格的静电能双尺度连续极限定理，揭示了微观偶极涨落的统计特性如何影响局部而非局部能量，为随机介质中的静电建模提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We derive a two-scale continuum limit for the electrostatic energy of random charge density fields that are stationary and ergodic under lattice translations. Each microscopic cell is charge neutral in every realization, while its dipole moment may fluctuate and have a nonzero mean. Under assumptions on the stationary microscopic potential and the finite-volume fields, the local and nonlocal energies converge almost surely to deterministic limits. The local limit consists of the ensemble-averaged microscopic Coulomb energy and a cell-depolarization term, whereas the effective polarization determines the nonlocal limit. The cell-depolarization terms cancel in the total energy, which consists of the ensemble-averaged microscopic cell energy and the macroscopic electrostatic field energy. The deterministic specialization recovers the periodic two-scale limit. For an independent-cell random-displacement model, we verify the assumptions of the continuum-limit theorem and show that centered microscopic fluctuations can change the local energy without changing the effective polarization or the nonlocal energy.

</details>


### [227] [Near-Field Spin Seebeck Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06411)
*Gaomin Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出并建立了由近场热辐射驱动的光子介导自旋塞贝克效应，利用单层过渡金属二硫化物与热发射体之间的真空间隙，通过Rashba自旋轨道耦合将光学轨道激发转化为电子自旋极化，展示了纳米尺度自旋操控的辐射路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自旋塞贝克效应依赖于界面热传导产生自旋电流，本文旨在探索一种全新的、由光子介导的自旋塞贝克效应，以利用近场热辐射实现纳米尺度电子自旋的远程操控。

Method: 采用单层过渡金属二硫化物（TMD）作为二维电子气材料，与热发射体之间以真空间隙隔开；通过近场热辐射驱动光学轨道激发，利用体系的Rashba自旋轨道耦合将轨道角动量转化为自旋极化。论文提出了两条互补机制：（1）手性热光子直接传递角动量；（2）磁化二维电子气对非偏振热涨落的整流作用。

Result: 证实了近场热辐射可以驱动自旋塞贝克效应，Rashba自旋轨道耦合成功将光学轨道激发转化为电子自旋极化，且两种互补机制均能有效工作。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了辐射热传输可作为纳米尺度电子自旋操控的新途径，拓展了自旋塞贝克效应的物理机制，为未来自旋电子学器件设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: The conventional spin Seebeck effect generates spin currents through interfacial thermal conduction. In this Letter, we establish a photon-mediated spin Seebeck effect driven by near-field thermal radiation. Using a monolayer transition metal dichalcogenide separated from a thermal emitter by a vacuum gap, we demonstrate Rashba spin-orbit coupling can convert optical orbital excitations into an electron spin polarization. This occurs via two complementary mechanisms: the direct transfer of angular momentum from chiral thermal photons, and the rectification of unpolarized thermal fluctuations by a magnetized two-dimensional electron gas. These findings unveil a radiative pathway for nanoscale electron spin manipulation.

</details>


### [228] [Emergent Chiral Metal Phase in Compressible Quantum Hall Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06532)
*Suparna Sahoo,Suvankar Purkait,Pooja Agarwal,Tanmay Maiti,Sourin Das,Vladimir Umansky,Biswajit Karmakar*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道了在可压缩量子霍尔流体中测量源极与无反射接触之间的透射电导，发现所有透射电导的总和普遍趋近于霍尔电导ν(e²/h)，该求和规则在整数与分数量子霍尔区间均成立，不受温度、样品几何、材料质量和准粒子相互作用变化的影响。理论上论证了强磁场下时间反演对称性破缺在近平坦势能景观和点状无序势中产生单向载流子轨迹（当屏蔽长度小于磁长度时），纵向电阻主要源于接触处的电化学势均衡而非体耗散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索可压缩量子霍尔流体中手性输运的内在规律，特别是透射电导求和规则是否具有普适性，并建立针对这一宽泛的二维无带隙系统（具备短程屏蔽和破缺时间反演对称性）的新输运理论范式。

Method: 实验上通过源极与无反射接触之间的透射电导测量，在整数和分数量子霍尔区间内系统变化温度、样品几何、材料质量和准粒子相互作用，检验透射电导总和与Hall电导的普适关系；采用浮动接触测量确认可压缩量子霍尔流体中手性输运的特征（抑制耗散输运且有明确手性）。理论上基于强磁场下时间反演对称性破缺、近平坦电势景观和点状无序势的框架，论证屏蔽长度小于磁长度时载流子沿单向轨迹运动。

Result: 实验上观察到所有透射电导之和普适地趋近于霍尔电导ν(e²/h)，这一求和规则在整数与分数量子霍尔区间保持成立，并且独立于温度、样品几何、材料质量和准粒子相互作用。浮动接触测量验证了可压缩量子霍尔流体中的手性输运行为。理论上揭示了该求和规则对应手性金属相的一种传导定律，且纵向电阻来自接触处的电化学势均衡而非体耗散。

Conclusion: 透射电导求和等于霍尔电导的规则作为手性金属相的一条传导定律被确立，为理解带有短程屏蔽和破缺时间反演对称性的宽泛二维无带隙系统中的手性输运提供了新物理范式。

Abstract: We report transmitted conductance measurements between a source and reflection-less contacts connected to a compressible quantum Hall fluid with filling fraction $ν$. We observe that total sum of transmitted conductances universally approaches Hall conductance $ν(e^2/h)$. The universality of this sum rule is established experimentally across integer and fractional quantum Hall regimes, remaining invariant under variation in temperatures, sample geometries, material qualities and quasi-particle interactions. Chiral transport in compressible quantum Hall fluids, characterized by suppressed dissipative transport with a distinct handedness, is confirmed by floating contact measurements. Consequently, this sum rule emerges as a conduction law of the chiral metal phase. Theoretically, we argue that time-reversal-symmetry breaking under a strong magnetic field within this chiral metal gives rise unidirectional trajectories of carriers in a nearly flat potential landscape with point-like disorder potentials, a regime realized when the screening length is smaller than the magnetic length. Within this chiral framework, longitudinal resistance does not originate from bulk dissipation but primarily from the equilibration of electrochemical potentials at the contacts. Our study introduces a new paradigm of chiral transport across a broad class of gapless two-dimensional systems characterized by short-range screening and broken time-reversal symmetry.

</details>


### [229] [Three-Dimensional Fermiology and Thickness-Tuned Magnetotransport in Single-Crystalline Antimony Flakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06561)
*Mikhail Gaponov,Jicheng Wang,Liang Zha,Rui Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过CVD生长的单晶Sb薄片（110-783 nm）结合纵向/霍尔磁输运及Shubnikov-de Haas振荡测量，发现随厚度增加，非饱和磁阻在2K、14T下提升近30倍达7.13×10^5%，而费米面主频Fα保持~99T不变，揭示厚度通过维度依赖的散射而非费米面重构来调控极端磁阻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 补偿半金属的极端磁阻同时受费米面几何和载流子弛豫影响，但在有限尺寸结构中这两种贡献难以区分，亟需系统研究以厘清其物理机制。

Method: 结合纵向与Hall磁输运测量以及随温度和角度变化的Shubnikov-de Haas振荡，对CVD生长的单晶Sb薄片（110-783 nm）进行系统分析，并对ρxx(B)和ρxy(B)进行联合三通道拟合。

Result: 随厚度增加，2K/14T下非饱和MR增大约30倍至7.13×10^5%；主频率Fα保持~99T无系统性偏移；联合分析表明该演化源于近补偿电子-空穴对迁移率提高而非主口袋重构；角度依赖测量证实闭合三维α口袋存在，335-377 T的高频扇区与L点口袋的电子β轨道一致。

Conclusion: 厚度通过维度依赖的散射调控极端MR，同时保持体材料类费米面拓扑不变，为理解有限尺寸系统中极端磁阻的物理机制提供了实验基础。

Abstract: The extreme magnetoresistance of compensated semimetals is governed by both the Fermi-surface geometry and carrier relaxation, but these contributions are difficult to disentangle in finite-size structures. Here, we combine longitudinal and Hall magnetotransport measurements with temperature- and angle-dependent Shubnikov--de Haas oscillations in single-crystalline Sb flakes grown by chemical vapor deposition (CVD), with thicknesses ranging from 110 to 783 nm. As thickness increases, the non-saturating MR at 2 K and 14 T rises nearly 30-fold, reaching $7.13\times10^{5}\%$, while the primary frequency $F_α$ remains approximately 99 T without any systematic shift. A joint three-channel analysis of $ρ_{xx}(B)$ and $ρ_{xy}(B)$ reveals that this evolution is driven by an increase in the mobility of a nearly compensated electron--hole pair, rather than by a reconstruction of the primary pockets. Angle-dependent measurements confirm the existence of a closed three-dimensional $α$ pocket, and a reproducible high-frequency sector (335-377 T) is consistent with the electron $β$ orbit of the $L$-point pockets in bulk Sb. Together, the transport and quantum-oscillation results show that thickness tunes extreme MR through dimension-dependent scattering while preserving bulk-like fermiology.

</details>


### [230] [Evidence of Haldane-Chain Physics in an Fe-Dehydroindigo Coordination Polymer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06684)
*Ritam Chakraborty,Shobhana Narasimhan,T. V. Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算（自旋-轨道耦合DFT）和磁性能量映射，研究了Fe-脱氢靛蓝自旋交叉配位聚合物在本征无衬底极限下是否支持Haldane链物理。计算得到近各向同性的反铁磁耦合J_H=32.206 meV及较弱的单离子各向异性，精确对角化和DMRG计算表明存在约13-14 meV的非简并基态与三重组激发之间的能隙，与自旋-1 Haldane能隙尺度一致，从而将孤立顺式脱氢靛蓝链确立为一种可支持Haldane链物理的材料特异性配位聚合物平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自旋交叉配位聚合物（Fe-脱氢靛蓝）在无衬底本征环境下能否承载Haldane链物理这一拓扑量子磁性现象，为材料设计提供新的化学平台。

Method: 采用自旋-轨道耦合密度泛函理论（SOC-DFT）计算并映射磁性能量参数，提取交换耦合常数J和单离子各向异性；随后利用精确对角化（ED）和密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）方法计算自旋链的基态、激发谱及动力学自旋结构因子。

Result: 获得近各向同性反铁磁耦合J_H=32.206 meV，单离子各向异性较弱；系统呈现非简并基态，与最低三重组激发之间存在约13-14 meV的能隙，处于自旋-1 Haldane能隙预期范围；各向异性仅造成低能模式的小分裂；动力学自旋结构因子的最低谱权重位于反铁磁波矢q=π附近。

Conclusion: 孤立顺式脱氢靛蓝链的微观磁性相互作用将其置于有能隙的量子磁性区间，与Haldane链物理一致，表明该配位聚合物可作为材料级的Haldane链研究平台。

Abstract: We investigate whether an Fe--dehydroindigo spin-crossover coordination polymer can support Haldane-chain physics in its intrinsic, substrate-free limit. Spin-orbit-coupled density-functional-theory calculations and magnetic energy mapping yield a nearly isotropic antiferromagnetic coupling, $J_{\mathrm H}=32.206$~meV, together with much weaker single-ion anisotropy. Using these parameters, exact diagonalization and density-matrix renormalization group calculations give a nondegenerate ground state separated from the lowest triplet-derived excitations by a gap of about $13$--$14$~meV, on the scale expected for the spin-1 Haldane gap. The anisotropy produces only a small splitting of the low-energy modes. The dynamical spin structure factor places the lowest spectral weight near the antiferromagnetic wave vector $q=π$. These results identify the isolated cis-dehydroindigo chain as a material-specific coordination-polymer platform whose microscopic interactions place it in a gapped regime consistent with Haldane-chain physics.

</details>


### [231] [Self-Trapping Enabled Highly Bright Momentum-Indirect Interlayer Excitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06927)
*Dong Yang,Zisheng Gong,Hao Wen,Yue Hu,Kaichen Jiang,Baixu Xiang,Weibo Gao,Qihua Xiong,Dehui Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文展示了在二维钙钛矿与单层过渡金属二硫化物（TMD）构成的异质结构中，实现了高亮度的动量间接层间激子发光，量子产率平均达35.2%（最高超过60%），比相应的TMD单层高出50倍以上，甚至比TMD基异质结构中的动量直接层间激子辐射复合效率高出两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层间激子在二维材料异质结构中具有大激子结合能和长寿命，是研究激子器件和多体量子现象的理想平台。然而，由于电子和空穴的空间分离，层间激子的振子强度比层内激子低两个数量级，实现高效率的层间激子发光仍然面临重大挑战，需要优化材料选择和器件制备。

Method: 研究人员构建了二维钙钛矿与单层TMD的异质结构。由于二维钙钛矿具有软晶格特性，产生了强烈的激子-声子耦合，诱导层间激子发生自俘获效应，从而显著提升了动量间接层间激子的辐射复合效率。

Result: 层间激子发光的量子产率平均达到35.2%，比相应的TMD单层高出50倍以上，最高值超过60%。该动量间接层间激子的辐射复合效率比TMD基异质结构中的动量直接层间激子高出两个数量级。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了通过激子自俘获机制实现高亮度层间激子发光的可能性，为实现高效率层间激子发射提供了新的物理见解，并为探索长寿命激子器件开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Interlayer excitons in two dimensional material heterostructures exhibit large exciton binding energies and long lifetimes, making them ideal platforms for studying excitonic devices and many body quantum phenomena. However, the spatially separated electron and hole nature of IXs reduces their oscillator strength by two orders of magnitude compared to intralayer excitons. Achieving high efficiency IX emission remains challenging and requires optimal material selection with appropriate momentum matching and meticulous device fabrication. Here we demonstrate a highly bright momentum indirect IX emission within heterostructures formed between 2D perovskites and monolayer transition metal dichalcogenides. The quantum yield of IX emission reaches 35.2% on average, over 50 times higher than that of the corresponding constituent TMD monolayer, with the highest value exceeding 60%. Notably, the radiative recombination efficiency of this momentum indirect IX exceeds that of momentum direct IXs in monolayer TMD-based heterostructures by two orders of magnitude. We suggest that the remarkably bright IX emission in our heterostructure originates from IX self trapping, induced by strong exciton phonon coupling arising from the soft lattice nature of the 2D perovskite. Our findings provide new insights into achieving high IX emission efficiency and open new avenues for exploring long lifetime excitonic devices.

</details>


### [232] [Theory of staggered damping-like spin-orbit torque on synthetic antiferromagnetic domain walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07055)
*Yuta Yamane,Takeshi Seki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在合成反铁磁体中普遍存在的交错阻尼类自旋轨道矩理论，推导出畴壁速度的闭合解析表达式，并预言可通过电流、面内磁场及层间交换耦合进行调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Néel（交错）自旋轨道矩与特殊晶体结构绑定，且只有类场分量被证实。作者希望探索更普适的交替阻尼类SOT机制及其在合成反铁磁中的实现。

Method: 基于Néel矢量拉格朗日量推导解析理论，计算畴壁速度的闭合表达式；通过微磁模拟验证理论预言，分析电流、面内磁场、层间交换耦合对畴壁运动的影响及Néel-Bloch转变阈值。

Result: 发现交错阻尼类SOT在两侧相同重金属夹持的合成反铁磁中普遍存在；畴壁速度对电流与面内磁场呈双线性依赖，且Néel壁与Bloch壁速度符号相反，可通过层间交换耦合进行调控。微磁模拟与理论一致，并预见了速度符号反转和标度律。

Conclusion: 畴壁结构可作为控制畴壁运动的调控手段，同时畴壁运动可提供其结构的电学读出方式。所预言的符号反转、标度律及转变阈值提供了直接的实验验证目标。

Abstract: The Néel, or staggered, spin-orbit torque (SOT) enables an efficient electrical manipulation of antiferromagnetic order. However, it has been tied to special crystal structures, and only its field-like component is established. We develop a theory of staggered damping-like SOT, which arises generically in synthetic antiferromagnets sandwiched by the same heavy metal on both sides. Deriving a Néel-vector Lagrangian, we obtain the domain-wall (DW) velocity in closed form: bilinear in current and in-plane field, opposite in sign for Néel and Bloch walls, and tunable through the interlayer exchange coupling. DW structure thus becomes a control knob for DW motion, while the DW motion, in turn, offers an electrical readout of its structure. Micromagnetic simulations confirm the theory. The predicted sign reversals and scaling laws of the DW velocity, together with the thresholds for the Néel-Bloch transition, give immediate experimental targets.

</details>


### [233] [Lindbladian Phase Geometry and Hall Transport in Open Bloch Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07228)
*Zhihao Jiang,Longjun Xiang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在迹保持的Lindblad动力学框架下发展了开放量子体系中直流线性响应理论，通过定义物理速度算子，发现了由Lindblad跳跃振幅对应的规范不变相位几何所产生的新Hall响应机制，即使传统Berry曲率反常Hall效应为零时也可产生有限Hall电导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bloch系统中的量子几何支撑着广泛的输运现象，但当Bloch电子与环境耦合时，量子几何输运如何被修正仍然鲜有探索。环境不仅会改变电子态，还会改变物理电流算子，因此有必要在开放量子体系框架下重新审视量子几何输运。

Method: 在迹保持的Lindblad动力学理论中定义物理速度为位置算子的Liouville时间导数，由此推导直流线性响应。将环境诱导的电流顶点贡献分解为动量空间旋度，从而揭示出由Lindblad跳跃振幅编码的规范不变相位几何所主宰的新Hall响应。在耗散耦合的领头阶下，分别分析对角和非对角跳跃振幅对应的几何贡献。

Result: 发现两种新的Hall响应机制：由非对角跳跃振幅产生的带间shift-vector Hall响应和由对角跳跃振幅产生的对角涡度（diagonal-vorticity）Hall响应。这两种机制即使当传统Berry曲率反常Hall效应严格为零时，仍可产生有限的Hall电导率。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了Lindblad相位几何作为开放量子物质中横向输运的独立几何起源，拓展了量子几何输运的理论框架至开放量子体系。

Abstract: Quantum geometry underlies a wide range of transport phenomena in Bloch systems. How quantum-geometric transport is modified when Bloch electrons are coupled to an environment, however, remains largely unexplored, even though the environment can alter both the electronic state and the physical current operator. Here we formulate dc linear response within a trace-preserving Lindblad kinetic theory by defining the physical velocity as the Liouvillian time derivative of the position operator. This construction reveals an environment-induced contribution to the current vertex whose momentum-space curl generates new Hall responses governed by the gauge-invariant phase geometry encoded by Lindblad jump amplitudes, which characterize electron-environment coupling. To leading order in the dissipative coupling, this geometry gives rise to interband shift-vector and diagonal-vorticity Hall responses arising from off-diagonal and diagonal jump amplitudes, respectively. Remarkably, both mechanisms can produce a finite Hall conductivity even when the conventional Berry-curvature anomalous Hall effect vanishes identically. Our work establishes Lindbladian phase geometry as an independent geometric origin of transverse transport in open quantum matter.

</details>


### [234] [Unveiling the Scaling Potential of Drain Merge through Active (DMtA) in CFETs: Breaking the Super-Via Bottlenecks and Unlocking New PPA Boosters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07329)
*Jingru Jiang,Haoran Lu,Kairong Guo,Yibo Zhang,Yifei Chen,Wanyue Peng,Yu Liu,Jiacheng Sun,Xiaoyan Xu,Ming Li,Yibo Lin,Runsheng Wang,Ru Huang,Heng Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了DMtA（Drain Merge through Active）技术在CFET器件中的DTCO全流程评估，通过降低DM寄生电容和实现DM宽度优化，使RO频率提升11.7%，结合主动区拓宽和相邻单元空间借用等策略，Ibex核心频率最高提升34.8%。DMtA还使原本仅限GAA工艺的Hyper-cells在CFET上得以实现，进一步带来8.7%的频率增益；配合布线后浮动输出引脚感知优化降低功耗5.3%，并通过保持单行单元兼容性将后PR核心面积缩减25.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在CFET器件中，传统的Drain Merge (DM)超通孔连接堆叠n/pFET的公共源漏端，但会阻碍寄生效应的进一步优化与单元缩放。为突破这一限制，需探索将DM嵌入有源区的新型技术方案并系统评估其全流程影响。

Method: 构建了涵盖工艺集成、接触构型相关(CTCD)紧凑建模、标准单元设计、环形振荡器(RO)评估以及32位RISC-V Ibex核的模块级PPA基准测试的全面DTCO框架，系统分析DMtA技术，并结合Active widening与Area Borrowing策略以及Hyper-cells架构，辅以布线后浮动输出引脚感知优化。

Result: DMtA相比传统DMtF使RO频率提升11.7%；结合Active widening和Area Borrowing使Ibex核最高频率提升34.8%；实现GAA专属Hyper-cells在CFET上的应用带来额外8.7%频率增益；浮动输出引脚感知优化降低功耗5.3%；通过2.5T单元缩放将后PR面积减少25.7%。

Conclusion: DMtA技术通过在CFET有源区嵌入DM，不仅克服了传统DM对寄生优化的阻碍，还解锁了多项此前受限的架构创新（如Hyper-cells和2.5T缩放），在频率、功耗和面积三方面均带来显著改善，为CFET平台的性能扩展提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Drain merge (DM), a super via vertically connecting the common S/D terminals of stacked n/pFETs in Complementary FETs (CFETs), blocks further parasitic optimization and cell scaling. For the first time, this work systematically investigates the state-of-the-art Drain Merge through Active (DMtA), a revolutionary technology reported recently with the DM embedded in the active region, through a comprehensive DTCO framework spanning process integration, contact-configuration-dependent (CTCD) compact modeling, standardcell design, RO evaluation and block-level PPA benchmark on a 32-bit RISC-V Ibex core. By reducing DM parasitics and enabling DM-width optimization, DMtA improves RO frequency by 11.7% over its conventional Drain Merge through field (DMtF) counterpart. Active widening and Area Borrowing, the latter first reported in [8] and exploiting spatial slack in adjacent cells to further enlarge the nanosheet width (WNS), increase the maximum Ibex-core frequency by up to 34.8%. More importantly, DMtA also enables the once GAA-exclusive Hyper-cells on CFETs by merging the active regions across adjacent cell rows, providing a further 8.7% frequency gain. A post-routing floating-output-pin-aware optimization further removes redundant S/D contacts (CTs) and reduces power by 5.3%. Finally, DMtA facilitates more area-efficient 2.5T cell scaling by preserving single-row cell compatibility, reducing post-PR core area by 25.7%.

</details>


### [235] [Trion-mediated quantum operations on a double-dot charge qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07342)
*Alexander Tsukanov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出了一种在半导体非对称双量子点中控制电荷量子比特的方案，通过激光脉冲将逻辑单电子态转换为辅助三重子态，恢复共振隧穿条件，实现高速可靠的单量子比特正交旋转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非对称双量子点中抑制隧穿耦合虽然可以提高量子比特的稳定性，但也使得实现相干控制变得困难，需要找到一种能够在保持抑制隧穿耦合的同时实现快速可靠量子门操作的方案。

Method: 利用激光脉冲将逻辑单电子态转换为辅助三重子态，借助粒子间库仑相互作用补偿结构不对称性，从而恢复共振隧穿条件；随后推导了执行正交单量子比特旋转的条件，并在二维结构中计算了量子比特参数的翻转操作保真度对激光场参数和耗散过程速率的依赖关系。

Result: 对于二维结构计算了量子比特的参数，获得了翻转操作保真度随激光场参数和耗散过程速率变化的依赖关系，表明该算法具有高速度和可靠性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于辅助三重子态和激光控制的方案实现了在抑制隧穿耦合情况下对双量子点电荷量子比特的高保真度、快速控制，是一种有效且可靠的量子比特操控方案。

Abstract: A scheme for controlling a charge qubit on a semiconductor asymmetric double quantum dot with suppressed tunnel coupling between individual quantum dots is proposed. Laser pulses convert the logical single-electron states of the qubit into auxiliary trion states, for which the resonant tunneling condition is restored due to the compensation of structural asymmetry by the Coulomb interaction of the particles. The conditions for performing orthogonal single-qubit rotations are formulated. For a two-dimensional structure, the parameters of the qubit are calculated, and the dependence of the fidelity of the inversion operation on the parameters of the laser field and the rates of dissipative processes is obtained. It is shown that the considered algorithm is characterized by high speed and reliability.

</details>


### [236] [Static-Dynamic Correlations and Complex Spin-Wave Eigenmodes in Single- and Multilayer Diamond-Shaped Nanomagnets Without Bias Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07368)
*Krishna Begari*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过微磁模拟系统研究了单层和多层菱形纳米磁体的静态与动态磁行为，发现可通过简单磁场初始化过程获得两种不同的剩余磁态，其共振特性可通过纳秒级磁场脉冲调控。多层结构因层间偶极耦合增强表现出更大的频率调谐范围（3 GHz），为可重构微波器件提供了有效途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱形纳米磁体为开发具有可重构特性的微波器件提供了合适的平台，但需要系统理解其静态和动态磁行为以实现频率可调谐功能。

Method: 采用微磁模拟方法，系统性地表征单层和多层菱形纳米磁体的静态与动态磁行为，通过简单磁场初始化获得不同剩余磁态，并施加纳秒级磁场脉冲改变其共振特性。

Result: 单层结构观察到0.9 GHz次GHz范围的明显共振频率偏移，多层结构则显示出更大达3 GHz的GHz范围频率偏移；多层结构更大的频率可调性主要归因于垂直耦合磁层间的增强偶极耦合，其改变了局部有效磁场并影响磁化动力学。

Conclusion: 菱形纳米磁体为可重构微波功能提供了一条简单有效的途径，在超低功耗、超快和频率可调谐微波器件中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Diamond-shaped nanomagnets provide a suitable platform for developing microwave devices with reconfigurable characteristics. In this study, the static and dynamic magnetic behaviour of single layer and multilayer diamond-shaped nanomagnets was systematically investigated using micromagnetic simulations. Two distinct remanent magnetic configurations were obtained through a simple magnetic field initialization process. These configurations exhibited different magnetization patterns and dynamic responses. Their resonance characteristics could be modified by applying a nanosecond-scale magnetic field pulse, enabling reconfigurable microwave operation. A clear resonance frequency shift in the sub-GHz (0.9 GHz) range was observed for the single-layer structure, while the multilayer structure exhibited a significantly larger frequency shift in the GHz range (3 GHz). The larger frequency tunability observed in the multilayer configuration arises primarily from the enhanced dipolar coupling between the vertically coupled magnetic layers, which modifies the local effective magnetic field and consequently influences the magnetization dynamics. These findings demonstrate that diamond-shaped nanomagnets can provide a simple and effective route toward reconfigurable microwave functionality, with potential applications in ultralow-power, ultrafast, and frequency tunable microwave devices.

</details>


### [237] [Simple invariants for band topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07775)
*Adam Yanis Chaou,Adolfo G. Grushin,Peru d'Ornellas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了由哈密顿量与位置算符构造的谱局域化算子，将其视为一种辅助的零维哈密顿量，其拓扑性质能够编码更高维母哈密顿量的拓扑相。该方法的分类将每个拓扑不变量归结为矩阵签名或Pfaffian符号的计算，从而系统性地为所有非相互作用拓扑态（包括晶态和非晶态系统）提供简单、高效且在实空间中计算的拓扑不变量，填补了此前弱拓扑相、旋转对称晶相、原子极限及无已知不变量相位的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管对非相互作用拓扑态已有深入了解，但尚无一种统一方法能够系统性地为所有晶态和非晶态系统提供简单且数值高效的拓扑不变量。现有的谱局域化形式体系无法处理某些弱拓扑相和旋转不变量晶相，散射不变量无法适用于原子极限，对称性指标方法也有其覆盖盲区，因此需要一种系统性的统一框架。

Method: 本文重新审视谱局域化算子（spectral localizer），将其视为一个辅助的零维哈密顿量，其拓扑不变量可以编码母哈密顿量对应的高维拓扑相。作者证明通过选择适当的局域化算子，所有拓扑不变量都可以归结为矩阵签名或Pfaffian符号的计算，两者在实空间中均易于解释且数值高效。

Result: 作者通过推导弱拓扑相和旋转不变晶相的简单实空间不变量来验证该方法的有效性，同时覆盖了此前超出散射不变量范围的原子极限、规避对称性指标方法盲区的相，以及此前完全没有已知不变量的相位。

Conclusion: 该工作为任何非相互作用拓扑不变量提供了一种系统性的构造方法，适用于晶态和非晶态系统，为分类和预测此前尚未探索的材料类别拓扑性质开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Despite the exhaustive understanding gathered around non-interacting topological states of matter, there is no single method capable of systematically delivering simple, numerically efficient topological invariants that is applicable to all crystalline and non-crystalline systems alike. Here we revisit the spectral localizer operator, constructed from the Hamiltonian and position operators, and show how it can be treated it as an auxiliary zero-dimensional Hamiltonian whose topology encodes the higher dimensional phases of the parent Hamiltonian. Its classification reduces every topological invariant to a matrix signature or the sign of a Pfaffian for an appropriate localizer, both of which are simple to interpret and efficient to compute in real space. We validate this approach by deriving simple real-space invariants for weak and rotationally invariant crystalline phases that were previously beyond the grasp of the spectral localizer formalism, atomic limits that escape scattering invariants, phases that evade symmetry-based indicator methods, as well as phases that had no previously known invariant. Our work provides a systematic way to construct any non-interacting topological invariant for a crystalline or non-crystalline systems, opening avenues to classify and predict the topology of previously unexplored classes of materials.

</details>


### [238] [Layer-Dependent Phonons, Excitons, and Magneto-Optical Phenomena in CrSBr: A Mini Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07425)
*Muhammad Aftab,Chinmay Kumar Mohanty,Zain Ashfaq,Warisha Mehmood,Xiaoli Wang,Clément Faugeras,Wajid Ali,Maciej R. Molas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文综述了二维层状磁性材料CrSBr从块体到单层极限范围内的层依赖振动与激子性质、磁光响应及其在自旋电子学、磁光和量子光子技术中的应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维层状磁性材料为探索低维磁性和多体相互作用提供了平台。CrSBr因其本征空气稳定性、半导体特性、强面内各向异性和A型反铁磁有序性，是先进自旋电子学和光电子应用的理想候选材料，全面理解其层依赖的物理性质至关重要。

Method: 系统综述了CrSBr的晶体结构、磁各向异性、层间自旋重取向，分析了其振动动力学和自旋-声子耦合，重点阐释了激子景观（包括磁场敏感的光致发光、局域激子态及带隙中Frenkel和Wannier-Mott激子的共存现象），并涵盖从块体到单层的尺度效应。

Result: 总结了CrSBr的层依赖振动和激子特性及其磁光响应的最新研究进展，揭示了其层间自旋重取向行为、自旋-声子耦合机制以及不同激子类型共存等关键物理现象。

Conclusion: CrSBr表现出丰富的层依赖物理特性，有望在自旋电子、磁光和量子光子技术领域得到应用；但相关挑战仍需进一步解决，以充分利用其独特的层依赖性质。

Abstract: Two-dimensional layered magnetic materials offer a versatile platform for exploring low-dimensional magnetism and coupled many-body interactions in these materials. Chromium sulfur bromide (CrSBr) is a promising candidate for advanced spintronic and optoelectronic applications because of its intrinsic air stability, semiconducting nature, strong in-plane anisotropy, and A-type antiferromagnetic ordering. This review summarizes recent advances in the understanding of the layer-dependent vibrational and excitonic properties of CrSBr, as well as its magneto-optical response, from bulk crystals to the monolayer limit. We examined its crystal structure, magnetic anisotropy, and interlayer spin reorientation, followed by insights into vibrational dynamics and spin-phonon coupling. Particular emphasis is placed on the excitonic landscape, including magnetic-field-sensitive photoluminescence, localized excitonic states, and the coexistence of Frenkel- and Wannier-Mott excitons in the bandgap. Finally, we discuss the challenges and prospects of harnessing the unique layer-dependent properties of CrSBr in spintronic, magneto-optical, and quantum photonic technologies.

</details>


### [239] [Charge-spin conversion in altermagnets: electronic anisotropy versus magnon-mediated spin drag](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07426)
*Koki Mizuno*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在统一模型中计算了金属铁磁体、反铁磁体和交错磁体的自旋分辨电导率，发现电荷-自旋转换的各向异性主要由电子自旋劈裂主导，而磁振子通道贡献微弱。磁振子介导的自旋翻转通道对C4对称性为偶，不能单独区分交错磁体与常规反铁磁体，但对降低体相B1g对称性的亚晶格选择性微扰呈线性响应，该响应在反铁磁体中恒为零、在铁磁体中很小、在交错磁体中有限，且不依赖于磁畴方向，因此无需单畴样品即可测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体（altermagnet）具有无净磁化或无自旋轨道耦合的动量依赖自旋劈裂，这引发了一个核心问题：其输运各向异性究竟源于电子还是磁振子，以及磁振子介导的通道能否区分交错磁体与常规反铁磁体。作者旨在厘清这些机制并寻找可实验区分的唯一定量特征。

Method: 作者在统一的紧束缚/连续模型中同时处理三种磁性体系（金属铁磁体、反铁磁体和交错磁体），采用磁振子自能处理自旋对角通道，并对自旋翻转通道保留到电子-磁振子耦合的最低阶。通过计算自旋分辨电导率分析各向异性来源，并考察亚晶格选择性微扰下的线性响应行为。

Result: 1) 电荷-自旋转换的各向异性由电子自旋劈裂主导，磁振子分支贡献甚微。2) 磁振子介导的自旋翻转通道在C4旋转下为偶，因此一阶各向异性中不含其信号，无法单独区分交错磁体与反铁磁体。3) 对降低B1g对称性的亚晶格选择性微扰，响应率在反铁磁体中恒为零，在铁磁体中很小，在交错磁体中有限且不依赖磁畴指标。
