Conclusion: 在无净磁化的磁性体系中，对亚晶格选择性微扰的线性响应（奇偶性质源于电子各向异性将自旋与亚晶格绑定）是交错磁序所独有的特征。该响应在磁畴指标下为偶，故无需制备单畴样品即可测量，这为实验探测交错磁体提供了可操作的观测量。

Abstract: Altermagnets acquire a momentum-dependent spin splitting without net magnetization or spin-orbit coupling.
  This raises the question of whether the transport anisotropy originates in the electrons or the magnons, and whether the magnon-mediated channel distinguishes an altermagnet from a conventional antiferromagnet.
  We compute the spin-resolved conductivity of a metallic ferromagnet, antiferromagnet, and altermagnet within a common model, treating the spin-diagonal channel with the magnon self-energy and the spin-flip channel to leading order in the electron-magnon coupling.
  The anisotropy of the charge-spin conversion is dominated by the electronic spin splitting, the magnon branches contributing only marginally.
  The magnon-mediated spin-flip channel is even under $C_{4}$, so it carries no signature at first order in the anisotropy and cannot on its own distinguish an altermagnet from a conventional antiferromagnet.
  It does, however, respond linearly to a sublattice-selective perturbation that lowers the bulk $B_{1g}$ symmetry.
  The resulting susceptibility vanishes identically in the antiferromagnet, is small in the ferromagnet, and is finite in the altermagnet, where the electronic anisotropy ties spin and sublattice together, so that among magnets with no net magnetization it is unique to altermagnetic order.
  Being even in the magnetic domain index, this response can be measured without preparing a single magnetic-domain sample.

</details>


### [240] [Divergent Orbital Diamagnetism from Chiral Edge States in Chern Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09065)
*Nobuyuki Okuma*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究发现带开放边界条件的Chern绝缘体也表现出与无质量狄拉克系统（如石墨烯）类似的发散轨道抗磁磁化率，其发散行为随系统线性尺寸线性增长，且对无序具有鲁棒性，揭示了其拓扑本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维无质量狄拉克系统（以石墨烯为代表）表现出随系统尺寸线性发散的轨道抗磁磁化率，而Chern绝缘体是否存在类似的巨大抗磁响应及其物理机制尚不清楚。作者希望探究拓扑边缘态与狄拉克系统中发散抗磁之间的深层联系。

Method: 作者从将手性边缘态视为放大的苯环类似物一半的视角出发，在开放边界条件下研究Chern绝缘体，分析其轨道磁化率随系统尺寸的标度行为，并通过引入无序扰动来检验该响应的鲁棒性。

Result: 研究发现Chern绝缘体在开放边界条件下展现出与无质量狄拉克系统相同的发散标度，即轨道抗磁磁化率随系统线性尺寸线性发散，且这一巨大抗磁性对无序具有鲁棒性，证明了其拓扑起源。

Conclusion: Chern绝缘体中的发散抗磁磁化率具有拓扑本质，并与无质量狄拉克系统的发散抗磁之间存在深刻联系，为理解拓扑材料中的磁响应特性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Two-dimensional massless Dirac systems, exemplified by graphene, are known to exhibit a divergent orbital diamagnetic susceptibility that scales linearly with system size. Motivated by viewing a chiral edge state as one half of an enlarged analogue of a benzene ring, we study Chern insulators under open boundary conditions and find the same divergent scaling. This giant diamagnetism is robust against disorder, revealing its topological nature. Our results further suggest a profound connection to the divergent diamagnetism of massless Dirac systems.

</details>


### [241] [Direct Realization of Near-Ideal Carbyne in Ultrathin Boron Nitride Nanotubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07443)
*Iryna Ivanenko,Pietro Marabotti,Yifan Zhang,Getulio Silva e Souza Júnior,Johannes M. A. Lechner,Pablo Hernández López,Martin Magg,Shivani Shivaprakash,Carlo Spartaco Casari,Sebastian Heeg,Benjamin S. Flavel*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过在超薄氮化硼纳米管中封装并热转化氢封端多炔，制备出近理想态卡拜（carbyne）链，消除了端基、有限长度和宿主扰动等次级效应，并通过统计拉曼光谱首次获得接近自由链本征性质的卡拜振动数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡拜作为sp杂化一维碳同素异形体，被预测为最坚硬材料，其电子和光学性质由单一结构参数（键长交替）决定。但其本征性质从未被直接测量：分子合成的链受端基和有限长度扰动，碳纳米管中生长的链又与宿主强耦合（频率重整化达110 cm^-1）。需要一种方法在近理想条件下研究卡拜。

Method: 将氢封端多炔（hydrogen-capped polyynes）封装在超薄氮化硼纳米管（BNNT）中——BNNT是碳纳米管的结构类似物但电绝缘。通过热转化将多炔转化为卡拜链。利用统计拉曼光谱（245个位置）测量振动频率分布、非谐性、键长交替，并检测光致发光。

Result: 振动频率分布比在碳纳米管中窄一个数量级；观测到的非谐性与卡拜类材料的普适定律一致；键长交替与自由链的相关计算吻合；未检测到光致发光（符合无扰动卡拜链偶极禁戒发射的预期）。端基、有限长度和宿主-客体扰动均降为次级效应。

Conclusion: 氮化硼纳米管为实验研究近理想态的卡拜提供了可行途径，首次实现对其本征性质的接近无扰动测量。

Abstract: Carbyne, the sp-hybridized one-dimensional allotrope of carbon, is predicted to be the stiffest known material, with electronic and optical properties set by a single structural parameter, the bond length alternation. However, its intrinsic properties have never been measured: chains synthesized through molecular chemistry carry endgroup and finite-length perturbations that persist even in the longest molecules available, while chains grown inside carbon nanotubes strongly couple to the host, which renormalizes their vibrational frequency by up to 110 cm$^{-1}$ in a diameter-dependent manner. Here, we show that encapsulating and thermally converting hydrogen-capped polyynes inside ultrathin boron nitride nanotubes, structural analogues of carbon nanotubes but electrically insulating, yields carbyne chains in a near-ideal regime, where endgroup, finite length, and host-guest perturbations are reduced to secondary effects. Statistical Raman spectroscopy across 245 locations returns a vibrational frequency distribution an order of magnitude narrower than in carbon nanotubes, an anharmonicity consistent with the universal law for carbyne-like materials, and a bond length alternation matching correlated calculations for the free chain. No photoluminescence is detected, despite the transparent host, as expected for the dipole-forbidden emission of an unperturbed carbyne chain. Boron nitride nanotubes give experimental access to carbyne in its near-ideal form.

</details>


### [242] [Topological Impurity Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09118)
*Arnab Seth,Itamar Kimchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发现无序缺陷（而非传统意义上的长程无序）可以在拓扑平庸的带绝缘体中诱导出新的拓扑相变，通过杂质束缚态的轨道依赖涡度形成拓扑杂质带（TIB）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究认为无序对拓扑相的效应要么是保持拓扑要么是破坏拓扑，或者通过微扰平均处理长程无序产生拓扑安德森绝缘体（TAI）。然而，这些方法依赖于将长程随机性微扰平均化，忽视了局域缺陷可能带来的额外效应。

Method: 理论框架以杂质束缚态的轨道依赖涡度（orbital-dependent vorticity）为核心，在Dirac锥相变附近解析推导，并利用Qi-Wu-Zhang和Haldane的陈绝缘体晶格模型进行数值验证。在3%缺陷密度下计算相图，区分TIB（拓扑杂质带）和TAI（拓扑安德森绝缘体）两种不同机制产生的拓扑相变。

Result: 数值计算表明，即使在初始模型为平庸带绝缘体的情况下，有限密度的随机分布缺陷也能产生多种新的拓扑相变。在3%缺陷密度下，相图表现出稳健的拓扑转变，且TIB和TAI两个不同区域均有体现。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一条局域缺陷驱动的拓扑相生成新路径——拓扑杂质带的形成，它无需依赖有序晶格或长程无序，为无序体系中的拓扑物态工程提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The effects of disorder on topological phases of matter are typically either preservation of topology or its destruction. For example, the quantum Hall effect's quantization and chiral edge mode remain robust until a strong-disorder percolation transition exits the topological phase. Disorder can also enlarge a preexisting topological phase, yielding a topological Anderson insulator (TAI). However, the reliance of TAI and other theoretical treatments on perturbatively averaging out long-wavelength randomness suggests that additional effects might appear when the disorder involves localized defects. Here we show that a finite density of randomly distributed defects can generate various new topological phase transitions. This route to disorder-induced topology can be viewed as the formation of topological impurity bands (TIB) and is applicable even if the initial clean model is a trivial band insulator. The theory relies on an orbital-dependent vorticity of impurity bound states, which we derive analytically near a generic Dirac cone phase transition as well as using the Chern insulator lattice models of Qi-Wu-Zhang and Haldane. Their numerically computed phase diagrams at 3% defect density show robust topological transitions in distinct TIB and TAI regimes, suggesting a local route to topological phases.

</details>


### [243] [Encapsulated macroscopic WS$_2$ monolayers enable room-temperature exciton-polariton lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07587)
*Shiyu Huang,Sander Scheel,Jiang Qu,Johannes Düreth,Dominik Horneber,Edith Wietek,Simon Widmann,Libo Ma,Monika Emmerling,Martin Kamp,Simon Betzold,Alexey Chernikov,Sven Höfling,Sebastian Klembt*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道了采用1-十二醇封装的WS₂单层，实现了毫米级大面积的均匀光学特性（均匀性距离接近300 μm），并将其集成到可调开放微腔中，实现了室温下的强耦合（Rabi分裂约31 meV），进而构建了二维极化激元kagome晶格，直接解析了其能带结构（包括Dirac色散带和弱色散的平带分支）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大面积、光学均匀的单层半导体是实现可扩展室温极化激元学及跨多个晶胞的极化激元晶格的关键前提，但传统机械剥离薄片存在尺寸小、光学不均及器件间差异大等障碍。

Method: 采用1-十二醇封装WS₂单层以获得毫米级均匀覆盖；将其集成于可调开放微腔中实现激子-光子强耦合；利用角度分辨光谱、动量空间与实空间成像等手段表征极化激元kagome晶格的能带结构与模式分布。

Result: 实现了室温强耦合（Rabi分裂≈31 meV），在s带中解析出Dirac色散带及弱色散的平带分支，与线性非相互作用模型吻合；并通过互补成像确认了键中心和位点中心模式轮廓。

Conclusion: 大面积WS₂单层结合开放微腔被确立为可扩展的极化激元能带工程平台，为在环境条件下探索合成量子材料提供了新途径。

Abstract: Large-area, optically homogeneous monolayer semiconductors are a critical prerequisite for scalable room-temperature polaritonics and for realizing polariton lattices extending across many unit cells. Yet, the small size, optical inhomogeneity, and device-to-device variability of conventional exfoliated flakes have remained major obstacles. Here, we overcome these limitations using 1-dodecanol-encapsulated WS$_2$ monolayers that combine millimeter-scale coverage with remarkably uniform optical properties over lateral distances approaching $300\,$$μ\mathrm{m}$. Integrated into a tunable open microcavity, these monolayers exhibit robust room-temperature exciton-photon strong coupling, evidenced by a pronounced anti-crossing and a Rabi splitting of $\hbar Ω_{\mathrm{R}} \approx 31\,\mathrm{meV}$. Leveraging the exceptional uniformity of this platform, we realize a two-dimensional polaritonic kagome lattice and directly resolve its characteristic band structure. Angle-resolved spectroscopy reveals Dirac dispersive bands together with a weakly dispersive flat-band-like branch within the $s$-band, in good agreement with a linear non-interacting model. Complementary momentum- and real-space imaging further identifies the associated bond-centered and site-centered mode profiles. These results establish large-area WS$_2$ monolayers in open microcavities as a scalable platform for engineering polariton band structures and exploring synthetic quantum materials under ambient conditions.

</details>


### [244] [Metal-Insulator Coexistence and Gap-Crossing Domain-Wall Modes in an Aubry-André Model with Nonlocal Hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07704)
*Xiarui Zhan,Mingsheng Tian,Qiongyi He,Kaiye Shi,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种具有系统尺度跳跃（非局域跳跃）的扩展Aubry-André模型中的粒子输运，揭示了实空间中金属-绝缘体共存的新奇量子相，以及具有梳状空间轮廓的畴壁模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡输运是现代物理学的核心主题，涵盖凝聚态物理到人工合成系统。理解非局域跳跃系统中局域化、非局域性与输运之间的丰富相互作用是本研究的主要动机。

Method: 采用具有系统尺度跳跃（跳跃范围与系统尺寸成正比）的扩展Aubry-André模型，在实空间中研究粒子输运行为，分析了有无准周期势条件下粒子的局域化与输运特性。

Result: 发现了实空间中金属-绝缘体共存区域，其中金属域和绝缘域共存并具有尖锐的空间边界。绝缘区域在无准周期势时呈现平带式局域化，在准周期势下呈现不同于传统指数局域化的多点局域化；同时粒子可在分离的金属域间自由传播。此外还发现了梳状空间轮廓的间隙跨越畴壁模式。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了非局域跳跃系统中局域化、非局域性与输运之间丰富的相互作用，为理解非平衡输运和新型局域化现象提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Nonequilibrium transport remains a central theme in modern physics, spanning from condensed matter to synthetic systems. Here, we investigate particle transport in an extended Aubry-André model with system-scale hopping, namely nonlocal hopping with a range proportional to the system size, and uncover a metal-insulator coexistence regime in real space, where metallic and insulating spatial domains coexist within the same system and are separated by sharp spatial boundaries. In the insulating region, particles exhibit flat-band-like localization in the absence of quasiperiodic potentials, while a quasiperiodic potential induces distinct multi-point localization, different from conventional exponential localization. Meanwhile, particles can freely propagate and tunnel across spatially disconnected metallic domains separated by the insulating region. Beyond this coexistence phase, we identify unconventional gap-crossing domain-wall modes with comb-like spatial profiles that mediate nonlocal, multi-point transport across separated metallic domains. Our findings reveal a rich interplay between localization, nonlocality, and transport in systems with nonlocal hopping.

</details>


### [245] [Creation and characterization of leviton excitations in tight-binding chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07820)
*Stephen R. McMillan,Thomas Frederiksen,Géza Giedke*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究有限一维紧束缚链中由洛伦兹电压脉冲驱动的leviton产生机制，发现仅在脉冲速度与晶格动力学匹配的中间参数区间才能形成干净的leviton态，并为评估晶格平台上的相干少电子输运建立了微观框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leviton是电子量子光学中的核心资源，但其产生与特性通常在连续散射框架中描述，而实际候选平台往往是有限、离散且受晶格色散强烈影响的系统。论文旨在弥合这一差距，研究leviton在有限离散晶格系统中的产生条件与质量退化机制。

Method: 采用单粒子密度矩阵方法，在有限一维紧束缚链上模拟非相互作用费米子受时变电压脉冲驱动的过程；通过计算平均激发数及其涨落来量化费米海之上激发的质量，并系统扫描脉冲时间尺度、系统尺寸、填充因子、脉冲幅度、电压降落几何等参数。

Result: 发现仅在中间参数区间（脉冲足够慢以被晶格动力学分辨，但又不能太慢以免截断和有限尺寸效应破坏洛伦兹轮廓）才能产生干净的leviton态；洛伦兹脉冲即使被截断也系统性优于非洛伦兹脉冲形状；进一步识别了与能带填充、脉冲幅度、线性电压降落几何及连续极限整数电荷条件残余偏差相关的有限晶格特征。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解超越理想连续极限的leviton形成提供了微观框架，并为评估晶格基平台上相干少电子输运的可行性奠定了基础。

Abstract: Levitons are minimal-excitation electronic wave packets generated by Lorentzian voltage pulses and constitute a central resource for electron quantum optics. Their creation and defining properties are usually formulated in continuum scattering descriptions, whereas many candidate platforms for integrated electronic quantum circuits are finite, discrete, and strongly shaped by lattice dispersion. We study leviton generation in finite one-dimensional tight-binding chains of non-interacting fermions driven by time-dependent voltage pulses. Using the single-particle density matrix, we resolve the excitation above the initial Fermi sea and quantify its quality through the average excitation number and its fluctuations. We find that clean leviton-like states emerge only in an intermediate regime where the pulse is slow enough to be resolved by the dynamics on the lattice, but not so slow that truncation and finite-size effects distort the Lorentzian profile in the time domain. Lorentzian pulses (even if truncated) systematically outperform non-Lorentzian pulse shapes in approaching the low-noise limit with increasing system size. We further identify finite-lattice signatures associated with band filling, pulse amplitude, linear voltage-drop geometry, and residual deviations from the continuum integer-charge condition. These results establish a microscopic framework for understanding leviton formation beyond the ideal continuum limit and for evaluating lattice-based platforms for coherent few-electron transport.

</details>


### [246] [Steady reservoir-mediated self-trapping of polariton condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07962)
*Pavel N. Kozhevin,Oleg I. Utesov,Igor S. Aranson,Sergey V. Koniakhin,Anton V. Nalitov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了激子-极化激元玻色凝聚体在紧聚焦非相干光泵浦下的自束缚态形成机制。研究表明，凝聚体对激发态储库的反作用通过玻色受激散射导致局部储库耗尽，从而形成自诱导势阱中的稳态凝聚体。该自束缚态由持续汇聚而非径向膨胀的极化激元流构成，甚至可在传统凝聚阈值以下存在，并在纳秒尺度上保持稳态后被子弹型不稳定排出，有望实现极化激元网络的脉冲神经形态动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统理论上紧聚焦非相干光束激发的激子-极化激元玻色凝聚体应形成静止的弹道膨胀态，但实验中观测到了紧凑的自束缚态，现有理论无法解释这一现象，需要新的物理机制来阐明其形成原理。

Method: 作者提出凝聚体通过玻色受激散射对光泵浦激子储库产生反作用（back-action），造成局部储库耗尽，从而形成自诱导的势阱将凝聚体限制其中。分析表明该自束缚态由持续汇聚的极化激子电流维持，并研究了其在纳秒尺度上的稳定性和后续的不稳定性演化。

Result: 该自束缚态即使低于传统定义的凝聚阈值也能存在，由汇聚而非膨胀的极化激子流支撑；状态在纳秒时间尺度上保持稳定，随后因子弹型不稳定性被从自诱导势阱中弹出，产生类脉冲行为。

Conclusion: 储库耗尽引起的自诱导势阱机制成功解释了实验观测的极化激元凝聚体自束缚态，且其子弹型不稳定特性可用于极化激元凝聚体网络中的脉冲神经形态计算。

Abstract: Nonequilibrium bosonic condensates of exciton-polaritons generated by tightly focused incoherent optical beams are typically expected to form a stationary ballistically expanding state with the notable exception of experimentally observed compact self-trapped states [Phys. Rev. Lett. 123, 047401 (2019)]. We show that the back-action of the condensate on the optically pumped excitonic reservoir via bosonic stimulated scattering can cause local reservoir depletion, producing a steady condensate confined in a self-induced potential trap. Notably, this self-trapped state is formed by persistent converging rather than radially expanding polariton currents and exists even below the conventionally defined condensation threshold. This state remains steady over nanosecond-scale times and is eventually ejected from the self-induced trap due to a bullet-type instability, enabling spiking neuromorphic dynamics in networks of polariton condensates.

</details>


### [247] [Nonlinear Topological Orbital Responses of Antiferromagnetic Skyrmions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08022)
*Amir N. Zarezad,Arne Brataas,Alireza Qaiumzadeh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文预测了在无自旋轨道耦合的PT对称六角反铁磁体中，孤立斯格明子会产生半经典非线性拓扑轨道响应，包括局部轨道霍尔电流修正和局部轨道累积，两者均与外加电场平方成正比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁斯格明子虽规避了斯格明子霍尔效应，但其传统拓扑电荷霍尔信号因补偿效应而受到抑制，因此需要寻找新的可探测的拓扑响应信号。

Method: 采用半经典理论框架，在扩散区、弱涌现场区域中分析自旋依赖的涌现洛伦兹力如何重塑载流子分布，计算局部轨道霍尔电流修正和局部轨道累积的二次电场响应。

Result: 电流修正需要自旋不对称的纵向散射；累积在自旋对称散射下仍存在。当自旋扩散长度远大于条带宽度时，电流修正仍存在而累积趋近于零。两种信号在电场反转下为偶函数，在斯格明子拓扑电荷Q反转下为奇函数，且与螺旋度无关。

Conclusion: 这些对称性质使得信号可被整流检测，并可将该机制与Q为偶对称的量子区拓扑轨道霍尔效应区分开来。

Abstract: Antiferromagnetic skyrmions evade the skyrmion Hall effect, but compensation suppresses their conventional topological charge Hall signal. We predict a semiclassical nonlinear topological orbital response of an isolated skyrmion in a $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric hexagonal antiferromagnet without spin-orbit coupling. In the diffusive, weak-emergent-field regime, the spin-dependent emergent Lorentz force reshapes the carrier distribution, generating a local orbital Hall-current correction and a local orbital accumulation, both quadratic in the applied electric field. The current correction requires spin-asymmetric longitudinal scattering, whereas the accumulation survives spin-symmetric scattering. When the spin-diffusion length greatly exceeds the strip width, the current correction persists while the accumulation approaches zero. Both signals are even under electric-field reversal, odd under reversal of the skyrmion topological charge $Q$, and helicity independent within this model. These symmetries enable rectified detection and distinguish this mechanism from the $Q$-even quantum-regime topological orbital Hall effect.

</details>


### [248] [Edge energy fluctuation: A unified theory of friction scaling laws in twisted layered material interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08063)
*Ze Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出边缘能量波动（EEF）概念，证明其决定了有限尺寸滑块的滑动能垒，并发现即使固定转角与滑动方向，滑块边缘切割方向不同也会产生显著摩擦各向异性及不同的摩擦标度定律，进而提出通过引入层级边缘实现扭曲层状材料界面摩擦工程的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华层状材料的层间扭转可产生莫尔超晶格，为调控电子与力学功能提供独特机遇，但有限尺寸滑块在扭转型界面中的滑动能垒机制尚不清楚，需要建立统一的物理图像并探索摩擦工程策略。

Method: 基于两个关键洞见（完整莫尔瓦片在滑动中势能不变，以及滑块边缘引起的对称破缺效应），提出边缘能量波动（EEF）概念，用于表征和预测有限尺寸滑块的滑动能量势垒。

Result: 研究发现即使在固定扭转角和滑动方向上，滑块边缘沿不同方向切割也会产生显著的摩擦各向异性，并导致不同的摩擦标度定律；进一步构建了通过引入层级边缘来调控扭转层状材料界面摩擦的工程范式。

Conclusion: EEF是控制扭转型界面中有限滑块滑动能垒的核心机制，边缘工程（尤其是层级边缘设计）为超滑器件中的摩擦控制提供了有前景的新途径。

Abstract: Interlayer twisting in van der Waals (vdW) layered materials generates moire superlattices, unlocking unique opportunities for engineering their electronic and mechanical functionalities. Here, building on two key insights - the invariance of the potential energy of complete moire tiles during sliding, and the symmetry-breaking effect induced by the slider edge - we propose the concept of edge energy fluctuation (EEF) and demonstrate that the EEF governs the sliding energy barrier of finite-sized sliders. Interestingly, we found that even at a fixed twist angle and sliding direction, significant frictional anisotropy emerges when the slider edge is cut along different directions, resulting in distinct friction scaling laws. Furthermore, we present a friction engineering paradigm for twisted layered material interfaces through the deliberate introduction of hierarchical edges, offering a promising route for friction control in superlubric devices.

</details>


### [249] [Electronic Orbital Angular Momentum Driven by Finite-Momentum Phonons: Beyond Chiral and Axial Phonons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08100)
*Yongho Park,Jeonghun Sohn,Sejoong Kim,Kyoung-Whan Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文证明有限动量声子即使在没有声子轴向性或手性的情况下也能产生电子轨道角动量（OAM），其响应由声子波矢q和频率ω控制。作者建立了规范场理论框架，通过幺正变换将声子位移吸收为作用于电子的涌现矢量势和标量势，推导了交流（AC）和直流（DC）响应的q-和ω-标度律。关键发现是：AC OAM在即使线偏振声子下也能产生，其大小和符号可由q调控；OAM的产生由声子几何与电子轨道织构之间的匹配所控制，而非仅由声子角动量决定；DC响应与电子Berry曲率的关联进一步支持声子角动量不是唯一的控制自由度。时间依赖紧束缚模拟独立证实了核心预测。该工作将声子驱动的OAM扩展到手性和轴向声子之外，并建立了可用表面声波实现的波矢可调轨道电子学路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为声子驱动的电子轨道角动量需要声子具备手性或轴向特性，但作者旨在突破这一限制，证明即使没有任何声子对称性（手性/轴向性），仅凭声子有限动量就能产生电子OAM，从而扩展轨道电子学的适用条件和物理机制。

Method: 作者发展了一套通用的规范场理论：通过幺正变换将声子位移场吸收为作用于电子的涌现矢量势和标量势，从而对OAM响应进行q和ω的微扰分类；推导交流与直流响应的标度律；并通过时间依赖紧束缚模拟（声学声子驱动下）对理论核心预测进行独立验证。

Result: 发现即使对线偏振声子，AC OAM响应依然存在，其大小和符号可通过波矢q调谐。OAM产生取决于声子几何与电子轨道织构的匹配而非声子本征角动量。DC响应与电子Berry曲率相连。紧束缚模拟独立确认了这些核心预测。

Conclusion: 该工作将声子驱动的OAM产生机制从手性/轴向声子拓展到一般声子情形，证明声子角动量并非电子OAM产生的唯一控制自由度，并提出了一种通过表面声波实现的、波矢可调的轨道电子学（orbitronics）新路径。

Abstract: We show that finite-momentum phonons generate electronic orbital angular momentum (OAM) even without phonon axiality or chirality, with the response controlled by the phonon wave vector $q$ and frequency $ω$. We develop a general gauge-field theory in which a unitary transformation absorbs the phonon displacement into emergent vector and scalar potentials acting on the electrons, providing a perturbative classification of the OAM response in $q$ and $ω$. We derive $q$- and $ω$-scaling laws for AC and DC responses. Notably, AC OAM arises even for linearly polarized phonons, with its magnitude and sign tunable by $q$. Its generation is governed by the matching between the phonon geometry and the electronic orbital texture rather than solely by the phonon angular momentum. The connection of the DC response to the electronic Berry curvature further supports that phonon angular momentum is not the only degree of freedom governing electronic OAM generation. Time-dependent tight-binding simulations under acoustic-phonon driving independently confirm the core predictions. Our results extend phonon-driven OAM beyond chiral and axial phonons and establish a wave-vector-tunable route to orbitronics, accessible with surface acoustic waves.

</details>


### [250] [Tunable topological narrow bands in twisted bilayer-trilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08104)
*Dong Wang,Federico Escudero,Zhen Zhan,Shengjun Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了四种堆叠构型（AB-ABC、BA-ABC、AB-ABA、BA-ABA）的双层-三层扭转石墨烯的低能能带结构与拓扑性质，利用紧束缚和连续模型揭示平带及其谷Chern数随扭转角、垂直电场和Hartree势的演化规律，并绘制了拓扑相图，为工程化可调控Chern数的拓扑平带提供了理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转多层石墨烯体系中，扭转角和堆叠方式对平带拓扑性质的影响尚不明确。理解堆叠、扭转角与电场之间的协同作用，对于实现具有可调Chern数的拓扑窄带以及探索分数Chern绝缘体等新奇量子态至关重要。

Method: 采用紧束缚模型和连续模型两种方法对四种堆叠构型的扭转双层-三层石墨烯进行计算，研究扭转角、垂直电场及自洽Hartree势作用下平带能带结构和谷Chern数的演化规律；通过隙闭合-重开过程识别拓扑相变，并在扭转角-电场参数空间中构建拓扑相图。

Result: 1) 两种模型在低能区吻合良好；2) 在较大扭转角和外电场下，平带发生拓扑相变，其总Chern数满足手性极限描述的求和规则；3) 平带与邻近远程带杂化时发生另一类拓扑相变，伴随隙闭合-重开过程及Chern数和电荷密度转移；4) 垂直电场是调控Chern带稳定性和相变的有效工具；5) Hartree势主要引起窄带的微弱带移与重塑，与电场结合时展现出丰富的拓扑相图。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了堆叠构型、扭转角与电场在调控扭转双层-三层石墨烯窄带及其拓扑性质中的相互作用机制，为在真实扭转多层石墨烯体系中工程化实现具有可调Chern数的拓扑窄带提供了重要理论依据和设计指导。

Abstract: We investigate the low-energy band structure and topology of twisted bilayer--trilayer graphene with four stacking configurations: AB--ABC, BA--ABC, AB--ABA, and BA--ABA. Using both tight-binding and continuum models, we first establish that the two approaches show good agreement in the band structure in low-energy regime. We then study the evolution of the flat bands and their valley Chern numbers as functions of twist angle, perpendicular electric field, and the self-consistent Hartree potential. At relatively large twist angles and under electric field, we find a topological transition between the narrow bands, with the total Chern number of the flat bands following the Chern number sum rules derived from the chiral-limit description. We also observe another type of topological transition when the flat bands hybridize with adjacent remote bands, where gap closing and reopening processes lead to Chern number and charge density transfer. By constructing topological phase diagrams in the space of twist angle and electric field, we show that the perpendicular electric field provides an efficient tuning knob for controlling the stability and transitions of the Chern bands. Finally, we find that the Hartree potential mainly induce weak band shifts and reshaping in the narrow bands. However, with a combination of Hartree potential and the electric fields, the narrow bands show rich topological phase diagram. Our results clarify the interplay between the stacking, twist angle and electric field in manipulating the narrow bands and their topology in twisted bilayer--trilayer graphene, and provide guidance for engineering topological narrow bands with tunable Chern numbers in realistic twisted multilayer graphene systems.

</details>


### [251] [The First Magic Angle Beyond the Chiral Limit in Twisted Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08347)
*Leonardo A. Navarro-Labastida,Pierre A. Pantaleon,Francisco Guinea,Gerardo G. Naumis*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了扭转双层石墨烯的平方哈密顿量描述，解释为何在晶格弛豫下第一魔角依然稳健而高阶魔角被强烈失稳。研究表明弛豫重塑了有效约束景观，使弛豫重正化隧穿比接近一个特殊约束点，此时对称约束势的振荡部分近乎抵消，使系统处于近均匀约束态；同时有限同子晶格隧穿激活了与手征轨道通道竞争的层间类电流通道。第一魔角因约束与类电流贡献保持平衡而幸存，高阶魔角因边带杂化更强及AA区域实空间局域化增强而失衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在超越手征极限的扭转双层石墨烯中，为何第一魔角在晶格弛豫下依然稳健，而高阶魔角被显著破坏，从而解释手征魔角层级结构崩溃的微观机制。

Method: 采用平方哈密顿量描述，从非手征Bistritzer–MacDonald模型出发（包含有限同子晶格隧穿），分析晶格弛豫对有效约束势的影响，揭示了弛豫重正化隧穿比接近某一特殊约束点，以及同子晶格隧穿所激活的额外层间类电流通道与手征轨道通道之间的竞争关系。

Result: 发现弛豫使系统处于近均匀约束区；第一魔角因约束贡献与类电流贡献平衡而得以保持，高阶魔角则因更强的远程边带杂化和围绕AA区域的实空间局域化增强而丧失该平衡，导致手征魔角层级整体崩溃。

Conclusion: 提供了一个单粒子机制来解释手征魔角层级的失稳，阐明了实验相关的第一魔角为何是手征平带结构中最稳定的残余特征，深化了对弛豫效应对魔角物理影响的理解。

Abstract: We develop a squared-Hamiltonian description of twisted bilayer graphene beyond the chiral limit to explain why the first magic angle remains robust under lattice relaxation, while higher-order magic angles are strongly destabilized. Starting from the non-chiral Bistritzer--MacDonald model with finite same-sublattice tunneling, we show that lattice relaxation reshapes the effective confinement landscape rather than acting as a simple perturbation of the chiral theory. A central result is that the realistic relaxation-renormalized tunneling ratio lies close to a special confinement point where the oscillatory part of the symmetric confinement potential nearly cancels. This places realistic twisted bilayer graphene near a nearly uniform confinement regime. At the same time, finite same-sublattice tunneling activates an additional inter-sublattice current-like channel that competes with the chiral orbital channel. The first magic angle survives because these confinement and current-like contributions remain balanced, whereas higher-order magic angles lose this balance through stronger remote-band hybridization and enhanced real-space localization around AA regions. Our results provide a single-particle mechanism for the breakdown of the chiral magic-angle hierarchy and clarify why the experimentally relevant first magic angle remains the most stable remnant of the chiral flat-band structure.

</details>


### [252] [From London to Morse via Binnig, Quate, and Gerber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08425)
*Sofia Alonso Perez,Matthew O. Blunt,Frederick Carlisle,Neil R. Champness,Janette L. Dunn,Matthew Edmondson,Rowan Evers,Connor Fields,Subhashis Gangopadhyay,James Hayton,Samuel P. Jarvis,Filipe Junqueira,Lev Kantorovich,Brian Kiraly,Natalio Krasnogor,Ioannis Lekkas,Morten Møller,Philip Moriarty,Chris Pakes,Emmanuelle Pauliac-Vaujour,Oliver Phillips,Adrian Radocea,Philipp Rahe,Mohammad Abdur Rashid,Hongqian Sang,Alex Saywell,Nikhil Seeja Sivakumar,Peter Sharp,Andrew Stannard,Julian Stirling,Adam Sweetman,Simon Taylor,Richard A. J. Woolley*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文是《原子力显微镜四十年》特刊中的一篇观点文章，回顾了诺丁汉大学课题组二十年来在AFM和STM领域的研究工作，核心主题是"探针作为主动参与者而非被动观察者"。研究覆盖从微观尺度到单化学键极限的力谱范围，包括范德华力/哈梅克力、氢键、共价键，以及通过垂直尖端-样品转移实现金属团簇的逐原子组装。文章还讨论了尖端诱导异质性对分子扩散首通动力学的影响，以及机器学习在自动化尖端驱动原子和分子操纵中的日益增长作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纪念Binnig、Quate和Gerber在1986年提出AFM的先驱性工作，响应他们在原始论文中预言的AFM将能够探测从弱范德华相互作用到强共价键的全谱系力相互作用。作者希望以自身课题组二十年研究成果为线索，系统展示探针作为"主动参与者"而非"被动观察者"这一核心视角如何贯穿从微观尺度到单化学键极限的力探测研究中，同时回应BQG关于尖端-样品力在STM中普遍存在且影响深远的洞见。

Method: 本文是综述性观点文章（perspective），回顾了诺丁汉大学课题组二十年来在原子力显微镜和扫描隧道显微镜领域的一系列实验研究。研究涉及的方法包括：探测从范德华力/哈梅克力到氢键再到共价键的力谱；通过垂直尖端-样品转移实现金属团簇的逐原子级组装；研究尖端诱导异质性对吸附分子扩散首通动力学的影响；以及探索机器学习在自动化尖端驱动原子/分子操纵中的应用。

Result: 研究系统地覆盖了从微观尺度到单化学键极限的力谱范围，包括范德华力/哈梅克力、氢键、共价键等不同类型的相互作用。此外，成功实现了通过垂直尖端-样品转移进行金属团簇的逐原子级组装，并揭示了尖端诱导异质性在分子扩散首通动力学中的关键作用，同时识别出获取不被探针扰动的非侵入性扩散势垒测量的挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作呼应了BQG关于探针-样品力在STM中普遍存在的原始观察，强调探针在纳米科学中不仅是测量工具，更是主动参与者。展望了机器学习在自动化尖端驱动的原子和分子操纵中的增长角色，预示着AFM技术从被动测量向主动操控的方向持续演进。

Abstract: In their landmark paper introducing the atomic force microscope [Phys. Rev. Lett. \textbf{56}, 930 (1986)], Binnig, Quate, and Gerber presciently anticipated that the technique would ultimately be capable of probing interactions running the gamut from weak van der Waals interactions to strong covalent bonding. They also highlighted that the tip-sample forces central to AFM are present, and often highly influential, in scanning tunnelling microscopy; indeed, this realisation directly inspired the invention of the force microscope. In this perspective for the \textit{Forty Years of AFM} special issue, we review selected aspects of two decades of work from our group at the University of Nottingham that span the force range highlighted by BQG and are united by a common, central theme: the probe as active participant rather than passive observer. Our selection of results also covers length- and correlation-scales from the microscopic right down to the single chemical bond limit, tracking a spectrum of interactions from van der Waals/Hamaker forces, through hydrogen bonding, to covalent bonds and, finally, atom-by-atom assembly of metal clusters via vertical tip-sample transfer. Echoing BQG's own observations on the prevalence of probe-sample forces in STM, we also discuss recent evidence that tip-induced heterogeneity underpins first-passage dynamics in molecular diffusion and highlight the challenges in acquiring non-invasive measurements of diffusion barriers for adsorbed molecules that are readily perturbed by the probe. We close with a perspective on machine learning's growing role in automating tip-driven atomic and molecular manipulation.

</details>


### [253] [Impurity as a probe of Berry curvature and wavefunction winding in gapped two-band models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08447)
*Reda Nabil,Pascal Simon,Andrej Mesaros*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文探索利用准粒子干涉来提取能带的非局域性质，证明在具有多谷的最小两带体系中，非磁性杂质诱导的局域态密度（LDOS）可以局域地探测各谷内的Berry曲率和波函数缠绕数。作者分析了Dirac、半Dirac、二次接触及更高阶接触等有能隙模型的点状杂质散射，发现足够强的杂质势使LDOS沿杂质周围等高线能明确显示波函数缠绕数，并建立了Berry曲率与缠绕数及色散参数之间的普适联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berry曲率和波函数缠绕数作为能带的重要拓扑/几何性质，通常难以通过局域谱学直接测量。本文动机在于探索是否可以利用近杂质准粒子干涉这一散射机制，通过光谱学（LDOS）手段间接、可观测地提取这些非局域能带性质，填补直接测量上的空白。

Method: 采用最小两带模型描述多谷体系，在点状非磁性杂质存在下，计算由散射诱导的LDOS。系统地分析Dirac、半Dirac、二次接触（quadratic touching）以及更高阶接触色散等有能隙模型，考察LDOS沿环绕杂质等高线的角向行为与径向振荡，从而提取波函数缠绕数并推导Berry曲率与缠绕数及色散参数间的解析关系。

Result: 表明在足够强的杂质势下，LDOS沿杂质周围等高线可以明确揭示波函数缠绕数；相反，在无隙的Dirac型色散中，缠绕信息可能被径向LDOS振荡掩盖。此外建立了谷内局域Berry曲率与波函数缠绕数及能量色散参数之间的一般性联系，使Berry曲率可从光谱LDOS信息中直接观测。

Conclusion: 非磁性杂质诱导的准粒子干涉是一种有效的光谱学探针，能够局域地提取多谷体系的波函数缠绕数与Berry曲率，为在实验上直接测量能带几何/拓扑性质提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We explore the possibility of using quasiparticle interference near impurities to extract non-local properties of bands. Focusing on the minimal two-band description of a system with multiple valleys, we show that the local density of states (LDOS) induced by non-magnetic impurities can effectively probe both the Berry curvature and the wavefunction winding, locally in each valley. We analyze gapped models based on Dirac, semi-Dirac, quadratic touching, and higher-order touching energy dispersions, in presence of a point-like potential scatterer. We find that for strong enough impurity potentials the LDOS along a contour around the impurity unambiguously shows the wavefunction winding number, in contrast to the case of gapless Dirac-like dispersions where the winding can be precluded by LDOS oscillations in the radial direction. We also establish a general connection between, on the one hand, the Berry curvature as a function of momentum locally in a valley, and on the other hand, the wavefunction winding number and the energy dispersion parameters, making hence the local Berry curvature directly observable from spectroscopic LDOS information.

</details>


### [254] [Memory Control of Ice Growth During Non-Equilibrium Freezing of Water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08535)
*Abhigyan Hazarika,Sudeep N. Punnathanam,Biman Bagchi,Prabal K. Maiti*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过TIP4P/Ice模型的分子动力学模拟，研究过冷水快速淬火至250 K时初始温度Ti对结晶动力学的影响，发现冻结时间tF对Ti呈非单调依赖，~300 K时结晶最慢，即初始更热的水可能比更冷的水冻结更快——这是Mpemba效应的分子尺度类比。非平稳广义朗之万方程框架表明双时记忆核保留系统热历史信息并直接影响结晶动力学，结构分析揭示五元环作为动力学陷阱，环类型间的关联调控冰状构型的可及性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过冷水的冻结是非平衡经典问题，但热历史对结晶的影响仍不清楚。作者希望阐明初始温度（热历史）如何塑造快速淬火后的结晶行为，揭示其中的分子机制。

Method: 采用TIP4P/Ice水模型的分子动力学模拟，将不同初始温度Ti的水快速淬火至250 K，通过监测氢键环形结构的演化来追踪结晶过程；构建非平稳广义朗之万方程框架分析双时记忆核；通过环结构关联性分析揭示动力学陷阱和冰状构型的调控机制。

Result: 发现冻结时间tF对初始温度Ti呈现非单调依赖，在约300 K处结晶最慢；初始更热的水可比较冷的水冻结更快，呈现分子尺度的Mpemba效应模拟。非平稳GLE框架显示双时记忆核保留热历史信息并直接调控结晶动力学；结构分析表明五元环作为动力学陷阱，环间的关联性调控冰状构型的可达性。

Conclusion: 研究确立了结构记忆（特别是氢键环形结构及其关联）作为非平衡相变的关键驱动力，揭示记忆驱动的冻结行为的分子起源，为理解Mpemba效应和热历史对结晶动力学的影响提供了微观尺度解释。

Abstract: Freezing of supercooled water is a classic non-equilibrium problem, yet the influence of thermal history on crystallization remains unclear. Using molecular dynamics simulations with the TIP4P/Ice model, we investigate how the initial temperature $T_i$ shapes freezing following rapid quenching to 250 K. By monitoring the evolution of hydrogen-bonded ring structures, we find a non-monotonic dependence of the freezing time $t_F$ on $T_i$, with the slowest crystallization occurring near 300 K. Remarkably, this means that initially hotter water can freeze faster than cooler water, a molecular-scale analogue of the Mpemba effect. A non-stationary generalized Langevin equation framework shows that two-time memory kernels retain information about the system's thermal past, directly influencing crystallization dynamics. Structural analysis further reveals that five-membered rings act as kinetic traps, while correlations among ring types regulate the accessibility of ice-like motifs. These results uncover a molecular origin of memory-driven freezing and establish structural memory as a key driver of non-equilibrium phase transitions.

</details>


### [255] [Equivalent Circuit Representation and Thermodynamic Limitations of Non-Adiabatic Spin-Transfer Torque effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08544)
*Wataru Koshibae*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了Thiele方程的等效电路表示，用于描述电流驱动畴壁动力学。研究发现非绝热自旋转移矩（β项）无法仅用无源元件表示，必须引入对应于负耗散的有源元件，这从热力学角度揭示了β项与驱动电流作为唯一功率源假设之间的根本矛盾，表明仅通过β项解释实验结果不能完全捕捉底层物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究电流驱动畴壁动力学中非绝热自旋转移矩（β项）的物理本质，通过等效电路方法分析其热力学一致性，以解释实验现象与理论之间的差距。

Method: 将Thiele方程映射为等效电路表示，分析β项在电路中的元件类型（无源vs有源），并基于热力学原理检验其自洽性。

Result: β项不能由无源元件表示，需要负耗散的有源元件；在驱动电流作为唯一功率源的假设下，β项的引入违反了热力学原理。

Conclusion: 仅通过β项解释实验不完整，需考虑与自旋动力学不同的其他效应，为理解畴壁动力学提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We present an equivalent circuit representation of the Thiele equation for the current-driven domain walls. We show that the non-adiabatic spin-transfer torque (β-term) cannot be represented by passive elements alone (such as resistors, inductors, and capacitors) and necessarily requires active elements, corresponding to negative dissipation. Consequently, assuming that the driving current is the only power source for the texture dynamics, directly incorporating the β-term introduces a fundamental contradiction with thermodynamic principles. This equivalent circuit theory indicates that the interpretation of experiments solely through the β-term does not fully capture the underlying physics, highlighting the need to account for effects distinct from the spin dynamics.

</details>


### [256] [Wafer-Level 1/f Noise Characterization of Scaled P-Type Nanosheets and Comparison with Planar HKMG Transistors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08674)
*Ruben Asanovski,Anabela Veloso,Ben Kaczer,Naoto Horiguchi,Jacopo Franco*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文对188个纳米片晶体管进行了晶圆级别的1/f噪声统计研究，并与大面积平面HKMG器件的噪声进行了基准对比，发现两者的有效陷阱密度相当，表明从平面架构转向GAA纳米片架构不会增加噪声，噪声仍由栅堆叠质量决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估从平面HKMG器件转移到GAA纳米片架构后，器件架构对可靠性的影响（特别是1/f噪声特性）是否会导致噪声恶化或陷阱密度变化。

Method: 对188个纳米片晶体管进行统计性1/f噪声数据分析，从噪声中提取介电层有效陷阱密度，与采用相同栅堆叠、相近热预算且未经专门可靠性退火的平面器件进行对比。

Result: 平面和纳米片两种架构中提取的有效陷阱密度相当，说明GAA纳米片架构的转变不会导致1/f噪声增加。

Conclusion: GAA纳米片架构的噪声性能仍由栅堆叠质量主导，架构转变本身不会引入额外的噪声退化。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive wafer-level 1/f noise study of scaled Gate-All-Around (GAA) p-type nanosheet transistors and benchmark them against large-area planar High- \k{appa} Metal Gate (HKMG) devices to assess the impact of device architecture on reliability. By statistically analyzing noise data from 188 nanosheets, we extract the effective trap density in the dielectric and compare it to that of planar transistors with identical gate stack, comparable processing thermal budgets, and no specific reliability anneals. The effective trap density extracted from 1/f noise is comparable in planar and nanosheet architectures. This indicates that transitioning to GAA nanosheets does not increase noise, which remains governed by gate stack quality.

</details>


### [257] [Observation of linear magnetoresistance and planar Hall effect in the Dirac semimetal PdTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08675)
*Bhawana Mehra,Bibek Ranjan Satapathy,V S Abhishek,Reena,Ritu Gupta,Yogesh Singh,S. Chakraverty,Amit Vashist*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文首次在高质量PdTe单晶中观测到线性磁电阻(LMR)和平面霍尔效应(PHE)，并通过详细分析证明两者均源于半经典输运机制（无序驱动的迁移率涨落和各向异性轨道磁电阻），而非拓扑机制（如Abrikosov量子极限或手性反常），强调在拓扑材料中区分拓扑输运与经典输运机制的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PdTe作为一种同时具有Dirac半金属性和超导性的材料，是探索拓扑超导和非常规磁输运现象的理想平台。虽然其超导性质已被广泛研究，但其详细的磁输运现象尚未被探索。作者希望通过研究LMR和PHE来揭示其输运机制的本质。

Method: 在高质量PdTe单晶中进行磁输运测量：(1)通过改变磁场方向分别测量面内(B∥I)和面外(B⊥I)构型下的磁电阻，观测到温度依赖的非饱和线性磁电阻及其在临界场Bc处由抛物线到线性的交叉；(2)通过面内旋转磁场观测平面霍尔效应，并对PHE振幅的场和温度依赖性及参数图进行详细分析。

Result: 观测到温度依赖的非饱和LMR（面内和面外构型），MR在临界场Bc处从抛物线依赖转变为线性依赖。详细分析表明LMR源于无序驱动的迁移率涨落而非Abrikosov量子极限机制。同时观测到显著的平面霍尔效应，其场和温度依赖性及参数图分析表明PHE主要源于各向异性轨道磁电阻而非手性反常。

Conclusion: 尽管PdTe中Dirac点接近费米能级，但观测到的LMR和PHE均可通过半经典输运框架理解。该工作强调了在拓扑材料中区分拓扑输运机制与传统经典输运机制的重要性。

Abstract: PdTe is a Dirac semimetal that also exhibits superconductivity, providing an intriguing platform to explore topological superconductivity and unconventional magnetotransport phenomena. While the superconducting properties of PdTe have been extensively studied in recent years, the detailed magnetotransport phenomena have remained unexplored. Here, we present the first observation of linear magnetoresistance (LMR) and the planar Hall effect (PHE) in a high-quality single crystal of PdTe. We observe temperature-dependent unsaturated LMR in both in-plane ($B \parallel I$) and out-of-plane ($B \perp I$) configurations. The magnetoresistance (MR) shows a crossover from parabolic to linear dependence at the critical field Bc, and detailed analysis indicates that disorder-driven mobility fluctuations are the origin rather than the Abrikosov quantum-limit mechanism. Furthermore, prominent PHE has been observed by rotating the magnetic field within the plane of the sample. The detailed analysis of the field and temperature dependence of PHE-amplitude, along with the parametric plot, suggests that PHE originates predominantly from the anisotropic orbital magnetoresistance rather than the chiral anomaly. Our results demonstrate that although the Dirac point is close to the Fermi level, the observed LMR and PHE in the Dirac semimetal PdTe can be understood within a semiclassical transport framework, highlighting the importance of distinguishing between topological and conventional classical transport mechanisms in topological materials

</details>


### [258] [Decorated electronic kagome lattice in twisted bilayer germanene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08750)
*Dennis J. Klaassen,Rian A. M. Ligthart,Andrés R. Botello Mendez,Lumen Eek,Esra van 't Westende,Paul L. de Boeij,Y. Wang,D. Vanmaekelbergh,Pantelis Bampoulis,Cristiane Morais Smith,Ingmar Swart,Zeila Zanolli,Harold J. W. Zandvliet*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究展示了在Ge2Pt衬底上的共度扭曲双层锗烯可产生两种电子性质截然不同的大角度莫尔相：奇宇称（sublattice交换下）为半导体，偶宇称为金属，且偶宇称相表现出具有C3对称性的装饰Kagome电子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工Kagome晶格可提供电子平带、几何阻挫和关联驱动的相，但在原子级可控的二维材料中实现仍然稀缺，亟需探索新型二维平台来实现Kagome电子态。

Method: 通过扫描隧道显微镜（STM）测量与密度泛函理论（DFT）计算相结合，研究Ge2Pt衬底上共度扭曲双层锗烯的两种大角度莫尔相，并分析其sublattice交换对称性（奇偶宇称）与电子结构的关系。

Result: 研究发现sublattice交换下奇宇称的共度扭曲双层为半导体，偶宇称的为金属；偶宇称扭曲双层中存在一个空态共振，展现出具有C3对称性的装饰Kagome结构。

Conclusion: 大角度扭曲锗烯可作为在翘曲二维材料中工程化Kagome类电子态的平台，为探索几何阻挫与关联物理提供了新材料体系。

Abstract: Artificial kagome lattices provide a route to electronic flat bands, geometric frustration, and correlation driven phases, but their realization in atomically controlled two-dimensional materials remains scarce. Here, we show that commensurate twisted bilayer germanene on Ge2Pt produces two electronically distinct large-angle moiré phases. Scanning tunneling microscopy measurements and density functional theory calculations reveal that commensurate twisted bilayers that are odd under an exchange of sublattices are semiconducting, whereas the twisted bilayers with an even parity are metallic. The twisted bilayers with an even parity host an empty state resonance that exhibits an emergent decorated kagome structure with a C3 symmetry. These results establish large-angle twisted germanene as a platform for engineering kagome-like electronic states in a buckled two-dimensional material.

</details>


### [259] [Non-perturbative theory of valley splitting in Si qubits from variational wave function: periodic effects of shear strain and asymptotic freedom in the wiggle-well potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08839)
*Johannes L. P. Steinschuld,Hendrik J. Bluhm,Seyed Akbar Jafari*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文基于谷轨道基（valleyor basis）构建了有限量子阱中谷间耦合的两带有效质量模型，提出了处理剪切应变和摆动阱势非微扰效应的解析框架。利用变分法得到均匀剪切应变下两最低态能量及能隙的闭式表达式，与精确对角化误差约1%；对摆动阱耦合，揭示了近共振区从摆动主导劈裂到轨道量子化的转变，表现类似自旋量子比特的渐近自由行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅量子阱量子比特中谷劈裂决定了自旋与谷自由度的杂化能量尺度，但其对界面结构的敏感性使其难以预测，需要开发能够处理非微扰效应的解析理论框架。

Method: 采用谷轨道基（valleyors），将硅量子阱问题表述为两带有效质量模型。对均匀剪切应变（V_s τ_2耦合），利用精确平面波谷轨道与硬壁包络构造变分态，推导出两最低态及其劈裂的闭式解析表达式。对摆动阱耦合（V_w cos(k_w z) τ_1），通过谷轨道表示分析共振附近的物理行为和状态转变。

Result: 变分结果与精确对角化在真实耦合参数下一致（误差约1%），揭示了|sin(k_min L)|振荡依赖关系；有限势垒高度主要重整化量子阱有效宽度。摆动阱耦合下系统在k_w=2k_1共振附近发生从摆动主导劈裂到轨道量子化的转变；高摆动振幅下，空箱（empty-box）能量尺度主导近共振谷劈裂，摆动阱仅为次领头修正，呈现类似渐近自由的行为；失谐时劈裂呈现宽峰共振结构并伴有类衍射旁瓣。

Conclusion: 该解析框架为预测和调控硅量子阱量子比特的谷劈裂提供了实用工具，包括通过调节剪切应变远离节点来增强谷劈裂的简单处方，并揭示了摆动阱势下新颖的物理行为（渐近自由及衍射旁瓣）及其对理解谷劈裂物理机制的重要意义。

Abstract: Valley splitting sets the energy scale at which spin and valley degrees of freedom hybridize in silicon quantum-well qubits, but its sensitivity to interface structure makes it difficult to predict. Using the valleyor basis, we formulate a two-band effective-mass model for intervalley coupling in a finite quantum well and develop an analytical framework for treating non-perturbative effects of shear strain and wiggle-well potentials. For a $z$-independent coupling $V_sτ_2$, corresponding to uniform shear strain, we construct a variational state from the exact plane-wave valleyors and a hard-wall envelope. This yields closed-form expressions for the two lowest states and their splitting, including the characteristic oscillatory $|\sin(k_{\rm min}L)|$ dependence. The variational result agrees with exact diagonalization to within $\sim1\%$ for realistic couplings, and provides a simple prescription for tuning the shear strain away from the nodes to enhance the valley splitting. We further show that finite barrier heights primarily renormalize the effective width of the quantum well while preserving the oscillatory dependence. For a wiggle-well coupling $V_w\cos(k_wz)τ_1$, the valleyor representation reveals a crossover near the $k_w=2k_1$ resonance from wiggle-dominated splitting to orbital quantization. Remarkably, at high wiggle-well amplitudes, the empty-box energy scale predominantly dictates the near-resonant valley splitting, while the wiggle-well potential enters only as a sub-leading correction. This behavior can be viewed as asymptotic freedom for spin qubits. Away from resonance, the splitting exhibits a broadly peaked resonance with diffraction-like side-lobes as a function of detuning.

</details>


### [260] [Probing Antialtermagnetism via Orbital-Field-Induced Spin Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08996)
*Zi-Ting Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用面内磁场的轨道效应将反交替磁体中隐藏在自旋简并能谱背后的自旋极化转化为可观测的自旋劈裂，从而实现无需层分辨即可识别隐藏磁有序参数的通用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在反交替磁体中，层间补偿可以将自旋极化隐藏在自旋简并的体能谱背后，阻碍了光谱学手段对底层磁有序的识别。研究者希望找到一种方法恢复光谱可及性，无需层分辨即可鉴定隐藏的交替磁性有序参数。

Method: 利用面内磁场的轨道效应，将隐藏的自旋织构转化为具有相反动量宇称的可观测自旋劈裂。一旦确定隐藏交换有序的宇称，通过弱场区域内的互补响应函数进一步分辨其波特征：在最小模型中，通过能带劈裂的带求和规则提取形状因子的动量梯度以区分隐藏偶宇称有序；对于奇宇称扇区，感应净自旋极化展现出特征性的场强与角度依赖关系。

Result: 确立了轨道场诱导的自旋劈裂作为识别隐藏交替磁有序参数的通用路径，能够在无需层分辨的条件下区分不同宇称和波特征的隐藏有序态。

Conclusion: 轨道效应使隐藏的自旋织构得以被光谱学观测，为反交替磁体的磁有序鉴定提供了一条无需层分辨的通用路线，拓展了对交替磁体材料表征的能力。

Abstract: Layer compensation can conceal spin polarization behind a spin-degenerate bulk spectrum in antialtermagnets, preventing spectroscopic identification of the underlying magnetic order. Here we show that the orbital effect of an in-plane magnetic field converts the hidden spin texture into an observable spin splitting of opposite momentum parity, thereby restoring spectroscopic access. Once the parity of the hidden exchange order is determined, complementary response functions in the weak-field regime further resolve its wave character: within a minimal model, we illustrate that a band sum rule over the induced band splittings isolates the momentum gradient of the form factor and differentiates hidden even-parity orders, whereas for odd-parity sectors the induced net spin polarization exhibits characteristic field-amplitude and angular dependences. These results establish orbital-field-induced spin splitting as a generic route to identifying the hidden altermagnetic order parameter without layer resolution.

</details>


### [261] [Hilbert space connectivity in non-Hermitian many-body systems: emergent scale-dependent amplification and constraint-induced skin localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09017)
*Zichang Hao,Wen-Tan Xue,Ching Hua Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了非厄米多体系统中希尔伯特空间连通性对谱放大和态局域化的普遍影响，发现希尔伯特空间图的结构（包括竞争性放大通道和全局反馈回路）决定了非常规的放大标度行为和局域化性质，粒子占据约束可移除特定希尔伯特空间路径，从而在原本互易的过程中产生稳健的单极和不对称双极趋肤效应局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子疤痕、分形子等奇异多体现象已被证明与希尔伯特空间碎裂相关，但之前在非厄米框架下，希尔伯特空间连通性作为多体物理的一个更普适、更基础的控制因素尚未被系统研究。相比实空间晶格，非厄米多体希尔伯特空间图具有更复杂的结构（竞争性放大通道和全局反馈回路），其影响需要深入理解。

Method: 本文通过分析非厄米多体系统的希尔伯特空间图结构，研究其连通性与谱放大和态局域化之间的关系。关注了超越简单实空间晶格的复杂图特征，包括多个竞争的放大通道以及由粒子对称性连接远程Fock态的全局反馈回路，并考察粒子占据约束对希尔伯特空间路径选择的影响。

Result: 发现了非厄米多体系统中具有非常规标度行为的放大现象及独特的局域化性质；粒子占据约束能够移除特定的希尔伯特空间路径，从而在互易过程中产生稳健的单极和不对称双极趋肤局域化。结果不仅适用于相互作用的玻色子模型，还具有更广泛的普适性。

Conclusion: 希尔伯特空间连通性可作为多体非厄米临界转变的多功能控制手段，拓展了该领域的理解边界，为设计非厄米多体系统中的奇异现象提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Various exotic many-body phenomena such as quantum scars and fractons have been linked to Hilbert space fragmentation. In this work, we find that in non-Hermitian settings, Hilbert space connectivity has an even more universal and fundamental influence, tightly controlling the nature of spectral amplification and state localization. Far more complicated than real-space lattices, non-Hermitian many-body Hilbert space graphs not only possess intricate competing amplification channels, but also global feedback loops connecting remote Fock states related by particle symmetry. These features lead to amplification behavior with unconventional scaling and localization properties. Particle occupation constraints can furthermore remove selected Hilbert space pathways, leading to robust unipolar and asymmetric bipolar skin localization in otherwise reciprocal processes. These results extend beyond simple interacting bosonic models and establish Hilbert space connectivity as a versatile control knob for many-body non-Hermitian critical transitions.

</details>
