# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [262] [A Heterogeneous General Model for Neuromorphic-Inspired Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05482)
*Matteo Mirigliano*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于输入依赖的随机权重网络（称为"基底"）的通用计算模型，以替代传统训练方法。基底权重通过输入触发的随机更新演化，权重系数间的相关性由矩阵值协方差核描述，并采用二次多项式权重函数实现。数值模拟表明随机权重演化中的相关性显著影响系统响应，为无传统权重训练的神经形态计算提供了潜在机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术界和工业界近年来致力于开发受生物神经系统分布式、自适应和事件驱动特性启发的计算架构，以降低传统训练方法的计算成本。然而，新兴计算系统和硬件缺乏通用模型与设计准则，这是当前面临的主要挑战。

Method: 提出一种通用模型，基于输入依赖的随机权重网络（基底）。基底权重通过输入触发的随机更新进行演化，权重系数间的相关性由矩阵值协方差核描述。采用二次多项式权重函数实现该框架，其中输入振幅控制随机扰动的幅度，基底依赖的距离决定相关结构。

Result: 数值模拟表明，随机权重演化中的相关性对系统响应有显著影响，提示这可能是无需传统权重训练的神经形态计算的一种潜在机制。

Conclusion: 本文旨在为该模型提供通用表述并识别其主要特性和特征，为新兴神经形态计算系统的设计提供理论基础。

Abstract: In recent years, both academia and industry have focused on the development of computational architectures inspired by the distributed, adaptive, and event-driven characteristics of biological neural systems, with the aim of reducing the computational cost associated with conventional training approaches [1]. However, a major challenge is the lack of general models and design guidelines for emerging computational systems and hardware. This work introduces a general model based on an input-dependent stochastic weight network, referred to as a substrate. The substrate weights evolve through input-triggered stochastic updates, with correlations between weight coefficients described by a matrix-valued covariance kernel. The proposed framework is implemented using quadratic polynomial weight functions, where the input amplitude controls the magnitude of the stochastic perturbation and a substrate-dependent distance determines the correlation structure. Numerical simulations show that correlations in the stochastic weight evolution significantly affect the system response, suggesting a potential mechanism for neuromorphic-inspired computation without conventional weight training. The aim of this work is to provide a general formulation of the model and identify its main properties and characteristics. 1 H. Jaeger, Towards a generalized theory comprising digital, neuromorphic and unconventional computing, Neuromorphic Comput. Eng., vol. 1, no. 1, p. 012002, Sep. 2021

</details>


### [263] [Autonomous Chaotic Time Series Prediction using Physical Neuromorphic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06395)
*Akshaya Rajesh,Yinhao Xu,Wave Ngampruetikorn,Zdenka Kuncic*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本研究通过模拟神经形态纳米线网络作为物理储层，实现了对Mackey-Glass混沌时间序列的完全自主闭环预测。在τ=18和τ=21两种时间延迟参数下，虚拟节点（VN）方法在T=100步预测中分别达到90.4%和89.7%的精度，而非虚拟节点方法分别达到81.5%和76.2%。长时程T=500步分析表明两种方法均能再现真实信号的主要吸引子结构和频谱特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储层计算（PRC）利用物理神经网络的涌现非线性动力学作为计算资源，是实现脑启发信息处理的有前景途径。本文旨在验证神经形态纳米线网络的固有动力学是否足以支持无需虚拟节点增强的自主混沌时间序列预测，同时探索其在不同复杂度混沌体系下的预测能力。

Method: 采用模拟的神经形态纳米线网络作为物理储层进行混沌时间序列预测。评估两种策略：（1）虚拟节点（VN）方法，通过时间复用扩展储层状态的特征空间；（2）非VN方法，直接使用所有物理节点读出不进行时间复用。对MG时间延迟参数τ=18和τ=21两种设置进行测试，后者代表更复杂的混沌体系。

Result: 短预测水平T=100时，VN方法在τ=18和τ=21分别达到90.4%和89.7%的自主预测精度，非VN方法分别为81.5%和76.2%。长时程T=500分析中，两种方法均能重建真实MG信号的定性吸引子结构和主导频谱内容，轨迹始终保持有界。VN方法在更复杂混沌体系（τ=21）中仅轻微下降0.7%，而非VN方法下降5.3%。

Conclusion: 神经形态纳米线网络的固有动力学足以支持有意义的自主混沌时间序列预测，无需虚拟节点增强。随着物理网络规模扩展至硬件中可实现的上百万节点，预测性能有望进一步提升。由于本研究基于模拟网络，结果外推至物理制造的大规模阵列仍需实验验证。

Abstract: Physical reservoir computing (PRC) with neuromorphic networks offers a promising approach to brain-inspired information processing, exploiting emergent nonlinear dynamics of physical neural networks as a computational resource. This study demonstrates fully autonomous closed-loop prediction of the Mackey--Glass (MG) chaotic time series using a simulated neuromorphic nanowire network as the physical reservoir. Two strategies are evaluated: the virtual node (VN) method, which expands the feature space by temporal multiplexing of reservoir states, and a non-VN approach that uses all physical node readouts directly without temporal multiplexing. Results are reported for two values of the MG time delay parameter, $τ= 18$ and $τ= 21$, the latter representing a more complex chaotic regime not previously evaluated for this class of physical reservoir. Over a short prediction horizon of $T = 100$ timesteps, the VN approach achieves autonomous prediction accuracies of $90.4$% and $89.7$% at $τ= 18$ and $τ= 21$, respectively, while the non-VN approach achieves $81.5$% and $76.2$%. Long-horizon analysis over $T = 500$ timesteps shows that both approaches reproduce the qualitative attractor structure and dominant spectral content of the true MG signal, with trajectories remaining bounded throughout. These results suggest that the intrinsic dynamics of neuromorphic nanowire networks are sufficient to support meaningful autonomous chaotic time series prediction without virtual node augmentation, and that performance may improve further as physical network sizes scale to the millions of nodes achievable in hardware. As this study uses simulated networks, extrapolation to physically fabricated large-scale arrays remains to be validated experimentally.

</details>


### [264] [Finite-rank multiplicative perturbations of rotationally invariant non-Hermitian random matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07220)
*Pierre Bousseyroux,Marc Potters*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究旋转不变非厄米随机矩阵的有限秩乘法形变模型 A(I+T)，刻画了离群特征值的涌现、涨落及特征向量重叠，提供了经典BBP框架的乘法非厄米对应版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典BBP理论针对加法微扰，而乘法微扰在非厄米随机矩阵背景下尚未被系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，为乘法形变下的离群特征值行为建立完整理论。

Method: 研究模型A(I+T)，其中A为大尺寸旋转不变非厄米随机矩阵，T为有限秩正规微扰。通过分析离群特征值的涌现条件、涨落性质及特征向量重叠来刻画模型行为。

Result: 完整刻画了乘法形变下离群特征值的涌现机制、统计涨落以及对应的特征向量重叠结构，建立了系统的理论框架。

Conclusion: 该结果为BBP理论在乘法非厄米设定下提供了对应版本，拓展了随机矩阵理论中微扰影响的研究范围。

Abstract: We study finite-rank multiplicative deformations of rotationally invariant non-Hermitian random matrices. More precisely, we consider models of the form $\mathbf{A}(\mathbf{I}+\mathbf{T})$, where $\mathbf{A}$ is a large rotationally invariant non-Hermitian random matrix, $\mathbf{T}$ is a finite-rank normal perturbation, and $\mathbf{I}$ denotes the identity matrix. We characterize the emergence of outlier eigenvalues, their fluctuations, and the associated eigenvector overlaps. Our results provide a multiplicative non-Hermitian counterpart to the classical Baik--Ben Arous--Péché framework.

</details>


### [265] [Storing Infinite Dynamical Attractors in Nonreciprocal Associative Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07341)
*Miguel Aguilera,Daniele De Martino*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种针对非互惠联想网络的动力学平均场理论，该网络可存储从极限环到奇异吸引子的多个动力学吸引子，并通过路径积分方法推导出自洽的动力学平均场方程，揭示了特征值退相干如何决定记忆存储能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人工作主要关注固定点吸引子的联想记忆存储，而对存储大量动力学吸引子（如极限环、混沌吸引子）的非互惠网络的存储能力与机制缺乏系统的理论框架。作者希望从谱结构出发理解动态记忆的检索容量边界及其放大机制。

Method: 采用淬火无序下的路径积分计算，推导出模式重叠、自相关和响应函数的自洽动力学平均场方程。通过对耦合矩阵特征值谱结构（相干对齐vs均匀分布）的分析，研究阻滞自相互作用与淬火噪声之间的反馈交互如何影响检索容量。

Result: 当特征值相干对齐时，零本征相位下恢复经典平衡容量界，而极限环吸引子的检索容量远低于此界限；当特征值均匀分布时，阻滞自相互作用与大部分淬火噪声相互抵消，动力学退化为有效单自旋过程，容量大幅提升。微观模拟验证了该理论对极限环和混沌吸引子的预测。

Conclusion: 特征值退相干是增强动力学记忆存储的关键机制：通过破坏特征值相干性，可使阻滞反馈与噪声大幅抵消，从而将复杂的多体动力学约化为有效单自旋问题，显著提升对极限环和混沌等动力学吸引子的存储容量。

Abstract: We develop a dynamical mean-field theory for nonreciprocal associative networks that store an extensive number of dynamical attractors, from limit cycles to strange attractors. Using a path integral calculation under quenched disorder, we derive self-consistent dynamical mean-field equations for pattern overlaps, autocorrelations and response functions. Memory retrieval capacity is governed by the spectral structure of the coupling matrices encoding stored patterns. When their eigenvalues are coherently aligned, retarded self-interactions and quenched noise feed back destructively: at zero eigenphase (fixed point attractors) the classical equilibrium capacity bound is recovered, while for limit cycles retrieval collapses far below it. In contrast, for uniformly distributed eigenphases, retarded self-interactions and much of the quenched noise cancels, reducing the dynamics to an effective single-spin process and amplifying capacity substantially. We validate the theory against microscopic simulations for limit-cycle and chaotic attractors, identifying eigenvalue decoherence as the mechanism enabling enhanced storage of dynamical memories.

</details>


### [266] [Graph neural networks and the energetic cavity method for combinatorial optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07456)
*Joe Bacchus George,George T. Cantwell*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究使用图神经网络（GNN）求解Ising模型的近似基态。作者发现未经修改的GNN性能劣于两种基于平均场近似的经典启发式方法（主特征向量方法和min-sum算法/能量腔方法）。通过对GNN进行小幅修改以融入这些启发式思想，性能得到显著提升。虽然改进后的方法在深度学习同类方法中具有竞争力，但模拟退火在相同计算代价下始终至少与深度学习方法表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解Ising模型的基态具有重要意义，因为许多组合优化问题可以转化为Ising模型。虽然精确求解困难，但存在许多有效启发式方法。作者希望探索GNN在这一问题上的潜力，并解决其性能落后于经典启发式方法的问题。

Method: 作者首先将原始的GNN与两种基于平均场的启发式方法（利用适当定义矩阵的主特征向量方法，以及min-sum算法/能量腔方法）进行性能对比。在此基础上，对GNN进行小型架构修改，将上述启发式的关键思想融入GNN设计中，以提升其求解Ising模型基态的能力。

Result: 未经修改的GNN在Ising模型基态求解上表现劣于两种经典启发式方法；经过小幅修改融入启发式信息后，GNN性能显著提升，在深度学习类方法中具有竞争力；然而，模拟退火在相同计算代价下始终至少与深度学习方法一样好。

Conclusion: 虽然将经典启发式方法融入GNN可以提升其求解Ising模型基态的性能，但在计算成本相当的情况下，深度学习方法并未超越传统的模拟退火算法，后者仍然是可靠且具有竞争力的基线方法。

Abstract: We study the use of graph neural networks (GNNs) for finding approximate ground states of Ising models. Efficiently finding these ground states is of broad significance because many combinatorial optimization problems can be formulated as an Ising model with the appropriate choice of couplings and fields. Exactly solving these problems is hard but there are many good heuristic methods. A lineage of these heuristics build from mean-field approximations: one approach uses the leading eigenvector of an appropriately defined matrix, another is the min-sum algorithm, also known as the energetic cavity method. Without modification, GNNs perform worse than both of these methods. We consider small modifications to the GNN to incorporate these heuristics and find that this considerably improves performance. While the modified approach is competitive against other deep-learning approaches, we still find that simulated annealing is reliably at least as good as deep learning methods for the same computational cost.

</details>


### [267] [A Bifurcation Theory for the Equilibria of Modern Hopfield Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07757)
*Vincenzo Maria Schimmenti,Matteo Ciarchi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文为一般凸对偶现代Hopfield网络建立了定点稳定性与分岔判据的通用理论框架，适用于任意存储模式统计分布，并系统展示了记忆相关性如何通过连续分岔组织层级吸引子的涌现，且在MNIST模式检索和造血细胞层级组织中定量验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Hopfield网络统一了联想记忆、Transformer注意力、扩散生成模型与生物吸引子动力学，其动力学由能量景观的组织及定点分岔决定。然而，除特定架构和理想化模式集合外，缺乏通用的分岔理论，限制了对其计算与生物行为的统一理解。

Method: 作者针对一般凸对偶现代Hopfield网络的定点，在存储模式的一般统计假设下推导了稳定性与分岔判据。该框架被应用于随机、块相关及无限层级模式集合，分析记忆相关性如何通过逐次分岔系统性地组织层级吸引子的涌现。理论预测进一步通过MNIST存储模式下的检索分岔实验及造血细胞身份层级重构得到定量验证。

Result: 推导出一般凸对偶现代Hopfield网络的定点稳定性与分岔判据，适用于一般模式统计；在随机、块相关和无限层级模式集合下揭示了记忆相关性如何通过逐次分岔驱动层级吸引子的涌现；理论定量描述了MNIST模式检索分岔，并重现了造血细胞身份的层级组织。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了现代Hopfield网络的通用分岔理论，将定点组织的涌现确立为其计算与生物行为背后的统一原则，弥合了不同架构与模式集合之间的理论鸿沟。

Abstract: Modern Hopfield networks provide a unifying framework for associative memory, transformer attention, diffusion-based generative models, and biological attractor dynamics, linking these systems through a common energy-based dynamics in which states are updated toward weighted combinations of stored patterns. Across these settings, network dynamics is determined by the organization of the energy landscape and the bifurcations of its fixed points. Despite their central role, a general theory of these bifurcations has remained unavailable beyond specific architectures and idealized pattern ensembles. Here we derive stability and bifurcation criteria for the fixed points of general convex-dual Modern Hopfield networks for a general statistics of the stored patterns. Applying this framework to random, block-correlated, and infinitely hierarchical pattern ensembles, we show how memory correlations systematically organize the emergence of hierarchical attractors through successive bifurcations. We further demonstrate that these predictions quantitatively describe retrieval bifurcations in MHNs storing patterns sampled from MNIST, and recapitulate the hierarchical organization of hematopoietic cell identities. Our results establish a general bifurcation theory for Modern Hopfield networks and identify the organization of fixed points as a unifying principle underlying their computational and biological behavior.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [268] [Quantum Quenches across the Bose-glass Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05589)
*Bo Song,Shaurya Bhave,Emmanuel Gottlob,Leanne Reeve,Ulrich Schneider*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了超冷玻色子在光学准晶中穿越超流-玻色玻璃相变后的非平衡动力学，发现快淬灭进入玻色玻璃相会引发动量空间的量子行走，而淬灭进入超流相则引发实空间量子行走并形成由Lieb-Robinson界限制的光锥结构。特征时间尺度与哈密顿量密切相关，在玻色玻璃相中主要受准无序强度控制，在超流相中受隧穿强度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准晶系统因其长程序与准无序之间的内在相互作用，为研究新奇量子现象提供了丰富平台。作者希望通过研究超流到玻色玻璃相变前后的量子淬灭动力学，理解准晶系统中量子多体动力学的独特行为。

Method: 使用超冷玻色子加载到光学准晶格中，通过调控淬灭速率穿越超流-玻色玻璃量子相变。分别进行快速淬灭和慢速淬灭实验，在动量空间和实空间观测量子行走行为，并分析特征时间尺度与系统参数（准无序强度、隧穿强度）的关联。

Result: 快速淬灭进入玻色玻璃相诱导动量空间中向更高动量阶扩散的量子行走；淬灭进入超流相则使初始局域原子在实空间形成由Lieb-Robinson界限制的光锥结构。特征时间尺度在玻色玻璃相主要由准无序强度支配，在超流相主要由隧穿强度支配。慢淬灭到更深晶格时观察到跨相变的相干性衰减。

Conclusion: 准晶系统中的动力学行为对淬灭方向和速率高度敏感，其时间尺度反映了底层哈密顿量的本质特征，为理解准晶系统中量子相变和非平衡动力学提供了新的实验视角。

Abstract: Due to their intrinsic interplay between long-range order and quasi-disorder, quasicrystalline systems provide a rich platform for investigating novel quantum phenomena. Here we study the non-equilibrium dynamics following quantum quenches across the superfluid to Bose-glass transition using ultracold bosons in an optical quasicrystal. Fast quenches into the Bose glass regime induce a quantum walk in momentum space that spreads over increasingly higher momentum orders. Conversely, quenches into the superfluid regime initiate a real-space quantum walk of initially localized atoms, forming a light-cone-like structure bounded by Lieb-Robinson limits. Characteristic timescales reveal strong links to the underlying Hamiltonian and are governed primarily by quasi-disorder strength in the Bose glass phase, and by tunneling strength in the superfluid phase. Finally, we analyze slower quenches into deeper lattices and observe the decay of coherence across the phase transition.

</details>


### [269] [Generating quantum error correcting codes from topological pre-thermal scars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05699)
*William N. Faugno,Frank Barrows,Terry A. Loring,Nathan Goldman,Alexander Cerjan*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个端到端框架，通过多体谱局部子（many-body spectral localizer）从稠密多体谱中自动识别量子多体疤痕态，并判定这些疤痕态能否构成可证明的近似量子纠错码。该框架在具备多体分级对称性的系统中，通过寻找在能量和诊断观测量上联合局域的态来识别疤痕，并结合拓扑指标确定其分级子空间。对于合适的同分级疤痕对，分级结构使分级奇错误可精确检测，局域性抑制分级偶错误积的逻辑作用，从而满足近似Knill-Laflamme条件并形成算子代数量子纠错结构。作者在一维和二维密度差依赖玻色子模型及PXP模型中展示了疤痕识别能力，并在二维玻色子系统中显式构造了近似量子码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体疤痕是嵌入在热化谱中的罕见非热态，有望用于量子信息的存储与操控。然而，缺乏一个通用的框架，既能在不了解微观疤痕机制的前提下自动识别这些非热态，又能判断它们何时可以作为量子纠错码使用。

Method: 开发基于多体谱局部子的端到端框架：在具有多体分级对称性的系统中，通过搜索在能量和诊断观测量上联合局域的态来识别候选疤痕态，同时利用拓扑指标的移动来确定疤痕所属的分级子空间。随后，利用分级结构使分级奇错误精确可检测，并通过疤痕的局域性抑制分级偶错误积的逻辑作用，得到近似Knill-Laflamme条件和算子代数QEC结构。

Result: 在一维和二维密度差依赖玻色子模型以及PXP模型中验证了疤痕识别框架的有效性，并在二维玻色子系统中显式构造了相应的近似量子纠错码，验证了近似Knill-Laflamme条件和算子代数量子纠错结构的成立。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从识别非热多体态到利用其保护量子信息的系统化路径，将赝谱方法（pseudospectral methods）确立为在相互作用量子系统中发现有用行为的广泛适用工具。

Abstract: Quantum many-body scars are rare nonthermal states embedded within otherwise thermalizing spectra, making them promising candidates for storing and manipulating quantum information. However, a general framework that can both identify such states without prior knowledge of their microscopic scarring mechanism and determine when they enable a quantum error correcting code has remained elusive. Here, we develop an end-to-end framework connecting nonthermal-state discovery to certifiable quantum error correction using a many-body spectral localizer. By searching for states jointly localized in energy and a diagnostic observable in systems with a many-body grading symmetry, the spectral localizer identifies candidate scars within dense many-body spectra in tandem with a topological index whose shifts determine their graded subspace. For suitable pairs of same-graded scars, the grading renders grading-odd errors exactly detectable, while their localization can suppress the logical action of sufficiently local grading-even error products, yielding an approximate Knill--Laflamme condition and an operator-algebra quantum error-correction structure. We demonstrate the scar-identification framework in one- and two-dimensional density-difference-dependent bosonic models and in the PXP model, and explicitly construct the resulting approximate quantum code in the one-dimensional bosonic system. Our results establish a systematic route from identifying nonthermal many-body states to exploiting them for protecting quantum information, and position pseudospectral methods as a broadly applicable tool for discovering useful behaviors in interacting quantum systems.

</details>


### [270] [Dynamical signatures of Hardcore-Boson Supersolid on the Triangular Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06487)
*Xingchuan Zhu,Qingyang Guo,Junsong Sun,Yujun Fu,Xingmin Huo,Yixuan Huang,Shiping Feng,Richard T. Scalettar,Huaiming Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模量子蒙特卡罗模拟和线性自旋波理论,研究了三角晶格硬核玻色-哈伯德模型中超固体的动力学特征,揭示了谱权重在动量空间的独特分离:横向谱在Γ和K点具有无隙的Goldstone模,纵向谱在K点具有无隙模并在M点具有类rotor极小值。虚时域中横向谱线在Γ和K点的共存是超固体的明确特征。强量子涨落会破坏尖锐准粒子图像,导致不对称线形和宽连续谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超固相(同时具有对角长程序和超流性)是一种奇特的量子物态,其在三角晶格硬核玻色-哈伯德模型中虽已被预测,但其动力学谱的明确特征(尤其是谱权重在动量空间中的分布模式)尚未被系统建立,需要明确这些特征以加深对量子磁性材料中自旋超固体类似物的理解。

Method: 采用大规模量子蒙特卡罗(QMC)模拟结合线性自旋波理论(LSWT),研究三角晶格硬核玻色-哈伯德模型的横向与纵向动力学谱,并通过直接分析虚时(imaginary-time)域的相关函数来表征超固相。

Result: 发现了谱权重在动量空间的明确分离:横向谱在Γ和K点显示无隙的Goldstone模,纵向谱在K点有无隙模且在M点有类rotor极小值。虚时域中Γ和K点横向谱线的共存是超固体无法被混淆的特征;强量子涨落还导致准粒子图像的破坏,表现为不对称线形与宽连续谱。

Conclusion: 本文确立了硬核玻色子超固体的明确动力学特征,为三角量子磁体中自旋超固体的实验和理论识别提供了关键见解和参考标准。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical signatures of the supersolid phase in the triangular-lattice hardcore Bose-Hubbard model via large-scale quantum Monte Carlo simulations and linear spin-wave theory. We reveal a distinct momentum-space separation in the spectral weights. The transverse spectrum shows gapless Goldstone modes at the $Γ$ and $K$ points, while the longitudinal spectrum features a gapless mode at $K$ and a roton-like minimum at $M$. Directly in the imaginary-time domain, the supersolid is unambiguously characterized by the coexistence of transverse spectral lines at $Γ$ and $K$. Furthermore, we show that strong quantum fluctuations induce a breakdown of the sharp quasiparticle picture, leading to asymmetric line shapes and broad continua. Our work establishes the definitive dynamical signatures of the hardcore-boson supersolid, providing key insights into analogous spin supersolids in triangular quantum magnets.

</details>


### [271] [Superfluidity in active quantum flocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06515)
*Byjesh N. Radhakrishnan,Reyhaneh Khasseh,Thomas Schmidt,Markus Heyl*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文发现主动量子羊群（active quantum flocks）可以同时展现超流性——除了其定向运动对应的对称性破缺外，还可打破U(1)对称性，产生非对角长程序。通过Lindblad动力学下的硬核玻色子微观模型，推导出单粒子密度矩阵的长波有效描述，证明羊群相会朝展示超流特征的非对角长程序失稳，揭示主动量子物质作为探索非平衡量子物质奇异相的新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动量子物质是生物物理与量子多体物理交叉的新兴领域，结合了生命系统的自组织与量子世界的相干性。此前已发现主动量子系统可展现群集现象（flocking），这是平衡量子物理中不存在的前所未有的集体现象。然而，主动量子羊群是否可能拥有更进一步的量子序（如超流性）尚未被探索，这构成了本研究的核心动机。

Method: 研究采用由Lindblad动力学控制、以硬核玻色子为载体的微观模型。作者推导出单粒子密度矩阵的有效长波长（long-wavelength）描述，并据此分析主动量子羊群中的对称性破缺结构及其失稳条件，以判断是否存在超流性对应的非对角长程序。

Result: 研究证明主动量子羊群除定向运动对应的对称性破缺外，还能额外打破U(1)对称性，产生非对角长程序（off-diagonal long-range order）。羊群相会发展出朝向超流性特征序的失稳，意味着该系统的超流态是可实现的。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了主动量子物质中隐藏的超流序，表明这类系统为发现平衡量子物理中不存在的新奇非平衡量子物质相提供了富有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Active quantum matter has very recently emerged at the intersection between bio- and quantum many-body physics, combining the self-organization of living systems with the coherence of the quantum world. Active quantum systems have been shown to exhibit flocking - a collective phenomenon with no precedent in equilibrium quantum physics. In this work we uncover an unexpected layer of quantum order in active quantum flocks: they can become superfluid. We show that, in addition to the symmetry breaking associated with their directed motion, these flocks can also break an additional U(1) symmetry, giving rise to off-diagonal long-range order. For a microscopic model of active hard-core bosons governed by Lindblad dynamics, we derive an effective long-wavelength description of the single-particle density matrix and demonstrate that the flocking phase develops an instability toward off-diagonal long-range order characteristic of superfluid behavior. Our findings reveal active quantum matter as a promising research direction for discovering exotic nonequilibrium phases of quantum matter.

</details>


### [272] [Nonequilibrium full counting statistics of multicomponent strongly-interacting quantum gases with defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07146)
*Ovidiu I. Patu,Oleksandr Gamayun,Catalin Pascu Moca*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一维多组分不可穿透气体在局部缺陷存在下从二分态释放的完全计数统计。自旋-电荷分离将多元生成函数简化为精确的Fredholm行列式，并通过散射数据同时获得瞬态动力学和非平衡稳态。研究发现密度和电流中存在由结点处多重束缚态引起的持续振荡，并论证了组分间相互作用导致电流随温度的线性低温修正（区别于单组分的二次修正）。平衡态下获得了具有t^{1/4}标度的自旋转移的M-Wright分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维多组分量子气体是研究自旋-电荷分离、非平衡动力学和量子输运的重要平台，尤其是在存在杂质或缺陷时。理解完全计数统计（FCS）可以揭示超越平均值的涨落信息，而此前对于多组分可积体系中含缺陷的FCS研究尚不充分。

Method: 利用自旋-电荷分离机制，将多组分不可穿透气体的多元生成函数约化为Fredholm行列式，其核由时间演化的单粒子轨道构成。通过散射数据表示这些轨道，从而同时处理瞬态动力学和非平衡稳态，并推导长时渐近行为。

Result: 导出了长时渐近的领头阶行为，识别出由结点处多重束缚态引起的密度和电流的持续振荡。发现组分间相互作用导致电流的低温柔和为线性修正（对比单组分情形的二次修正）。平衡态下自旋转移服从M-Wright分布，时间标度为t^{1/4}。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了含局域缺陷的多组分不可穿透气体完全计数统计的精确解析框架，揭示了束缚态驱动的持续振荡现象以及多组分相互作用的独特低温标度行为，为理解一维量子多体系统的涨落与输运提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We investigate the full counting statistics of a one-dimensional multicomponent impenetrable gas released from a bipartite state in the presence of a local defect. Spin--charge separation reduces the multivariate generating function to an exact Fredholm determinant whose kernel is built from time-evolved single-particle orbitals. Expressing these orbitals in terms of scattering data gives access to both transient dynamics and the nonequilibrium steady state. We derive the leading long-time asymptotics and identify persistent oscillations in densities and currents caused by multiple bound states at the junction. We argue that interactions between components lead to a linear low-temperature correction to the current, in contrast to the quadratic correction in the single-component case. At equilibrium, we obtain the M-Wright distribution of spin transfer with $t^{1/4}$ scaling.

</details>


### [273] [Precision tests of nonlinear and stochastic modifications of quantum mechanics with a 1D quantum gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07195)
*Yi Zeng,Angelo Bassi,Grigori E. Astrakharchik,Yanliang Guo,Manuele Landini,Hanns-Christoph Nägerl*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个基于超冷铯-133原子的一维管阵列冷原子平台，用于精密检验薛定谔方程的非线性和随机修改。利用亚稳super-Tonks-Girardeau气体测试客观坍缩模型，预计对连续自发局域化（CSL）坍缩率的灵敏度可达λ_c≈1.8×10⁻¹¹ s⁻¹，比现有最强约束低一个数量级；同时通过探测Tonks-Girardeau气体的对称保护呼吸模频率来检验确定性非线性修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学与经典界面的基本问题——波函数坍缩机制尚不明确，存在两类修改薛定谔方程的候选理论：随机型（如CSL模型）和确定型非线性修改。现有约束要么不够强，要么对坍缩噪声谱的截断敏感性不足，需要更精密的实验平台来测试这些新物理假说。

Method: 利用二维光晶格形成均匀的一维管阵列，装载强关联超冷¹³³Cs原子。两个测量方案：(1) 利用亚稳super-Tonks-Girardeau气体测试CSL坍缩模型；(2) 探测谐振阱中Tonks-Girardeau气体的对称保护呼吸模频率以检验确定性非线性修改。核心优势在于可独立调谐原子数和线性密度，利用两者不同的标度规律将新物理信号与技术系统误差清晰区分。

Result: 基于现有实验装置的校准测试，预测对CSL坍缩率的灵敏度为λ_c≈1.8×10⁻¹¹ s⁻¹（相关长度r_c=10⁻⁷ m），比现有最强约束低约一个数量级，且该约束对坍缩噪声谱的截断具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该冷原子平台可同时检验随机型和确定型两类量子力学修改理论，提供比现有最佳约束更强的灵敏度，为量子力学基础检验开辟了新的实验途径。

Abstract: We propose a cold-atom platform for precision tests of nonlinear and stochastic modifications to the Schrödinger equation, utilizing strongly correlated ultracold $^{133}$Cs in a uniform array of one-dimensional tubes formed by a two-dimensional optical lattice. Objective collapse models can be tested using the metastable super-Tonks-Girardeau gas. Based on a calibration test performed on our existing experimental setup, we project a sensitivity to the Continuous Spontaneous Localization collapse rate of $λ_c \simeq 1.8\times10^{-11}\,\mathrm{s^{-1}}$ at a correlation length of $r_c = 10^{-7}\,$m. This would establish a new bound an order of magnitude below the strongest existing constraint that remains robust against a cutoff in the collapse noise spectrum. Additionally, we can test deterministic nonlinear modifications by probing the symmetry-protected breathing mode frequency of the Tonks-Girardeau gas in harmonic traps. Crucial to both measurement schemes is the capability to independently tune the atom number and the linear density. By exploiting the different scaling laws associated with these parameters, we can cleanly distinguish genuine new physics signals from technical systematics.

</details>


### [274] [Spontaneous counterflow in rotating supersolids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07405)
*Silvia Trabucco,Elena Poli,Massimo Mannarelli,Francesca Ferlaino*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过亥姆霍兹分解表明，旋转超固体中看似矛盾的超流与刚体式响应实际上源于同一个受无旋约束的微观速度场，其中不可压缩分量携带涡旋环量，而可压缩分量由系统固有密度调制产生并驱动反向流，从而为刚体/超流分割提供了微观解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转超固体同时呈现量子化涡旋与晶体有序，这一看似矛盾的状态通常需要借助超流氦中发展出的概念，以超流流动与刚体式运动的双响应来现象学描述，但缺乏对其统一微观机制的理解。

Method: 应用亥姆霍兹分解将微观速度场解耦为无散不可压缩分量（携带涡旋环量）与无旋可压缩分量（由固有密度调制产生），并分别分析无涡旋与有涡旋两种情形下的流体响应。

Result: 无涡旋时，响应完全编码在可压缩场中：液滴随外驱动共转，而间隙流体形成反向流以保持环量为零；超过涡旋成核阈值后，不可压缩分量携带常用量子化环量，可压缩场则抵消涡旋引起的角动量，使总跳变小于无调制凝聚体。

Conclusion: 旋转超固体可被重新表述为一种流动-反流动响应，为现象学上刚体/超流分割提供了微观层面的解释。

Abstract: Rotating supersolids challenge the conventional notion of superfluid flow: quantized vortices coexist with crystalline order arising from the system's intrinsic density modulation. Drawing on concepts developed in the context of superfluid helium, this seemingly paradoxical state is often described phenomenologically in terms of a dual response, combining superfluid flow with rigid-body-like motion. Here we show that these apparently distinct behaviors emerge from a single microscopic velocity field, globally constrained by irrotationality. By applying a Helmholtz decomposition, we disentangle a divergence-free incompressible component, which carries vortex circulation, from a curl-free compressible component generated by the intrinsic density modulation. In absence of vortices, the response is entirely encoded in the compressible field: the droplets co-rotate with the external drive, while the interstitial fluid develops an oppositely directed counterflow that preserves vanishing circulation. Above the vortex nucleation threshold, the incompressible component carries the usual quantized circulation, whereas the compressible field counteracts the vortex-induced angular momentum, reducing the total jump with respect to an unmodulated condensate. Our results recast supersolid rotation in terms of a flow-counterflow response, providing a microscopic interpretation of the phenomenological rigid/superfluid partitioning.

</details>


### [275] [Where Is the \(p\)-Wave Superfluid? Correlated Phase Diagram of Microwave-Shielded Polar Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07744)
*Tao Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了微波屏蔽极性费米分子中手性px±ipy超流性存在的参数区间。通过结合Fermi-hypernetted-chain Euler-Lagrange理论与关联基配对理论，在统一框架中处理关联孔、密度响应、流体稳定性与p波配对。发现最大配对能隙出现在长波自旋不稳定性稳定侧附近，并揭示了单微波匹配方案的内在局限，提出双微波匹配方案可消除此约束，最终将分子p波超流性的探索转化为定量优化问题，为拓扑弱配对相的实现提供了明确路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性px±ipy超流性为拓扑量子物质和非阿贝尔激发提供了直接途径，但配对在强关联和竞争性不稳定性共存时能否存活的参数区域此前尚未解决。作者的目标是确定微波屏蔽极性分子中该参数区域。

Method: 将微观分子相互作用坍缩为普适的准二维形式（由相互作用长度σ和单一无量纲耦合表征），结合Fermi-hypernetted-chain Euler-Lagrange理论与关联基配对理论，统一处理关联孔、密度响应、均匀流体稳定性与p波配对。并将该普适区域映射到单/双微波屏蔽方案。

Result: 最大配对能隙出现在二维密度4×10⁻²/σ²的长波自旋不稳定性稳定侧。单微波匹配时，减弱吸引力到最优配对区间会同时削弱碰撞屏蔽，存在内在局限；双微波匹配通过补偿长程吸引力同时保留大排斥核来消除此约束。并识别了有利于p波超流性的分子质量与偶极矩区域。

Conclusion: 这些结果将分子p波超流性的搜寻转变为定量优化问题，为实验实现拓扑弱配对相提供了明确的理论指导与实验条件。

Abstract: Chiral $p_x\!\pm\! i p_y$ superfluidity in fermionic polar molecules offers a direct route to topological quantum matter with non-Abelian excitations, yet the parameter regime in which pairing survives strong correlations and competing instabilities has remained unresolved. Here we determine this regime for microwave-shielded polar molecules. Under tight confinement along the \(z\) direction, the microscopic molecular interaction collapses onto a universal quasi-two-dimensional form specified by an interaction length $σ$, defined as the position of its attractive minimum, and a single dimensionless coupling. We combine Fermi-hypernetted-chain Euler--Lagrange theory with correlated-basis pairing theory, thereby treating the correlation hole, density response, stability of the homogeneous fluid, and $p$-wave pairing within one framework. The largest pairing gap occurs immediately on the stable side of a long-wavelength spinodal instability at a two-dimensional density $4\times10^{-2}/σ^2$. Mapping this universal regime onto microscopic shielding schemes exposes an intrinsic limitation of single-microwave dressing: weakening the attraction to the optimal pairing range simultaneously weakens collisional shielding. Dual-microwave dressing removes this constraint by compensating the long-range attraction while retaining a large repulsive core. Imposing explicit requirements on collisional loss, confinement, and microwave-amplitude stability, we identify the regime of molecular mass and dipole moment favorable for \(p\)-wave superfluidity and determine experimentally realistic microwave and confinement conditions for its realization. These results turn the search for molecular $p$-wave superfluidity into a quantitative optimization problem and define a route toward the topological weak-pairing phase.

</details>


### [276] [Limit Cycles in a Photonic Dimer with Tuneable Non-Hermitian Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07967)
*Kevin J. H. Peters,Peter Schnorrenberg,Daniel Ehrmanntraut,Nikolas Longen,Julian Schmitt*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文在实验上实现了染料填充双阱微腔中两个光凝聚体之间的可调非厄米（纯耗散）相互作用，并通过与分子库的局域耦合产生了有效耗散光子相互作用。研究表明，相干隧穿与耗散相互作用的耦合可以稳定极限环振荡，绘制的动力学相图揭示了超临界和亚临界Hopf分岔，从而验证了耗散相互作用作为驱动-耗散系统中组织集体非线性动力学的一种机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保守（厄米）相互作用已被广泛研究并显示出从自束缚到图案形成等丰富的非线性现象，但纯粹耗散（非厄米）相互作用是否也能产生类似的丰富动力学和非常规系统状态在很大程度上仍未被探索。

Method: 在染料填充的双阱微腔中制备两个耦合的光凝聚体，通过局域耦合到分子库产生有效耗散光子相互作用。通过调节库耦合强度并结合相干隧穿，实验绘制了包含稳定不动点和极限环的动力学相图，并建立了实验验证的理论模型以分析分岔行为。

Result: 实验观察到相干隧穿与耗散相互作用之间的相互作用稳定了极限环振荡；通过调节库耦合实现了对相互作用强度的直接控制；动力学相图显示超临界和亚临界Hopf分岔，伴随迟滞、双稳态和可激性现象。

Conclusion: 耗散相互作用能够作为驱动-耗散系统中组织集体非线性动力学的有效机制，为通过受控耗散探索非平衡多体物理开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Interactions govern the emergence of collective behaviour in classical and quantum many-body systems. While conservative interactions are well known to generate nonlinear phenomena ranging from self-trapping to pattern formation, it remains largely unexplored whether purely dissipative -- i.e., non-Hermitian -- interactions can give rise to similarly rich dynamics and nontrivial system states. Here, we experimentally realise tuneable non-Hermitian interactions in two coupled condensates of light confined within a dye-filled double-well microcavity. Local coupling to molecular reservoirs generates the effective dissipative photon interactions. We show that the interplay between coherent tunnelling and interactions stabilises limit-cycle oscillations, a hallmark of nonlinear dynamics traditionally associated with Hermitian nonlinearities. By tuning the reservoir coupling, we map out the dynamical phase diagram comprising stable fixed points and limit cycles, thereby demonstrating direct control over the interaction strength. Our experimentally validated model reveals both supercritical and subcritical Hopf bifurcations, giving rise to hysteresis, bistability and excitability. These results validate dissipative interactions as a mechanism for organising collective nonlinear dynamics in driven-dissipative systems and pave the way towards exploring nonequilibrium many-body physics through controlled dissipation.

</details>


### [277] [Increase of Magnetic Trap Loading Efficiency for $^{39}\mathrm{K}$ Bose--Einstein Condensation Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08208)
*Yihao Cheng,Xiaoyang Shen,Weixin Li,Hao Du,Hongwu Zhao,Lin Xia*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该研究通过优化磁阱装载过程，将装载效率提高了约15%，并在光阱中蒸发冷却后获得了约1.2×10^5个原子的近纯^39K玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实验过程中，较大的原子数对于生成高质量的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体至关重要。

Method: 通过优化磁阱（MT）装载过程来提高装载效率。由于装载时间远短于原子在磁阱中的寿命，装载过程中因有限阱寿命导致的原子损失可以忽略不计。

Result: 磁阱装载效率提高了约15%，随后在光阱中经蒸发冷却获得了约1.2×10^5个原子的近纯^39K凝聚体。

Conclusion: 优化磁阱装载过程可以显著提高装载效率，从而有利于在后续光阱蒸发冷却中制备高品质、大原子数的^39K玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体。

Abstract: During the experimental sequence, a large atom number is important for the creation of high-quality Bose--Einstein condensates. We report an experimental increase of about 15% in magnetic-trap (MT) loading efficiency by optimizing the MT loading process. The loading time is much shorter than the lifetime of the atoms in the MT, which means atom loss during loading due to the finite trap lifetime can be neglected. We obtain an almost pure $^{39}\mathrm{K}$ condensate with $1.2\times10^{5}$ atoms after evaporative cooling in our optical trap.

</details>


### [278] [Large-Chern-number flat bands, anomalous Dirac cones, and unconventional superfluidity in square-lattice systems with SU(N) non-Abelian gauge fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08223)
*Wentao Zhong,Zhongbo Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维方格上均匀SU(N)非阿贝尔规范场下的拓扑能带结构与超流相。以SU(4)模型（对应Hofstadter模型通量α=1/4）为例，发现最低与最高能带为携带Chern数C=-4的孤立Chern带，两条中间能带虽接触形成16个无质量Dirac锥，但其总Chern数为C=8。推广到SU(N)系统后揭示了能带拓扑的奇偶结构：N为奇数时所有能带均孤立且携带非零Chern数；N为偶数时两条中间能带在N²个Dirac点处接触。随N增大，最高和最低能带趋于平坦且Berry曲率更均匀，为分数Chern绝缘相提供了平台。进一步研究spin-3/2 SU(4)模型中的吸引Hubbard相互作用，发现非阿贝尔规范场破坏五重态配对的隐藏SO(5)简并，选出不同的向列超流态，且中间能带填充区域的谱可承载拓扑保护的Bogoliubov费米面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索均匀SU(N)非阿贝尔规范场在二维方格系统中产生的丰富拓扑能带结构，并研究其与吸引相互作用结合时可能出现的非常规超流相，为合成SU(N)冷原子系统提供拓扑能带物理与超流性的研究起点。

Method: 从与Hofstadter模型（通量α=1/4）相关的SU(4)规范场模型出发，通过能带拓扑分析计算Chern数及边缘态；推广至一般SU(N)系统以揭示奇偶结构；采用数值对角化等方法研究带平坦度与Berry曲率；对spin-3/2 SU(4)模型引入on-site吸引Hubbard相互作用，分析部分填充下的超流相与Bogoliubov费米面。

Result: SU(4)模型中最低/最高带为Chern数C=-4的孤立Chern带并产生四个手性边缘态；中间两带接触形成16个Dirac锥，组合Chern数为C=8。SU(N)推广显示奇偶结构：N奇时所有带孤立且拓扑非平凡，N偶时中间两带在N²个Dirac点接触。N增大时最上/最低带趋于平带且Berry曲率更均匀。SU(4)吸引相互作用下，规范场破坏SO(5)简并选出向列超流态，中带填充区域出现Bogoliubov费米面。

Conclusion: 均匀SU(N)非阿贝尔规范场系统提供了丰富的能带拓扑结构（含奇偶依赖特性）和可调的平台以实现分数Chern绝缘相及非常规超流态（包括向列超流与Bogoliubov费米面），为合成SU(N)冷原子系统的实验探索奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We study topological band structures and superfluid phases in two-dimensional square-lattice systems with homogeneous SU($N$) non-Abelian gauge fields. Starting from an SU(4) gauge-field model related to the Hofstadter model with flux $α=1/4$, we show that the lowest and highest bands are isolated Chern bands that carry Chern numbers $C=-4$ and give rise to four chiral edge modes in a strip geometry. Remarkably, although the two middle bands touch and form $16$ gapless Dirac cones, their combined Chern number is $C=8$. We then generalize the construction to SU($N$) systems and reveal an even--odd structure of the band topology: when $N$ is odd, all bands are isolated and carry nonzero Chern numbers; when $N$ is even, the two middle bands touch at $N^{2}$ Dirac points, while all other bands remain isolated and topologically nontrivial. We find that the uppermost and lowermost bands become increasingly flat and their Berry curvature becomes more uniform as $N$ increases, providing a promising platform for realizing fractional Chern insulating phases. We further examine the spin-$3/2$ SU(4) model with on-site attractive Hubbard interactions, exploring its superfluid phases at partial filling. We find that the non-Abelian gauge field breaks the hidden SO(5) degeneracy of the quintet pairing and selects distinct nematic superfluid states. For fillings in the middle-band regime, the resulting spectrum can host topologically protected Bogoliubov Fermi surfaces. Our results provide a starting point for exploring both topological band physics and unconventional superfluidity in synthetic SU($N$) cold-atom systems.

</details>


### [279] [Exciton-polariton condensates in epsilon-near-zero cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08655)
*Ege Özgün*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于epsilon-near-zero（ENZ）人工腔的平台，可在ENZ频率附近增强光-物质耦合，并理论上论证了该平台实现激子极化激元凝聚的可能性。通过基于驱动-耗散库方法的速率方程模型计算凝聚泵浦阈值，并与分布式布拉格反射（DBR）腔进行优劣对比，同时讨论了平均场理论在模型中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索新型腔体平台以增强光-物质耦合并实现激子极化激元凝聚，弥补传统DBR腔在耦合强度和阈值等方面的局限性。

Method: 提出ENZ人工腔结构，利用驱动-耗散库方法的速率方程模型计算凝聚泵浦阈值，并与DBR腔进行对比分析，同时评估平均场理论的适用性。

Result: 理论上证明了ENZ人工腔平台可实现激子极化激元凝聚，获得了泵浦阈值并与DBR腔进行了优缺点比较，验证了平均场理论的适用范围。

Conclusion: ENZ人工腔为增强光-物质耦合和实现低阈值激子极化激元凝聚提供了有前景的替代平台，但需要根据具体条件权衡其与DBR腔的优劣。

Abstract: We propose an epsilon-near-zero (ENZ) artificial cavity that can enhance light-matter coupling around the ENZ frequency and theoretically show the possibility of exciton-polariton condensation in the suggested platform. By using a rate equation model based on the driven-dissipative reservoir approach, we calculate the pump threshold for condensation and then compare the suggested platform's advantages/disadvantages with distributed Bragg reflector cavities. We also discuss the validity of the mean field theory within our model.

</details>


### [280] [Renormalization of One-Dimensional Semirelativistic Bosons with Contact Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09068)
*Fatih Erman,O. Teoman Turgut*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维自旋less玻色子，其具有半相对论性的自旋less-Salpeter色散关系及吸引性的成对接触相互作用。由于大动量下色散趋于线性，接触相互作用按幂次计数是边缘的并产生对数紫外发散。作者通过扩展Fock空间和Schur补余表示的预解式构造了重整化多体理论，用物理的零总动量两体束缚能消除裸耦合。得到的与截断无关的预解式在每个固定粒子数扇区定义了自伴哈密顿量。他们显式处理了两体问题，并在范数-预解意义上证明了非相对论极限重现吸引性Lieb-Liniger哈密顿量。作者还在重整化理论中直接建立了平均场近似，在无质量和深束缚的大粒子数区域，该近似预测了一种指数增长的束缚尺度，其指数由一个一维变分问题决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机是解决一维半相对论性（spinless-Salpeter色散）自旋less玻色子系统中吸引接触相互作用带来的对数紫外发散问题。由于线性色散使接触相互作用成为边缘算符，标准的非相对论处理方法失效，需要发展一种重整化的多体理论框架来获得截断无关的物理结果。

Method: 作者采用扩展Fock空间和Schur-complement预解式表示法构造重整化理论，以物理的零总动量两体束缚能替代裸耦合常数。通过严格控制每个固定粒子数扇区的自伴性，建立了无截断依赖的哈密顿量。同时推导了非相对论极限与Lieb-Liniger模型之间的关系，并在重整化框架内建立了平均场近似。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 证明非相对论极限在范数-预解意义下收敛于吸引性Lieb-Liniger哈密顿量；(2) 在无质量和深束缚大粒子数区域，平均场近似预测束缚尺度呈指数增长，其指数由一维变分问题决定；(3) 在每一固定粒子数扇区中构造了自伴的重整化哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 该工作为半相对论性色散下的一维多体量子系统提供了严格的重整化框架，弥合了相对论性动理学与量子多体理论之间的鸿沟，并揭示了边缘接触相互作用在强束缚区域导致的非微扰性指数增长行为。

Abstract: We study one-dimensional spinless bosons with semirelativistic spinless-Salpeter dispersion and attractive pairwise contact interactions. Because the dispersion becomes linear at large momentum, the contact interaction is marginal by power counting and produces a logarithmic ultraviolet divergence. We construct the renormalized many-body theory using an enlarged Fock space and a Schur-complement representation of the resolvent, eliminating the bare coupling in favor of the physical zero-total-momentum two-body bound-state energy. The resulting cutoff-independent resolvent defines a self-adjoint Hamiltonian in each fixed particle-number sector. We treat the two-body problem explicitly and show, in the norm-resolvent sense, that the nonrelativistic limit reproduces the attractive Lieb--Liniger Hamiltonian. We also formulate a mean-field approximation directly within the renormalized theory. In the massless and deeply bound large-particle-number regimes, it predicts an exponentially increasing binding scale whose exponent is determined by a one-dimensional variational problem.

</details>


### [281] [Few-body bound states in the anyon-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09125)
*Isaac Tesfaye,Christina Mascherbauer,Joyce Kwan,Perrin Segura,Yanfei Li,Markus Greiner,Luis Santos,André Eckardt,Brice Bakkali-Hassani*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该论文理论上证明了任意子-Hubbard模型（AHM）除已知的玻色子/费米子之外的任意子统计特性外，还存在精确的双体连续谱束缚态（BIC）以及真正的三体和四体束缚态。这些束缚态由纯运动学机制形成，而非传统吸引/排斥相互作用，因此具有快速手性输运特性，并可通过三粒子扩散动力学实验直接探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维系统中的量子统计预言了介于玻色子和费米子之间的任意子（anyon）激发。虽然后者的典型实现是二维拓扑有序态，但一维任意子-Hubbard模型（AHM）中的任意子交换统计也已受到关注，且近期已有实验观测。作者希望进一步揭示AHM中超越传统相互作用束缚态的、由纯运动学机制导致的精确多体束缚态结构及其物理性质。

Method: 作者对任意子-Hubbard模型进行理论分析，证明对于任意有限统计相位角θ≠0，系统存在精确的双体连续谱束缚态（BIC）；同时识别出真正的三体和四体束缚态。文中提供了三体束缚态的简单变分近似方法，并解释其束缚机制。

Result: 发现AHM在任意θ≠0时都承载精确的双体BIC以及真正的三体和四体束缚态。与由吸引（或排斥）相互作用稳定、具有大有效质量的常规束缚态不同，这些集团束缚态由纯运动学机制束缚，赋予其快速手性输运性质。此外，作者展示了从三个局域粒子的膨胀动力学可以直接在实验中探测三体束缚态的签名。

Conclusion: AHM不仅是一维任意子物理的实验实现平台，而且其中的束缚态完全不同于传统相互作用驱动的束缚态：它们是运动学起源的、具有手性输运特征的快速移动团簇。该工作提供了三体束缚态的理论描述及实验探测方案，为理解一维任意子系统的非平凡多体束缚态动力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: Quantum statistics in low-dimensional systems predicts anyonic particles with fractional exchange statistics which are neither that of bosons nor fermions. While anyons are typically found in two dimensions as excitations of topologically-ordered states of matter, anyon-like exchange statistics has also been discussed in one dimension, for instance, in the context of the anyon-Hubbard model (AHM), the physics of which has recently been observed in experiment [Kwan et al., arXiv:2306.01737; Dhar et al., arXiv:2412.21131; and Bakkali-Hassani et al., arXiv:2602.20421]. The AHM can be formulated in terms of bosons featuring density-dependent Peierls phases, described by a statistical phase angle $θ$, which controls asymmetric transport and the formation of dynamically bound pairs at finite momentum. Here, we show theoretically that the AHM also hosts exact two-body bound states in the continuum (BICs) for arbitrary $θ\neq 0$, and genuine three- and four-body bound states. Unlike conventional bound states stabilized by attractive (or repulsive) interactions, which are energetically localized with a large effective mass, these clusters here are bound by a purely kinematic mechanism endowing them with fast chiral transport properties. We provide a simple variational approximation to the three-body bound states and explain their binding mechanism. Moreover, we show that the signatures of three-body bound states in the AHM can be directly probed experimentally from the expansion dynamics starting from three localized particles.

</details>


### [282] [Anderson orthogonality scaling in the Rabi-driven heavy Fermi polaron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09129)
*Michael Rautenberg,Tobias Krom,Eugen Dizer,Olivier Bleu,Eleonora Lippi,Tilman Enss,Manfred Salmhofer,Lauriane Chomaz,Matthias Weidemüller*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过相干驱动超冷$^6$Li-$^{133}$Cs混合物中的重费米极化子，探测了Anderson正交性突变（AOC）的签名，观察到Rabi频率对驱动强度的幂律依赖关系，且指数与AOC预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AOC是费米海中局域微扰引起巨大多体响应的范式现象，但在实验上探测其签名具有挑战性。作者希望通过利用相干驱动的杂质来观测和研究AOC的特征。

Method: 在超冷$^6$Li-$^{133}$Cs混合物中，通过相干驱动重费米极化子来探测AOC。测量Rabi频率随驱动强度的变化规律，并进行有限温度模拟来验证实验结果。

Result: 观察到Rabi频率对驱动强度呈幂律依赖，指数与AOC理论预测一致；有限温度模拟定量复现了观测的标度行为，表明AOC签名超越理想零温无限质量极限仍然存在；Rabi振荡的阻尼揭示了极化子退相干，表现出非单调的驱动依赖性，与当前理论定性一致。

Conclusion: 相干驱动的杂质可作为通过局域相干控制探索量子多体动力学的多功能探针，为研究AOC等多体现象提供了新的实验途径。

Abstract: The Anderson orthogonality catastrophe (AOC) is a paradigmatic many-body phenomenon in which a local perturbation induces a macroscopic response of a Fermi sea. We probe signatures of the AOC by coherently driving heavy Fermi polarons in an ultracold $^6$Li-$^{133}$Cs mixture. We observe a power-law dependence of the measured Rabi frequency on the drive strength, with exponents consistent with AOC predictions. Finite-temperature simulations quantitatively reproduce the observed scaling, indicating that AOC signatures persist beyond the idealized zero-temperature, infinite-mass limit. The damping of the Rabi oscillations provides access to polaron dephasing and reveals a nonmonotonic drive dependence, qualitatively consistent with current theories. Our results establish coherently driven impurities as a versatile probe of quantum many-body dynamics through local coherent control.

</details>
